论文标题包含关键词 'reward' 的论文列表

[No.1] ARIA: Training Language Agents with Intention-driven Reward Aggregation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: ARI：基于意图驱动奖励聚合的训练语言代理
类别: deep learning
作者: Ruihan Yang;Yikai Zhang;Aili Chen;Xintao Wang;Jiangjie Chen;Siyu Yuan;Deqing Yang;Yanghua Xiao;
机构: Fudan University;ByteDance;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=eumRwpgdMU
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00539
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled agents to perform complex reasoning and decision-making through free-form language interactions. However, in open-ended language action environments (e.g., negotiation or question-asking games), the action space can be formulated as a joint distribution over tokens, resulting in an exponentially large action space. Sampling actions in such a space can lead to extreme reward sparsity, which brings large reward variance, hindering effective reinforcement learning (RL). To address this, we propose ARIA, a method that Aggregates Rewards in Intention space to enable efficient and effective language Agents training. ARIA aims to project natural language actions from the high-dimensional joint token distribution space into a low-dimensional intention space, where semantically similar actions are clustered and assigned shared rewards. This intention-aware reward aggregation reduces reward variance by densifying reward signals, fostering better policy optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ARIA not only significantly reduces policy gradient variance, but also delivers substantial performance gains of an average of 9.95% across four downstream tasks, consistently outperforming offline and online RL baselines.
中摘: 

大型语言模型（LLMs）使智能体能够通过自由形式的语言交互完成复杂的推理与决策。然而，在开放式语言动作环境（如谈判或问答游戏）中，动作空间可被建模为词元的联合分布，从而导致动作空间呈指数级膨胀。在此类空间中采样动作可能导致奖励极度稀疏，进而产生较大的奖励方差，阻碍了有效的强化学习（RL）。为解决此问题，我们提出了 ARIA，一种通过在意图空间中聚合奖励以实现高效且有效的语言智能体训练的方法。ARIA 旨在将自然语言动作从高维联合词元分布空间投影至低维意图空间，在该空间中语义相似的动作被聚合并分配共享奖励。这种意图感知的奖励聚合通过使奖励信号更加密集来降低奖励方差，从而促进更好的策略优化。大量实验表明，ARIA 不仅显著降低了策略梯度方差，还在四个下游任务中带来了平均 9.95% 的显著性能提升，持续优于离线和在线强化学习基线。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.2] SCAN: Self-Denoising Monte Carlo Annotation for Robust Process Reward Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: SCAN: 自去噪蒙特卡洛注释以实现稳健的过程奖励学习
类别: deep learning
作者: Yuyang Ding;Xinyu Shi;Juntao Li;xiaobo liang;Zhaopeng Tu;Min Zhang;
机构: Soochow University;Tencent;Harbin Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ifsyZYYDNs
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16548
英摘: Process reward models (PRMs) offer fine-grained, step-level evaluations that facilitate deeper reasoning processes in large language models (LLMs), proving effective in complex tasks like mathematical reasoning. However, developing PRMs is challenging due to the high cost and limited scalability of human-annotated data. Synthetic data from Monte Carlo (MC) estimation is a promising alternative but suffers from a high noise ratio, which can cause overfitting and hinder large-scale training. In this work, we conduct a preliminary study on the noise distribution in synthetic data from MC estimation, identifying that annotation models tend to both underestimate and overestimate step correctness due to limitations in their annotation capabilities. Building on these insights, we propose Self-Denoising Monte Carlo Annotation (SCAN), an efficient data synthesis and noise-tolerant learning framework. Our key findings indicate that: (1) Even lightweight models (e.g., 1.5B parameters) can produce high-quality annotations through a self-denoising strategy, enabling PRMs to achieve superior performance with only 6% the inference cost required by vanilla MC estimation. (2) With our robust learning strategy, PRMs can effectively learn from this weak supervision, achieving a 39.2 F1 score improvement (from 19.9 to 59.1) in ProcessBench. Despite using only a compact synthetic dataset, our models surpass strong baselines, including those trained on large-scale human-annotated datasets such as PRM800K. Furthermore, performance continues to improve as we scale up the synthetic data, highlighting the potential of SCAN for scalable, cost-efficient, and robust PRM training.
中摘: 

过程奖励模型（PRMs）能够提供细粒度、步骤级的评估，有助于深化大型语言模型（LLMs）的推理过程，在数学推理等复杂任务中被证明行之有效。然而，由于人工标注数据成本高且可扩展性有限，PRMs 的开发面临挑战。利用蒙特卡洛（MC）估计生成的合成数据是一个有前景的替代方案，但其存在高噪声率问题，可能导致过拟合并阻碍大规模训练。在这项工作中，我们对来自 MC 估计的合成数据中的噪声分布进行了初步研究，发现由于标注能力的限制，标注模型往往同时低估和高估步骤的正确性。基于这些见解，我们提出了自去噪蒙特卡洛标注（SCAN），这是一个高效的数据合成与抗噪声学习框架。我们的主要发现表明：（1）即使轻量级模型（例如 15 亿参数）也能通过自去噪策略生成高质量标注，使 PRMs 仅需标准蒙特卡洛估计所需 6% 的推理成本即可实现优越性能；（2）借助我们的鲁棒学习策略，PRMs 能够从这种弱监督中有效学习，在 ProcessBench 上将 F1 分数提升了 39.2 分（从 19.9 提高至 59.1）。尽管仅使用了紧凑的合成数据集，我们的模型仍超越了强大的基线模型，包括那些在 PRM800K 等大规模人工标注数据集上训练的模型。此外，随着合成数据规模的扩大，性能持续提升，这突显了 SCAN 在实现可扩展、成本效益高且鲁棒的 PRM 训练方面的潜力。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.4] DISCOVER: Automated Curricula for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: DISCOVER：自动化的稀疏奖励强化学习课程发现方法
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Leander Diaz-Bone;Marco Bagatella;Jonas Hübotter;Andreas Krause;
机构: ETH Zurich;Max Planck Institute for Intelligent Systems;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=guZBnsKPsw
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19850
英摘: Sparse-reward reinforcement learning (RL) can model a wide range of highly complex tasks. Solving sparse-reward tasks is RL's core premise, requiring efficient exploration coupled with long-horizon credit assignment, and overcoming these challenges is key for building self-improving agents with superhuman ability. Prior work commonly explores with the objective of solving many sparse-reward tasks, making exploration of individual high-dimensional, long-horizon tasks intractable. We argue that solving such challenging tasks requires solving simpler tasks that are relevant to the target task, i.e., whose achieval will teach the agent skills required for solving the target task. We demonstrate that this sense of direction, necessary for effective exploration, can be extracted from existing RL algorithms, without leveraging any prior information. To this end, we propose a method for directed sparse-reward goal-conditioned very long-horizon RL (DISCOVER), which selects exploratory goals in the direction of the target task. We connect DISCOVER to principled exploration in bandits, formally bounding the time until the target task becomes achievable in terms of the agent's initial distance to the target, but independent of the volume of the space of all tasks. We then perform a thorough evaluation in high-dimensional environments. We find that the directed goal selection of DISCOVER solves exploration problems that are beyond the reach of prior state-of-the-art exploration methods in RL.
中摘: 

稀疏奖励强化学习（RL）能够建模广泛的高度复杂任务。解决稀疏奖励任务是强化学习的核心前提，需要高效的探索与长视界信用分配相结合，而克服这些挑战对于构建具有超人类能力的自改进智能体至关重要。先前工作通常以解决大量稀疏奖励任务为目标进行探索，这使得对单个高维、长视界任务的探索变得难以处理。我们认为，解决此类具有挑战性的任务需要先解决与目标任务相关的更简单任务，即完成这些任务将使智能体习得解决目标任务所需的技能。我们证明了，这种对有效探索至关重要的方向感可以从现有的强化学习算法中提取，而无需利用任何先验信息。为此，我们提出了一种定向稀疏奖励目标条件式超长视界强化学习（DISCOVER）方法，该方法选择指向目标任务方向的探索性目标。我们将 DISCOVER 与多臂老虎机中的原则性探索联系起来，形式化地界定了目标任务变得可实现所需的时间，该时间取决于智能体与目标的初始距离，但与所有任务空间的规模无关。随后，我们在高维环境中进行了全面评估。我们发现，DISCOVER 的定向目标选择能够解决先前强化学习中最先进探索方法无法触及的探索问题。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.5] Principled Fine-tuning of LLMs from User-Edits: A Medley of Preference, Supervision, and Reward
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于用户编辑的原理性微调：偏好、监督与奖励的集合
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Dipendra Misra;Aldo Pacchiano;Ta-Chung Chi;Ge Gao;
机构: Databricks;Boston University;;Cornell University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Em9QmNobh0
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19055
英摘: We study how to fine-tune LLMs using user-edit deployment data consisting of a set of context, an agent's response, and user edits. This deployment data is naturally generated by users in applications such as LLMs-based writing assistants and coding agents. The _natural_ origin of user edits makes it a desired source for adapting and personalizing LLMs. In this setup, there emerges a unification of various feedback types namely preferences, supervised labels, and cost that are typically studied separately in the literature. In this paper, we initiate the theoretical investigation of learning from user edits. We first derive bounds for learning algorithms that learn from each of these feedback types. We prove that these algorithms have different trade-offs depending upon the user, data distribution, and model class. We then propose a simple ensembling procedure to jointly learn from these feedback types. On two domains adapted from Gao et al. 2024, we show our ensembling procedure outperforms these methods that learn from individual feedback. Further, we show that our proposed procedure can robustly adapt to different user-edit distributions at test time.
中摘: 

我们研究如何利用由一组上下文、智能体响应及用户编辑构成的用户编辑部署数据来微调大语言模型。此类部署数据是在基于大语言模型的写作助手和代码智能体等应用中由用户自然生成的。用户编辑的自然来源使其成为适配和个性化大语言模型的理想数据源。在此设定下，出现了各类反馈类型的统一，即偏好、监督标签和成本，而这些通常在文献中被单独研究。本文率先开展了从用户编辑中进行学习的理论探究。我们首先推导出针对每种反馈类型的学习算法的界限。我们证明这些算法具有不同的权衡，这取决于用户、数据分布和模型类别。随后，我们提出了一种简单的集成方法，以联合利用这些反馈类型进行学习。在两个改编自 Gao 等人 2024 年的领域上，我们表明我们的集成方法优于仅从单一反馈学习的方法。此外，我们表明，所提出的方法能够在测试时稳健地适应不同的用户编辑分布。

[No.12] Offline RL by Reward-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Conversation Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于奖励加权微调的离线对话优化强化学习
类别: deep learning
作者: Subhojyoti Mukherjee;Viet Dac Lai;Raghavendra Addanki;Ryan A. Rossi;Seunghyun Yoon;Trung Bui;Anup Rao;Jayakumar Subramanian;Branislav Kveton;
机构: Adobe;University of Wisconsin;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=WAFD6VYIEa
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06964
英摘: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) is a variant of RL where the policy is learned from a previously collected dataset of trajectories and rewards. In our work, we propose a practical approach to offline RL with large language models (LLMs). We recast the problem as reward-weighted fine-tuning, which can be solved using similar techniques to supervised fine-tuning (SFT). To showcase the value of our approach, we apply it to learning short-horizon question-answering policies of a fixed length, where the agent reasons about potential answers or asks clarifying questions. Our work stands in a stark contrast to state-of-the-art methods in this domain, based on SFT and direct preference optimization, which have additional hyper-parameters and do not directly optimize for rewards. We compare to them empirically, and report major gains in both optimized rewards and language quality.
中摘: 

离线强化学习（RL）是强化学习的一种变体，其策略是从预先收集的轨迹和奖励数据集中学习而来的。在本研究中，我们提出了一种结合大型语言模型（LLMs）的离线强化学习实用方法。我们将该问题重构为奖励加权微调，可采用与监督微调（SFT）相似的技术进行求解。为展示该方法的价值，我们将其应用于学习固定长度的短视界问答策略，其中智能体会推理潜在答案或提出澄清性问题。我们的工作与该领域基于监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化的最先进方法形成了鲜明对比，后者不仅引入了额外的超参数，且未直接针对奖励进行优化。通过实证比较，我们在优化奖励和语言质量方面均取得了显著提升。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.13] On Feasible Rewards in Multi-Agent Inverse Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 多智能体逆强化学习中的可行奖励研究
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Till Freihaut;Giorgia Ramponi;
机构: University of Zurich;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=qu6mRbSnUs
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15046
英摘: Multi-agent Inverse Reinforcement Learning (MAIRL) aims to recover agent reward functions from expert demonstrations. We characterize the feasible reward set in Markov games, identifying all reward functions that rationalize a given equilibrium. However, equilibrium-based observations are often ambiguous: a single Nash equilibrium can correspond to many reward structures, potentially changing the game's nature in multi-agent systems. We address this by introducing entropy-regularized Markov games, which yield a unique equilibrium while preserving strategic incentives. For this setting, we provide a sample complexity analysis detailing how errors affect learned policy performance. Our work establishes theoretical foundations and practical insights for MAIRL.
中摘: 

多智能体逆强化学习（MAIRL）旨在从专家示范中恢复智能体的奖励函数。我们刻画了马尔可夫博弈中的可行奖励集，识别出所有能够解释给定均衡的奖励函数。然而，基于均衡的观测往往存在歧义：单个纳什均衡可能对应多种奖励结构，从而可能改变多智能体系统中的博弈性质。我们通过引入熵正则化马尔可夫博弈来解决这一问题，该设定在保留策略激励的同时能产生唯一均衡。针对该设定，我们提供了样本复杂度分析，详细阐述了误差如何影响学习到的策略性能。我们的工作为 MAIRL 奠定了理论基础并提供了实践启示。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.14] Finite-Sample Analysis of Policy Evaluation for Robust Average Reward Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 有限样本分析下的策略评估在鲁棒平均奖励强化学习中的应用
类别: theory
作者: Yang Xu;Washim Uddin Mondal;Vaneet Aggarwal;
机构: Purdue University;Indian Institute of Technology Kanpur;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=F7BpMMN01x
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16816
英摘: We present the first finite-sample analysis of policy evaluation in robust average-reward Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). Prior work in this setting have established only asymptotic convergence guarantees, leaving open the question of sample complexity. In this work, we address this gap by showing that the robust Bellman operator is a contraction under a carefully constructed semi-norm, and developing a stochastic approximation framework with controlled bias. Our approach builds upon Multi-Level Monte Carlo (MLMC) techniques to estimate the robust Bellman operator efficiently. To overcome the infinite expected sample complexity inherent in standard MLMC, we introduce a truncation mechanism based on a geometric distribution, ensuring a finite expected sample complexity while maintaining a small bias that decays exponentially with the truncation level. Our method achieves the order-optimal sample complexity of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ for robust policy evaluation and robust average reward estimation, marking a significant advancement in robust reinforcement learning theory.
中摘: 

本文首次对鲁棒平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中的策略评估进行了有限样本分析。此前在此设定下的研究仅确立了渐近收敛性保证，使得样本复杂度的问题仍未解决。在本工作中，我们通过证明鲁棒贝尔曼算子在精心构造的半范数下具有收缩性，并构建了一个具有可控偏差的随机近似框架，从而弥补了这一空白。我们的方法基于多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）技术来高效估计鲁棒贝尔曼算子。为了克服标准 MLMC 中固有的无限期望样本复杂度，我们引入了一种基于几何分布的截断机制，在确保期望样本复杂度有限的同时，保持较小的偏差，且该偏差随截断水平呈指数级衰减。我们的方法在鲁棒策略评估和鲁棒平均奖励估计方面达到了 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ 的阶最优样本复杂度，标志着鲁棒强化学习理论的重要进展。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.15] Pairwise Calibrated Rewards for Pluralistic Alignment
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 群体校准奖励以实现多元一致性
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Daniel Halpern;Evi Micha;Ariel D. Procaccia;Itai Shapira;
机构: Google;Harvard University;University of Southern California;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=dtH7hOwTeS
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06298
英摘: Current alignment pipelines presume a single, universal notion of desirable behavior. However, human preferences often diverge across users, contexts, and cultures. As a result, disagreement collapses into the majority signal and minority perspectives are discounted. To address this, we propose reflecting diverse human preferences through a distribution over multiple reward functions, each inducing a distinct aligned policy. The distribution is learned directly from pairwise preference without annotator identifiers or predefined groups. Instead, annotator disagreements are treated as informative soft labels. Our central criterion is pairwise calibration: for every pair of candidate responses, the proportion of reward functions preferring one response matches the fraction of annotators with that preference. We prove that even a small outlier-free ensemble can accurately represent diverse preference distributions. Empirically, we introduce and validate a practical training heuristic to learn such ensembles, and demonstrate its effectiveness through improved calibration, implying a more faithful representation of pluralistic values.
中摘: 

当前的对齐流程假定存在一种单一的、普遍适用的理想行为观念。然而，人类偏好常常在不同用户、情境和文化之间存在差异。因此，分歧会汇聚成多数信号，而少数派观点则被忽视。为解决这一问题，我们提出通过多个奖励函数上的分布来反映多样化的人类偏好，每个奖励函数对应一个独特的对齐策略。该分布直接从成对偏好中学习，无需依赖标注者标识或预定义群体。相反，标注者的分歧被视作具有信息量的软标签。我们的核心标准是成对校准：对于每一对候选回答，偏好其中一个回答的奖励函数比例应与持有该偏好的标注者比例相匹配。我们证明了，即使是一个规模较小且无离群值的集成模型，也能准确表示多样化的偏好分布。实证方面，我们提出并验证了一种学习此类集成模型的实际训练启发式策略，并通过改进的校准效果证明了其有效性，这意味着对多元价值观的表示更加忠实。

[No.16] What Makes a Reward Model a Good Teacher? An Optimization Perspective
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 什么是好的奖励模型？从优化的角度来看
类别: deep learning
作者: Noam Razin;Zixuan Wang;Hubert Strauss;Stanley Wei;Jason D. Lee;Sanjeev Arora;
机构: Princeton University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7pufO0SJAC
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15477
英摘: The success of Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) critically depends on the quality of the reward model. However, while this quality is primarily evaluated through accuracy, it remains unclear whether accuracy fully captures what makes a reward model an effective teacher. We address this question from an optimization perspective. First, we prove that regardless of how accurate a reward model is, if it induces low reward variance, then the RLHF objective suffers from a flat landscape. Consequently, even a perfectly accurate reward model can lead to extremely slow optimization, underperforming less accurate models that induce higher reward variance. We additionally show that a reward model that works well for one language model can induce low reward variance, and thus a flat objective landscape, for another. These results establish a fundamental limitation of evaluating reward models solely based on accuracy or independently of the language model they guide. Experiments using models of up to 8B parameters corroborate our theory, demonstrating the interplay between reward variance, accuracy, and reward maximization rate. Overall, our findings highlight that beyond accuracy, a reward model needs to induce sufficient variance for efficient optimization.
中摘: 

人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）的成功在很大程度上取决于奖励模型的质量。然而，虽然这种质量主要通过准确率进行评估，但准确率是否足以全面衡量一个奖励模型作为有效教师的能力，目前尚不清楚。我们从优化的角度来探讨这一问题。首先，我们证明，无论奖励模型多么准确，如果它导致奖励方差较低，那么 RLHF 目标函数将面临平坦的优化景观。因此，即使一个准确率完美的奖励模型也可能导致优化速度极慢，其表现甚至不如那些能诱导更高奖励方差的准确率较低的模型。此外，我们还表明，一个对某种语言模型效果良好的奖励模型，对于另一种语言模型却可能诱导低奖励方差，从而导致平坦的目标优化景观。这些结果表明，仅依据准确率评估奖励模型，或在脱离其所引导的语言模型的情况下独立评估奖励模型，都存在根本性的局限。使用参数规模最高达 80 亿的模型进行的实验印证了我们的理论，揭示了奖励方差、准确率与奖励最大化速率之间的相互作用。总体而言，我们的发现强调，除准确率外，奖励模型还需要诱导足够的方差以实现高效优化。

像大型语言模型（LLMs）这类高效的对话智能体必须个性化其交互，以适应教育、医疗等不同领域中用户的偏好、性格及属性。诸如基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）之类的当前方法，往往优先考虑有用性和安全性，但在培养真正具有同理心、自适应和个性化的对话方面尚显不足。现有的个性化方法通常依赖大量的用户历史，这限制了它们在新用户或上下文信息有限的用户身上的有效性。为了应对这些局限性，我们提出利用用户模型，将基于好奇心的内在奖励融入多轮强化学习（RLHF）中。这种新颖的奖励机制鼓励 LLM 智能体通过优化对话来提升用户模型的准确性，从而主动推断用户特征。因此，该智能体通过更深入地了解用户，提供更个性化的交互体验。我们在两个截然不同的领域证明了该方法的有效性：在对话推荐任务中显著提升个性化性能，以及在教育环境中根据不同的学习风格个性化对话。与传统的多轮 RLHF 相比，我们展示了该方法改进的泛化能力，同时保持了对话质量。我们的方法为创建更个性化、自适应且更具吸引力的对话智能体提供了一条有前景的解决方案。

来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward

英摘: Language models must be adapted to understand and follow user instructions. Reinforcement learning is widely used to facilitate this -- typically using fixed criteria such as "helpfulness" and "harmfulness". In our work, we instead propose using flexible, instruction-specific criteria as a means of broadening the impact that reinforcement learning can have in eliciting instruction following. We propose "Reinforcement Learning from Checklist Feedback" (RLCF). From instructions, we extract checklists and evaluate how well responses satisfy each item - using both AI judges and specialized verifier programs - then combine these scores to compute rewards for RL. We compare RLCF with other alignment methods applied to a strong instruction following model (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) on five widely-studied benchmarks -- RLCF is the only method to improve performance on every benchmark, including a 4-point boost in hard satisfaction rate on FollowBench, a 6-point increase on InFoBench, and a 3-point rise in win rate on Arena-Hard. [No.20] ReDit: Reward Dithering for Improved LLM Policy Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: ReDit: 提高LLM策略优化的奖励抖动方法
类别: deep learning
作者: Chenxing Wei;Jiarui Yu;Ying Tiffany He;Hande Dong;Yao Shu;Fei Yu;
机构: Shenzhen University;Tencent;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Guangming Lab;GM Research Laboratory;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18631
英摘: DeepSeek-R1 has successfully enhanced Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning capabilities through its rule-based reward system. While it's a ''perfect'' reward system that effectively mitigates reward hacking, such reward functions are often discrete. Our experimental observations suggest that discrete rewards can lead to gradient anomaly, unstable optimization, and slow convergence. To address this issue, we propose ReDit (Reward Dithering), a method that dithers the discrete reward signal by adding simple random noise. With this perturbed reward, exploratory gradients are continuously provided throughout the learning process, enabling smoother gradient updates and accelerating convergence. The injected noise also introduces stochasticity into flat reward regions, encouraging the model to explore novel policies and escape local optima. Experiments across diverse tasks demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of ReDit. On average, ReDit achieves performance comparable to vanilla GRPO with only approximately 10% the training steps, and furthermore, still exhibits a 4% performance improvement over vanilla GRPO when trained for a similar duration. Visualizations confirm significant mitigation of gradient issues with ReDit. Moreover, theoretical analyses are provided to further validate these advantages.
中摘: 

DeepSeek-R1 通过其基于规则的奖励系统成功增强了大型语言模型（LLM）的推理能力。尽管这是一个“完美”的奖励系统，能有效缓解奖励黑客问题，但此类奖励函数往往是离散的。我们的实验观察表明，离散奖励可能导致梯度异常、优化不稳定和收敛缓慢。为解决这一问题，我们提出了 ReDit（奖励抖动），这是一种通过添加简单随机噪声对离散奖励信号进行抖动的方法。利用这种扰动后的奖励，学习过程中持续提供探索性梯度，从而实现更平滑的梯度更新并加速收敛。注入的噪声还在平坦奖励区域引入了随机性，鼓励模型探索新策略并跳出局部最优解。跨多种任务的实验证明了 ReDit 的有效性和效率。平均而言，ReDit 仅需约 10% 的训练步数即可达到与原始 GRPO 相当的性能，此外，在训练时长相近的情况下，其性能仍比原始 GRPO 高出 4%。可视化结果证实，ReDit 显著缓解了梯度问题。此外，我们还提供了理论分析以进一步验证这些优势。

[No.21] ProtInvTree: Deliberate Protein Inverse Folding with Reward-guided Tree Search
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: ProtInvTree：奖励导向树搜索的刻意蛋白质逆折叠
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Mengdi Liu;Xiaoxue Cheng;Zhangyang Gao;Hong Chang;Cheng Tan;Shiguang Shan;Xilin Chen;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Renmin University of China;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Westlake University;Zhejiang University;;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00925
英摘: Designing protein sequences that fold into a target 3D structure, known as protein inverse folding, is a fundamental challenge in protein engineering. While recent deep learning methods have achieved impressive performance by recovering native sequences, they often overlook the one-to-many nature of the problem: multiple diverse sequences can fold into the same structure. This motivates the need for a generative model capable of designing diverse sequences while preserving structural consistency. To address this trade-off, we introduce ProtInvTree, the first reward-guided tree-search framework for protein inverse folding. ProtInvTree reformulates sequence generation as a deliberate, step-wise decision-making process, enabling the exploration of multiple design paths and exploitation of promising candidates through self-evaluation, lookahead, and backtracking. We propose a two-stage focus-and-grounding action mechanism that decouples position selection and residue generation. To efficiently evaluate intermediate states, we introduce a jumpy denoising strategy that avoids full rollouts. Built upon pretrained protein language models, ProtInvTree supports flexible test-time scaling by expanding the search depth and breadth without retraining. Empirically, ProtInvTree outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple benchmarks, generating structurally consistent yet diverse sequences, including those far from the native ground truth.
中摘: 

设计能够折叠成目标三维（3D）结构的蛋白质序列，即蛋白质逆折叠，是蛋白质工程领域的一项根本性挑战。尽管近期的深度学习方法通过恢复天然序列取得了令人瞩目的性能，但它们往往忽略了该问题的一对多本质：多种多样的序列可以折叠成相同的结构。这促使我们需要一种生成模型，能够在保持结构一致性的同时设计多样化的序列。为解决这一权衡问题，我们引入了 ProtInvTree，这是首个用于蛋白质逆折叠的奖励引导树搜索框架。ProtInvTree 将序列生成重构为一种有意识的、逐步的决策过程，使得通过自我评估、前瞻和回溯来探索多种设计路径并利用有前景的候选序列成为可能。我们提出了一种两阶段的聚焦与锚定动作机制，实现了位置选择与残基生成的解耦。为了高效评估中间状态，我们引入了一种跳跃去噪策略，该策略避免了完整的展开过程。基于预训练蛋白质语言模型，ProtInvTree 支持灵活的测试时扩展，无需重新训练即可扩大搜索的深度和广度。实验结果表明，ProtInvTree 在多个基准测试上均优于最先进的基线方法，能够生成结构一致且多样化的序列，其中包括那些与天然真实序列差异较大的序列。

[No.22] Capturing Individual Human Preferences with Reward Features
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 根据奖励特征捕捉个体人类偏好
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Andre Barreto;Vincent Dumoulin;Yiran Mao;Mark Rowland;Nicolas Perez-Nieves;Bobak Shahriari;Yann Dauphin;Doina Precup;Hugo Larochelle;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17338
英摘: Reinforcement learning from human feedback usually models preferences using a reward function that does not distinguish between people. We argue that this is unlikely to be a good design choice in contexts with high potential for disagreement, like in the training of large language models. We formalise and analyse the problem of learning a reward model that can be specialised to a user. Using the principle of empirical risk minimisation, we derive a probably approximately correct (PAC) bound showing the dependency of the approximation error on the number of training examples, as usual, and also on the number of human raters who provided feedback on them. Based on our theoretical findings, we discuss how to best collect pairwise preference data and argue that adaptive reward models should be beneficial when there is considerable disagreement among users. We also propose a concrete architecture for an adaptive reward model. Our approach leverages the observation that individual preferences can be captured as a linear combination of a set of general reward features. We show how to learn such features and subsequently use them to quickly adapt the reward model to a specific individual, even if their preferences are not reflected in the training data. We present experiments with large language models illustrating our theoretical results and comparing the proposed architecture with a non-adaptive baseline. Consistent with our analysis, the benefits provided by our model increase with the number of raters and the heterogeneity of their preferences. We also show that our model compares favourably to adaptive counterparts, including those performing in-context personalisation.
中摘: 

人类反馈强化学习通常采用不区分个体的奖励函数来对偏好进行建模。我们认为，在具有高分歧潜力的场景（如大型语言模型的训练）中，这不太可能是一个良好的设计选择。我们形式化并分析了学习一个可针对用户进行定制的奖励模型的问题。利用经验风险最小化原则，我们推导了一个“可能近似正确”（PAC）界，表明近似误差不仅像通常那样依赖于训练样本的数量，还依赖于为其提供反馈的人类评估者的数量。基于我们的理论发现，我们讨论了如何最优地收集成对偏好数据，并论证当用户之间存在显著分歧时，自适应奖励模型应当是有益的。我们还提出了一种自适应奖励模型的具体架构。我们的方法基于这样一个观察：个体偏好可以被捕捉为一组通用奖励特征的线性组合。我们展示了如何学习这些特征，进而利用它们快速将奖励模型适配到特定个体，即使其偏好并未反映在训练数据中。我们开展了大型语言模型实验，以阐释我们的理论结果，并将所提出的架构与非自适应基线进行了对比。与我们的分析一致，该模型带来的效益随着评估者数量的增加及其偏好异质性的增强而提升。我们还表明，我们的模型与自适应同类模型相比表现更优，包括那些执行上下文个性化的模型。

[No.23] Guiding LLM Decision-Making with Fairness Reward Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: 基于公平性奖励模型引导大语言模型决策

英摘: Large language models are increasingly used to support high-stakes decisions, potentially influencing who is granted bail or receives a loan. [No.24] ToolRL: Reward is All Tool Learning Needs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 工具强化学习：奖励是工具强化学习所需的一切

英摘: Current Large Language Models (LLMs) often undergo supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to acquire tool use capabilities. However, SFT struggles to generalize to unfamiliar or complex tool use scenarios. Recent advancements in reinforcement learning (RL), particularly with R1-like models, have demonstrated promising reasoning and generalization abilities. Yet, reward design for tool use presents unique challenges: multiple tools may be invoked with diverse parameters, and coarse-grained reward signals, such as answer matching, fail to offer the finegrained feedback required for effective learning. In this work, we present the first comprehensive study on reward design for tool selection and application tasks within the RL paradigm. We systematically explore a wide range of reward strategies, analyzing their types, scales, granularity, and temporal dynamics. Building on these insights, we propose a principled reward design tailored for tool use tasks and apply it to train LLMs using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Empirical evaluations across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach yields robust, scalable, and stable training, achieving a 17% improvement over base models and a 15% gain over SFT models. These results highlight the critical role of thoughtful reward design in enhancing the tool use capabilities and generalization performance of LLMs. All the codes are released to facilitate future research.
中摘: 

当前大型语言模型（LLMs）通常需经过监督微调（SFT）以获取工具使用能力。然而，SFT 在泛化至不熟悉或复杂的工具使用场景方面存在困难。强化学习（RL）的最新进展，特别是基于类似 R1 的模型，已展现出具有潜力的推理和泛化能力。然而，工具使用中的奖励设计面临独特的挑战：可能调用多个具有不同参数的工具，而像答案匹配这样的粗粒度奖励信号无法提供有效学习所需的细粒度反馈。在本工作中，我们提出了强化学习（RL）范式下针对工具选择与应用任务奖励设计的首个综合性研究。我们系统地探索了广泛的奖励策略，分析了其类型、规模、粒度及时间动态。基于这些见解，我们提出了一种专为工具使用任务设计的、基于原则的奖励方案，并利用组相对策略优化（GRPO）对其进行大型语言模型训练。跨多个不同基准的实证评估表明，我们的方法实现了稳健、可扩展且稳定的训练，相比基座模型提升了 17%，相比 SFT 模型提升了 15%。这些结果突显了精心设计的奖励方案在增强大型语言模型工具使用能力和泛化性能方面的关键作用。所有代码均已开源发布，以促进未来的研究。

[No.26] Open-World Drone Active Tracking with Goal-Centered Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 开放世界无人机目标导向奖励驱动的主动追踪
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Haowei Sun;Jinwu Hu;Zhirui Zhang;Haoyuan Tian;Xinze Xie;Yufeng Wang;Xiaohua Xie;Yun Lin;Zhuliang Yu;Mingkui Tan;
机构: South China University of Technology;Sun Yat-sen University;Harbin Engineering University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Ly2wXKIByI
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00744
英摘: Drone Visual Active Tracking aims to autonomously follow a target object by controlling the motion system based on visual observations, providing a more practical solution for effective tracking in dynamic environments. However, accurate Drone Visual Active Tracking using reinforcement learning remains challenging due to the absence of a unified benchmark and the complexity of open-world environments with frequent interference. To address these issues, we pioneer a systematic solution. First, we propose DAT, the first open-world drone active air-to-ground tracking benchmark. It encompasses 24 city-scale scenes, featuring targets with human-like behaviors and high-fidelity dynamics simulation. DAT also provides a digital twin tool for unlimited scene generation. Additionally, we propose a novel reinforcement learning method called GC-VAT, which aims to improve the performance of drone tracking targets in complex scenarios. Specifically, we design a Goal-Centered Reward to provide precise feedback across viewpoints to the agent, enabling it to expand perception and movement range through unrestricted perspectives. Inspired by curriculum learning, we introduce a Curriculum-Based Training strategy that progressively enhances the tracking performance in complex environments. Besides, experiments on simulator and real-world images demonstrate the superior performance of GC-VAT, achieving a Tracking Success Rate of approximately 72% on the simulator. The benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/SHWplus/DAT_Benchmark.
中摘: 

无人机视觉主动追踪旨在通过基于视觉观测控制运动系统，自主跟随目标物体，为动态环境下的有效追踪提供一种更实用的解决方案。然而，由于缺乏统一基准以及开放世界环境复杂且存在频繁干扰，利用强化学习实现准确的无人机视觉主动追踪仍然具有挑战性。为了解决这些问题，我们开创性地提出了一种系统性解决方案。首先，我们提出了 DAT，这是首个开放世界无人机主动“空对地”追踪基准。该基准涵盖 24 个城市级场景，目标具有类人行为特征，并采用高保真动力学仿真。DAT 还提供了一种数字孪生工具，用于支持无限制的场景生成。此外，我们提出了一种名为 GC-VAT 的新型强化学习方法，旨在提升无人机在复杂场景中的目标追踪能力。具体而言，我们设计了“以目标为中心的奖励”机制，向智能体提供跨视角的精确反馈，使其能够通过不受限的视角扩展感知范围和运动范围。受课程学习启发，我们引入了一种基于课程的训练策略，以逐步增强复杂环境下的追踪性能。此外，在模拟器及真实世界图像上的实验验证了 GC-VAT 的优越性能，其在模拟器上的追踪成功率约为 72%。该基准及代码可通过以下网址获取：https://github.com/SHWplus/DAT_Benchmark。

[No.27] Regret Analysis of Average-Reward Unichain MDPs via an Actor-Critic Approach
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 通过演员-评论员方法分析平均回报单链MDP的后悔分析
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Swetha Ganesh;Vaneet Aggarwal;
机构: Purdue University;Indian Institute of Science;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=rl2aAyJfQz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19986
英摘: Actor-Critic methods are widely used for their scalability, yet existing theoretical guarantees for infinite-horizon average-reward Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often rely on restrictive ergodicity assumptions. We propose NAC-B, a Natural Actor-Critic with Batching, that achieves order-optimal regret of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ in infinite-horizon average-reward MDPs under the unichain assumption, which permits both transient states and periodicity. This assumption is among the weakest under which the classic policy gradient theorem remains valid for average-reward settings. NAC-B employs function approximation for both the actor and the critic, enabling scalability to problems with large state and action spaces. The use of batching in our algorithm helps mitigate potential periodicity in the MDP and reduces stochasticity in gradient estimates, and our analysis formalizes these benefits through the introduction of the constants $C_{\text{hit}}$ and $C_{\text{tar}}$, which characterize the rate at which empirical averages over Markovian samples converge to the stationary distribution.
中摘: 

演员 - 评论家方法因其可扩展性而被广泛应用，然而，针对无限视界平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的现有理论保证往往依赖于限制性遍历性假设。我们提出了 NAC-B，一种带有批处理机制的自然演员 - 评论家方法，它在单链假设下实现了无限视界平均奖励 MDP 中的 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 最优阶遗憾，该假设允许瞬态状态和周期性并存。该假设属于在平均奖励设置下经典策略梯度定理依然成立的最弱假设之一。NAC-B 对演员和评论家均采用函数逼近，使其能够扩展到具有大规模状态和动作空间的问题。我们在算法中采用批处理有助于缓解 MDP 中潜在的周期性，并降低梯度估计中的随机性；我们的分析通过引入常数 $C_{\text{hit}}$ 和 $C_{\text{tar}}$ 形式化了这些益处，这两个常数刻画了基于马尔可夫样本的经验平均收敛到平稳分布的速率。

[No.29] Beyond Scalar Rewards: An Axiomatic Framework for Lexicographic MDPs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 超越标量奖励：序数MDP的公理框架
类别: theory
作者: Mehran Shakerinava;Siamak Ravanbakhsh;Adam Oberman;
机构: McGill University;Mila;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=cUy3tYIOS5
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12049
英摘: Recent work has formalized the reward hypothesis through the lens of expected utility theory, by interpreting reward as utility. Hausner's foundational work showed that dropping the continuity axiom leads to a generalization of expected utility theory where utilities are lexicographically ordered vectors of arbitrary dimension. In this paper, we extend this result by identifying a simple and practical condition under which preferences cannot be represented by scalar rewards, necessitating a 2-dimensional reward function. We provide a full characterization of such reward functions, as well as the general d-dimensional case, in Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) under a memorylessness assumption on preferences. Furthermore, we show that optimal policies in this setting retain many desirable properties of their scalar-reward counterparts, while in the Constrained MDP (CMDP) setting -- another common multiobjective setting -- they do not.
中摘: 

近期研究通过期望效用理论的视角形式化了奖励假设，将奖励诠释为效用。豪斯纳的基础工作表明，放弃连续性公理将导致期望效用理论的一种推广，其中的效用为具有任意维度的字典序有序向量。在本文中，我们扩展了这一结果，通过识别出一个简单且实用的条件，在此条件下偏好无法由标量奖励表示，从而需要采用二维奖励函数。我们在马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）中，基于偏好满足无记忆性假设，提供了此类奖励函数以及一般 d 维情形的完整刻画。此外，我们表明，在此设定下的最优策略保留了许多与其标量奖励对应策略相似的优良性质，而在约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）设定下——另一种常见的多目标设定——则不具备这些性质。

[No.30] Pre-Trained Policy Discriminators are General Reward Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 预训练策略鉴别器是一般性奖励模型
类别: deep learning
作者: Shihan Dou;Shichun Liu;Yuming Yang;Yicheng Zou;Yunhua Zhou;Shuhao Xing;Chenhao Huang;Qiming Ge;haijun Lv;Demin Song;Songyang Gao;Chengqi Lyu;Enyu Zhou;Honglin Guo;Zhiheng Xi;Qipeng Guo;Wenwei Zhang;Tao Gui;Qi Zhang;Xipeng Qiu;Xuanjing Huang;Kai Chen;
机构: ;Fudan University;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Shanghai Innovation Institution;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=efwbxMJ5M6
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05197
英摘: We offer a novel perspective on reward modeling by formulating it as a policy discriminator, which quantifies the difference between two policies to generate a reward signal, guiding the training policy towards a target policy with desired behaviors. Based on this conceptual insight, we propose a scalable pre-training method named Policy Discriminative Learning (POLAR), which trains a reward model (RM) to discern identical policies and discriminate different ones. Unlike traditional reward modeling methods relying on absolute preferences, POLAR captures the relative difference between one policy and an arbitrary target policy, which is a scalable, high-level optimization objective suitable for modeling generic ranking relationships. Leveraging the POLAR pre-training paradigm, we present a series of RMs with parameter scales from 1.8B to 7B. Empirical results show that POLAR substantially outperforms traditional non-pre-trained methods, significantly enhancing RM performance. For instance, POLAR-7B could improve preference accuracy from 54.8% to 81.0% on STEM tasks and from 57.9% to 85.5% on creative writing tasks compared to SOTA baselines. POLAR also shows robust generalization capabilities in RLHF using Reinforcement Fine-tuning (RFT), providing reliable reward signals and markedly enhancing policy performance--improving LLaMa3.1-8B from an average of 47.36% to 56.33% and Qwen2.5-32B from 64.49% to 70.47% on 20 benchmarks. Moreover, scaling experiments reveal a clear power-law relationship between computation and performance, supported by linear correlation coefficients approaching 0.99. The impressive performance, strong generalization, and scaling properties suggest that POLAR is a promising direction for developing general and strong reward models.
中摘: 

我们提出了一种关于奖励建模的新颖视角，将其形式化为策略判别器，通过量化两个策略之间的差异来生成奖励信号，引导训练策略向具有期望行为的目标策略演进。基于这一概念洞察，我们提出了一种名为策略判别性学习（POLAR）的可扩展预训练方法，该方法训练一个奖励模型（RM）以辨别相同策略并区分不同策略。与依赖绝对偏好的传统奖励建模方法不同，POLAR 捕捉单一策略与任意目标策略之间的相对差异，这是一种可扩展的、高层优化目标，适用于建模通用的排序关系。借助 POLAR 预训练范式，我们构建了一系列参数量从 1.8B 到 7B 的奖励模型。实证结果表明，POLAR 显著优于传统的非预训练方法，大幅提升了奖励模型的性能。例如，与最先进（SOTA）基线相比，POLAR-7B 在 STEM 任务上的偏好准确率从 54.8% 提升至 81.0%，在创意写作任务上从 57.9% 提升至 85.5%。POLAR 在基于强化微调（RFT）的人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）中也展现了强大的泛化能力，能够提供可靠的奖励信号，并显著提升策略性能——在 20 个基准测试上，将 LLaMa3.1-8B 的平均表现从 47.36% 提升至 56.33%，将 Qwen2.5-32B 从 64.49% 提升至 70.47%。此外，扩展实验揭示了计算量与性能之间清晰的幂律关系，线性相关系数接近 0.99 为这一关系提供了支持。卓越的性能、强大的泛化能力及扩展特性表明，POLAR 是开发通用且强大的奖励模型的一个有前途的方向。

[No.31] Reward-Instruct: A Reward-Centric Approach to Fast Photo-Realistic Image Generation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于奖励的方法：一种以奖励为中心的快速照片级真实图像生成 approach
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Yihong Luo;Tianyang Hu;Weijian Luo;Kenji Kawaguchi;Jing Tang;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Chinese University of Hong Kong;National University of Singapore;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=qspsIrurcR
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13070
英摘: This paper addresses the challenge of achieving high-quality and fast image generation that aligns with complex human preferences. While recent advancements in diffusion models and distillation have enabled rapid generation, the effective integration of reward feedback for improved abilities like controllability and preference alignment remains a key open problem. Existing reward-guided post-training approaches targeting accelerated few-step generation often deem diffusion distillation losses indispensable. However, in this paper, we identify an interesting yet fundamental paradigm shift: as conditions become more specific, well-designed reward functions emerge as the primary driving force in training strong, few-step image generative models. Motivated by this insight, we introduce Reward-Instruct, a novel and surprisingly simple reward-centric approach for converting pre-trained base diffusion models into reward-enhanced few-step generators. Unlike existing methods, Reward-Instruct does not rely on expensive yet tricky diffusion distillation losses. Instead, it iteratively updates the few-step generator's parameters by directly sampling from a reward-tilted parameter distribution. Such a training approach entirely bypasses the need for expensive diffusion distillation losses, making it favorable to scale in high image resolutions. Despite its simplicity, Reward-Instruct yields surprisingly strong performance. Our extensive experiments on text-to-image generation have demonstrated that Reward-Instruct achieves state-of-the-art results in visual quality and quantitative metrics compared to distillation-reliant methods, while also exhibiting greater robustness to the choice of reward function.
中摘: 

本文致力于解决实现高质量、快速且与复杂人类偏好对齐的图像生成的挑战。尽管扩散模型和蒸馏的最新进展已实现了快速生成，但有效整合奖励反馈以提升可控性和偏好对齐等能力，仍是一个关键难题。现有的针对加速少步生成的奖励引导后训练方法通常视扩散蒸馏损失为不可或缺。然而，在本文中，我们识别出一种有趣且根本性的范式转变：随着条件变得更加具体，设计良好的奖励函数成为训练强大少步图像生成模型的主要驱动力。受此洞察启发，我们引入了 Reward-Instruct，这是一种新颖且简单得令人惊讶的以奖励为中心的方法，用于将预训练的基础扩散模型转换为奖励增强的少步生成器。与现有方法不同，Reward-Instruct 不依赖昂贵且棘手的扩散蒸馏损失。相反，它通过直接从奖励倾斜的参数分布中采样来迭代更新少步生成器的参数。这种训练方法完全避免了昂贵的扩散蒸馏损失，使其有利于扩展到高分辨率图像。尽管简单，Reward-Instruct 却取得了令人惊讶的优异性能。我们在文生图生成上的大量实验表明，与依赖蒸馏的方法相比，Reward-Instruct 在视觉质量和定量指标方面均达到了最先进水平，同时在对奖励函数选择的鲁棒性方面也表现出更强的能力。

[No.32] Risk-Averse Total-Reward Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 风险规避总回报强化学习

英摘: Risk-averse total-reward Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) offer a promising framework for modeling and solving undiscounted infinite-horizon objectives. [No.33] Rectifying Shortcut Behaviors in Preference-based Reward Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于偏好奖励学习中的捷径行为矫正
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Wenqian Ye;Guangtao Zheng;Aidong Zhang;
机构: University of Virginia;Accenture;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=m51t6RKfGH
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19050
英摘: In reinforcement learning from human feedback, preference-based reward models play a central role in aligning large language models to human-aligned behavior. However, recent studies show that these models are prone to reward hacking and often fail to generalize well due to over-optimization. They achieve high reward scores by exploiting shortcuts, that is, exploiting spurious features (e.g., response verbosity, agreeable tone, or sycophancy) that correlate with human preference labels in the training data rather than genuinely reflecting the intended objectives. In this paper, instead of probing these issues one at a time, we take a broader view of the reward hacking problem as shortcut behaviors and introduce a principled yet flexible approach to mitigate shortcut behaviors in preference-based reward learning. Inspired by the invariant theory in the kernel perspective, we propose Preference-based Reward Invariance for Shortcut Mitigation (PRISM), which learns group-invariant kernels with feature maps in a closed-form learning objective. Experimental results in several benchmarks show that our method consistently improves the accuracy of the reward model on diverse out-of-distribution tasks and reduces the dependency on shortcuts in downstream policy models, establishing a robust framework for preference-based alignment.
中摘: 

在基于人类反馈的强化学习中，基于偏好的奖励模型在使大语言模型与人类偏好对齐方面发挥着核心作用。然而，近期研究表明，这些模型容易出现奖励作弊问题，且往往因过度优化而难以良好泛化。它们通过利用捷径获得高奖励分数，即利用训练数据中与人类偏好标签相关但并未真正反映预期目标的虚假特征（例如，回复冗长、顺从的语气或迎合行为）。在本文中，我们未对这些问题逐一探究，而是采取更广阔的视角将奖励作弊视为捷径行为，并提出了一种既严谨又灵活的方法来缓解偏好奖励学习中的捷径行为。受核视角下不变性理论的启发，我们提出了基于偏好奖励不变性的捷径缓解方法（PRISM），该方法在闭式学习目标中学习带有特征映射的群不变核。多个基准上的实验结果表明，我们的方法一致地提升了奖励模型在多种分布外任务上的准确性，并降低了下游策略模型对捷径的依赖，从而为基于偏好的对齐建立了一个鲁棒的框架。

[No.34] Information-Theoretic Reward Decomposition for Generalizable RLHF
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 信息论奖励分解在通用化RLHF中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Liyuan Mao;Haoran Xu;Amy Zhang;Weinan Zhang;Chenjia Bai;
机构: ;University of Texas at Austin;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;China Telecom;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ONebBepE9l
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06020
英摘: A generalizable reward model is crucial in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) as it enables correctly evaluating unseen prompt-response pairs. However, existing reward models lack this ability, as they are typically trained by increasing the reward gap between chosen and rejected responses, while overlooking the prompts that the responses are conditioned on. Consequently, when the trained reward model is evaluated on prompt-response pairs that lie outside the data distribution, neglecting the effect of prompts may result in poor generalization of the reward model. To address this issue, we decompose the reward value into two independent components: prompt-free reward and prompt-related reward. Prompt-free reward represents the evaluation that is determined only by responses, while the prompt-related reward reflects the reward that derives from both the prompt and the response. We extract these two components from an information-theoretic perspective, which requires no extra models. Subsequently, we propose a new reward learning algorithm by prioritizing data samples based on their prompt-free reward values. Through toy examples, we demonstrate that the extracted prompt-free and prompt-related rewards effectively characterize two parts of the reward model. Further, standard evaluations show that our method improves both the alignment performance and the generalization capability of the reward model.
中摘: 

在基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）中，具备泛化能力的奖励模型至关重要，因为它能够正确评估未见过的提示 - 响应对。然而，现有奖励模型缺乏这种能力，因为它们通常是通过拉大被选择响应与被拒绝响应之间的奖励差距进行训练的，同时忽略了响应所依赖的提示。因此，当训练好的奖励模型在超出数据分布的提示 - 响应对上进行评估时，忽略提示的影响可能会导致奖励模型的泛化性能不佳。为了解决这一问题，我们将奖励值分解为两个独立的部分：无提示奖励和与提示相关的奖励。无提示奖励代表仅由响应决定的评估，而与提示相关的奖励则反映了源于提示和响应两者的奖励。我们从信息论的角度提取这两个分量，且该方法不需要额外的模型。随后，我们提出了一种新的奖励学习算法，该算法根据数据样本的无提示奖励值对其进行优先级排序。通过简单示例，我们证明了提取出的无提示奖励和与提示相关的奖励能够有效表征奖励模型的两个组成部分。此外，标准评估结果表明，我们的方法同时提升了奖励模型的对齐性能和泛化能力。

[No.35] Think-RM: Enabling Long-Horizon Reasoning in Generative Reward Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: Think-RM：在生成奖励模型中实现长远推理能力
类别: deep learning
作者: Ilgee Hong;Changlong Yu;Liang Qiu;Weixiang Yan;Zhenghao Xu;Haoming Jiang;Qingru Zhang;Qin Lu;Xin Liu;Chao Zhang;Tuo Zhao;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Google;Amazon;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;OpenAI;Microsoft;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=UfQAFbP6xq
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16265
英摘: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has become a powerful post-training paradigm for aligning large language models with human preferences. A core challenge in RLHF is constructing accurate reward signals, where the conventional Bradley-Terry reward models (BT RMs) often suffer from sensitivity to data size and coverage, as well as vulnerability to reward hacking. Generative reward models (GenRMs) offer a more robust alternative by generating chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales followed by a final reward. However, existing GenRMs rely on shallow, vertically scaled reasoning, limiting their capacity to handle nuanced or complex (e.g., reasoning-intensive) tasks. Moreover, their pairwise preference outputs are incompatible with standard RLHF algorithms that require pointwise reward signals. In this work, we introduce Think-RM, a training framework that enables long-horizon reasoning in GenRMs by modeling an internal thinking process. Rather than producing structured, externally provided rationales, Think-RM generates flexible, self-guided reasoning traces that support advanced capabilities such as self-reflection, hypothetical reasoning, and divergent reasoning. To elicit these reasoning abilities, we first warm-up the models by supervised fine-tuning (SFT) over long CoT data. We then further improve the model's long-horizon abilities by rule-based reinforcement learning (RL). In addition, we propose a novel pairwise RLHF pipeline that directly optimizes policies using pairwise preference rewards, eliminating the need for pointwise reward conversion and enabling more effective use of Think-RM outputs. Experiments show that Think-RM achieves state-of-the-art results on RM-Bench, outperforming both BT RM and vertically scaled GenRM by 8%. When combined with our pairwise RLHF pipeline, it demonstrates superior end-policy performance compared to traditional approaches.
中摘: 

来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）已成为一种强大的后训练范式，用于将大语言模型与人类偏好对齐。RLHF 的一个核心挑战在于构建准确的奖励信号，传统的 Bradley-Terry 奖励模型（BT RMs）通常存在对数据规模和覆盖范围敏感的问题，并且容易受到奖励作弊的影响。生成式奖励模型（GenRMs）提供了一种更稳健的替代方案，通过先生成思维链（CoT）理由，随后给出最终奖励。然而，现有的 GenRMs 依赖于浅层、垂直扩展的推理，限制了其处理细微或复杂（例如推理密集型）任务的能力。此外，其成对偏好输出与需要单点奖励信号的标准 RLHF 算法不兼容。在这项工作中，我们引入了 Think-RM，这是一个训练框架，通过建模内部思维过程，使 GenRMs 能够实现长程推理。Think-RM 并非生成结构化的、外部提供的理由，而是生成灵活的、自我引导的推理轨迹，支持自我反思、假设性推理和发散性推理等高级能力。为了激发这些推理能力，我们首先通过长 CoT 数据的监督微调（SFT）对模型进行预热。随后，我们通过基于规则的强化学习（RL）进一步提升了模型的长程能力。此外，我们提出了一种新颖的成对 RLHF 流程，该流程利用成对偏好奖励直接优化策略，消除了对单点奖励转换的需求，并能更有效地利用 Think-RM 的输出。实验表明，Think-RM 在 RM-Bench 上取得了最先进（SOTA）的结果，优于 BT RM 和垂直扩展的 GenRM 8%。当与我们提出的成对 RLHF 流程结合时，它表现出比传统方法更优越的最终策略性能。

[No.37] Mitigating Reward Over-optimization in Direct Alignment Algorithms with Importance Sampling
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 使用重要性采样减轻直接对齐算法中的奖励过度优化问题

作者: Nguyen Minh Phuc;Ngoc-Hieu Nguyen;Duy Minh Ho Nguyen;Anji Liu;An Mai;Binh T. Nguyen;Daniel Sonntag;Khoa D Doan;

英摘: Direct Alignment Algorithms (DAAs) such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) have emerged as alternatives to the standard Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values. However, these methods are more susceptible to over-optimization, in which the model drifts away from the reference policy, leading to degraded performance as training progresses. This paper proposes a novel importance-sampling approach to mitigate the over-optimization problem of offline DAAs. This approach, called (IS-DAAs), multiplies the DAA objective with an importance ratio that accounts for the reference policy distribution. IS-DAAs additionally avoid the high variance issue associated with importance sampling by clipping the importance ratio to a maximum value. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that IS-DAAs can effectively mitigate over-optimization, especially under low regularization strength, and achieve better performance than other methods designed to address this problem. Our implementations are provided publicly at this link.
中摘: 

直接对齐算法（DAAs），例如直接偏好优化（DPO），已成为将大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类价值观对齐的标准方法——基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）——的替代方案。然而，这些方法更容易受到过度优化的影响，即模型会偏离参考策略，导致随着训练的进行性能下降。本文提出了一种新颖的重要性采样方法，旨在缓解离线直接对齐算法（DAAs）的过度优化问题。该方法被称为（IS-DAAs），它将 DAA 目标函数乘以一个考虑参考策略分布的重要性比率。IS-DAAs 还通过将重要性比率截断至最大值，避免了与重要性采样相关的高方差问题。我们的广泛实验表明，IS-DAAs 能够有效缓解过度优化问题，尤其是在低正则化强度下，并且能够达到比其他旨在解决此问题的方法更好的性能。我们的实现已通过此链接公开提供。

[No.38] Provably Efficient Online RLHF with One-Pass Reward Modeling
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 可验证高效的一次性奖励建模在线RLHF
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Long-Fei Li;Yu-Yang Qian;Peng Zhao;Zhi-Hua Zhou;
机构: 
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=41mBrhy6U4
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07193
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has shown remarkable success in aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences. Traditional RLHF methods rely on a fixed dataset, which often suffers from limited coverage. To this end, online RLHF has emerged as a promising direction, enabling iterative data collection and refinement. Despite its potential, this paradigm faces a key bottleneck: the requirement to continuously integrate new data into the dataset and re-optimize the model from scratch at each iteration, resulting in computational and storage costs that grow linearly with the number of iterations. In this work, we address this challenge by proposing a one-pass reward modeling method that eliminates the need to store historical data and achieves constant-time updates per iteration. Specifically, we first formalize RLHF as a contextual preference bandit and develop a new algorithm based on online mirror descent with a tailored local norm, replacing the standard maximum likelihood estimation for reward modeling. We then apply it to various online RLHF settings, including passive data collection, active data collection, and deployment-time adaptation. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that our method enhances both statistical and computational efficiency. Finally, we design practical algorithms for LLMs and conduct experiments with the Llama-3-8B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct models on Ultrafeedback and Mixture2 datasets, validating the effectiveness of our approach.
中摘: 

来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）在将大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类偏好对齐方面取得了显著成功。传统的 RLHF 方法依赖于固定数据集，往往存在覆盖范围有限的问题。为此，在线 RLHF 已成为一个充满前景的方向，能够实现数据的迭代收集与优化。尽管具有潜力，但该范式面临一个关键瓶颈：需要在每次迭代中持续将新数据整合到数据集中，并从零开始重新优化模型，导致计算和存储成本随迭代次数呈线性增长。在本工作中，我们通过提出一种单次遍历奖励建模方法来应对这一挑战，该方法消除了存储历史数据的需求，并实现了每次迭代的常数时间更新。具体而言，我们首先将 RLHF 形式化为上下文偏好老虎机，并开发了一种基于具有定制局部范数的在线镜像下降的新算法，以取代用于奖励建模的标准最大似然估计。随后，我们将其应用于各种在线 RLHF 场景，包括被动数据收集、主动数据收集以及部署时适应。我们提供了理论保证，表明我们的方法同时提升了统计和计算效率。最后，我们设计了针对大型语言模型的实用算法，并在 Ultrafeedback 和 Mixture2 数据集上使用 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 和 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 模型进行了实验，验证了该方法的有效性。

来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: Reward model (RM) plays a pivotal role in reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF) for aligning large language models (LLMs). However, classical RMs trained on human preferences are vulnerable to reward hacking and generalize poorly to out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs. By contrast, strong LLM judges equipped with reasoning capabilities demonstrate superior generalization, even without additional training, but incur significantly higher inference costs, limiting their applicability in online RLHF. In this work, we propose an uncertainty-based routing framework that efficiently complements a fast RM with a strong but costly LLM judge. [No.41] LASeR: Learning to Adaptively Select Reward Models with Multi-Arm Bandits
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: LASeR：利用多臂 bandits 适应性选择奖励模型的学习方法
类别: deep learning
作者: Duy Nguyen;Archiki Prasad;Elias Stengel-Eskin;Mohit Bansal;
机构: University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill;University of North Carolina;University of Texas at Austin;
类型: Poster

英摘: Reward Models (RMs) are crucial to aligning large language models (LLMs), but the degree to which an RM specialized to one task (e.g. writing) generalizes to new tasks (e.g. math) is often not known a priori, often making using only one fixed RM to train LLMs suboptimal. However, optimizing LLMs with multiple RMs simultaneously can incur a prohibitively high computational cost and lead to conflicting signals from different RMs that may degrade performance. To address these challenges, we introduce LASeR (Learning to Adaptively Select Rewards), which frames reward model selection as a multi-armed bandit problem, efficiently and iteratively training LLMs using multiple RMs by selecting the most well-suited RM for each instance. On commonsense and math reasoning tasks, we show that LASeR boosts iterative LLM training, improving the absolute average accuracy of Llama-3-8B over three datasets by 2.67% over an ensemble of RM scores while also showing superior efficiency (e.g., a 2x speedup). Moreover, on WildChat (open-ended instruction-following tasks), LASeR leads to a 72.69% AlpacaEval win rate over the RM score ensemble baseline. Extending to long-context generation, LASeR improves by 2.96 F1 points (avg.) on single-document QA tasks and 2.97 F1 points on few-shot learning over the RM score ensemble baseline with best-of-n sampling.
中摘:

[No.43] Limited Preference Data? Learning Better Reward Model with Latent Space Synthesis

命中: reward
标题: 有限偏好数据？通过潜在空间合成学习更好的奖励模型
类别: deep learning
作者: Leitian Tao;Xuefeng Du;Sharon Li;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;Nanyang Technological University;University of Wisconsin;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=dbaYQyruY2
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26074
英摘: Reward modeling, crucial for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences, is often bottlenecked by the high cost of preference data. Existing textual data synthesis methods are computationally expensive. We propose a novel framework LENS for synthesizing preference data directly in the LLM's latent embedding space. Our method employs a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) to learn a structured latent representation of response embeddings. By performing controlled perturbations in this latent space and decoding back to the embedding space, we efficiently generate diverse, semantically consistent synthetic preference pairs, bypassing costly text generation and annotation. We provide theoretical guarantees that our synthesized pairs approximately preserve original preference ordering and improve reward model generalization. Empirically, our latent-space synthesis significantly outperforms text-based augmentation on standard benchmarks, achieving superior results while being 18x faster in generation and using a 16,000x smaller model. Our work offers a scalable and effective alternative for enhancing reward modeling through efficient data augmentation. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/deeplearning-wisc/lens
中摘: 

奖励建模对于将大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类偏好对齐至关重要，但往往受限于偏好数据的高成本。现有的文本数据合成方法计算成本高昂。我们提出了一个名为 LENS 的新颖框架，用于直接在大型语言模型的潜在嵌入空间中合成偏好数据。我们的方法采用变分自编码器（VAE）来学习响应嵌入的结构化潜在表示。通过在潜在空间中执行受控扰动并解码回嵌入空间，我们高效地生成了多样化且语义一致的合成偏好对，从而避免了昂贵的文本生成和标注过程。我们提供了理论保证，证明我们的合成对近似保留了原始偏好排序，并提升了奖励模型的泛化能力。实验表明，在标准基准上，我们的潜在空间合成显著优于基于文本的增强方法，在取得更优结果的同时，生成速度快 18 倍，且使用的模型规模小 16000 倍。我们的工作提供了一种可扩展且有效的替代方案，通过高效的数据增强来增强奖励建模。代码公开可用，网址为 https://github.com/deeplearning-wisc/lens。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.44] Trust, But Verify: A Self-Verification Approach to Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 信任但验证：一种基于可验证奖励的自我验证强化学习方法
类别: deep learning
作者: Xiaoyuan Liu;Tian Liang;Zhiwei He;Jiahao Xu;Wenxuan Wang;Pinjia He;Zhaopeng Tu;Haitao Mi;Dong Yu;
机构: Tencent;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Renmin University of China;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gA3fFAEXNT
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13445
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise in complex reasoning, with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) being a key enhancement strategy. However, a prevalent issue is ``superficial self-reflection'', where models fail to robustly verify their own outputs. We introduce RISE (Reinforcing Reasoning with Self-Verification), a novel online RL framework designed to tackle this. RISE explicitly and simultaneously trains an LLM to improve both its problem-solving and self-verification abilities within a single, integrated RL process. The core mechanism involves leveraging verifiable rewards from an outcome verifier to provide on-the-fly feedback for both solution generation and self-verification tasks. In each iteration, the model generates solutions, then critiques its own on-policy generated solutions, with both trajectories contributing to the policy update. Extensive experiments on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that RISE consistently improves model's problem-solving accuracy while concurrently fostering strong self-verification skills. Our analyses highlight the advantages of online verification and the benefits of increased verification compute. Additionally, RISE models exhibit more frequent and accurate self-verification behaviors during reasoning. These advantages reinforce RISE as a flexible and effective path towards developing more robust and self-aware reasoners.
中摘: 

大型语言模型（LLMs）在复杂推理领域展现出巨大潜力，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是一项关键的增强策略。然而，一个普遍存在的问题是“浅层自我反思”，即模型无法稳健地验证其自身输出。我们提出了 RISE（通过自我验证强化推理），这是一个旨在解决该问题的新颖在线强化学习框架。RISE 在一个单一且整合的强化学习过程中，明确且同时训练大型语言模型，以同步提升其问题解决和自我验证能力。其核心机制在于利用来自结果验证器的可验证奖励，为解决方案生成和自我验证任务提供实时反馈。在每次迭代中，模型首先生成解决方案，随后对其基于当前策略生成的解决方案进行自我批判，这两条轨迹均有助于策略更新。在多样化数学推理基准上的广泛实验表明，RISE 持续提高了模型的问题解决准确率，同时培养了强大的自我验证能力。我们的分析突出了在线验证的优势以及增加验证计算资源的益处。此外，RISE 模型在推理过程中表现出更为频繁和准确的自我验证行为。这些优势进一步确立了 RISE 作为开发更加稳健且具有自我意识推理模型的一条灵活而有效的途径。

[No.45] Unveiling Chain of Step Reasoning for Vision-Language Models with Fine-grained Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 揭示具有细粒度奖励的视觉语言模型的步骤推理链
类别: deep learning
作者: Honghao Chen;Xingzhou Lou;Xiaokun Feng;Kaiqi Huang;Xinlong Wang;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=D8nHwexHNv
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19003
英摘: Chain of thought reasoning has demonstrated remarkable success in large language models, yet its adaptation to vision-language reasoning remains an open challenge with unclear best practices. Existing attempts typically employ reasoning chains at a coarse-grained level, which struggles to perform fine-grained structured reasoning and, more importantly, are difficult to evaluate the reward and quality of intermediate reasoning. In this work, we delve into chain of step reasoning for vision-language models, enabling assessing reasoning step quality accurately and leading to effective reinforcement learning and inference-time scaling with fine-grained rewards. We present a simple, effective, and fully transparent framework, including the step-level reasoning data, process reward model (PRM), and reinforcement learning training. With the proposed approaches, our models set strong baselines with consistent improvements on challenging vision-language benchmarks. More importantly, we conduct a thorough empirical analysis and ablation study, unveiling the impact of each component and several intriguing properties of inference-time scaling. We believe this paper serves as a baseline for vision-language models and offers insights into more complex multimodal reasoning. Our dataset, PRM, and code will be available at https://github.com/baaivision/CoS.
中摘: 

思维链推理在大型语言模型中已取得显著成功，然而将其应用于视觉 - 语言推理仍是一个开放挑战，缺乏明确的最佳实践。现有的尝试通常在粗粒度层面采用推理链，这不仅难以进行细粒度结构化推理，更重要的是，难以评估中间推理步骤的奖励和质量。在这项工作中，我们深入研究了针对视觉语言模型的步骤链推理，从而实现了对推理步骤质量的准确评估，并促成了带有细粒度奖励的有效强化学习与推理时扩展。我们提出了一个简单、有效且完全透明的框架，包含步骤级推理数据、过程奖励模型（PRM）以及强化学习训练。通过所提出的方法，我们的模型确立了强有力的基准，并在具有挑战性的视觉 - 语言基准上实现了持续的性能提升。更重要的是，我们进行了彻底的实证分析与消融实验，揭示了各个组件的影响以及推理时扩展的几个有趣特性。我们相信本文可作为视觉语言模型的基准，并为更复杂的多模态推理提供见解。我们的数据集、PRM 及代码将公开于 https://github.com/baaivision/CoS。

[No.46] MTRec: Learning to Align with User Preferences via Mental Reward Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: MTRec：通过心理奖励模型实现用户偏好的对齐
类别: applications
作者: Mengchen Zhao;Yifan Gao;Yaqing Hou;Xiangyang Li;Pengjie Gu;Zhenhua Dong;Ruiming Tang;Yi Cai;
机构: South China University of Technology;Dalian University of Technology;;Kuaishou Technology;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=1mILyDyPDf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22807
英摘: Recommendation models are predominantly trained using implicit user feedback, since explicit feedback is often costly to obtain. However, implicit feedback, such as clicks, does not always reflect users' real preferences. For example, a user might click on a news article because of its attractive headline, but end up feeling uncomfortable after reading the content. In the absence of explicit feedback, such erroneous implicit signals may severely mislead recommender systems. In this paper, we propose MTRec, a novel sequential recommendation framework designed to align with real user preferences by uncovering their internal satisfaction on recommended items. Specifically, we introduce a mental reward model to quantify user satisfaction and propose a distributional inverse reinforcement learning approach to learn it. The learned mental reward model is then used to guide recommendation models to better align with users' real preferences. Our experiments show that MTRec brings significant improvements to a variety of recommendation models. We also deploy MTRec on an industrial short video platform and observe a 7 percent increase in average user viewing time.
中摘: 

推荐模型主要基于隐式用户反馈进行训练，因为获取显式反馈通常成本高昂。然而，诸如点击之类的隐式反馈并不总能反映用户的真实偏好。例如，用户可能因为标题具有吸引力而点击一篇新闻文章，但在阅读内容后却感到不适。在缺乏显式反馈的情况下，此类错误的隐式信号可能会严重误导推荐系统。在本文中，我们提出了 MTRec，这是一种新颖的序列推荐框架，旨在通过揭示用户对推荐项目的内在满意度来使其与真实用户偏好保持一致。具体而言，我们引入了一个心理奖励模型来量化用户满意度，并提出了一种分布逆强化学习方法来对其进行学习。随后，该学到的心理奖励模型被用于引导推荐模型更好地与用户的真实偏好对齐。我们的实验表明，MTRec 为多种推荐模型带来了显著提升。我们还将 MTRec 部署于一个工业级短视频平台上，观察到用户平均观看时长提升了 7%。

[No.47] Diversity as a Reward: Fine-Tuning LLMs on a Mixture of Domain-Undetermined Data
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 多样性作为奖励：在领域未确定的数据混合上微调LLMs
类别: deep learning
作者: Zhenqing Ling;Daoyuan Chen;Liuyi Yao;Qianli Shen;Yaliang Li;Ying Shen;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Alibaba Group;national university of singaore, National University of Singapore;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=AJJ5fBB7JY
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04380
英摘: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) using diverse datasets is crucial for enhancing their overall performance across various domains. In practical scenarios, existing methods based on modeling the mixture proportions of data composition often struggle with data whose domain labels are missing, imprecise or non-normalized, while methods based on data selection usually encounter difficulties in balancing multi-domain performance. To address these challenges, in this work, we investigate the role of data diversity in enhancing the overall abilities of LLMs by empirically constructing contrastive data pools and theoretically deriving explanations. Building upon the insights gained, we propose a new method that gives the LLM a dual identity: an output model to cognitively probe and select data based on diversity reward, as well as an input model to be tuned with the selected data. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method notably boosts performance across domain-undetermined data and a series of foundational downstream tasks when applied to various advanced LLMs. We release our code and hope this study can shed light on the understanding of data diversity and advance feedback-driven data-model co-design for LLMs.
中摘: 

利用多样化数据集微调大型语言模型（LLMs）对于提升其在各领域的整体性能至关重要。在实际场景中，基于对数据构成混合比例建模的现有方法往往难以应对领域标签缺失、不精确或未标准化的数据，而基于数据选择的方法则通常在平衡多领域性能方面遇到困难。为应对这些挑战，本文通过经验构建对比数据池并进行理论推导，探究了数据多样性在增强大型语言模型整体能力中的作用。基于所得洞察，我们提出了一种新方法，赋予大型语言模型双重身份：一方面作为输出模型，基于多样性奖励对数据进行认知探测与选择；另一方面作为输入模型，利用所选数据进行微调。大量实验表明，将所提方法应用于各种先进大型语言模型时，能显著提升其在领域未定数据及一系列基础下游任务上的表现。我们已开源代码，希望本研究能增进对数据多样性的理解，并推动面向大型语言模型的反馈驱动数据 - 模型协同设计。

[No.48] Improved Regret Bounds for Linear Bandits with Heavy-Tailed Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 改进的线性bandit模型在重尾奖励情况下的遗憾界
类别: theory
作者: Artin Tajdini;Jonathan Scarlett;Kevin Jamieson;
机构: University of Washington;National University of Singapore;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=uih8cWS3JF
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04775
英摘: We study stochastic linear bandits with heavy-tailed rewards, where the rewards have a finite $(1+ε)$-absolute central moment bounded by $\upsilon$ for some $ε\in (0,1]$. We improve both upper and lower bounds on the minimax regret compared to prior work. When $\upsilon = \mathcal{O}(1)$, the best prior known regret upper bound is $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$. While a lower with the same scaling has been given, it relies on a construction using $\upsilon = \mathcal{O}(d)$, and adapting the construction to the bounded-moment regime with $\upsilon = \mathcal{O}(1)$ yields only a $Ω(d^{\fracε{1+ε}} T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$ lower bound. This matches the known rate for multi-armed bandits and is generally loose for linear bandits, in particular being $\sqrt{d}$ below the optimal rate in the finite-variance case ($ε= 1$). We propose a new elimination-based algorithm guided by experimental design, which achieves regret $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d^{\frac{1+3ε}{2(1+ε)}} T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$, thus improving the dependence on $d$ for all $ε\in (0,1)$ and recovering a known optimal result for $ε= 1$. We also establish a lower bound of $Ω(d^{\frac{2ε}{1+ε}} T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$, which strictly improves upon the multi-armed bandit rate and highlights the hardness of heavy-tailed linear bandit problems. For finite action sets, we derive similarly improved upper and lower bounds for regret. Finally, we provide action set dependent regret upper bounds showing that for some geometries, such as $l_p$-norm balls for $p \le 1 + ε$, we can further reduce the dependence on $d$, and we can handle infinite-dimensional settings via the kernel trick, in particular establishing new regret bounds for the Matérn kernel that are the first to be sublinear for all $ε\in (0, 1]$.
中摘: 

我们研究具有重尾奖励的随机线性老虎机，其中奖励具有有限的 $(1+ε)$ 阶绝对中心矩，且由 $\upsilon$ 界定，其中 $ε\in (0,1]$。与先前工作相比，我们改进了极小化极大遗憾的上界和下界。当 $\upsilon = \mathcal{O}(1)$ 时，先前已知的最佳遗憾上界为 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$。虽然已有具有相同缩放比例的下界被提出，但它依赖于使用 $\upsilon = \mathcal{O}(d)$ 的构造，而将该构造适配到 $\upsilon = \mathcal{O}(1)$ 的有界矩情形仅能给出 $Ω(d^{\frac{ε}{1+ε}} T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$ 下界。这与已知的多臂老虎机速率相匹配，但对于线性老虎机通常较为宽松，特别是在有限方差情形（$ε= 1$）下，比最优速率小 $\sqrt{d}$ 倍。我们提出了一种由实验设计指导的新型基于剔除的算法，该算法实现了遗憾 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d^{\frac{1+3ε}{2(1+ε)}} T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$，从而改进了所有 $ε\in (0,1)$ 下对维度 $d$ 的依赖，并在 $ε= 1$ 时恢复了已知的最优结果。我们还建立了一个 $Ω(d^{\frac{2ε}{1+ε}} T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$ 的下界，该下界严格优于多臂老虎机的速率，并突显了重尾线性老虎机问题的难度。对于有限动作集，我们也推导了遗憾的相应改进上界和下界。最后，我们提供了依赖于动作集的遗憾上界，表明对于某些几何结构，例如 $p \le 1 + ε$ 时的 $l_p$-范数球，我们可以进一步降低对维度 $d$ 的依赖；此外，我们可以利用核技巧处理无限维设定，特别是为 Matérn 核建立了新的遗憾界，这是首个在所有 $ε \in (0, 1]$ 范围内均呈现次线性特性的界限。

[No.49] Diffusion Model as a Noise-Aware Latent Reward Model for Step-Level Preference Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于噪声感知潜在奖励模型的扩散模型在步骤级偏好优化中的应用
类别: applications
作者: Tao Zhang;Cheng Da;Kun Ding;Huan Yang;kun jin;Yan Li;Tingting Gao;Di ZHANG;Shiming Xiang;Chunhong Pan;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;;Institute of Automation;Kuaishou Technology;01.AI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=YB9VGCClv9
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01051
英摘: Preference optimization for diffusion models aims to align them with human preferences for images. Previous methods typically use Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as pixel-level reward models to approximate human preferences. However, when used for step-level preference optimization, these models face challenges in handling noisy images of different timesteps and require complex transformations into pixel space. In this work, we show that pre-trained diffusion models are naturally suited for step-level reward modeling in the noisy latent space, as they are explicitly designed to process latent images at various noise levels. Accordingly, we propose the Latent Reward Model (LRM), which repurposes components of the diffusion model to predict preferences of latent images at arbitrary timesteps. Building on LRM, we introduce Latent Preference Optimization (LPO), a step-level preference optimization method conducted directly in the noisy latent space. Experimental results indicate that LPO significantly improves the model's alignment with general, aesthetic, and text-image alignment preferences, while achieving a 2.5-28x training speedup over existing preference optimization methods. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/Kwai-Kolors/LPO.
中摘: 

扩散模型的偏好优化旨在使其与人类对图像的偏好对齐。以往的方法通常使用视觉 - 语言模型（VLMs）作为像素级奖励模型，以近似人类偏好。然而，当用于步级偏好优化时，这些模型在处理不同时间步的噪声图像方面面临挑战，且需要复杂的变换以映射至像素空间。在这项工作中，我们表明预训练扩散模型天然适合在噪声潜空间中进行步级奖励建模，因为它们被明确设计用于处理处于不同噪声水平的潜图像。因此，我们提出了潜奖励模型（LRM），它复用了扩散模型的组件来预测任意时间步潜图像的偏好。基于 LRM，我们提出了潜偏好优化（LPO），这是一种直接在噪声潜空间中进行的步级偏好优化方法。实验结果表明，LPO 显著提高了模型在通用、审美以及图文对齐偏好方面的一致性，同时相较于现有的偏好优化方法，实现了 2.5 至 28 倍的训练加速。我们的代码和模型可在 https://github.com/Kwai-Kolors/LPO 获取。

[No.50] Nabla-R2D3: Effective and Efficient 3D Diffusion Alignment with 2D Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题:  nabla-R2D3：有效的基于2D奖励的3D扩散对齐方法
类别: applications
作者: Qingming LIU;Zhen Liu;Dinghuai Zhang;Kui Jia;
机构: ;Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Dk2qprCnu8
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15684
英摘: Generating high-quality and photorealistic 3D assets remains a longstanding challenge in 3D vision and computer graphics. Although state-of-the-art generative models, such as diffusion models, have made significant progress in 3D generation, they often fall short of human-designed content due to limited ability to follow instructions, align with human preferences, or produce realistic textures, geometries, and physical attributes. In this paper, we introduce Nabla-R2D3, a highly effective and sample-efficient reinforcement learning alignment framework for 3D-native diffusion models using 2D rewards. Built upon the recently proposed Nabla-GFlowNet method, which matches the score function to reward gradients in a principled manner for reward finetuning, our Nabla-R2D3 enables effective adaptation of 3D diffusion models using only 2D reward signals. Extensive experiments show that, unlike vanilla finetuning baselines which either struggle to converge or suffer from reward hacking, Nabla-R2D3 consistently achieves higher rewards and reduced prior forgetting within a few finetuning steps.
中摘: 

生成高质量且照片级真实感的 3D 资产，在 3D 视觉与计算机图形学领域仍是一个长期存在的挑战。尽管最先进的生成模型（例如扩散模型）在 3D 生成方面取得了显著进展，但由于遵循指令的能力有限、与人类偏好对齐不足，或无法生成逼真的纹理、几何结构和物理属性，其产出往往难以媲美人类设计的内容。在本文中，我们提出了 Nabla-R2D3，这是一种针对原生 3D 扩散模型、利用 2D 奖励信号的高度有效且样本高效的强化学习对齐框架。该框架依托于最近提出的 Nabla-GFlowNet 方法，后者以原则性的方式将评分函数与奖励梯度匹配以进行奖励微调；我们的 Nabla-R2D3 使得仅使用 2D 奖励信号即可实现对 3D 扩散模型的有效适配。大量实验表明，与那些要么难以收敛、要么遭受奖励黑客问题的原始微调基线不同，Nabla-R2D3 仅在少数微调步数内就能持续获得更高的奖励，并有效减少了先验遗忘。

[No.51] ReAgent-V: A Reward-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Video Understanding
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: ReAgent-V：一种基于奖励的多代理视频理解框架
类别: applications
作者: Yiyang Zhou;Yangfan He;Yaofeng Su;Siwei Han;Joel Jang;Gedas Bertasius;Mohit Bansal;Huaxiu Yao;
机构: University of North Carolina;Fudan University;University of Washington;University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=D1Iw4Unvfc
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01300
英摘: Video understanding is fundamental to tasks such as action recognition, video reasoning, and robotic control. Early video understanding methods based on large vision-language models (LVLMs) typically adopt a single-pass reasoning paradigm without dynamic feedback, limiting the model's capacity to self-correct and adapt in complex scenarios. Recent efforts have attempted to address this limitation by incorporating reward models and reinforcement learning to enhance reasoning, or by employing tool-agent frameworks. However, these approaches face several challenges, including high annotation costs, reward signals that fail to capture real-time reasoning states, and low inference efficiency. To overcome these issues, we propose ReAgent-V, a novel agentic video understanding framework that integrates efficient frame selection with real-time reward generation during inference. These reward signals not only guide iterative answer refinement through a multi-perspective reflection mechanism-adjusting predictions from conservative, neutral, and aggressive viewpoints-but also enable automatic filtering of high-quality data for supervised fine-tuning (SFT), direct preference optimization (DPO), and group relative policy optimization (GRPO). ReAgent-V is lightweight, modular, and extensible, supporting flexible tool integration tailored to diverse tasks. Extensive experiments on 12 datasets across three core applications-video understanding, video reasoning enhancement, and vision-language-action model alignment-demonstrate significant gains in generalization and reasoning, with improvements of up to 6.9%, 2.1%, and 9.8%, respectively, highlighting the effectiveness and versatility of the proposed framework.
中摘: 

视频理解是动作识别、视频推理和机器人控制等任务的基础。早期的基于大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的视频理解方法通常采用缺乏动态反馈的单次推理范式，限制了模型在复杂场景中自我纠正和适应的能力。最近的研究尝试通过引入奖励模型和强化学习来增强推理，或采用工具 - 智能体框架来解决这一限制。然而，这些方法面临诸多挑战，包括高昂的标注成本、无法捕捉实时推理状态的奖励信号以及低下的推理效率。为克服这些问题，我们提出了 ReAgent-V，这是一种新颖的智能体视频理解框架，它在推理过程中集成了高效的帧选择与实时奖励生成。这些奖励信号不仅通过多视角反思机制指导迭代答案优化——从保守、中立和激进视角调整预测——还能自动过滤高质量数据，用于监督微调（SFT）、直接偏好优化（DPO）和组相对策略优化（GRPO）。ReAgent-V 具有轻量、模块化和可扩展的特点，支持针对多样化任务定制的灵活工具集成。在视频理解、视频推理增强和视觉 - 语言 - 动作模型对齐这三个核心应用的 12 个数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，该方法在泛化和推理方面取得了显著提升，提升幅度分别高达 6.9%、2.1% 和 9.8%，凸显了所提出框架的有效性和多功能性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.52] Systematic Reward Gap Optimization for Mitigating VLM Hallucinations
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 系统化奖励差距优化以减轻大语言模型幻觉问题
类别: deep learning
作者: Lehan He;Zeren Chen;Zhelun Shi;Tianyu Yu;Lu Sheng;Jing Shao;
机构: Beihang University;;Tsinghua University;Shanghai AI Laboratory;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=fJRuMulPkc
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17265
英摘: The success of Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) in mitigating hallucinations in Vision Language Models (VLMs) critically hinges on the true reward gaps within preference pairs. However, current methods, typically relying on ranking or rewriting strategies, often struggle to optimize these reward gaps in a systematic way during data curation. A core difficulty lies in precisely characterizing and strategically manipulating the overall reward gap configuration, that is, the deliberate design of how to shape these reward gaps within each preference pair across the data. To address this, we introduce Topic-level Preference Rewriting(TPR), a novel framework designed for the systematic optimization of reward gap configuration. Through selectively replacing semantic topics within VLM responses with model's own resampled candidates for targeted rewriting, TPR can provide topic-level control over fine-grained semantic details. This precise control enables advanced data curation strategies, such as progressively adjusting the difficulty of rejected responses, thereby sculpting an effective reward gap configuration that guides the model to overcome challenging hallucinations. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate TPR achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple hallucination benchmarks, outperforming previous methods by an average of 20%. Notably, it significantly reduces hallucinations by up to 93% on ObjectHal-Bench, and also exhibits superior data efficiency towards robust and cost-effective VLM alignment. Code and datasets are available at https://tpr-dpo.github.io .
中摘: 

直接偏好优化（DPO）在缓解视觉语言模型（VLMs）幻觉方面的成功，关键取决于偏好对内的真实奖励差距。然而，当前方法通常依赖排序或重写策略，往往难以在数据策展过程中系统地优化这些奖励差距。核心难点在于精确刻画并策略性地操控整体奖励差距配置，即如何在数据中刻意设计每个偏好对内部奖励差距的塑造方式。为解决这一问题，我们引入了主题级偏好重写（TPR），这是一个旨在系统优化奖励差距配置的新框架。通过选择性地将 VLM 响应中的语义主题替换为模型自身重采样的候选项进行针对性重写，TPR 能够实现对细粒度语义细节的主题级控制。这种精确控制使得高级数据策展策略成为可能，例如逐步调整被拒绝响应的难度，从而塑造出有效的奖励差距配置，引导模型克服具有挑战性的幻觉。综合实验表明，TPR 在多个幻觉基准上达到了最先进性能，平均优于先前方法 20%。值得注意的是，它在 ObjectHal-Bench 上将幻觉减少了高达 93%，并且在实现稳健且具成本效益的 VLM 对齐方面展现出优越的数据效率。代码和数据集可在 https://tpr-dpo.github.io 获取。

[No.53] Finite-Time Bounds for Average-Reward Fitted Q-Iteration
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 有限时间界对于平均奖励拟合Q迭代
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Jongmin Lee;Ernest K. Ryu;
机构: Seoul National University;University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=FxyG5xHjxb
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17391
英摘: Although there is an extensive body of work characterizing the sample complexity of discounted-return offline RL with function approximations, prior work on the average-reward setting has received significantly less attention, and existing approaches rely on restrictive assumptions, such as ergodicity or linearity of the MDP. In this work, we establish the first sample complexity results for average-reward offline RL with function approximation for weakly communicating MDPs, a much milder assumption. To this end, we introduce Anchored Fitted Q-Iteration, which combines the standard Fitted Q-Iteration with an anchor mechanism. We show that the anchor, which can be interpreted as a form of weight decay, is crucial for enabling finite-time analysis in the average-reward setting. We also extend our finite-time analysis to the setup where the dataset is generated from a single-trajectory rather than IID transitions, again leveraging the anchor mechanism.
中摘: 

尽管已有大量工作刻画了采用函数逼近的折扣回报离线强化学习的样本复杂度，但以往关于平均奖励设定的研究受到的关注显著较少，且现有方法依赖于诸如马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的遍历性或线性等限制性假设。在本文中，我们首次确立了针对弱连通马尔可夫决策过程的、采用函数逼近的平均奖励离线强化学习的样本复杂度结果，这是一个更为宽松的假设。为此，我们提出了锚定拟合 Q 迭代，该方法将标准拟合 Q 迭代与锚定机制相结合。我们表明，锚定机制（可解释为一种权重衰减形式）对于在平均奖励设定中实现有限时间分析至关重要。此外，我们还利用锚定机制，将有限时间分析扩展到了数据集由单轨迹而非独立同分布转移生成的设定中。
