[No.57] Reward-Aware Proto-Representations in Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于奖励感知的原型表示在强化学习中的应用
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Hon Tik Tse;Siddarth Chandrasekar;Marlos C. Machado;
机构: University of Alberta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=8WdkG7g6Az
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16217
英摘: In recent years, the successor representation (SR) has attracted increasing attention in reinforcement learning (RL), and it has been used to address some of its key challenges, such as exploration, credit assignment, and generalization. The SR can be seen as representing the underlying credit assignment structure of the environment by implicitly encoding its induced transition dynamics. However, the SR is reward-agnostic. In this paper, we discuss a similar representation that also takes into account the reward dynamics of the problem. We study the default representation (DR), a recently proposed representation with limited theoretical (and empirical) analysis. Here, we lay some of the theoretical foundation underlying the DR in the tabular case by (1) deriving dynamic programming and (2) temporal-difference methods to learn the DR, (3) characterizing the basis for the vector space of the DR, and (4) formally extending the DR to the function approximation case through default features. Empirically, we analyze the benefits of the DR in many of the settings in which the SR has been applied, including (1) reward shaping, (2) option discovery, (3) exploration, and (4) transfer learning. Our results show that, compared to the SR, the DR gives rise to qualitatively different, reward-aware behaviour and quantitatively better performance in several settings.
中摘: 

近年来，后继表示（SR）在强化学习（RL）领域日益受到关注，并被用于解决其中的一些关键挑战，如探索、信用分配和泛化。SR 可以被视为通过隐式编码其诱导的转移动态，来表征环境的底层信用分配结构。然而，SR 与奖励无关。在本文中，我们探讨了一种类似的表示，它也考虑了问题的奖励动态。我们研究了默认表示（DR），这是一种近期提出的表示，此前针对其进行的理论和（实证）分析较为有限。在此，我们在表格型情形下为 DR 奠定了一些理论基础，具体包括：（1）推导动态规划和（2）时序差分方法来学习 DR；（3）刻画 DR 向量空间的基础；以及（4）通过默认特征将 DR 形式化地扩展到函数逼近情形。在实证方面，我们分析了 DR 在 SR 已有应用的许多场景中的优势，包括：（1）奖励塑造，（2）选项发现，（3）探索，以及（4）迁移学习。我们的结果表明，与 SR 相比，DR 产生了定性不同且具备奖励感知特性的行为，并在多种场景下实现了定量上更优的性能。

[No.58] Inference-Time Reward Hacking in Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 大型语言模型中的推理时奖励劫持
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Hadi Khalaf;Claudio Mayrink Verdun;Alex Oesterling;Himabindu Lakkaraju;Flavio Calmon;
机构: Harvard University;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=hSX7Dd8dxy
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19248
英摘: A common paradigm to improve the performance of large language models is optimizing for a reward model. Reward models assign a numerical score to an LLM's output that indicates, for example, how likely it is to align with user preferences or safety goals. However, reward models are never perfect. They inevitably function as proxies for complex desiderata such as correctness, helpfulness, and safety. By overoptimizing for a misspecified reward, we can subvert intended alignment goals and reduce overall performance, a phenomenon commonly referred to as reward hacking. In this work, we characterize reward hacking in inference-time alignment and demonstrate when and how we can mitigate it by hedging on the proxy reward. We study this phenomenon under Best-of-$n$ (BoN) and Soft Best-of-$n$ (SBoN), and we introduce Best-of-Poisson (BoP) that provides an efficient, near-exact approximation of the optimal reward-KL divergence policy at inference time. We show that the characteristic pattern of hacking as observed in practice (where the true reward first increases before declining) is an inevitable property of a broad class of inference-time mechanisms, including BoN and BoP. To counter this effect, we introduce HedgeTune, an efficient algorithm to find the optimal inference-time parameter. We demonstrate that hedging mitigates reward hacking and achieves superior reward-distortion tradeoffs on math, reasoning, and human-preference setups.
中摘: 

提升大型语言模型性能的一种常见范式是优化奖励模型。奖励模型为大型语言模型的输出分配一个数值分数，该分数指示了（例如）该输出与用户偏好或安全目标对齐的可能性。然而，奖励模型从不完美。它们不可避免地充当了正确性、有用性和安全性等复杂需求的代理。通过对设定错误的奖励进行过度优化，我们可能会颠覆预期的对齐目标并降低整体性能，这一现象通常被称为“奖励黑客”（reward hacking）。在这项工作中，我们刻画了推理时对齐中的奖励黑客现象，并展示了在何时以及如何通过对代理奖励进行对冲来缓解该问题。我们在最佳-$n$（BoN）和软最佳-$n$（SBoN）框架下研究了这一现象，并引入了泊松最佳（BoP），它能在推理时提供对最优奖励-KL 散度策略的高效、近乎精确的近似。我们表明，实践中观察到的奖励黑客行为的特征模式（真实奖励先升后降）是包括 BoN 和 BoP 在内的一大类推理时机制的必然属性。为了应对这种效应，我们引入了 HedgeTune，一种用于寻找最优推理时参数的高效算法。我们证明，对冲策略缓解了奖励黑客行为，并在数学、推理和人类偏好任务设置上实现了更优的奖励 - 失真权衡。

[No.59] Deployment Efficient Reward-Free Exploration with Linear Function Approximation

类别: theory

[No.60] ResponseRank: Data-Efficient Reward Modeling through Preference Strength Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 数据高效奖励建模通过偏好强度学习
类别: general machine learning
作者: Timo Kaufmann;Yannick Metz;Daniel A. Keim;Eyke Hüllermeier;
机构: Ludwig-Maximilians-Universität München;ETH Zurich;Universität Konstanz;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=3JlBQRvod7
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25023
英摘: Binary choices, as often used for reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), convey only the direction of a preference. A person may choose apples over oranges and bananas over grapes, but which preference is stronger? Strength is crucial for decision-making under uncertainty and generalization of preference models, but hard to measure reliably. Metadata such as response times and inter-annotator agreement can serve as proxies for strength, but are often noisy and confounded. We propose ResponseRank to address the challenge of learning from noisy strength signals. Our method uses relative differences in proxy signals to rank responses to pairwise comparisons by their inferred preference strength. To control for systemic variation, we compare signals only locally within carefully constructed strata. This enables robust learning of utility differences consistent with strength-derived rankings while making minimal assumptions about the strength signal. Our contributions are threefold: (1) ResponseRank, a novel method that robustly learns preference strength by leveraging locally valid relative strength signals; (2) empirical evidence of improved sample efficiency and robustness across diverse tasks: synthetic preference learning (with simulated response times), language modeling (with annotator agreement), and RL control tasks (with simulated episode returns); and (3) the Pearson Distance Correlation (PDC), a novel metric that isolates cardinal utility learning from ordinal accuracy.
中摘: 

二元选择（常用于人类反馈强化学习（RLHF））仅传达偏好的方向。一个人可能选择苹果而非橙子，选择香蕉而非葡萄，但哪种偏好更强呢？偏好强度对于不确定性下的决策制定以及偏好模型的泛化至关重要，但难以可靠地衡量。诸如响应时间和标注者间一致性等的元数据可用作强度的代理，但它们通常具有噪声且存在混淆。我们提出了 ResponseRank，以应对从噪声强度信号中学习所带来的挑战。我们的方法利用代理信号的相对差异，根据推断出的偏好强度对成对比较中的响应进行排序。为控制系统性差异，我们仅在精心构建的分层内局部地比较信号。这使得能够在对强度信号做出最小假设的情况下，稳健地学习与基于强度排名一致的效用差异。我们的贡献主要体现在三个方面：(1) ResponseRank，一种新颖的方法，通过利用局部有效的相对强度信号来鲁棒地学习偏好强度；(2) 跨多种任务的改进样本效率和鲁棒性的实证证据，包括：合成偏好学习（使用模拟响应时间）、语言建模（使用标注者一致性）以及强化学习控制任务（使用模拟回合回报）；(3) 皮尔逊距离相关系数（PDC），一种新颖的指标，用于将基数效用学习与序数准确性区分开来。

[No.67] NaDRO: Leveraging Dual-Reward Strategies for LLMs Training on Noisy Data
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward

[No.69] Enhancing the Outcome Reward-based RL Training of MLLMs with Self-Consistency Sampling
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 通过自我一致性采样增强MLLMs基于奖励的RL训练效果
类别: deep learning
作者: Jiahao Wang;Weiye Xu;Aijun Yang;Wengang Zhou;Lewei Lu;Houqiang Li;Xiaohua Wang;Jinguo Zhu;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong University;University of Science and Technology of China;SenseTime;Shanghai AI Laboratory;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=cGkfMGQdCy
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10648
英摘: Outcome-reward reinforcement learning (RL) is a common and increasingly significant way to refine the step-by-step reasoning of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In the multiple-choice setting - a dominant format for multimodal reasoning benchmarks - the paradigm faces a significant yet often overlooked obstacle: unfaithful trajectories that guess the correct option after a faulty chain of thought receive the same reward as genuine reasoning, which is a flaw that cannot be ignored. We propose Self-Consistency Sampling (SCS) to correct this issue. For each question, SCS (i) introduces small visual perturbations and (ii) performs repeated truncation and resampling of an initial trajectory; agreement among the resulting trajectories yields a differentiable consistency score that down-weights unreliable traces during policy updates. Based on Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct, plugging SCS into RLOO, GRPO, and REINFORCE++ series improves accuracy by up to 7.7 percentage points on six multimodal benchmarks with negligible extra computation. SCS also yields notable gains on both Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct and InternVL3-8B, offering a simple, general remedy for outcome-reward RL in MLLMs.
中摘: 

结果奖励强化学习（RL）是一种常见且日益重要的方法，用于优化多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的逐步推理。在多选题设置——即多模态推理基准的主流格式——中，该范式面临一个显著但常被忽视的障碍：那些在错误思维链后猜中正确选项的不忠实轨迹，与真实推理获得相同的奖励，这是一个不可忽视的缺陷。我们提出自一致性采样（SCS）来纠正这一问题。对于每个问题，SCS（i）引入微小的视觉扰动，（ii）对初始轨迹进行重复截断和重采样；所得轨迹之间的一致性产生了一个可微的一致性分数，该分数在策略更新过程中降低不可靠轨迹的权重。基于 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct，将 SCS 整合至 RLOO、GRPO 和 REINFORCE++ 系列中，在六个多模态基准上将准确率提高了多达 7.7 个百分点，且额外计算开销几乎可忽略不计。SCS 在 Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct 和 InternVL3-8B 上也取得了显著收益，为 MLLMs 中的结果奖励强化学习提供了一种简单、通用的解决方案。

[No.73] Dual-Stage Value-Guided Inference with Margin-Based Reward Adjustment for Fast and Faithful VLM Captioning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于边界值导向的两阶段推断与奖励调整的快速忠实VLM字幕生成
类别: deep learning
作者: Ankan Deria;Adinath Madhavrao Dukre;Feilong Tang;Sara Atito;Sudipta Roy;Muhammad Awais;Muhammad Haris Khan;Imran Razzak;
机构: Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;Jio Institute;Indian Institute Of Technology–Ropar (IIT–Ropar);Monash University;;University of New South Wales;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=KstkQi6e7q
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15649
英摘: Despite significant advances in inference-time search for vision-language models (VLMs), existing approaches remain both computationally expensive and prone to unpenalized, low-confidence generations which often lead to persistent hallucinations. We introduce \textbf{Value-guided Inference with Margin-based Reward (ViMaR)}, a two-stage inference framework that improves both efficiency and output fidelity by combining a temporal-difference value model with a margin-aware reward adjustment. In the first stage, we perform a single pass to identify the highest-value caption among diverse candidates. In the second stage, we selectively refine only those segments that were overlooked or exhibit weak visual grounding, thereby eliminating frequently rewarded evaluations. A calibrated margin-based penalty discourages low-confidence continuations while preserving descriptive richness. Extensive experiments across multiple VLM architectures demonstrate that ViMaR generates captions that are significantly more reliable, factually accurate, detailed, and explanatory, while achieving over 4$\times$ speedup compared to existing value-guided methods. Specifically, we show that ViMaR trained solely on LLaVA Mistral-7B, \textit{generalizes effectively to guide decoding in a stronger unseen model}. To further validate this, we adapt the ViMaR to steer generation in LLaVA-OneVision-Qwen2-7B, leading to consistent improvements in caption quality and demonstrating robust cross-model guidance. This cross-model generalization highlights ViMaR's flexibility and modularity, positioning it as a scalable and transferable inference-time decoding strategy. Furthermore, when ViMaR-generated captions are used for self-training, the underlying models achieve substantial gains across a broad suite of visual comprehension benchmarks, underscoring the potential of fast, accurate, and self-improving VLM pipelines.
中摘: 

尽管视觉 - 语言模型（VLMs）在推理时搜索方面取得了显著进展，但现有方法仍存在计算成本高且容易产生未受惩罚的低置信度生成等问题，往往导致持续性幻觉。我们提出了**基于边际奖励的价值引导推理（ViMaR）**，这是一种两阶段推理框架，通过结合时序差分价值模型与边际感知奖励调整机制，提升了推理效率和输出保真度。在第一阶段，我们执行单次前向遍历，从多样化的候选描述中识别出价值最高的描述。在第二阶段，我们仅选择性优化那些被遗漏或表现出弱视觉关联的片段，从而消除了频繁进行的奖励评估。经过校准的基于边际的惩罚机制能够抑制低置信度的后续生成，同时保持描述的丰富性。在多种 VLM 架构上的广泛实验表明，ViMaR 生成的描述在可靠性、事实准确性、详细程度和解释性方面显著优于现有方法，且相比现有的价值引导方法实现了超过 4 倍的速度提升。具体而言，我们展示了仅在 LLaVA Mistral-7B 上训练的 ViMaR，能够有效地泛化以指导更强未见模型的解码过程。为进一步验证这一点，我们将 ViMaR 适配用于引导 LLaVA-OneVision-Qwen2-7B 的生成，实现了描述质量的一致提升，展示了鲁棒的跨模型引导能力。这种跨模型泛化能力突显了 ViMaR 的灵活性和模块化特性，使其成为一种可扩展、可迁移的推理时解码策略。此外，当利用 ViMaR 生成的描述进行自训练时，基础模型在广泛的视觉理解基准测试中均取得了实质性提升，凸显了快速、准确且具备自改进能力的 VLM 流程的潜力。

[No.77] Asymmetric REINFORCE for off-Policy Reinforcement Learning: Balancing positive and negative rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 不对称REINFORCE在离策强化学习中的应用：平衡正负奖励
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Charles Arnal;Gaëtan Narozniak;Vivien Cabannes;Yunhao Tang;Julia Kempe;Remi Munos;
机构: Meta;Sorbonne Université;Ecole Polytechnique;;Google;INRIA;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Ql3sENn0mi
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20520
英摘: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to align large language models (LLMs). Off-policy methods offer greater implementation simplicity and data efficiency than on-policy techniques, but often result in suboptimal performance. In this work, we study the intermediate range of algorithms between off-policy RL and supervised fine-tuning by analyzing a simple off-policy REINFORCE algorithm, where the advantage is defined as $A=r-V$, with $r$ a reward and $V$ some tunable baseline. Intuitively, lowering $V$ emphasizes high-reward samples, while raising it penalizes low-reward ones more heavily. We first provide a theoretical analysis of this off-policy REINFORCE algorithm, showing that when the baseline $V$ lower-bounds the expected reward, the algorithm enjoys a policy improvement guarantee. Our analysis reveals that while on-policy updates can safely leverage both positive and negative signals, off-policy updates benefit from focusing more on positive rewards than on negative ones. We validate our findings experimentally in a controlled stochastic bandit setting and through fine-tuning state-of-the-art LLMs on reasoning tasks.
中摘: 
强化学习（RL）正日益广泛用于对齐大语言模型（LLM）。离策略方法比同策略技术具有更大的实现简便性和数据效率，但往往会导致次优性能。在这项工作中，我们通过分析一种简单的离策略 REINFORCE 算法，研究了介于离策略强化学习（RL）与监督微调之间的算法中间范围，其中优势定义为 $A=r-V$，$r$ 为奖励，$V$ 为某个可调节的基线。直观上，降低 $V$ 会突出高奖励样本，而提高 $V$ 则会更严厉地惩罚低奖励样本。我们首先对该离策略 REINFORCE 算法进行了理论分析，表明当基线 $V$ 作为期望奖励的下界时，该算法能保证策略改进。我们的分析表明，虽然同策略更新可以安全地利用正负信号，但离策略更新受益于更多地关注正奖励而非负奖励。我们通过受控的随机多臂老虎机设置实验，以及通过对推理任务上的最先进大语言模型进行微调，验证了我们的发现。

[No.79] MRO: Enhancing Reasoning in Diffusion Language Models via Multi-Reward Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: MRO：通过多奖励优化增强扩散语言模型的推理能力
类别: deep learning
作者: Chenglong Wang;Yang Gan;Hang Zhou;Chi Hu;Yongyu Mu;Kai Song;MuRun Yang;Bei Li;Chunliang Zhang;Tongran Liu;JingBo Zhu;Zhengtao Yu;Tong Xiao;
机构: Northeastern University;;Meituan;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Kunming University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ZTfqehfcEJ
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21473
英摘: Recent advances in diffusion language models (DLMs) have presented a promising alternative to traditional autoregressive large language models (LLMs). However, DLMs still lag behind LLMs in reasoning performance, especially as the number of denoising steps decreases. Our analysis reveals that this shortcoming arises primarily from the independent generation of masked tokens across denoising steps, which fails to capture the token correlation. In this paper, we define two types of token correlation: intra-sequence correlation and inter-sequence correlation, and demonstrate that enhancing these correlations improves reasoning performance. To this end, we propose a Multi-Reward Optimization (MRO) approach, which encourages DLMs to consider the token correlation during the denoising process. More specifically, our MRO approach leverages test-time scaling, reject sampling, and reinforcement learning to directly optimize the token correlation with multiple elaborate rewards. Additionally, we introduce group step and importance sampling strategies to mitigate reward variance and enhance sampling efficiency. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that MRO not only improves reasoning performance but also achieves significant sampling speedups while maintaining high performance on reasoning benchmarks.
中摘: 

扩散语言模型（DLMs）的最新进展为传统的自回归大型语言模型（LLMs）提供了一种颇具前景的替代方案。然而，DLMs 在推理性能上仍落后于 LLMs，尤其是在去噪步骤数量减少的情况下。我们的分析表明，这一缺点主要源于去噪步骤之间掩码 Token 的独立生成，这未能捕捉 Token 之间的相关性。本文定义了两种 Token 相关性类型：序列内相关性和序列间相关性，并证明增强这些相关性可以提高推理性能。为此，我们提出了一种多奖励优化（MRO）方法，该方法鼓励 DLMs 在去噪过程中考虑 Token 相关性。更具体地说，我们的 MRO 方法利用测试时扩展、拒绝采样和强化学习，通过多种精心设计的奖励直接优化 Token 相关性。此外，我们引入了分组步骤和重要性采样策略，以缓解奖励方差并提升采样效率。通过大量实验，我们证明 MRO 不仅提高了推理性能，还在保持推理基准上的高性能的同时实现了显著的采样加速。

[No.80] DreamPRM: Domain-reweighted Process Reward Model for Multimodal Reasoning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: DreamPRM：领域加权过程奖励模型在多模态推理中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Qi Cao;Ruiyi Wang;Ruiyi Zhang;Sai Ashish Somayajula;Pengtao Xie;
机构: University of California, San Diego;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ZyiBk1ZinG
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20241
英摘: Reasoning has substantially improved the performance of large language models (LLMs) on complicated tasks. Central to the current reasoning studies, Process Reward Models (PRMs) offer a fine-grained evaluation of intermediate reasoning steps and guide the reasoning process. However, extending PRMs to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) introduces challenges. Since multimodal reasoning covers a wider range of tasks compared to text-only scenarios, the resulting distribution shift from the training to testing sets is more severe, leading to greater generalization difficulty. Training a reliable multimodal PRM, therefore, demands large and diverse datasets to ensure sufficient coverage. However, current multimodal reasoning datasets suffer from a marked quality imbalance, which degrades PRM performance and highlights the need for an effective data selection strategy. To address the issues, we introduce DreamPRM, a domain-reweighted training framework for multimodal PRMs which employs bi-level optimization. In the lower-level optimization, DreamPRM performs fine-tuning on multiple datasets with domain weights, allowing the PRM to prioritize high-quality reasoning signals and alleviating the impact of dataset quality imbalance. In the upper-level optimization, the PRM is evaluated on a separate meta-learning dataset; this feedback updates the domain weights through an aggregation loss function, thereby improving the generalization capability of trained PRM. Extensive experiments on multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks covering both mathematical and general reasoning show that test-time scaling with DreamPRM consistently improves the performance of state-of-the-art MLLMs. Further comparisons reveal that DreamPRM's domain-reweighting strategy surpasses other data selection methods and yields higher accuracy gains than existing test-time scaling approaches.
中摘: 

推理显著提升了大型语言模型（LLMs）在复杂任务上的性能。作为当前推理研究的核心，过程奖励模型（PRMs）能够对中间推理步骤进行细粒度评估，并引导推理过程。然而，将 PRM 扩展至多模态大型语言模型（MLLMs）引入了挑战。由于多模态推理相比纯文本场景涵盖了更广泛的任务范围，导致训练集与测试集之间的分布偏移更为严重，从而带来了更大的泛化难度。因此，训练可靠的多模态 PRM 需要大规模且多样化的数据集以确保充分覆盖。然而，当前的多模态推理数据集存在显著的质量不均衡问题，这降低了 PRM 的性能，并凸显了对有效数据选择策略的需求。为解决这些问题，我们提出了 DreamPRM，这是一个采用双层优化的多模态 PRM 领域重加权训练框架。在下层优化中，DreamPRM 在多个带有领域权重的数据集上进行微调，使 PRM 能够侧重高质量的推理信号，并缓解数据集质量不均衡的影响。在上层优化中，PRM 在独立的元学习数据集上进行评估；该反馈通过聚合损失函数更新领域权重，从而提升训练后 PRM 的泛化能力。在涵盖数学和通用推理的多个多模态推理基准上的大量实验表明，结合 DreamPRM 的推理时扩展持续提升了最先进多模态大型语言模型的性能。进一步的比较显示，DreamPRM 的领域重加权策略优于其他数据选择方法，且带来了比现有推理时扩展方法更高的准确率提升。

[No.81] Stop Summation: Min-Form Credit Assignment Is All Process Reward Model Needs for Reasoning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: reward
标题: 停止求和：最小形式信用分配即可满足过程奖励模型的推理需求
类别: deep learning
作者: Jie Cheng;Gang Xiong;Ruixi Qiao;Lijun Li;Chao Guo;Junle Wang;Yisheng Lv;Fei-Yue Wang;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;;Shanghai AI Laboratory;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=3Sxby0hH1q
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15275
英摘: Process reward models (PRMs) have proven effective for test-time scaling of Large Language Models (LLMs) on challenging reasoning tasks. However, reward hacking issues with PRMs limit their successful application in reinforcement fine-tuning. In this paper, we identify the main cause of PRM-induced reward hacking: the canonical summation-form credit assignment in reinforcement learning (RL), which defines the value as cumulative gamma-decayed future rewards, easily induces LLMs to hack steps with high rewards. To address this, we propose PURE: Process sUpervised Reinforcement lEarning. The key innovation of PURE is a min-form credit assignment that formulates the value function as the minimum of future rewards. This method significantly alleviates reward hacking by limiting the value function range and distributing advantages more reasonably. Through extensive experiments on 3 base models, we show that PRM-based approaches enabling min-form credit assignment achieve comparable reasoning performance to verifiable reward-based methods within only 30% steps. In contrast, the canonical sum-form credit assignment collapses training even at the beginning! Additionally, when we supplement PRM-based fine-tuning with just 10% verifiable rewards, we further alleviate reward hacking and produce the best fine-tuned model based on Qwen2.5-Math-7B in our experiments, achieving 82.5% accuracy on AMC23 and 53.3% average accuracy across 5 benchmarks. Moreover, we summarize the observed reward hacking cases and analyze the causes of training collapse. We release our code and model weights at https://github.com/CJReinforce/PURE.
中摘: 

过程奖励模型（PRMs）已被证明在具有挑战性的推理任务上对大语言模型（LLMs）的推理时扩展非常有效。然而，PRMs 存在的奖励欺骗问题限制了其在强化微调中的成功应用。在本文中，我们确定了 PRM 引发奖励欺骗的主要原因：强化学习（RL）中经典的求和形式信用分配，该方法将价值定义为累积的伽马衰减未来奖励，这容易诱导 LLM 利用高奖励步骤进行奖励欺骗。为解决这一问题，我们提出了 PURE：过程监督强化学习（Process sUpervised Reinforcement lEarning）。PURE 的关键创新在于一种最小值形式信用分配，它将价值函数构建为未来奖励的最小值。该方法通过限制价值函数范围并更合理地分配优势，显著减轻了奖励欺骗。通过对 3 个基础模型进行的广泛实验，我们表明，启用最小值形式信用分配的基于 PRM 的方法仅需 30% 的步骤，即可达到与基于可验证奖励的方法相当的推理性能。相比之下，经典的求和形式信用分配甚至在训练初期就会导致崩溃！此外，当我们仅使用 10% 的可验证奖励补充基于 PRM 的微调时，我们进一步缓解了奖励欺骗，并在实验中基于 Qwen2.5-Math-7B 获得了最佳微调模型，在 AMC23 上达到 82.5% 的准确率，在 5 个基准测试上的平均准确率达到 53.3%。此外，我们总结了观测到的奖励欺骗案例，并分析了训练崩溃的原因。我们的代码和模型权重已发布在 https://github.com/CJReinforce/PURE。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.97] REBEL: Reinforcement Learning via Regressing Relative Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: REBEL：通过回归相对奖励进行强化学习
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Zhaolin Gao;Jonathan Daniel Chang;Wenhao Zhan;Owen Oertell;Gokul Swamy;Kianté Brantley;Thorsten Joachims;J. Andrew Bagnell;Jason D. Lee;Wen Sun;
机构: Cornell University;Princeton University;Carnegie Mellon University;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/92983
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16767
英摘: While originally developed for continuous control problems, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) has emerged as the work-horse of a variety of reinforcement learning (RL) applications, including the fine-tuning of generative models. Unfortunately, PPO requires multiple heuristics to enable stable convergence (e.g. value networks, clipping), and is notorious for its sensitivity to the precise implementation of these components. In response, we take a step back and ask what a minimalist RL algorithm for the era of generative models would look like. We propose REBEL, an algorithm that cleanly reduces the problem of policy optimization to regressing the relative reward between two completions to a prompt in terms of the policy, enabling strikingly lightweight implementation. In theory, we prove that fundamental RL algorithms like Natural Policy Gradient can be seen as variants of REBEL, which allows us to match the strongest known theoretical guarantees in terms of convergence and sample complexity in the RL literature. REBEL can also cleanly incorporate offline data and be extended to handle the intransitive preferences we frequently see in practice. Empirically, we find that REBEL provides a unified approach to language modeling and image generation with stronger or similar performance as PPO and DPO, all while being simpler to implement and more computationally efficient than PPO. When fine-tuning Llama-3-8B-Instruct, REBEL achieves strong performance in AlpacaEval 2.0, MT-Bench, and Open LLM Leaderboard.
中摘: 

虽然最初是为连续控制问题而开发的，近端策略优化（PPO）已成为各类强化学习（RL）应用的主力，包括生成模型的微调。不幸的是，PPO 需要多种启发式方法才能实现稳定收敛（例如价值网络、截断），且因其对这些组件的精确实现高度敏感而备受诟病。对此，我们退一步思考，生成式模型时代的极简强化学习算法会是什么样子。我们提出了 REBEL，这是一种能够清晰地将策略优化问题简化为基于策略对同一提示的两个补全之间的相对奖励进行回归的算法，从而实现了极其轻量化的实施。理论上，我们证明了像自然策略梯度（Natural Policy Gradient）这样的基础强化学习算法都可以被视为 REBEL 的变体，这使得我们能够在收敛性和样本复杂度方面达到强化学习文献中最强的已知理论保证。REBEL 还可以无缝整合离线数据，并扩展以处理我们在实践中经常遇到的非传递性偏好问题。实验结果表明，REBEL 提供了一种统一的语言建模和图像生成方法，在性能上与 PPO 和 DPO 相当甚至更强，同时实现更简单且计算效率高于 PPO。在微调 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 时，REBEL 在 AlpacaEval 2.0、MT-Bench 和 Open LLM Leaderboard 上均取得了优异的性能。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.98] The Value of Reward Lookahead in Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 强化学习中的奖励前瞻价值
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Nadav Merlis;Dorian Baudry;Vianney Perchet;
机构: Ecole Nationale de la Statistique et de l'Administration Economique;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94968
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11637
英摘: In reinforcement learning (RL), agents sequentially interact with changing environments while aiming to maximize the obtained rewards. Usually, rewards are observed only after acting, and so the goal is to maximize the expected cumulative reward. Yet, in many practical settings, reward information is observed in advance -- prices are observed before performing transactions; nearby traffic information is partially known; and goals are oftentimes given to agents prior to the interaction. In this work, we aim to quantifiably analyze the value of such future reward information through the lens of competitive analysis. In particular, we measure the ratio between the value of standard RL agents and that of agents with partial future-reward lookahead. We characterize the worst-case reward distribution and derive exact ratios for the worst-case reward expectations. Surprisingly, the resulting ratios relate to known quantities in offline RL and reward-free exploration. We further provide tight bounds for the ratio given the worst-case dynamics. Our results cover the full spectrum between observing the immediate rewards before acting to observing all the rewards before the interaction starts.
中摘: 

在强化学习（RL）中，智能体按顺序与变化的环境进行交互，旨在最大化所获得的奖励。通常，奖励仅在采取行动后才能被观察到，因此目标是最大化期望累积奖励。然而，在许多实际场景中，奖励信息往往是提前获知的——例如在执行交易前会观察到价格；附近的部分交通信息已知；并且目标通常在交互发生前就提供给智能体。在本工作中，我们旨在通过竞争分析的视角，定量地分析此类未来奖励信息的价值。具体而言，我们衡量了标准 RL 智能体与具备部分未来奖励前瞻能力的智能体之间价值的比率。我们刻画了最坏情况下的奖励分布，并推导出了最坏情况下奖励期望的精确比率。令人惊讶的是，所得到的比率与离线强化学习和无奖励探索中的已知量密切相关。此外，针对最坏情况下的系统动态，我们还提供了该比率的紧确界。我们的结果涵盖了从采取行动前观察即时奖励，到交互开始前观察所有奖励的整个范围。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.99] Towards the Transferability of Rewards Recovered via Regularized Inverse Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 通过正则化逆强化学习回收的奖励的可转移性研究
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Andreas Schlaginhaufen;Maryam Kamgarpour;
机构: EPFL;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93854
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01793
英摘: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to infer a reward from expert demonstrations, motivated by the idea that the reward, rather than the policy, is the most succinct and transferable description of a task [Ng et al., 2000]. However, the reward corresponding to an optimal policy is not unique, making it unclear if an IRL-learned reward is transferable to new transition laws in the sense that its optimal policy aligns with the optimal policy corresponding to the expert's true reward. Past work has addressed this problem only under the assumption of full access to the expert's policy, guaranteeing transferability when learning from two experts with the same reward but different transition laws that satisfy a specific rank condition [Rolland et al., 2022]. In this work, we show that the conditions developed under full access to the expert's policy cannot guarantee transferability in the more practical scenario where we have access only to demonstrations of the expert. Instead of a binary rank condition, we propose principal angles as a more refined measure of similarity and dissimilarity between transition laws. Based on this, we then establish two key results: 1) a sufficient condition for transferability to any transition laws when learning from at least two experts with sufficiently different transition laws, and 2) a sufficient condition for transferability to local changes in the transition law when learning from a single expert. Furthermore, we also provide a probably approximately correct (PAC) algorithm and an end-to-end analysis for learning transferable rewards from demonstrations of multiple experts.
中摘: 

逆强化学习（IRL）旨在从专家示范中推断奖励函数，其动机在于奖励（而非策略）是对任务最精炼且可迁移的描述 [Ng et al., 2000]。然而，对应于最优策略的奖励函数并非唯一，这使得 IRL 学习到的奖励函数是否可迁移至新的状态转移律尚不明确，即其最优策略是否与对应于专家真实奖励的最优策略保持一致。以往的工作仅在假设能够完全获取专家策略的情况下解决了这一问题，保证了在从具有相同奖励函数但处于满足特定秩条件的不同状态转移律下的两个专家处学习时的可迁移性 [Rolland et al., 2022]。在本文中，我们表明，基于完全访问专家策略所提出的条件，并不能保证在更为实际的场景下（即仅能访问专家示范）的奖励函数可迁移性。我们摒弃二元秩条件，提出主角度作为衡量状态转移律之间相似性与差异性的更精细度量。基于此，我们得出两个关键结果：1）当从至少两个转移律存在足够差异的专家处学习时，实现向任意状态转移律迁移的充分条件；2）当从单个专家处学习时，实现向状态转移律局部变化迁移的充分条件。此外，我们还提供了一个概率近似正确（PAC）算法，以及从多位专家示范中学习可迁移奖励的端到端分析。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.102] ReMoDetect: Reward Models Recognize Aligned LLM's Generations
来源: NeurIPS 2024

标题: ReMoDetect：奖励模型识别对齐的LLM生成内容

[No.103] Intrinsic Robustness of Prophet Inequality to Strategic Reward Signaling
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 固有的先知不等式对战略奖励信号的鲁棒性
类别: algorithmic game theory
作者: Wei Tang;Haifeng Xu;Ruimin Zhang;Derek Zhu;
机构: Columbia University;University of Chicago;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95492
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18269
英摘: Prophet inequality concerns a basic optimal stopping problem and states that simple threshold stopping policies -- i.e., accepting the first reward larger than a certain threshold -- can achieve tight $\frac{1}{2}$-approximation to the optimal prophet value. Motivated by its economic applications, this paper studies the robustness of this approximation to natural strategic manipulations in which each random reward is associated with a self-interested player who may selectively reveal his realized reward to the searcher in order to maximize his probability of being selected.
  We say a threshold policy is $α$(-strategically)-robust if it (a) achieves the $α$-approximation to the prophet value for strategic players; and (b) meanwhile remains a $\frac{1}{2}$-approximation in the standard non-strategic setting. Starting with a characterization of each player's optimal information revealing strategy, we demonstrate the intrinsic robustness of prophet inequalities to strategic reward signaling through the following results: (1) for arbitrary reward distributions, there is a threshold policy that is $\frac{1-\frac{1}{e}}{2}$-robust, and this ratio is tight; (2) for i.i.d. reward distributions, there is a threshold policy that is $\frac{1}{2}$-robust, which is tight for the setting; and (3) for log-concave (but non-identical) reward distributions, the $\frac{1}{2}$-robustness can also be achieved under certain regularity assumptions.
中摘: 

先知不等式涉及一个基本的最优停时问题，并指出简单的阈值停止策略——即接受第一个大于特定阈值的奖励——可以实现最优先知值的紧 $\frac{1}{2}$ 近似。受其经济应用的启发，本文研究了这一近似在面对自然策略性操纵时的鲁棒性，其中每个随机奖励都关联着一个自利的参与者，该参与者可能会选择性地将其已实现奖励透露给搜索者，以最大化自己被选中的概率。

若一个阈值策略（a）针对策略性参与者实现了先知值的 $α$ 近似；并且（b）同时在标准的非策略设定下仍保持 $\frac{1}{2}$ 近似，则称该阈值策略为 $α$-（策略）鲁棒的。我们从刻画每位参与者的最优信息揭示策略入手，通过以下结果展示了先知不等式对策略性奖励信号传递具有内在的鲁棒性：(1) 对于任意奖励分布，存在一个阈值策略是 $\frac{1-\frac{1}{e}}{2}$-鲁棒的，且该比率是紧的；(2) 对于独立同分布奖励分布，存在一个阈值策略是 $\frac{1}{2}$-鲁棒的，这对于该设定是紧的；(3) 对于对数凹（但非相同分布）奖励分布，在某些正则性假设下，也可以实现 $\frac{1}{2}$-鲁棒性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.104] Calibrated Self-Rewarding Vision Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 校准的自我奖励视觉语言模型
类别: deep learning architectures
作者: Yiyang Zhou;Zhiyuan Fan;Dongjie Cheng;Sihan Yang;Zhaorun Chen;Chenhang Cui;Xiyao Wang;Yun Li;Linjun Zhang;Huaxiu Yao;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Tianjin University;Sichuan University;Shanghai Artificial Intelligence Laboratory;University of Chicago;University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;University of Maryland;University of North Carolina;Rutgers University;University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93685
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14622
英摘: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have made substantial progress by integrating pre-trained large language models (LLMs) and vision models through instruction tuning. Despite these advancements, LVLMs often exhibit the hallucination phenomenon, where generated text responses appear linguistically plausible but contradict the input image, indicating a misalignment between image and text pairs. This misalignment arises because the model tends to prioritize textual information over visual input, even when both the language model and visual representations are of high quality. Existing methods leverage additional models or human annotations to curate preference data and enhance modality alignment through preference optimization. These approaches may not effectively reflect the target LVLM's preferences, making the curated preferences easily distinguishable. Our work addresses these challenges by proposing the Calibrated Self-Rewarding (CSR) approach, which enables the model to self-improve by iteratively generating candidate responses, evaluating the reward for each response, and curating preference data for fine-tuning. In the reward modeling, we employ a step-wise strategy and incorporate visual constraints into the self-rewarding process to place greater emphasis on visual input. Empirical results demonstrate that CSR enhances performance and reduces hallucinations across ten benchmarks and tasks, achieving substantial improvements over existing methods by 7.62%. Our empirical results are further supported by rigorous theoretical analysis, under mild assumptions, verifying the effectiveness of introducing visual constraints into the self-rewarding paradigm. Additionally, CSR shows compatibility with different vision-language models and the ability to incrementally improve performance through iterative fine-tuning. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/YiyangZhou/CSR.
中摘: 

大型视觉 - 语言模型（LVLMs）通过指令微调整合预训练大型语言模型（LLMs）与视觉模型，取得了显著进展。尽管取得这些进步，LVLMs 仍经常表现出幻觉现象，即生成的文本回复看似语言通顺，却与输入图像相矛盾，这表明图像与文本对之间存在不对齐。这种不对齐源于模型倾向于优先考虑文本信息而非视觉输入，即使语言模型和视觉表征的质量都很高。现有方法利用额外模型或人工标注来构建偏好数据，并通过偏好优化增强模态对齐。这些方法可能无法有效反映目标 LVLM 的偏好，使得构建的偏好数据容易被识别。我们的工作通过提出校准自奖励（CSR）方法来解决这些挑战，该方法使模型能够通过迭代生成候选回复、评估每个回复的奖励并构建微调所需的偏好数据来实现自我改进。在奖励建模方面，我们采用分步策略，并将视觉约束纳入自奖励过程中，以加强对视觉输入的重视。实证结果表明，CSR 在十个基准和任务上提升了性能并减少了幻觉，与现有方法相比取得了 7.62% 的显著提升。我们的实证结果进一步得到了严格理论分析的支持，在温和假设下，验证了在自奖励范式中引入视觉约束的有效性。此外，CSR 显示出与不同视觉 - 语言模型的兼容性，并具备通过迭代微调逐步提升性能的能力。我们的数据和代码可在 https://github.com/YiyangZhou/CSR 获取。

[No.105] Expectation Alignment: Handling Reward Misspecification in the Presence of Expectation Mismatch
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 期望对齐：在期望不匹配情况下处理奖励设定错误

英摘: Detecting and handling misspecified objectives, such as reward functions, has been widely recognized as one of the central challenges within the domain of Artificial Intelligence (AI) safety research. However, even with the recognition of the importance of this problem, we are unaware of any works that attempt to provide a clear definition for what constitutes (a) misspecified objectives and (b) successfully resolving such misspecifications. In this work, we use the theory of mind, i.e., the human user's beliefs about the AI agent, as a basis to develop a formal explanatory framework called Expectation Alignment (EAL) to understand the objective misspecification and its causes. Our EAL framework not only acts as an explanatory framework for existing works but also provides us with concrete insights into the limitations of existing methods to handle reward misspecification and novel solution strategies. 检测和处理目标设定错误（例如奖励函数）已被广泛视为人工智能（AI）安全研究领域内的核心挑战之一。然而，即使人们已认识到这一问题的重要性，我们尚未发现任何试图明确界定（a）什么是目标设定错误以及（b）如何成功解决此类错误的工作。在这项工作中，我们以“心智理论”（即人类用户关于人工智能代理的信念）为基础，开发了一个名为“期望对齐”（Expectation Alignment, EAL）的形式化解释框架，旨在理解目标设定错误及其成因。我们的 EAL 框架不仅作为现有工作的解释框架，还为我们提供了关于现有处理奖励设定错误方法的局限性以及新型解决策略的具体见解。利用这些见解，我们提出了一种新的交互算法，该算法利用指定奖励来推断用户关于系统行为的潜在期望。我们展示了如何通过将推理问题转化为线性规划问题来高效地实现该算法。我们在一系列标准的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）基准上评估了我们的方法。

来源: NeurIPS 2024

英摘: The application of reinforcement learning in traffic signal control (TSC) has been extensively researched and yielded notable achievements. To meet the needs of practical applications, we introduce DiffLight, a novel conditional diffusion model for TSC under data-missing scenarios in the offline setting. [No.108] Catastrophic Goodhart: regularizing RLHF with KL divergence does not mitigate heavy-tailed reward misspecification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 灾难性的戈德哈伯效应：使用KL散度正则化RLHF并不能缓解严重的奖励错指定量化
类别: safety in machine learning
作者: Thomas Kwa;Drake Thomas;Adrià Garriga-Alonso;
机构: ;Anthropic;FAR;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94961
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.14503
英摘: When applying reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), the reward is learned from data and, therefore, always has some error. It is common to mitigate this by regularizing the policy with KL divergence from a base model, with the hope that balancing reward with regularization will achieve desirable outcomes despite this reward misspecification. We show that when the reward function has light-tailed error, optimal policies under less restrictive KL penalties achieve arbitrarily high utility. However, if error is heavy-tailed, some policies obtain arbitrarily high reward despite achieving no more utility than the base model--a phenomenon we call catastrophic Goodhart. We adapt a discrete optimization method to measure the tails of reward models, finding that they are consistent with light-tailed error. However, the pervasiveness of heavy-tailed distributions in many real-world applications indicates that future sources of RL reward could have heavy-tailed error, increasing the likelihood of reward hacking even with KL regularization.
中摘: 

在应用基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）时，奖励模型是从数据中学习得到的，因此始终存在一定误差。通常的做法是利用与基础模型的 KL 散度对策略进行正则化，期望通过平衡奖励与正则化，即便存在奖励设定偏差，也能取得理想结果。我们表明，当奖励函数具有轻尾误差时，在约束较宽松的 KL 惩罚下，最优策略可实现任意高的效用。然而，如果误差具有重尾特性，某些策略获得的奖励可任意高，尽管其效用并未超过基础模型——我们称这一现象为“灾难性古德哈特”。我们调整了一种离散优化方法来测量奖励模型的尾部特征，发现它们与轻尾误差相符。然而，许多现实应用中重尾分布的普遍性表明，未来 RL 奖励的来源可能具有重尾误差，即便采用 KL 正则化，也会增加奖励欺骗的可能性。

[No.109] Achieving Tractable Minimax Optimal Regret in Average Reward MDPs
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 在平均奖励MDP中实现可处理的最小最大最优遗憾
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Victor Boone;Zihan Zhang;
机构: Université Grenoble Alpes;Princeton University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95107
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01234
英摘: In recent years, significant attention has been directed towards learning average-reward Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). However, existing algorithms either suffer from sub-optimal regret guarantees or computational inefficiencies. In this paper, we present the first tractable algorithm with minimax optimal regret of $\widetilde{\mathrm{O}}(\sqrt{\mathrm{sp}(h^*) S A T})$, where $\mathrm{sp}(h^*)$ is the span of the optimal bias function $h^*$, $S \times A$ is the size of the state-action space and $T$ the number of learning steps. Remarkably, our algorithm does not require prior information on $\mathrm{sp}(h^*)$. Our algorithm relies on a novel subroutine, Projected Mitigated Extended Value Iteration (PMEVI), to compute bias-constrained optimal policies efficiently. This subroutine can be applied to various previous algorithms to improve regret bounds.
中摘: 

近年来，针对平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）的学习受到了广泛关注。然而，现有算法要么面临次优的遗憾界，要么存在计算效率低下的问题。在本文中，我们提出了首个具有极小极大最优遗憾 $\widetilde{\mathrm{O}}(\sqrt{\mathrm{sp}(h^*) S A T})$ 的可处理算法，其中 $\mathrm{sp}(h^*)$ 是最优偏置函数 $h^*$ 的跨度，$S \times A$ 是状态 - 动作空间的大小，$T$ 为学习步数。值得注意的是，我们的算法无需关于 $\mathrm{sp}(h^*)$ 的先验信息。我们的算法依赖于一种新颖的子程序——投影缓解扩展价值迭代（PMEVI），以高效计算偏置约束下的最优策略。该子程序可应用于多种先前算法，以改进遗憾界。

[No.110] Reward Machines for Deep RL in Noisy and Uncertain Environments
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 在嘈杂和不确定环境中用于深度强化学习的奖励机器
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Andrew C Li;Zizhao Chen;Toryn Q. Klassen;Pashootan Vaezipoor;Rodrigo Toro Icarte;Sheila A. McIlraith;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00120
英摘: Reward Machines provide an automaton-inspired structure for specifying instructions, safety constraints, and other temporally extended reward-worthy behaviour. By exposing the underlying structure of a reward function, they enable the decomposition of an RL task, leading to impressive gains in sample efficiency. Although Reward Machines and similar formal specifications have a rich history of application towards sequential decision-making problems, they critically rely on a ground-truth interpretation of the domain-specific vocabulary that forms the building blocks of the reward function--such ground-truth interpretations are elusive in the real world due in part to partial observability and noisy sensing. In this work, we explore the use of Reward Machines for Deep RL in noisy and uncertain environments. We characterize this problem as a POMDP and propose a suite of RL algorithms that exploit task structure under uncertain interpretation of the domain-specific vocabulary. Through theory and experiments, we expose pitfalls in naive approaches to this problem while simultaneously demonstrating how task structure can be successfully leveraged under noisy interpretations of the vocabulary.

[No.112] Scaling Laws for Reward Model Overoptimization in Direct Alignment Algorithms
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 直接对齐算法中奖励模型过度优化的缩放法则
类别: natural language processing
作者: Rafael Rafailov;Yaswanth Chittepu;Ryan Park;Harshit Sikchi;Joey Hejna;W. Bradley Knox;Chelsea Finn;Scott Niekum;
机构: Stanford University;University of Massachusetts Amherst;University of Texas at Austin;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93540
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02900
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has been crucial to the recent success of Large Language Models (LLMs), however, it is often a complex and brittle process. In the classical RLHF framework, a reward model is first trained to represent human preferences, which is in turn used by an online reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm to optimize the LLM. A prominent issue with such methods is reward over-optimization or reward hacking, where performance as measured by the learned proxy reward model increases, but true quality plateaus or even deteriorates. Direct Alignment Algorithms (DDAs) like Direct Preference Optimization have emerged as alternatives to the classical RLHF pipeline by circumventing the reward modeling phase. However, although DAAs do not use a separate proxy reward model, they still commonly deteriorate from over-optimization. While the so-called reward hacking phenomenon is not well-defined for DAAs, we still uncover similar trends: at higher KL budgets, DAA algorithms exhibit similar degradation patterns to their classic RLHF counterparts. In particular, we find that DAA methods deteriorate not only across a wide range of KL budgets but also often before even a single epoch of the dataset is completed. Through extensive empirical experimentation, this work formulates and formalizes the reward over-optimization or hacking problem for DAAs and explores its consequences across objectives, training regimes, and model scales.
中摘: 

人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）对于大型语言模型（LLM）近期的成功至关重要，然而，它通常是一个复杂且脆弱的过程。在经典 RLHF 框架中，首先训练一个奖励模型以表征人类偏好，该模型随后被在线强化学习（RL）算法用于优化大型语言模型。此类方法的一个突出问题在于奖励过度优化或奖励黑客行为，即通过学习到的代理奖励模型衡量的性能有所提升，但真实质量却趋于平稳甚至恶化。直接对齐算法（DAA），如直接偏好优化，作为替代经典 RLHF 流程的方法出现，通过规避奖励建模阶段来运作。然而，尽管 DAA 不使用单独的代理奖励模型，它们仍普遍因过度优化而性能退化。虽然所谓的奖励黑客现象在 DAA 中尚未明确定义，但我们仍发现了类似趋势：在较高的 KL 预算下，DAA 算法表现出与其经典 RLHF 对应方法相似的退化模式。特别是，我们发现 DAA 方法的性能退化不仅出现在广泛的 KL 预算范围内，而且往往甚至在完成数据集的一个训练周期之前就发生了。通过广泛的实证实验，本工作对 DAA 的奖励过度优化或黑客行为问题进行了定义和形式化，并探讨了其在目标、训练策略和模型规模方面的后果。

[No.113] Global Rewards in Restless Multi-Armed Bandits
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 不朽奖赏在激动人心的多臂 bandit 问题中的应用
类别: bandits
作者: Naveen Janaki Raman;Zheyuan Ryan Shi;Fei Fang;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96734
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00738
英摘: Restless multi-armed bandits (RMAB) extend multi-armed bandits so pulling an arm impacts future states. Despite the success of RMABs, a key limiting assumption is the separability of rewards into a sum across arms. We address this deficiency by proposing restless-multi-armed bandit with global rewards (RMAB-G), a generalization of RMABs to global non-separable rewards. To solve RMAB-G, we develop the Linear- and Shapley-Whittle indices, which extend Whittle indices from RMABs to RMAB-Gs. We prove approximation bounds but also point out how these indices could fail when reward functions are highly non-linear. To overcome this, we propose two sets of adaptive policies: the first computes indices iteratively, and the second combines indices with Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). Empirically, we demonstrate that our proposed policies outperform baselines and index-based policies with synthetic data and real-world data from food rescue.
中摘: 

躁动多臂老虎机（RMAB）扩展了多臂老虎机模型，使得拉动一个“臂”会影响未来的状态。尽管 RMAB 取得了成功，但其一个关键的限制性假设是奖励在各臂之间是可分离的（即可表示为各臂奖励之和）。我们通过提出带全局奖励的躁动多臂老虎机（RMAB-G）来解决这一缺陷，这是将 RMAB 推广至具有全局不可分离奖励的情形。为解决 RMAB-G 问题，我们开发了线性 Whittle 指数和 Shapley-Whittle 指数，这些指数将 Whittle 指数从 RMAB 扩展到了 RMAB-G。我们证明了近似界限，但也指出了当奖励函数高度非线性时这些指数可能失效的情况。为克服这一问题，我们提出了两组自适应策略：第一组迭代计算指数，第二组将指数与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）相结合。实证结果表明，在合成数据和来自食物救援的真实世界数据上，我们提出的策略均优于基准方法和基于指数的策略。

[No.114] Beyond Optimism: Exploration With Partially Observable Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 超越乐观主义：在部分可观测奖励下的探索
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Simone Parisi;Alireza Kazemipour;Michael Bowling;
机构: University of Alberta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93919
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13909
英摘: Exploration in reinforcement learning (RL) remains an open challenge. RL algorithms rely on observing rewards to train the agent, and if informative rewards are sparse the agent learns slowly or may not learn at all. To improve exploration and reward discovery, popular algorithms rely on optimism. But what if sometimes rewards are unobservable, e.g., situations of partial monitoring in bandits and the recent formalism of monitored Markov decision process? In this case, optimism can lead to suboptimal behavior that does not explore further to collapse uncertainty. With this paper, we present a novel exploration strategy that overcomes the limitations of existing methods and guarantees convergence to an optimal policy even when rewards are not always observable. We further propose a collection of tabular environments for benchmarking exploration in RL (with and without unobservable rewards) and show that our method outperforms existing ones.
中摘: 

强化学习（RL）中的探索仍然是一个开放性挑战。强化学习算法依赖于观察奖励来训练智能体，如果信息性奖励稀疏，智能体可能学习缓慢甚至完全无法学习。为了改善探索和奖励发现，主流算法通常依赖于乐观性原则。但如果有时奖励是不可观测的呢？例如多臂老虎机中的部分监控情形以及最近提出的监控马尔可夫决策过程形式化体系。在这种情况下，乐观性可能导致次优行为，使得智能体无法通过进一步探索来降低不确定性。本文提出了一种新颖的探索策略，克服了现有方法的局限性，即使在奖励并非总是可观测的情况下，也能保证收敛至最优策略。此外，我们还提出了一系列表格型环境，用于基准测试强化学习中的探索（涵盖奖励可观测与不可观测的情况），结果表明我们的方法优于现有方法。

[No.115] Subwords as Skills: Tokenization for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 子词作为技能：稀疏奖励强化学习中的分词方法
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: David Yunis;Justin Jung;Falcon Z Dai;Matthew Walter;
机构: Springtail.ai;Toyota Technological Institute at Chicago;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94819
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.04459
英摘: Exploration in sparse-reward reinforcement learning is difficult due to the requirement of long, coordinated sequences of actions in order to achieve any reward. Moreover, in continuous action spaces there are an infinite number of possible actions, which only increases the difficulty of exploration. One class of methods designed to address these issues forms temporally extended actions, often called skills, from interaction data collected in the same domain, and optimizes a policy on top of this new action space. Typically such methods require a lengthy pretraining phase, especially in continuous action spaces, in order to form the skills before reinforcement learning can begin. Given prior evidence that the full range of the continuous action space is not required in such tasks, we propose a novel approach to skill-generation with two components. First we discretize the action space through clustering, and second we leverage a tokenization technique borrowed from natural language processing to generate temporally extended actions. Such a method outperforms baselines for skill-generation in several challenging sparse-reward domains, and requires orders-of-magnitude less computation in skill-generation and online rollouts. Our code is available at \url{https://github.com/dyunis/subwords_as_skills}.
中摘: 

稀疏奖励强化学习中的探索之所以困难，是因为达成任何奖励都需要长且协调的动作序列。此外，在连续动作空间中，存在无限多种可能的动作，这进一步增加了探索的难度。一类旨在解决这些问题的方法从同一领域收集的交互数据中构建时间扩展动作（通常称为技能），并在此新的动作空间之上优化策略。通常，此类方法需要一个漫长的预训练阶段，尤其是在连续动作空间中，以便在强化学习开始之前形成技能。鉴于先前证据表明此类任务并不需要连续动作空间的全部范围，我们提出了一种新的技能生成方法，包含两个组成部分。首先，我们通过聚类对动作空间进行离散化；其次，我们利用借用自自然语言处理的分词技术来生成时间扩展动作。该方法在几个具有挑战性的稀疏奖励领域中的技能生成表现优于基线，且在技能生成和在线轨迹执行方面的计算量减少了几个数量级。我们的代码可在 \url{https://github.com/dyunis/subwords_as_skills} 获取。

[No.118] Regularizing Hidden States Enables Learning Generalizable Reward Model for LLMs
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 正则化隐藏状态使LLMs能够学习可泛化的奖励模型
类别: natural language processing
作者: Rui Yang;Ruomeng Ding;Yong Lin;Huan Zhang;Tong Zhang;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Georgia Institute of Technology;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93927
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10216
英摘: Reward models trained on human preference data have been proven to effectively align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human intent within the framework of reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). However, current reward models have limited generalization capabilities to unseen prompts and responses, which can lead to an unexpected phenomenon known as reward over-optimization, resulting in a decline in actual performance due to excessive optimization of rewards. While previous research has advocated for constraining policy optimization, our study introduces a novel approach to enhance the reward model's generalization ability against distribution shifts by regularizing the hidden states. Specifically, we retain the base model's language model head and incorporate a suite of text-generation losses to preserve the hidden states' text-generation capabilities, while concurrently learning a reward head behind the same hidden states. Our experimental results demonstrate that the introduced regularization technique markedly improves the accuracy of learned reward models across a variety of out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks and effectively alleviates the over-optimization issue in RLHF, offering a more reliable and robust preference learning paradigm.
中摘: 

基于人类偏好数据训练的奖励模型已被证实，能够在人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）框架内，有效地使大型语言模型（LLM）与人类意图对齐。然而，当前的奖励模型对未见过的提示词和回复的泛化能力有限，这可能导致一种被称为奖励过优化的意外现象，从而因过度优化奖励而导致实际性能下降。尽管之前的研究主张约束策略优化，但本研究提出了一种新颖的方法，通过对隐藏状态进行正则化，增强奖励模型对分布偏移的泛化能力。具体而言，我们保留了基模型的语言模型头，并引入一组文本生成损失以保持隐藏状态的文本生成能力，同时在相同的隐藏状态之上学习一个奖励头。我们的实验结果表明，所引入的正则化技术在多种分布外（OOD）任务上显著提高了所学奖励模型的准确性，并有效缓解了 RLHF 中的过优化问题，提供了一种更可靠且稳健的偏好学习范式。

[No.119] SimPO: Simple Preference Optimization with a Reference-Free Reward
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: SimPO：无参考奖励的简单偏好优化
类别: natural language processing
作者: Yu Meng;Mengzhou Xia;Danqi Chen;
机构: University of Virginia;Princeton University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96741
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14734
英摘: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used offline preference optimization algorithm that reparameterizes reward functions in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) to enhance simplicity and training stability. In this work, we propose SimPO, a simpler yet more effective approach. The effectiveness of SimPO is attributed to a key design: using the average log probability of a sequence as the implicit reward. This reward formulation better aligns with model generation and eliminates the need for a reference model, making it more compute and memory efficient. Additionally, we introduce a target reward margin to the Bradley-Terry objective to encourage a larger margin between the winning and losing responses, further improving the algorithm's performance. We compare SimPO to DPO and its latest variants across various state-of-the-art training setups, including both base and instruction-tuned models such as Mistral, Llama 3, and Gemma 2. We evaluate on extensive chat-based evaluation benchmarks, including AlpacaEval 2, MT-Bench, and Arena-Hard. Our results demonstrate that SimPO consistently and significantly outperforms existing approaches without substantially increasing response length. Specifically, SimPO outperforms DPO by up to 6.4 points on AlpacaEval 2 and by up to 7.5 points on Arena-Hard. Our top-performing model, built on Gemma-2-9B-it, achieves a 72.4% length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2, a 59.1% win rate on Arena-Hard, and ranks 1st on Chatbot Arena among <10B models with real user votes.
中摘: 

直接偏好优化（DPO）是一种广泛使用的离线偏好优化算法，它重新参数化了来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）中的奖励函数，旨在提高简洁性和训练稳定性。在这项工作中，我们提出了 SimPO，这是一种更简单且更有效的方法。SimPO 的有效性归因于一个关键设计：使用序列的平均对数概率作为隐式奖励。这种奖励公式更好地与模型生成对齐，消除了对参考模型的需求，从而使其在计算和内存方面更为高效。此外，我们在 Bradley-Terry 目标函数中引入了目标奖励间隔，以鼓励胜出回答与失败回答之间形成更大的差距，从而进一步提升算法性能。我们在各种最先进的训练设置中将 SimPO 与 DPO 及其最新变体进行了比较，涵盖的基础模型和指令微调模型包括 Mistral、Llama 3 和 Gemma 2。我们在广泛的基于对话的评估基准上进行了评估，包括 AlpacaEval 2、MT-Bench 和 Arena-Hard。结果表明，SimPO 无需显著增加响应长度，即可持续且显著地优于现有方法。具体而言，在 AlpacaEval 2 上，SimPO 优于 DPO 多达 6.4 分；在 Arena-Hard 上，则多达 7.5 分。我们表现最佳的模型基于 Gemma-2-9B-it 构建，在 AlpacaEval 2 上实现了 72.4% 的长度控制胜率，在 Arena-Hard 上实现了 59.1% 的胜率，并在 Chatbot Arena 的<10B 模型类别中凭借真实用户投票位列第 1。

[No.120] Robot Policy Learning with Temporal Optimal Transport Reward
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于时间最优传输奖励的机器人政策学习
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Yuwei Fu;Haichao Zhang;Di Wu;Wei Xu;Benoit Boulet;
机构: McGill University;Horizon Robotics;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95612
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21795
英摘: Reward specification is one of the most tricky problems in Reinforcement Learning, which usually requires tedious hand engineering in practice. One promising approach to tackle this challenge is to adopt existing expert video demonstrations for policy learning. Some recent work investigates how to learn robot policies from only a single/few expert video demonstrations. For example, reward labeling via Optimal Transport (OT) has been shown to be an effective strategy to generate a proxy reward by measuring the alignment between the robot trajectory and the expert demonstrations. However, previous work mostly overlooks that the OT reward is invariant to temporal order information, which could bring extra noise to the reward signal. To address this issue, in this paper, we introduce the Temporal Optimal Transport (TemporalOT) reward to incorporate temporal order information for learning a more accurate OT-based proxy reward. Extensive experiments on the Meta-world benchmark tasks validate the efficacy of the proposed method. Code is available at: https://github.com/fuyw/TemporalOT
中摘: 

奖励设计是强化学习中最棘手的问题之一，在实践中通常需要进行繁琐的人工设计。应对这一挑战的一种有前景的方法是采用现有的专家视频演示来进行策略学习。一些近期的研究工作探讨了如何仅从单个或少数几个专家视频演示中学习机器人策略。例如，利用最优传输（OT）进行奖励标注已被证明是一种有效的策略，通过测量机器人轨迹与专家演示之间的一致性来生成代理奖励。然而，以往的工作大多忽视了 OT 奖励具有时序不变性，这可能会给奖励信号引入额外的噪声。为解决这一问题，本文引入了时序最优传输（TemporalOT）奖励，通过融入时序顺序信息来学习更准确的基于 OT 的代理奖励。在 Meta-world 基准任务上进行的广泛实验验证了所提出方法的有效性。代码地址：https://github.com/fuyw/TemporalOT

[No.121] Getting More Juice Out of the SFT Data: Reward Learning from Human Demonstration Improves SFT for LLM Alignment
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 从人类示范中获取更多SFT数据：奖励学习改善LLM对齐的SFT方法
类别: natural language processing
作者: Jiaxiang Li;Siliang Zeng;Hoi To Wai;Chenliang Li;Alfredo Garcia;Mingyi Hong;
机构: University of Minnesota;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Texas A&M University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93598
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17888
英摘: Aligning human preference and value is an important requirement for contemporary foundation models. State-of-the-art techniques such as Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) often consist of two stages: 1) supervised fine-tuning (SFT), where the model is fine-tuned by learning from human demonstration data; 2) Preference learning, where preference data is used to learn a reward model, which is in turn used by a reinforcement learning (RL) step to fine-tune the model. Such reward model serves as a proxy to human preference, and it is critical to guide the RL step towards improving the model quality. In this work, we argue that the SFT stage significantly benefits from learning a reward model as well. Instead of using the human demonstration data directly via supervised learning, we propose to leverage an Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) technique to simultaneously build an reward model and a policy model. This approach leads to new SFT algorithms that are not only efficient to implement, but are robust to the presence of low-quality supervised learning data. Moreover, we discover a connection between the proposed IRL based approach, and a recent line of works called Self-Play Fine-tune (SPIN). Theoretically, we show that the proposed algorithms converge to the stationary solutions of the IRL problem. Empirically, we align 1B and 7B models using proposed methods and evaluate them on a reward benchmark model and the HuggingFace Open LLM Leaderboard. The proposed methods show significant performance improvement over existing SFT approaches. Our results indicate that it is beneficial to leverage reward learning throughout the entire alignment process.
中摘: 

对齐人类偏好与价值观是现代基础模型的重要要求。最先进的技术，如来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF），通常包含两个阶段：1) 监督微调（SFT），即模型通过学习人类示范数据进行微调；2) 偏好学习，即利用偏好数据学习奖励模型，随后该模型被强化学习（RL）步骤用于微调模型。此类奖励模型充当人类偏好的代理，对于引导 RL 步骤提高模型质量至关重要。

在这项工作中，我们认为 SFT 阶段也能从学习奖励模型中显著受益。我们不直接通过监督学习使用人类示范数据，而是提出利用逆向强化学习（IRL）技术同时构建奖励模型和策略模型。这种方法产生了新的 SFT 算法，这些算法不仅实施高效，而且对低质量监督学习数据的存在具有鲁棒性。

此外，我们发现提出的基于 IRL 的方法与最近一系列称为自我博弈微调（SPIN）的工作之间存在联系。理论上，我们证明了所提出的算法收敛至 IRL 问题的稳态解。在实验上，我们使用提出的方法对齐 1B 和 7B 模型，并在奖励基准模型和 HuggingFace 开源 LLM 排行榜上进行评估。提出的方法相较于现有 SFT 方法显示出显著的性能提升。我们的结果表明，在整个对齐过程中利用奖励学习是有益的。

[No.123] Rule Based Rewards for Language Model Safety

[No.130] Provably Efficient Interactive-Grounded Learning with Personalized Reward

[No.131] InfoRM: Mitigating Reward Hacking in RLHF via Information-Theoretic Reward Modeling
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: InfoRM：通过信息论奖励建模在RLHF中缓解奖励作弊
类别: natural language processing
作者: Yuchun Miao;Sen Zhang;Liang Ding;Rong Bao;Lefei Zhang;Dacheng Tao;
机构: Wuhan University;University of Sydney;Zhejiang University;Fudan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96739
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.09345
英摘: Despite the success of reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) in aligning language models with human values, reward hacking, also termed reward overoptimization, remains a critical challenge. This issue primarily arises from reward misgeneralization, where reward models (RMs) compute reward using spurious features that are irrelevant to human preferences. In this work, we tackle this problem from an information-theoretic perspective and propose a framework for reward modeling, namely InfoRM, by introducing a variational information bottleneck objective to filter out irrelevant information. Notably, we further identify a correlation between overoptimization and outliers in the IB latent space of InfoRM, establishing it as a promising tool for detecting reward overoptimization. Inspired by this finding, we propose the Cluster Separation Index (CSI), which quantifies deviations in the IB latent space, as an indicator of reward overoptimization to facilitate the development of online mitigation strategies. Extensive experiments on a wide range of settings and RM scales (70M, 440M, 1.4B, and 7B) demonstrate the effectiveness of InfoRM. Further analyses reveal that InfoRM's overoptimization detection mechanism is not only effective but also robust across a broad range of datasets, signifying a notable advancement in the field of RLHF. The code will be released upon acceptance.
中摘: 

尽管基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）在使语言模型与人类价值观对齐方面取得了成功，但奖励黑客，也称为奖励过优化，仍然是一个关键挑战。该问题主要源于奖励泛化错误，即奖励模型（RMs）使用与人类偏好无关的虚假特征来计算奖励。在这项工作中，我们从信息论视角解决这一问题，并提出了一种奖励建模框架，名为 InfoRM，通过引入变分信息瓶颈目标来过滤无关信息。值得注意的是，我们进一步发现了过优化与 InfoRM 的 IB 潜在空间中的异常值之间存在关联，确立了其作为检测奖励过优化的一种有前景的工具。受此发现的启发，我们提出了聚类分离指数（CSI），用于量化 IB 潜在空间中的偏差，并将其作为奖励过优化的指标，以促进在线缓解策略的开发。在广泛设置和不同奖励模型规模（70M、440M、1.4B 和 7B）上的大量实验证明了 InfoRM 的有效性。进一步分析表明，InfoRM 的过优化检测机制不仅有效，而且在广泛的数据集上也具有鲁棒性，标志着 RLHF 领域的显著进步。代码将在论文录用后开源。

[No.132] Learning to Assist Humans without Inferring Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 学习辅助人类而不推断奖励
类别: human-AI interaction
作者: Vivek Myers;Evan Ellis;Sergey Levine;Benjamin Eysenbach;Anca Dragan;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;Google;Princeton University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94848
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02623
英摘: Assistive agents should make humans' lives easier. Classically, such assistance is studied through the lens of inverse reinforcement learning, where an assistive agent (e.g., a chatbot, a robot) infers a human's intention and then selects actions to help the human reach that goal. This approach requires inferring intentions, which can be difficult in high-dimensional settings. We build upon prior work that studies assistance through the lens of empowerment: an assistive agent aims to maximize the influence of the human's actions such that they exert a greater control over the environmental outcomes and can solve tasks in fewer steps. We lift the major limitation of prior work in this area--scalability to high-dimensional settings--with contrastive successor representations. We formally prove that these representations estimate a similar notion of empowerment to that studied by prior work and provide a ready-made mechanism for optimizing it. Empirically, our proposed method outperforms prior methods on synthetic benchmarks, and scales to Overcooked, a cooperative game setting. Theoretically, our work connects ideas from information theory, neuroscience, and reinforcement learning, and charts a path for representations to play a critical role in solving assistive problems.
中摘: 

辅助智能体应使人类生活更加轻松便捷。传统上，此类辅助作用是通过逆强化学习视角来研究的，在此视角下，辅助智能体（如聊天机器人、机器人）推断人类意图，随后选择行动以帮助人类达成该目标。这种方法需要推断意图，而在高维环境中这可能非常困难。我们建立在以往通过“赋能”视角研究辅助作用的工作基础之上：辅助智能体的目标是最大化人类行动的影响力，使其对环境结果拥有更强的控制力，并能以更少的步骤完成任务。我们利用对比后继表示，突破了该领域先前工作的一个主要局限——即难以扩展到高维设置。我们形式化地证明了这些表示估算了与先前工作所研究的“赋能”相似的概念，并为优化它提供了一个现成的机制。实证结果表明，我们提出的方法在合成基准测试中优于先前方法，并且能够扩展到 Overcooked（一种合作游戏环境）。在理论层面，我们的工作融合了信息论、神经科学和强化学习的思想，并规划了一条让表示学习在解决辅助问题中发挥关键作用的路径。

[No.133] Reciprocal Reward Influence Encourages Cooperation From Self-Interested Agents
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: 互惠回报影响促进自利代理者的合作
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: John Luoyu Zhou;Weizhe Hong;Jonathan Kao;
机构: University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94874
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01641
英摘: Cooperation between self-interested individuals is a widespread phenomenon in the natural world, but remains elusive in interactions between artificially intelligent agents. Instead, naive reinforcement learning algorithms typically converge to Pareto-dominated outcomes in even the simplest of social dilemmas. An emerging literature on opponent shaping has demonstrated the ability to reach prosocial outcomes by influencing the learning of other agents. However, such methods differentiate through the learning step of other agents or optimize for meta-game dynamics, which rely on privileged access to opponents' learning algorithms or exponential sample complexity, respectively. To provide a learning rule-agnostic and sample-efficient alternative, we introduce Reciprocators, reinforcement learning agents which are intrinsically motivated to reciprocate the influence of opponents' actions on their returns. This approach seeks to modify other agents' $Q$-values by increasing their return following beneficial actions (with respect to the Reciprocator) and decreasing it after detrimental actions, guiding them towards mutually beneficial actions without directly differentiating through a model of their policy. We show that Reciprocators can be used to promote cooperation in temporally extended social dilemmas during simultaneous learning. Our code is available at https://github.com/johnlyzhou/reciprocator/.
中摘: 

自利个体之间的合作在自然界中是一种普遍现象，但在人工智能体之间的交互中却仍难以实现。相反，即使是面对最简单的社会困境，朴素的强化学习算法通常也会收敛至帕累托被支配的结果。关于对手塑造的新兴文献已证明，通过影响其他智能体的学习可以实现亲社会结果。然而，此类方法要么通过其他智能体的学习步骤进行微分，要么优化元博弈动态，这分别依赖于对对手学习算法的特权访问或指数级样本复杂度。为了提供一种与学习规则无关且样本高效的替代方案，我们引入了 Reciprocators（互惠者），这是一种强化学习智能体，其内在动机在于回报对手行为对自身回报的影响。该方法试图通过增加（相对于 Reciprocator）有利行为后的回报，并减少有害行为后的回报，来修改其他智能体的 Q 值，从而在不直接通过其策略模型进行微分的情况下，引导它们采取互利行动。我们表明，Reciprocators 可用于在同步学习期间促进时间扩展型社会困境中的合作。我们的代码发布于 https://github.com/johnlyzhou/reciprocator/。

[No.135] ReNO: Enhancing One-step Text-to-Image Models through Reward-based Noise Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: reward
标题: ReNO：通过基于奖励的噪声优化提升一阶段文本到图像模型
类别: diffusion based models
作者: Luca Eyring;Shyamgopal Karthik;Karsten Roth;Alexey Dosovitskiy;Zeynep Akata;
机构: Snap Inc.;University of Tuebingen;Inceptive;Helmholtz Zentrum München;Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95513
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04312
英摘: Text-to-Image (T2I) models have made significant advancements in recent years, but they still struggle to accurately capture intricate details specified in complex compositional prompts. While fine-tuning T2I models with reward objectives has shown promise, it suffers from "reward hacking" and may not generalize well to unseen prompt distributions. In this work, we propose Reward-based Noise Optimization (ReNO), a novel approach that enhances T2I models at inference by optimizing the initial noise based on the signal from one or multiple human preference reward models. Remarkably, solving this optimization problem with gradient ascent for 50 iterations yields impressive results on four different one-step models across two competitive benchmarks, T2I-CompBench and GenEval. Within a computational budget of 20-50 seconds, ReNO-enhanced one-step models consistently surpass the performance of all current open-source Text-to-Image models. Extensive user studies demonstrate that our model is preferred nearly twice as often compared to the popular SDXL model and is on par with the proprietary Stable Diffusion 3 with 8B parameters. Moreover, given the same computational resources, a ReNO-optimized one-step model outperforms widely-used open-source models such as SDXL and PixArt-$α$, highlighting the efficiency and effectiveness of ReNO in enhancing T2I model performance at inference time. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/ReNO.
中摘: 

近年来，文生图像（T2I）模型取得了显著进步，但它们仍难以准确捕捉复杂组合提示词中指定的精细细节。虽然利用奖励目标微调 T2I 模型已展现出前景，但该方法存在“奖励黑客”问题，且可能无法很好地泛化到未见过的提示词分布。在本研究中，我们提出了基于奖励的噪声优化（ReNO），这是一种新颖的方法，旨在通过在推理阶段基于一个或多个人类偏好奖励模型的信号来优化初始噪声，从而增强 T2I 模型。值得注意的是，仅需通过梯度上升进行 50 次迭代求解该优化问题，便在两个具有竞争力的基准测试 T2I-CompBench 和 GenEval 上的四种不同单步模型中取得了令人印象深刻的成果。在 20 至 50 秒的计算预算内，经 ReNO 增强的单步模型始终超越当前所有开源文生图像模型的性能。广泛的用户研究表明，与流行的 SDXL 模型相比，我们的模型被偏好的频率几乎高出两倍，且与拥有 80 亿参数的专有模型 Stable Diffusion 3 相当。此外，在相同的计算资源下，经 ReNO 优化的单步模型优于广泛使用的开源模型（如 SDXL 和 PixArt-α），突显了 ReNO 在推理时增强 T2I 模型性能的效率和有效性。代码可在 https://github.com/ExplainableML/ReNO 获取。

[No.136] Direct Preference Optimization: Your Language Model is Secretly a Reward Model
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 直接偏好优化：你的语言模型实际上是奖励模型
类别: 
作者: Rafael Rafailov;Archit Sharma;Eric Mitchell;Christopher D Manning;Stefano Ermon;Chelsea Finn;
机构: Stanford University;Google;
类型: Oral
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72164
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.18290
英摘: While large-scale unsupervised language models (LMs) learn broad world knowledge and some reasoning skills, achieving precise control of their behavior is difficult due to the completely unsupervised nature of their training. Existing methods for gaining such steerability collect human labels of the relative quality of model generations and fine-tune the unsupervised LM to align with these preferences, often with reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). However, RLHF is a complex and often unstable procedure, first fitting a reward model that reflects the human preferences, and then fine-tuning the large unsupervised LM using reinforcement learning to maximize this estimated reward without drifting too far from the original model. In this paper we introduce a new parameterization of the reward model in RLHF that enables extraction of the corresponding optimal policy in closed form, allowing us to solve the standard RLHF problem with only a simple classification loss. The resulting algorithm, which we call Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), is stable, performant, and computationally lightweight, eliminating the need for sampling from the LM during fine-tuning or performing significant hyperparameter tuning. Our experiments show that DPO can fine-tune LMs to align with human preferences as well as or better than existing methods. Notably, fine-tuning with DPO exceeds PPO-based RLHF in ability to control sentiment of generations, and matches or improves response quality in summarization and single-turn dialogue while being substantially simpler to implement and train.
中摘: 

虽然大规模无监督语言模型（LMs）学习到了广泛的常识和一定的推理能力，但由于其训练完全无监督的特性，实现对模型行为的精确控制十分困难。现有的获取此类可控性的方法会收集人类对模型生成结果相对质量的标注，并微调无监督语言模型以与这些偏好对齐，通常采用来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）。然而，RLHF 是一个复杂且通常不稳定的过程，首先需拟合一个反映人类偏好的奖励模型，随后利用强化学习微调大型无监督语言模型，以最大化该估计奖励，同时避免过度偏离原始模型。在本文中，我们提出了一种 RLHF 奖励模型的新参数化方法，使得能够以闭式形式提取相应的最优策略，从而允许我们仅使用简单的分类损失即可解决标准的 RLHF 问题。该算法被称为直接偏好优化（DPO），其具有稳定、高效且计算轻量级的特点，消除了微调期间从语言模型采样的需求，也无需进行大量的超参数调优。我们的实验表明，DPO 能够将语言模型微调至与人类偏好对齐，效果与现有方法相当或更好。值得注意的是，在控制生成内容情感的能力上，基于 DPO 的微调超越了基于 PPO 的 RLHF，并在摘要和单轮对话的响应质量上与之相当或有所提升，同时其实现和训练过程要简单得多。

[No.137] Behavior Alignment via Reward Function Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 行为对齐通过奖励函数优化
类别: 
作者: Dhawal Gupta;Yash Chandak;Scott M. Jordan;Philip S. Thomas;Bruno Castro da Silva;
机构: University of Massachusetts Amherst;Stanford University;University of Alberta;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71752
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.19007
英摘: Designing reward functions for efficiently guiding reinforcement learning (RL) agents toward specific behaviors is a complex task. This is challenging since it requires the identification of reward structures that are not sparse and that avoid inadvertently inducing undesirable behaviors. Naively modifying the reward structure to offer denser and more frequent feedback can lead to unintended outcomes and promote behaviors that are not aligned with the designer's intended goal. Although potential-based reward shaping is often suggested as a remedy, we systematically investigate settings where deploying it often significantly impairs performance. To address these issues, we introduce a new framework that uses a bi-level objective to learn \emph{behavior alignment reward functions}. These functions integrate auxiliary rewards reflecting a designer's heuristics and domain knowledge with the environment's primary rewards. Our approach automatically determines the most effective way to blend these types of feedback, thereby enhancing robustness against heuristic reward misspecification. Remarkably, it can also adapt an agent's policy optimization process to mitigate suboptimalities resulting from limitations and biases inherent in the underlying RL algorithms. We evaluate our method's efficacy on a diverse set of tasks, from small-scale experiments to high-dimensional control challenges. We investigate heuristic auxiliary rewards of varying quality -- some of which are beneficial and others detrimental to the learning process. Our results show that our framework offers a robust and principled way to integrate designer-specified heuristics. It not only addresses key shortcomings of existing approaches but also consistently leads to high-performing solutions, even when given misaligned or poorly-specified auxiliary reward functions.
中摘: 

设计奖励函数以有效地引导强化学习（RL）智能体实现特定行为是一项复杂的任务。这具有挑战性，因为它需要识别非稀疏的奖励结构，同时避免无意中诱导不良行为。朴素地修改奖励结构以提供密集且频繁的反馈可能会导致意外后果，并促成与设计者预期目标不一致的行为。尽管基于势函数的奖励塑造常被视为一种补救措施，但我们系统地研究了部署该方法经常显著损害性能的场景。为解决这些问题，我们引入了一个新的框架，该框架采用双层目标来学习行为对齐奖励函数。这些函数将反映设计者启发式方法和领域知识的辅助奖励与环境的主要奖励相结合。我们的方法自动确定混合这些类型反馈的最有效方式，从而增强了对启发式奖励错误指定的鲁棒性。值得注意的是，它还能适应智能体的策略优化过程，以减轻源于底层强化学习算法固有局限性和偏差的次优性。我们在涵盖从小规模实验到高维控制挑战的多样化任务上评估了该方法的有效性。我们研究了质量各异的启发式辅助奖励——其中一些对学习过程有益，而另一些则有害。我们的结果表明，我们的框架提供了一种稳健且合乎原则的方式来整合设计者指定的启发式规则。它不仅解决了现有方法的关键不足，而且即使在接收到未对齐或指定不佳的辅助奖励函数时，仍能一贯地产生高性能的解决方案。

[No.138] Decentralized Randomly Distributed Multi-agent Multi-armed Bandit with Heterogeneous Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 去中心化的随机分布多代理多臂bandit问题（具有异质奖励）
类别: 
作者: Mengfan Xu;Diego Klabjan;
机构: Northwestern University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72349
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.05579
英摘: We study a decentralized multi-agent multi-armed bandit problem in which multiple clients are connected by time dependent random graphs provided by an environment. The reward distributions of each arm vary across clients and rewards are generated independently over time by an environment based on distributions that include both sub-exponential and sub-gaussian distributions. Each client pulls an arm and communicates with neighbors based on the graph provided by the environment. The goal is to minimize the overall regret of the entire system through collaborations. To this end, we introduce a novel algorithmic framework, which first provides robust simulation methods for generating random graphs using rapidly mixing Markov chains or the random graph model, and then combines an averaging-based consensus approach with a newly proposed weighting technique and the upper confidence bound to deliver a UCB-type solution. Our algorithms account for the randomness in the graphs, removing the conventional doubly stochasticity assumption, and only require the knowledge of the number of clients at initialization. We derive optimal instance-dependent regret upper bounds of order $\log{T}$ in both sub-gaussian and sub-exponential environments, and a nearly optimal mean-gap independent regret upper bound of order $\sqrt{T}\log T$ up to a $\log T$ factor. Importantly, our regret bounds hold with high probability and capture graph randomness, whereas prior works consider expected regret under assumptions and require more stringent reward distributions.
中摘: 

我们研究一种去中心化的多智能体多臂老虎机问题，其中多个客户端由环境提供的时变随机图相互连接。每个臂的奖励分布在各个客户端之间各不相同，且环境根据涵盖次指数和次高斯分布的分布，独立地随时间生成奖励。每个客户端选取一个臂，并基于环境提供的图与邻居进行通信。目标是通过协作最小化整个系统的整体遗憾。为此，我们引入了一种新颖的算法框架，该框架首先提供了利用快速混合马尔可夫链或随机图模型生成随机图的鲁棒模拟方法，随后将基于平均的共识方法与一种新提出的加权技术及上置信界相结合，从而提供了一种 UCB 型解决方案。我们的算法考虑了图中的随机性，去除了常规的双重随机性假设，且仅在初始化时仅需获知客户端的数量。我们在次高斯和次指数环境中推导了阶数为 $\log{T}$ 的最优实例依赖遗憾上界，以及阶数为 $\sqrt{T}\log T$（至多相差一个 $\log T$ 因子）的几乎最优均值间隙无关遗憾上界。重要的是，我们的遗憾界限以高概率成立并体现了图的随机性，而先前工作则是在假设下考虑期望遗憾，且要求更为严格的奖励分布。

[No.139] Fine-Grained Human Feedback Gives Better Rewards for Language Model Training
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 细粒度的人工反馈为语言模型训练提供更好的奖励
类别: 
作者: Zeqiu Wu;Yushi Hu;Weijia Shi;Nouha Dziri;Alane Suhr;Prithviraj Ammanabrolu;Noah A. Smith;Mari Ostendorf;Hannaneh Hajishirzi;
机构: University of Washington;Allen Institute for Artificial Intelligence;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72428
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01693
英摘: Language models (LMs) often exhibit undesirable text generation behaviors, including generating false, toxic, or irrelevant outputs. Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) - where human preference judgments on LM outputs are transformed into a learning signal - has recently shown promise in addressing these issues. However, such holistic feedback conveys limited information on long text outputs; it does not indicate which aspects of the outputs influenced user preference; e.g., which parts contain what type(s) of errors. In this paper, we use fine-grained human feedback (e.g., which sentence is false, which sub-sentence is irrelevant) as an explicit training signal. We introduce Fine-Grained RLHF, a framework that enables training and learning from reward functions that are fine-grained in two respects: (1) density, providing a reward after every segment (e.g., a sentence) is generated; and (2) incorporating multiple reward models associated with different feedback types (e.g., factual incorrectness, irrelevance, and information incompleteness). We conduct experiments on detoxification and long-form question answering to illustrate how learning with such reward functions leads to improved performance, supported by both automatic and human evaluation. Additionally, we show that LM behaviors can be customized using different combinations of fine-grained reward models. We release all data, collected human feedback, and codes at https://FineGrainedRLHF.github.io.
中摘: 

语言模型（LMs）经常表现出不理想的文本生成行为，包括生成虚假信息、毒性内容或不相关的输出。人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）——即把人类对语言模型输出的偏好判断转化为学习信号——最近在解决这些问题方面显示出潜力。然而，这种整体性反馈在长文本输出方面传达的信息有限；它无法指出输出的哪些具体方面影响了用户偏好；例如，哪些部分包含何种类型的错误。在本文中，我们利用细粒度的人类反馈（例如，哪个句子是虚假的，哪个句子片段是不相关的）作为显式训练信号。我们提出了细粒度人类反馈强化学习（Fine-Grained RLHF）框架，该框架能够从两个方面的细粒度奖励函数中进行训练和学习：(1) 密度，即在生成每个片段（例如，一个句子）后提供奖励；(2) 整合与不同反馈类型相关联的多个奖励模型（例如，事实错误、不相关性和信息不完整）。我们在去毒和长文本问答任务上进行了实验，以说明利用此类奖励函数进行学习如何带来性能提升，并得到了自动评估和人工评估的支持。此外，我们展示了语言模型的行为可以通过不同组合的细粒度奖励模型进行定制。我们在 https://FineGrainedRLHF.github.io 发布了所有数据、收集的人类反馈和代码。
