[No.141] Would I have gotten that reward? Long-term credit assignment by counterfactual contribution analysis
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 我会得到那个奖励吗？逆事实贡献分析的长期信用分配
类别: 
作者: Alexander Meulemans;Simon Schug;Seijin Kobayashi;Nathaniel Daw;Greg Wayne;
机构: Swiss Federal Institute of Technology;Google;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/69923
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.16803
英摘: To make reinforcement learning more sample efficient, we need better credit assignment methods that measure an action's influence on future rewards. Building upon Hindsight Credit Assignment (HCA), we introduce Counterfactual Contribution Analysis (COCOA), a new family of model-based credit assignment algorithms. Our algorithms achieve precise credit assignment by measuring the contribution of actions upon obtaining subsequent rewards, by quantifying a counterfactual query: 'Would the agent still have reached this reward if it had taken another action?'. We show that measuring contributions w.r.t. rewarding states, as is done in HCA, results in spurious estimates of contributions, causing HCA to degrade towards the high-variance REINFORCE estimator in many relevant environments. Instead, we measure contributions w.r.t. rewards or learned representations of the rewarding objects, resulting in gradient estimates with lower variance. We run experiments on a suite of problems specifically designed to evaluate long-term credit assignment capabilities. By using dynamic programming, we measure ground-truth policy gradients and show that the improved performance of our new model-based credit assignment methods is due to lower bias and variance compared to HCA and common baselines. Our results demonstrate how modeling action contributions towards rewarding outcomes can be leveraged for credit assignment, opening a new path towards sample-efficient reinforcement learning.
中摘: 

为了使强化学习更具样本效率，我们需要更好的信用分配方法来衡量动作对未来奖励的影响。在基于后视信用分配（HCA）的基础上，我们提出了反事实贡献分析（COCOA），这是一类新的基于模型的信用分配算法。我们的算法通过量化一个反事实查询：“如果智能体采取了其他动作，是否仍能获得该奖励？”，来衡量动作在获得后续奖励时的贡献，从而实现精确的信用分配。我们表明，像 HCA 那样相对于奖励状态测量贡献，会导致贡献的虚假估计，致使 HCA 在许多相关环境中退化至具有高方差的 REINFORCE 估计器。相反，我们相对于奖励或奖励对象的已学习表征来测量贡献，从而得到方差更低的梯度估计。我们在一系列专门设计用于评估长期信用分配能力的问题上进行了实验。通过利用动态规划，我们测量了真实策略梯度，并表明我们新的基于模型的信用分配方法相比 HCA 和常见基线表现更佳，这是由于它们具有更低的偏差和方差。我们的结果表明，通过建模动作对奖励结果的贡献可用于信用分配，为样本高效的强化学习开辟了一条新路径。

[No.142] Inverse Preference Learning: Preference-based RL without a Reward Function
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 逆向偏好学习：基于偏好的RL而无需奖励函数
类别: 
作者: Joey Hejna;Dorsa Sadigh;
机构: Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70866
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.15363
英摘: Reward functions are difficult to design and often hard to align with human intent. Preference-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms address these problems by learning reward functions from human feedback. However, the majority of preference-based RL methods naïvely combine supervised reward models with off-the-shelf RL algorithms. Contemporary approaches have sought to improve performance and query complexity by using larger and more complex reward architectures such as transformers. Instead of using highly complex architectures, we develop a new and parameter-efficient algorithm, Inverse Preference Learning (IPL), specifically designed for learning from offline preference data. Our key insight is that for a fixed policy, the $Q$-function encodes all information about the reward function, effectively making them interchangeable. Using this insight, we completely eliminate the need for a learned reward function. Our resulting algorithm is simpler and more parameter-efficient. Across a suite of continuous control and robotics benchmarks, IPL attains competitive performance compared to more complex approaches that leverage transformer-based and non-Markovian reward functions while having fewer algorithmic hyperparameters and learned network parameters. Our code is publicly released.
中摘: 

奖励函数的设计往往具有挑战性，且难以与人类意图对齐。基于偏好的强化学习（RL）算法通过从人类反馈中学习奖励函数来解决这些问题。然而，大多数基于偏好的 RL 方法朴素地将监督奖励模型与现成的 RL 算法相结合。当代方法试图通过使用更大、更复杂的奖励架构（如 Transformer）来提高性能和查询复杂度。与使用高度复杂的架构不同，我们开发了一种新的、参数高效的算法——逆偏好学习（IPL），专门设计用于从离线偏好数据中学习。我们的关键洞察是，对于固定策略，Q 函数编码了关于奖励函数的所有信息，从而有效地使二者可以互换。利用这一洞察，我们完全消除了对所学奖励函数的需求。我们提出的算法更为简单且参数更高效。在一系列连续控制和机器人基准测试中，IPL 取得了与那些利用基于 Transformer 和非马尔可夫奖励函数的更复杂方法相竞争的性能，同时所需的算法超参数和学习网络参数更少。我们的代码已公开发布。

[No.143] MIMEx: Intrinsic Rewards from Masked Input Modeling
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: MIMEx：掩码输入建模固有奖励
类别: 
作者: Toru Lin;Allan Jabri;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70874
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.08932
英摘: Exploring in environments with high-dimensional observations is hard. One promising approach for exploration is to use intrinsic rewards, which often boils down to estimating "novelty" of states, transitions, or trajectories with deep networks. Prior works have shown that conditional prediction objectives such as masked autoencoding can be seen as stochastic estimation of pseudo-likelihood. We show how this perspective naturally leads to a unified view on existing intrinsic reward approaches: they are special cases of conditional prediction, where the estimation of novelty can be seen as pseudo-likelihood estimation with different mask distributions. From this view, we propose a general framework for deriving intrinsic rewards -- Masked Input Modeling for Exploration (MIMEx) -- where the mask distribution can be flexibly tuned to control the difficulty of the underlying conditional prediction task. We demonstrate that MIMEx can achieve superior results when compared against competitive baselines on a suite of challenging sparse-reward visuomotor tasks.
中摘: 

在高维观测环境下进行探索是困难的。探索的一种有前景的方法是使用内在奖励，这通常归结为利用深度网络对状态、转移或轨迹的“新颖性”进行估计。先前的研究表明，诸如掩码自编码之类的条件预测目标可被视为伪似然的随机估计。我们展示了这一视角如何自然地导出对现有内在奖励方法的统一看法：它们是条件预测的特例，其中新颖性的估计可视为采用不同掩码分布的伪似然估计。基于此视角，我们提出了一种推导内在奖励的通用框架——用于探索的掩码输入建模（MIMEx）——其中掩码分布可灵活调节，以控制底层条件预测任务的难度。我们证明，在一系列具有挑战性的稀疏奖励视觉运动任务上，与具有竞争力的基线相比，MIMEx 能够取得优越的结果。

[No.144] Efficient Algorithms for Generalized Linear Bandits with Heavy-tailed Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 高效算法在一般化线性 bandit 中处理重尾奖励问题
类别: 
作者: Bo Xue;Yimu Wang;Yuanyu Wan;Jinfeng Yi;Lijun Zhang;
机构: City University of Hong Kong;University of Waterloo;Zhejiang University;JD;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71450
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.18701
英摘: This paper investigates the problem of generalized linear bandits with heavy-tailed rewards, whose $(1+ε)$-th moment is bounded for some $ε\in (0,1]$. Although there exist methods for generalized linear bandits, most of them focus on bounded or sub-Gaussian rewards and are not well-suited for many real-world scenarios, such as financial markets and web-advertising. To address this issue, we propose two novel algorithms based on truncation and mean of medians. These algorithms achieve an almost optimal regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(dT^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$, where $d$ is the dimension of contextual information and $T$ is the time horizon. Our truncation-based algorithm supports online learning, distinguishing it from existing truncation-based approaches. Additionally, our mean-of-medians-based algorithm requires only $O(\log T)$ rewards and one estimator per epoch, making it more practical. Moreover, our algorithms improve the regret bounds by a logarithmic factor compared to existing algorithms when $ε=1$. Numerical experimental results confirm the merits of our algorithms.
中摘: 

本文研究了具有厚尾收益的广义线性老虎机问题，其中 $(1+ε)$-阶矩对某个 $ε\in (0,1]$ 有界。尽管存在针对广义线性老虎机的方法，但大多数方法侧重于有界或次高斯收益，并不十分适用于许多现实世界场景，例如金融市场和网络广告。为解决这一问题，我们提出了两种基于截断和中位数均值的新算法。这些算法实现了几乎最优的遗憾界 $\widetilde{O}(dT^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$，其中 $d$ 是上下文信息的维度，$T$ 是时间视界。我们的基于截断的算法支持在线学习，使其区别于现有的基于截断的方法。此外，我们的基于中位数均值的算法每个轮次仅需 $O(\log T)$ 个收益和一个估计器，使其更具实用性。而且，当 $ε=1$ 时，与现有算法相比，我们的算法将遗憾界改进了一个对数因子。数值实验结果证实了我们算法的优势。

[No.145] Rethinking Incentives in Recommender Systems: Are Monotone Rewards Always Beneficial?
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 重新思考推荐系统中的激励机制：单调奖励总是有益的吗？
类别: 
作者: Fan Yao;Chuanhao Li;Karthik Abinav Sankararaman;Yiming Liao;Yan Zhu;Qifan Wang;Hongning Wang;Haifeng Xu;
机构: Meta;University of Virginia;University of Chicago;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.07893
英摘: The past decade has witnessed the flourishing of a new profession as media content creators, who rely on revenue streams from online content recommendation platforms. The reward mechanism employed by these platforms creates a competitive environment among creators which affect their production choices and, consequently, content distribution and system welfare. It is thus crucial to design the platform's reward mechanism in order to steer the creators' competition towards a desirable welfare outcome in the long run. This work makes two major contributions in this regard: first, we uncover a fundamental limit about a class of widely adopted mechanisms, coined Merit-based Monotone Mechanisms, by showing that they inevitably lead to a constant fraction loss of the optimal welfare. To circumvent this limitation, we introduce Backward Rewarding Mechanisms (BRMs) and show that the competition game resultant from BRMs possesses a potential game structure. BRMs thus naturally induce strategic creators' collective behaviors towards optimizing the potential function, which can be designed to match any given welfare metric. In addition, the BRM class can be parameterized to allow the platform to directly optimize welfare within the feasible mechanism space even when the welfare metric is not explicitly defined.
中摘: 

过去十年见证了一种新兴职业的蓬勃发展，即媒体内容创作者，他们的收入来源于在线内容推荐平台。这些平台所采用的奖励机制在创作者之间营造了竞争环境，影响了他们的生产选择，进而影响了内容分发和系统福利。因此，设计平台的奖励机制以在长期内引导创作者的竞争朝向理想的福利结果至关重要。本文在此方面做出了两项主要贡献：首先，我们揭示了一类广泛采用的机制（称为“基于功绩的单调机制”）的根本局限，通过证明它们不可避免地会导致最优福利出现恒定比例的损失。为规避这一局限，我们引入了向后奖励机制（BRMs），并证明由 BRMs 引发的竞争博弈具有势博弈结构。因此，BRMs 自然地促使策略性创作者的集体行为趋向于优化势函数，该势函数可被设计为与任何给定的福利指标相匹配。此外，BRM 类可以进行参数化设计，使得平台能够在可行的机制空间内直接优化福利，即便福利指标未被明确定义。

[No.146] Restless Bandits with Average Reward: Breaking the Uniform Global Attractor Assumption
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 平均奖励下的不安分强盗：打破均匀全局吸引子假设
类别: 
作者: Yige Hong;Qiaomin Xie;Yudong Chen;Weina Wang;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;University of Wisconsin-Madison;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71907
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.00196
英摘: We study the infinite-horizon restless bandit problem with the average reward criterion, in both discrete-time and continuous-time settings. A fundamental goal is to efficiently compute policies that achieve a diminishing optimality gap as the number of arms, $N$, grows large. Existing results on asymptotic optimality all rely on the uniform global attractor property (UGAP), a complex and challenging-to-verify assumption. In this paper, we propose a general, simulation-based framework, Follow-the-Virtual-Advice, that converts any single-armed policy into a policy for the original $N$-armed problem. This is done by simulating the single-armed policy on each arm and carefully steering the real state towards the simulated state. Our framework can be instantiated to produce a policy with an $O(1/\sqrt{N})$ optimality gap. In the discrete-time setting, our result holds under a simpler synchronization assumption, which covers some problem instances that violate UGAP. More notably, in the continuous-time setting, we do not require \emph{any} additional assumptions beyond the standard unichain condition. In both settings, our work is the first asymptotic optimality result that does not require UGAP.
中摘: 

我们在离散时间和连续时间两种设定下，研究了具有平均奖励准则的无限视界非平静多臂问题。一个基本目标是随着臂数 $N$ 的增大，高效地计算出最优性间隙不断缩小的策略。关于渐近最优性的现有结果均依赖于一致全局吸引子性质（UGAP），这是一种复杂且难以验证的假设。在本文中，我们提出了一个通用的、基于模拟的框架——遵循虚拟建议（Follow-the-Virtual-Advice），该框架可将任何单臂策略转换为针对原始 $N$ 臂问题的策略。其实现方式是在每一臂上模拟单臂策略，并仔细引导真实状态向模拟状态靠近。我们的框架可实例化以生成具有 $O(1/\sqrt{N})$ 最优性间隙的策略。在离散时间设定下，我们的结果在一种更简单的同步假设下即成立，该假设涵盖了一些不满足 UGAP 的问题实例。更为重要的是，在连续时间设定下，除了标准的单链条件外，我们不需要任何额外的假设。在这两种设定下，我们的工作都是首个不需要 UGAP 的渐近最优性结果。

[No.147] Direct Preference-based Policy Optimization without Reward Modeling
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 直接基于偏好策略优化无需建模奖励
类别: 
作者: Gaon An;Junhyeok Lee;Xingdong Zuo;Norio Kosaka;Kyung-Min Kim;Hyun Oh Song;
机构: Seoul National University;NAVER Corporation;University of London;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72248
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.12842
英摘: Preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) is an approach that enables RL agents to learn from preference, which is particularly useful when formulating a reward function is challenging. Existing PbRL methods generally involve a two-step procedure: they first learn a reward model based on given preference data and then employ off-the-shelf reinforcement learning algorithms using the learned reward model. However, obtaining an accurate reward model solely from preference information, especially when the preference is from human teachers, can be difficult. Instead, we propose a PbRL algorithm that directly learns from preference without requiring any reward modeling. To achieve this, we adopt a contrastive learning framework to design a novel policy scoring metric that assigns a high score to policies that align with the given preferences. We apply our algorithm to offline RL tasks with actual human preference labels and show that our algorithm outperforms or is on par with the existing PbRL methods. Notably, on high-dimensional control tasks, our algorithm surpasses offline RL methods that learn with ground-truth reward information. Finally, we show that our algorithm can be successfully applied to fine-tune large language models.
中摘: 

基于偏好的强化学习（PbRL）是一种使强化学习智能体能够从偏好中学习的方法，这在制定奖励函数具有挑战性时尤为有用。现有的 PbRL 方法通常涉及一个两步流程：首先基于给定的偏好数据学习奖励模型，然后利用学习到的奖励模型采用现成的强化学习算法。然而，仅凭偏好信息获取准确的奖励模型，特别是当偏好来自人类教师时，可能相当困难。相反，我们提出了一种 PbRL 算法，它直接从偏好中学习，无需任何奖励建模。为实现这一目标，我们采用对比学习框架设计了一种新颖的策略评分指标，该指标为与给定偏好相一致的策略赋予高分。我们将我们的算法应用于具有实际人类偏好标签的离线强化学习任务，结果表明我们的算法优于或与现有的 PbRL 方法相当。值得注意的是，在高维控制任务上，我们的算法超越了那些利用真实奖励信息进行学习的离线强化学习方法。最后，我们证明了我们的算法可以成功应用于微调大型语言模型。

[No.148] DIFFER:Decomposing Individual Reward for Fair Experience Replay in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 差别：在多智能体强化学习中公平经验回放的个体奖励分解
类别: 
作者: Xunhan Hu;Jian Zhao;Wengang Zhou;Ruili Feng;Houqiang Li;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72548
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.10574
英摘: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is a challenging task, as agents must learn complex and diverse individual strategies from a shared team reward. However, existing methods struggle to distinguish and exploit important individual experiences, as they lack an effective way to decompose the team reward into individual rewards. To address this challenge, we propose DIFFER, a powerful theoretical framework for decomposing individual rewards to enable fair experience replay in MARL. By enforcing the invariance of network gradients, we establish a partial differential equation whose solution yields the underlying individual reward function. The individual TD-error can then be computed from the solved closed-form individual rewards, indicating the importance of each piece of experience in the learning task and guiding the training process. Our method elegantly achieves an equivalence to the original learning framework when individual experiences are homogeneous, while also adapting to achieve more muscular efficiency and fairness when diversity is observed.Our extensive experiments on popular benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our theory and method, demonstrating significant improvements in learning efficiency and fairness.
中摘: 

协作式多智能体强化学习（MARL）是一项具有挑战性的任务，因为智能体必须从共享的团队奖励中学习复杂且多样的个体策略。然而，现有方法难以区分和利用重要的个体经验，因为它们缺乏一种有效的将团队奖励分解为个体奖励的方法。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了 DIFFER，这是一个强大的理论框架，用于分解个体奖励以实现 MARL 中的公平经验回放。通过强制网络梯度的不变性，我们建立了一个偏微分方程，其解得出了底层个体奖励函数。随后，可根据求解出的闭式个体奖励计算个体 TD 误差，这表明了学习任务中每条经验的重要性，并指导训练过程。当个体经验同质时，我们的方法优雅地实现了与原始学习框架的等价性，而当观察到多样性时，该方法也能适应以实现更高效与公平性。我们在常用基准上进行的广泛实验验证了我们理论与方法的有效性，表明在学习效率和公平性方面均有显著提升。

[No.149] Consistent Aggregation of Objectives with Diverse Time Preferences Requires Non-Markovian Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 具有多样化时间偏好的目标一致聚合需要非马尔可夫奖励
类别: 
作者: Silviu Pitis;
机构: 
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70049
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.00435
英摘: As the capabilities of artificial agents improve, they are being increasingly deployed to service multiple diverse objectives and stakeholders. However, the composition of these objectives is often performed ad hoc, with no clear justification. This paper takes a normative approach to multi-objective agency: from a set of intuitively appealing axioms, it is shown that Markovian aggregation of Markovian reward functions is not possible when the time preference (discount factor) for each objective may vary. It follows that optimal multi-objective agents must admit rewards that are non-Markovian with respect to the individual objectives. To this end, a practical non-Markovian aggregation scheme is proposed, which overcomes the impossibility with only one additional parameter for each objective. This work offers new insights into sequential, multi-objective agency and intertemporal choice, and has practical implications for the design of AI systems deployed to serve multiple generations of principals with varying time preference.
中摘: 

随着人工智能智能体能力的不断提升，它们正日益被部署以实现多种多样的目标并服务于不同的利益相关者。然而，这些目标的构成往往是临时的，缺乏明确的理论依据。本文采用规范方法来研究多目标智能体问题：基于一组直观合理的公理，本文证明，当每个目标的时间偏好（贴现因子）可能不同时，马尔可夫奖励函数的马尔可夫聚合是不可行的。由此可知，最优的多目标智能体必须包含相对于各目标而言是非马尔可夫的奖励函数。为此，本文提出了一种实用的非马尔可夫聚合方案，该方案仅通过为每个目标增加一个额外参数，便克服了上述不可行性。本研究为序贯多目标智能体和跨期选择问题提供了新的见解，并对设计服务于时间偏好各异的多代委托人的 AI 系统具有重要的实际意义。

[No.150] Uncertainty Estimation for Safety-critical Scene Segmentation via Fine-grained Reward Maximization
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 通过细粒度奖励最大化进行安全关键场景分割的不确定性估计
类别: 
作者: Hongzheng Yang;Cheng Chen;Yueyao Chen;Markus Scheppach;Hon Chi Yip;Qi Dou;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.02719
英摘: Uncertainty estimation plays an important role for future reliable deployment of deep segmentation models in safety-critical scenarios such as medical applications. However, existing methods for uncertainty estimation have been limited by the lack of explicit guidance for calibrating the prediction risk and model confidence. In this work, we propose a novel fine-grained reward maximization (FGRM) framework, to address uncertainty estimation by directly utilizing an uncertainty metric related reward function with a reinforcement learning based model tuning algorithm. This would benefit the model uncertainty estimation through direct optimization guidance for model calibration. Specifically, our method designs a new uncertainty estimation reward function using the calibration metric, which is maximized to fine-tune an evidential learning pre-trained segmentation model for calibrating prediction risk. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on two large safety-critical surgical scene segmentation datasets under two different uncertainty estimation settings. With real-time one forward pass at inference, our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a clear margin on all the calibration metrics of uncertainty estimation, while maintaining a high task accuracy for the segmentation results. [No.151] A State Representation for Diminishing Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 递减奖励的一种状态表示方法
类别: 
作者: Ted Moskovitz;Samo Hromadka;Ahmed Touati;Diana L Borsa;Maneesh Sahani;
机构: University College London;Meta;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72705
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.03710
英摘: A common setting in multitask reinforcement learning (RL) demands that an agent rapidly adapt to various stationary reward functions randomly sampled from a fixed distribution. In such situations, the successor representation (SR) is a popular framework which supports rapid policy evaluation by decoupling a policy's expected discounted, cumulative state occupancies from a specific reward function. However, in the natural world, sequential tasks are rarely independent, and instead reflect shifting priorities based on the availability and subjective perception of rewarding stimuli. Reflecting this disjunction, in this paper we study the phenomenon of diminishing marginal utility and introduce a novel state representation, the $λ$ representation ($λ$R) which, surprisingly, is required for policy evaluation in this setting and which generalizes the SR as well as several other state representations from the literature. We establish the $λ$R's formal properties and examine its normative advantages in the context of machine learning, as well as its usefulness for studying natural behaviors, particularly foraging.
中摘: 

多任务强化学习（RL）中一种常见的设定要求智能体能够快速适应从固定分布中随机采样的各种平稳奖励函数。在此类情境下，后继表示（SR）是一种流行的框架，它通过将策略的期望折扣累计状态占用与特定奖励函数解耦，从而支持快速策略评估。然而，在自然界中，顺序任务很少相互独立，而是反映了基于奖励刺激可用性和主观感知而动态变化的优先级。为了反映这一差异，在本文中我们研究了边际效用递减现象，并引入了一种新颖的状态表示，即λ表示（λR）。令人惊讶的是，该表示在此设定下是策略评估所必需的，并且它泛化了 SR 以及文献中的其他几种状态表示。我们确立了λR 的形式属性，考察了其在机器学习背景下的规范优势，以及其在研究自然行为（特别是觅食行为）方面的有用性。

[No.154] Reward-Directed Conditional Diffusion: Provable Distribution Estimation and Reward Improvement
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 奖励导向的条件扩散：可证明的分布估计与奖励改进
类别: 
作者: Hui Yuan;Kaixuan Huang;Chengzhuo Ni;Minshuo Chen;Mengdi Wang;
机构: Princeton University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72821
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.07055
英摘: We explore the methodology and theory of reward-directed generation via conditional diffusion models. Directed generation aims to generate samples with desired properties as measured by a reward function, which has broad applications in generative AI, reinforcement learning, and computational biology. We consider the common learning scenario where the data set consists of unlabeled data along with a smaller set of data with noisy reward labels. Our approach leverages a learned reward function on the smaller data set as a pseudolabeler. From a theoretical standpoint, we show that this directed generator can effectively learn and sample from the reward-conditioned data distribution. Additionally, our model is capable of recovering the latent subspace representation of data. Moreover, we establish that the model generates a new population that moves closer to a user-specified target reward value, where the optimality gap aligns with the off-policy bandit regret in the feature subspace. The improvement in rewards obtained is influenced by the interplay between the strength of the reward signal, the distribution shift, and the cost of off-support extrapolation. We provide empirical results to validate our theory and highlight the relationship between the strength of extrapolation and the quality of generated samples.

[No.155] Reward Scale Robustness for Proximal Policy Optimization via DreamerV3 Tricks
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 通过DreamerV3技巧增强 proximal policy optimization 的奖励尺度稳健性
类别: 
作者: Ryan Sullivan;Akarsh Kumar;Shengyi Huang;John P Dickerson;Joseph Suarez;
机构: University of Maryland;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Drexel University;Optimized Markets, Inc;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72315
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.17805
英摘: Most reinforcement learning methods rely heavily on dense, well-normalized environment rewards. DreamerV3 recently introduced a model-based method with a number of tricks that mitigate these limitations, achieving state-of-the-art on a wide range of benchmarks with a single set of hyperparameters. This result sparked discussion about the generality of the tricks, since they appear to be applicable to other reinforcement learning algorithms. Our work applies DreamerV3's tricks to PPO and is the first such empirical study outside of the original work. Surprisingly, we find that the tricks presented do not transfer as general improvements to PPO. We use a high quality PPO reference implementation and present extensive ablation studies totaling over 10,000 A100 hours on the Arcade Learning Environment and the DeepMind Control Suite. Though our experiments demonstrate that these tricks do not generally outperform PPO, we identify cases where they succeed and offer insight into the relationship between the implementation tricks. In particular, PPO with these tricks performs comparably to PPO on Atari games with reward clipping and significantly outperforms PPO without reward clipping.

[No.156] Guide Your Agent with Adaptive Multimodal Rewards

英摘: Developing an agent capable of adapting to unseen environments remains a difficult challenge in imitation learning. This work presents Adaptive Return-conditioned Policy (ARP), an efficient framework designed to enhance the agent's generalization ability using natural language task descriptions and pre-trained multimodal encoders. Our key idea is to calculate a similarity between visual observations and natural language instructions in the pre-trained multimodal embedding space (such as CLIP) and use it as a reward signal. We then train a return-conditioned policy using expert demonstrations labeled with multimodal rewards. This results in superior generalization performances even when faced with unseen text instructions, compared to existing text-conditioned policies. To improve the quality of rewards, we also introduce a fine-tuning method for pre-trained multimodal encoders, further enhancing the performance. [No.157] Regularity as Intrinsic Reward for Free Play
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward

英摘: We propose regularity as a novel reward signal for intrinsically-motivated reinforcement learning. [No.158] Read and Reap the Rewards: Learning to Play Atari with the Help of Instruction Manuals

英摘: High sample complexity has long been a challenge for RL. On the other hand, humans learn to perform tasks not only from interaction or demonstrations, but also by reading unstructured text documents, e.g., instruction manuals. Instruction manuals and wiki pages are among the most abundant data that could inform agents of valuable features and policies or task-specific environmental dynamics and reward structures. Therefore, we hypothesize that the ability to utilize human-written instruction manuals to assist learning policies for specific tasks should lead to a more efficient and better-performing agent. [No.159] Reward Imputation with Sketching for Contextual Batched Bandits
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 基于素描的奖励推断与上下文批量多臂老虎机问题
类别: 
作者: Xiao Zhang;Ninglu Shao;Zihua Si;Jun Xu;Wenhan Wang;Hanjing Su;Ji-Rong Wen;
机构: Renmin University of China;Tencent;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72337
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.06719
英摘: Contextual batched bandit (CBB) is a setting where a batch of rewards is observed from the environment at the end of each episode, but the rewards of the non-executed actions are unobserved, resulting in partial-information feedback. Existing approaches for CBB often ignore the rewards of the non-executed actions, leading to underutilization of feedback information. In this paper, we propose an efficient approach called Sketched Policy Updating with Imputed Rewards (SPUIR) that completes the unobserved rewards using sketching, which approximates the full-information feedbacks. We formulate reward imputation as an imputation regularized ridge regression problem that captures the feedback mechanisms of both executed and non-executed actions. To reduce time complexity, we solve the regression problem using randomized sketching. We prove that our approach achieves an instantaneous regret with controllable bias and smaller variance than approaches without reward imputation. Furthermore, our approach enjoys a sublinear regret bound against the optimal policy. We also present two extensions, a rate-scheduled version and a version for nonlinear rewards, making our approach more practical. Experimental results show that SPUIR outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on synthetic, public benchmark, and real-world datasets.
中摘: 

上下文批量多臂老虎机（CBB）是一种设定，其中在每个回合结束时从环境中观察到一批奖励，但未尝试动作的奖励未被观察到，从而造成部分信息反馈。CBB 的现有方法通常忽略未尝试动作的奖励，导致反馈信息利用不足。本文提出了一种名为“带插补奖励的草图策略更新”（SPUIR）的高效方法，该方法利用草图技术补全未观察到的奖励，从而近似全信息反馈。我们将奖励插补形式化为一个插补正则化岭回归问题，该问题刻画了已执行和未尝试动作的反馈机制。为了降低时间复杂度，我们使用随机草图求解该回归问题。我们证明了，与不进行奖励插补的方法相比，我们的方法能够实现具有可控偏差和更小方差的瞬时遗憾。此外，我们的方法相对于最优策略具有次线性遗憾界。我们还提出了两种扩展：一种是速率调度版本，另一种是适用于非线性奖励的版本，使我们的方法更具实用性。实验结果表明，SPUIR 在合成、公共基准和真实世界数据集上均优于最先进基线。

[No.160] Provably Robust Temporal Difference Learning for Heavy-Tailed Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 可验证稳健的时间差分学习方法用于重尾奖励
类别: 
作者: Semih Cayci;Atilla Eryilmaz;
机构: RWTH Aachen University;Ohio State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71853
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.11455
英摘: In a broad class of reinforcement learning applications, stochastic rewards have heavy-tailed distributions, which lead to infinite second-order moments for stochastic (semi)gradients in policy evaluation and direct policy optimization. In such instances, the existing RL methods may fail miserably due to frequent statistical outliers. In this work, we establish that temporal difference (TD) learning with a dynamic gradient clipping mechanism, and correspondingly operated natural actor-critic (NAC), can be provably robustified against heavy-tailed reward distributions. It is shown in the framework of linear function approximation that a favorable tradeoff between bias and variability of the stochastic gradients can be achieved with this dynamic gradient clipping mechanism. In particular, we prove that robust versions of TD learning achieve sample complexities of order $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p}})$ and $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1-\frac{1}{p}})$ with and without the full-rank assumption on the feature matrix, respectively, under heavy-tailed rewards with finite moments of order $(1+p)$ for some $p\in(0,1]$, both in expectation and with high probability. We show that a robust variant of NAC based on Robust TD learning achieves $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-4-\frac{2}{p}})$ sample complexity. We corroborate our theoretical results with numerical experiments.
中摘: 

在一大类强化学习应用中，随机奖励服从重尾分布，这导致策略评估和直接策略优化中的随机（半）梯度具有无穷大的二阶矩。在此类情况下，由于频繁的统计异常值，现有的强化学习方法可能会严重失效。在这项工作中，我们证明了带有动态梯度裁剪机制的时序差分（TD）学习，以及相应实施的自然演员 - 评论家（NAC），能够针对重尾奖励分布实现可证明的鲁棒性。在线性函数近似的框架下表明，这种动态梯度裁剪机制可以实现随机梯度的偏差与变异性之间的有利权衡。特别是，我们证明了在具有 $(1+p)$ 阶有限矩的重尾奖励下（对于某些 $p\in(0,1]$），TD 学习的鲁棒版本分别在特征矩阵具有满秩假设和不具有该假设的情况下，实现了量级为 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p}})$ 和 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1-\frac{1}{p}})$ 的样本复杂度，且该结论在期望意义和高概率下均成立。我们表明，基于鲁棒 TD 学习的 NAC 鲁棒变体实现了 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-4-\frac{2}{p}})$ 的样本复杂度。我们通过数值实验验证了我们的理论结果。

英摘: Recent advances in machine learning have shown that Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) can improve machine learning models and align them with human preferences. In this paper, we propose to adapt similar RL-based methods to unsupervised object discovery, i.e. learning to detect objects from LiDAR points without any training labels. [No.162] Kullback-Leibler Maillard Sampling for Multi-armed Bandits with Bounded Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 凯莱布-利比-马尔登采样在有界奖励多臂 bandit 中的应用
类别: 
作者: Hao Qin;Kwang-Sung Jun;Chicheng Zhang;
机构: University of Arizona;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70020
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.14989
英摘: We study $K$-armed bandit problems where the reward distributions of the arms are all supported on the $[0,1]$ interval. It has been a challenge to design regret-efficient randomized exploration algorithms in this setting. Maillard sampling \cite{maillard13apprentissage}, an attractive alternative to Thompson sampling, has recently been shown to achieve competitive regret guarantees in the sub-Gaussian reward setting \cite{bian2022maillard} while maintaining closed-form action probabilities, which is useful for offline policy evaluation. In this work, we propose the Kullback-Leibler Maillard Sampling (KL-MS) algorithm, a natural extension of Maillard sampling for achieving KL-style gap-dependent regret bound. We show that KL-MS enjoys the asymptotic optimality when the rewards are Bernoulli and has a worst-case regret bound of the form $O(\sqrt{μ^*(1-μ^*) K T \ln K} + K \ln T)$, where $μ^*$ is the expected reward of the optimal arm, and $T$ is the time horizon length.

我们研究 $K$ 臂老虎机问题，其中各臂的奖励分布均支撑于 $[0,1]$ 区间。在此设定下，设计遗憾高效的随机探索算法一直是一个挑战。Maillard 采样 \cite{maillard13apprentissage} 作为汤普森采样的一个有吸引力的替代方案，最近已被证明在次高斯奖励设定 \cite{bian2022maillard} 下能够实现具有竞争力的遗憾保证，同时保持行动概率的解析形式，这有利于离线策略评估。在本文中，我们提出了 Kullback-Leibler Maillard Sampling（KL-MS）算法，这是 Maillard 采样为实现 KL 风格间隙依赖遗憾界而进行的自然扩展。我们证明了当奖励服从伯努利分布时，KL-MS 具有渐近最优性，并且其最坏情况遗憾界具有 $O(\sqrt{μ^*(1-μ^*) K T \ln K} + K \ln T)$ 的形式，其中 $μ^*$ 是最优臂的期望奖励，$T$ 是时间跨度长度。

[No.163] Tackling Heavy-Tailed Rewards in Reinforcement Learning with Function Approximation: Minimax Optimal and Instance-Dependent Regret Bounds
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 使用函数逼近处理强化学习中的重尾奖励：最小最大最优和实例依赖的后悔界
类别: 
作者: Jiayi Huang;Han Zhong;Liwei Wang;Lin Yang;
机构: Peking University;University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70887
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.06836
英摘: While numerous works have focused on devising efficient algorithms for reinforcement learning (RL) with uniformly bounded rewards, it remains an open question whether sample or time-efficient algorithms for RL with large state-action space exist when the rewards are \emph{heavy-tailed}, i.e., with only finite $(1+ε)$-th moments for some $ε\in(0,1]$. In this work, we address the challenge of such rewards in RL with linear function approximation. We first design an algorithm, \textsc{Heavy-OFUL}, for heavy-tailed linear bandits, achieving an \emph{instance-dependent} $T$-round regret of $\tilde{O}\big(d T^{\frac{1-ε}{2(1+ε)}} \sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^T ν_t^2} + d T^{\frac{1-ε}{2(1+ε)}}\big)$, the \emph{first} of this kind. Here, $d$ is the feature dimension, and $ν_t^{1+ε}$ is the $(1+ε)$-th central moment of the reward at the $t$-th round. We further show the above bound is minimax optimal when applied to the worst-case instances in stochastic and deterministic linear bandits. We then extend this algorithm to the RL settings with linear function approximation. Our algorithm, termed as \textsc{Heavy-LSVI-UCB}, achieves the \emph{first} computationally efficient \emph{instance-dependent} $K$-episode regret of $\tilde{O}(d \sqrt{H \mathcal{U}^*} K^\frac{1}{1+ε} + d \sqrt{H \mathcal{V}^* K})$. Here, $H$ is length of the episode, and $\mathcal{U}^*, \mathcal{V}^*$ are instance-dependent quantities scaling with the central moment of reward and value functions, respectively. We also provide a matching minimax lower bound $Ω(d H K^{\frac{1}{1+ε}} + d \sqrt{H^3 K})$ to demonstrate the optimality of our algorithm in the worst case. Our result is achieved via a novel robust self-normalized concentration inequality that may be of independent interest in handling heavy-tailed noise in general online regression problems.
中摘: 

尽管众多工作致力于设计适用于具有均匀有界奖励的强化学习（RL）的高效算法，但当奖励是 \emph{重尾} 的（即对于某个 $ε\in(0,1]$ 仅具有有限的 $(1+ε)$ 阶矩）时，针对具有大规模状态 - 动作空间的强化学习，是否存在样本或时间高效的算法，仍然是一个未解之谜。在本文中，我们致力于应对具有线性函数近似的强化学习（RL）中此类奖励带来的挑战。我们首先针对重尾线性多臂老虎机设计了一种算法，\textsc{Heavy-OFUL}，实现了 \emph{实例依赖} 的 $T$ 轮遗憾界为 $\tilde{O}\big(d T^{\frac{1-ε}{2(1+ε)}} \sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^T ν_t^2} + d T^{\frac{1-ε}{2(1+ε)}}\big)$，这是 \emph{首个} 此类结果。其中，$d$ 为特征维度，$ν_t^{1+ε}$ 表示第 $t$ 轮奖励的 $(1+ε)$ 阶中心矩。我们进一步证明，当应用于随机和确定性线性多臂老虎机中的最坏情况实例时，上述界限达到了极小极大最优。随后，我们将该算法扩展至具有线性函数近似的强化学习（RL）设置中。我们的算法，命名为 \textsc{Heavy-LSVI-UCB}，实现了 \emph{首个} 计算高效的 \emph{实例依赖} 的 $K$ 集遗憾界 $\tilde{O}(d \sqrt{H \mathcal{U}^*} K^\frac{1}{1+ε} + d \sqrt{H \mathcal{V}^* K})$。其中，$H$ 为集的长度，$\mathcal{U}^*, \mathcal{V}^*$ 为实例依赖量，其尺度分别由奖励中心矩和价值函数中心矩决定。我们还提供了一个匹配的下界 $Ω(d H K^{\frac{1}{1+ε}} + d \sqrt{H^3 K})$，以证明我们的算法在最坏情况下的最优性。我们的结果是通过一种新颖的鲁棒自规范化集中不等式获得的，该不等式在处理一般在线回归问题中的重尾噪声方面也具有独立的研究价值。

[No.164] Inverse Reinforcement Learning with the Average Reward Criterion
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 基于平均奖励准则的逆强化学习
类别: 
作者: Feiyang Wu;Jingyang Ke;Anqi Wu;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71307
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.14608
英摘: We study the problem of Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) with an average-reward criterion. The goal is to recover an unknown policy and a reward function when the agent only has samples of states and actions from an experienced agent. Previous IRL methods assume that the expert is trained in a discounted environment, and the discount factor is known. This work alleviates this assumption by proposing an average-reward framework with efficient learning algorithms. We develop novel stochastic first-order methods to solve the IRL problem under the average-reward setting, which requires solving an Average-reward Markov Decision Process (AMDP) as a subproblem. To solve the subproblem, we develop a Stochastic Policy Mirror Descent (SPMD) method under general state and action spaces that needs $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ steps of gradient computation. Equipped with SPMD, we propose the Inverse Policy Mirror Descent (IPMD) method for solving the IRL problem with a $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)$ complexity. To the best of our knowledge, the aforementioned complexity results are new in IRL. Finally, we corroborate our analysis with numerical experiments using the MuJoCo benchmark and additional control tasks.
中摘: 

我们研究基于平均奖励准则的逆强化学习（IRL）问题。目标是当智能体仅拥有来自专家的状态和动作样本时，恢复未知的策略和奖励函数。以往的 IRL 方法假设专家是在折扣环境中训练的，且折扣因子已知。本文通过提出具有高效学习算法的平均奖励框架，放宽了这一假设。我们开发了新颖的随机一阶方法来求解平均奖励设置下的 IRL 问题，这需要将平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程（AMDP）作为子问题进行求解。为了解决该子问题，我们在一般状态和动作空间下开发了一种随机策略镜像下降（SPMD）方法，该方法需要 $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ 步梯度计算。借助 SPMD，我们提出了逆策略镜像下降（IPMD）方法，用于求解具有 $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)$ 复杂度的 IRL 问题。据我们所知，上述复杂度结果在 IRL 领域尚属首次。最后，我们利用 MuJoCo 基准和额外的控制任务，通过数值实验验证了我们的分析。

[No.165] Performance Bounds for Policy-Based Average Reward Reinforcement Learning Algorithms
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 基于策略的平均奖励强化学习算法的性能边界
类别: 
作者: Yashaswini Murthy;Mehrdad Moharrami;R. Srikant;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71759
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.01450
英摘: Many policy-based reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms can be viewed as instantiations of approximate policy iteration (PI), i.e., where policy improvement and policy evaluation are both performed approximately. In applications where the average reward objective is the meaningful performance metric, discounted reward formulations are often used with the discount factor being close to $1,$ which is equivalent to making the expected horizon very large. However, the corresponding theoretical bounds for error performance scale with the square of the horizon. Thus, even after dividing the total reward by the length of the horizon, the corresponding performance bounds for average reward problems go to infinity. Therefore, an open problem has been to obtain meaningful performance bounds for approximate PI and RL algorithms for the average-reward setting. In this paper, we solve this open problem by obtaining the first finite-time error bounds for average-reward MDPs, and show that the asymptotic error goes to zero in the limit as policy evaluation and policy improvement errors go to zero.
中摘: 

许多基于策略的强化学习（RL）算法可被视为近似策略迭代（PI）的实例化，即策略改进和策略评估均近似执行。在平均奖励目标被视为有意义性能指标的应用场景中，通常使用折扣奖励公式，且折扣因子接近 1，这相当于使期望时域非常大。然而，相应的误差性能理论界限随期望时域的平方成比例。因此，即使将总奖励除以期时域长度，平均奖励问题相应的性能界限仍会趋于无穷大。因此，获取适用于平均奖励设置的近似 PI 和 RL 算法的有意义性能界限一直是一个开放问题。在本文中，我们通过获得平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）的有限时间误差界限解决了这一开放问题，并表明当策略评估和策略改进误差趋于零时，渐近误差在极限下也趋于零。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.167] Video Prediction Models as Rewards for Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 视频预测模型作为强化学习的奖励
类别: 
作者: Alejandro Escontrela;Ademi Adeniji;Wilson Yan;Ajay Jain;Xue Bin Peng;Ken Goldberg;Youngwoon Lee;Danijar Hafner;Pieter Abbeel;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;Simon Fraser University;University of Toronto;Covariant;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72159
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.14343
英摘: Specifying reward signals that allow agents to learn complex behaviors is a long-standing challenge in reinforcement learning. A promising approach is to extract preferences for behaviors from unlabeled videos, which are widely available on the internet. We present Video Prediction Rewards (VIPER), an algorithm that leverages pretrained video prediction models as action-free reward signals for reinforcement learning. Specifically, we first train an autoregressive transformer on expert videos and then use the video prediction likelihoods as reward signals for a reinforcement learning agent. VIPER enables expert-level control without programmatic task rewards across a wide range of DMC, Atari, and RLBench tasks. Moreover, generalization of the video prediction model allows us to derive rewards for an out-of-distribution environment where no expert data is available, enabling cross-embodiment generalization for tabletop manipulation. We see our work as starting point for scalable reward specification from unlabeled videos that will benefit from the rapid advances in generative modeling. Source code and datasets are available on the project website: https://escontrela.me/viper
中摘: 

指定允许智能体学习复杂行为的奖励信号是强化学习中一个长期存在的挑战。一种有前景的方法是从互联网上广泛可用的无标签视频中提取行为偏好。我们提出了视频预测奖励（VIPER），一种利用预训练视频预测模型作为强化学习无需动作奖励信号的算法。具体而言，我们首先训练自回归 Transformer 以处理专家视频，然后将视频预测似然性用作强化学习智能体的奖励信号。VIPER 能够在广泛的 DMC、Atari 和 RLBench 任务上，无需程序化任务奖励即可实现专家级控制。此外，视频预测模型的泛化能力使我们能够为没有专家数据可用的分布外环境生成奖励，从而实现桌面操作的跨具身泛化。我们将这项工作视为从无标签视频中进行可扩展奖励指定的起点，这将受益于生成模型的快速发展。源代码和数据集可在项目网站上获取：https://escontrela.me/viper

[No.168] Extracting Reward Functions from Diffusion Models
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 从扩散模型中提取奖励函数
类别: 
作者: Felipe Pinto Coelho Nuti;Tim Franzmeyer;Joao F. Henriques;
机构: Citadel Securities;University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71865
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01804
英摘: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable results in image generation, and have similarly been used to learn high-performing policies in sequential decision-making tasks. Decision-making diffusion models can be trained on lower-quality data, and then be steered with a reward function to generate near-optimal trajectories. We consider the problem of extracting a reward function by comparing a decision-making diffusion model that models low-reward behavior and one that models high-reward behavior; a setting related to inverse reinforcement learning. We first define the notion of a relative reward function of two diffusion models and show conditions under which it exists and is unique. We then devise a practical learning algorithm for extracting it by aligning the gradients of a reward function -- parametrized by a neural network -- to the difference in outputs of both diffusion models. Our method finds correct reward functions in navigation environments, and we demonstrate that steering the base model with the learned reward functions results in significantly increased performance in standard locomotion benchmarks. Finally, we demonstrate that our approach generalizes beyond sequential decision-making by learning a reward-like function from two large-scale image generation diffusion models. The extracted reward function successfully assigns lower rewards to harmful images.
中摘: 

扩散模型在图像生成方面已取得显著成果，同样也被应用于在序列决策任务中学习高性能策略。决策扩散模型可以在较低质量的数据上进行训练，随后利用奖励函数进行引导，以生成近最优轨迹。我们探讨了通过比较一个建模低奖励行为的决策扩散模型和一个建模高奖励行为的决策扩散模型来提取奖励函数的问题；这是一个与逆强化学习相关的设定。我们首先定义了两种扩散模型之间的相对奖励函数概念，并阐述了其存在且唯一的条件。随后，我们设计了一种实用的学习算法来提取该函数，即通过将奖励函数（由神经网络参数化）的梯度与两个扩散模型的输出差异进行对齐。我们的方法能够在导航环境中找到正确的奖励函数，并且我们证明，利用学习到的奖励函数引导基础模型，能够在标准运动基准中带来显著的性能提升。最后，我们证明该方法具有超越序列决策的泛化能力，即通过从两个大规模图像生成扩散模型中学习类奖励函数来实现。提取出的奖励函数成功地为有害图像赋予了较低奖励。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.169] Rewarded soups: towards Pareto-optimal alignment by interpolating weights fine-tuned on diverse rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 奖励汤：通过在多样化的奖励上插值调整权重以实现帕累托最优对齐
类别: 
作者: Alexandre Rame;Guillaume Couairon;Corentin Dancette;Jean-Baptiste Gaya;Mustafa Shukor;Laure Soulier;Matthieu Cord;
机构: Université Pierre et Marie Curie - Paris 6;Sorbonne University;Meta;Sorbonne Université;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70592
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04488
英摘: Foundation models are first pre-trained on vast unsupervised datasets and then fine-tuned on labeled data. Reinforcement learning, notably from human feedback (RLHF), can further align the network with the intended usage. Yet the imperfections in the proxy reward may hinder the training and lead to suboptimal results; the diversity of objectives in real-world tasks and human opinions exacerbate the issue. This paper proposes embracing the heterogeneity of diverse rewards by following a multi-policy strategy. Rather than focusing on a single a priori reward, we aim for Pareto-optimal generalization across the entire space of preferences. To this end, we propose rewarded soup, first specializing multiple networks independently (one for each proxy reward) and then interpolating their weights linearly. This succeeds empirically because we show that the weights remain linearly connected when fine-tuned on diverse rewards from a shared pre-trained initialization. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for text-to-text (summarization, Q&A, helpful assistant, review), text-image (image captioning, text-to-image generation, visual grounding, VQA), and control (locomotion) tasks. We hope to enhance the alignment of deep models, and how they interact with the world in all its diversity.
中摘: 

基础模型首先在海量的无监督数据集上进行预训练，随后在标注数据上进行微调。强化学习，尤其是基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF），可以进一步使网络与预期用途对齐。然而，代理奖励中的缺陷可能会阻碍训练并导致次优结果；现实任务目标的多样性以及人类观点的差异加剧了这一问题。本文提出通过采用多策略方法来接纳多样奖励的异质性。与专注于单一先验奖励不同，我们的目标是在整个偏好空间中实现帕累托最优泛化。为此，我们提出了“奖励汤”，即首先独立地专门训练多个网络（每个网络对应一个代理奖励），然后线性地插值它们的权重。实证表明这一方法是有效的，因为我们展示了从共享的预训练初始化开始，在多样奖励上进行微调时，权重仍然保持线性连接。我们验证了该方法在文对文（摘要、问答、有用助手、评论）、文对图（图像描述、文生图、视觉定位、视觉问答）以及控制（运动）任务中的有效性。我们希望能增强深度模型的对齐效果，以及它们与充满多样性的世界的互动方式。

[No.170] Multi-Objective Intrinsic Reward Learning for Conversational Recommender Systems
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 多目标内在奖励学习在对话推荐系统中的应用
类别: 
作者: Zhendong Chu;Nan Wang;Hongning Wang;
机构: University of Virginia;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70352
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.20109
英摘: Conversational Recommender Systems (CRS) actively elicit user preferences to generate adaptive recommendations. Mainstream reinforcement learning-based CRS solutions heavily rely on handcrafted reward functions, which may not be aligned with user intent in CRS tasks. Therefore, the design of task-specific rewards is critical to facilitate CRS policy learning, which remains largely under-explored in the literature. In this work, we propose a novel approach to address this challenge by learning intrinsic rewards from interactions with users. Specifically, we formulate intrinsic reward learning as a multi-objective bi-level optimization problem. The inner level optimizes the CRS policy augmented by the learned intrinsic rewards, while the outer level drives the intrinsic rewards to optimize two CRS-specific objectives: maximizing the success rate and minimizing the number of turns to reach a successful recommendation in conversations. To evaluate the effectiveness of our approach, we conduct extensive experiments on three public CRS benchmarks. The results show that our algorithm significantly improves CRS performance by exploiting informative learned intrinsic rewards.
中摘: 

对话推荐系统（CRS）主动挖掘用户偏好以生成自适应推荐。主流的基于强化学习的 CRS 解决方案高度依赖于手工设计的奖励函数，这些函数可能无法与 CRS 任务中的用户意图保持一致。因此，设计特定任务的奖励对于促进 CRS 策略学习至关重要，而这一点在现有文献中仍未得到充分探索。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新颖的方法来解决这一挑战，即通过与用户的交互学习内在奖励。具体而言，我们将内在奖励学习建模为一个多目标双层优化问题。内层优化由学习到的内在奖励增强的 CRS 策略，而外层则驱动内在奖励以优化两个 CRS 特定目标：最大化成功率并最小化在对话中达成成功推荐所需的对话轮数。为了评估我们方法的有效性，我们在三个公共 CRS 基准数据集上进行了广泛的实验。结果表明，通过利用具有信息量的学习到的内在奖励，我们的算法显著提升了 CRS 性能。

[No.171] Interpretable Reward Redistribution in Reinforcement Learning: A Causal Approach
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: reward
标题: 可解释的强化学习奖励重分配：一种因果方法
类别: 
作者: Yudi Zhang;Yali Du;Biwei Huang;Ziyan Wang;Jun Wang;Meng Fang;Mykola Pechenizkiy;
机构: Eindhoven University of Technology;University of California, San Diego;King's College London;University College London;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70073
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.18427
英摘: A major challenge in reinforcement learning is to determine which state-action pairs are responsible for future rewards that are delayed. Reward redistribution serves as a solution to re-assign credits for each time step from observed sequences. While the majority of current approaches construct the reward redistribution in an uninterpretable manner, we propose to explicitly model the contributions of state and action from a causal perspective, resulting in an interpretable reward redistribution and preserving policy invariance. In this paper, we start by studying the role of causal generative models in reward redistribution by characterizing the generation of Markovian rewards and trajectory-wise long-term return and further propose a framework, called Generative Return Decomposition (GRD), for policy optimization in delayed reward scenarios. Specifically, GRD first identifies the unobservable Markovian rewards and causal relations in the generative process. Then, GRD makes use of the identified causal generative model to form a compact representation to train policy over the most favorable subspace of the state space of the agent. Theoretically, we show that the unobservable Markovian reward function is identifiable, as well as the underlying causal structure and causal models. Experimental results show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods and the provided visualization further demonstrates the interpretability of our method. The project page is located at https://reedzyd.github.io/GenerativeReturnDecomposition/.
中摘: 

强化学习面临的一个主要挑战是确定哪些状态 - 动作对负责未来的延迟奖励。奖励重分配提供了一种解决方案，旨在从观测序列中为每个时间步重新分配信用。尽管大多数当前方法以不可解释的方式构建奖励重分配，但我们提出从因果视角显式建模状态和动作的贡献，从而实现可解释的奖励重分配并保持策略不变性。在本文中，我们首先通过刻画马尔可夫奖励和轨迹层面长期回报的生成，研究因果生成模型在奖励重分配中的作用，并进一步提出了一个名为生成回报分解（Generative Return Decomposition, GRD）的框架，用于延迟奖励场景下的策略优化。具体而言，GRD 首先识别生成过程中不可观测的马尔可夫奖励及其因果关系。随后，GRD 利用识别出的因果生成模型构建紧凑表示，以便在智能体状态空间中最有利的子空间上训练策略。理论上，我们证明了不可观测的马尔可夫奖励函数是可识别的，其潜在的因果结构及因果模型亦是如此。实验结果表明，我们的方法优于最先进的方法，所提供的可视化结果进一步展示了我们方法的可解释性。项目页面位于 https://reedzyd.github.io/GenerativeReturnDecomposition/.

来源: ICLR 2025

英摘: Reward models are critical in techniques like Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) and Inference Scaling Laws, where they guide language model alignment and select optimal responses. Despite their importance, existing reward model benchmarks often evaluate models by asking them to distinguish between responses generated by models of varying power. However, this approach fails to assess reward models on subtle but critical content changes and variations in style, resulting in a low correlation with policy model performance. To this end, we introduce RM-Bench, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate reward models based on their sensitivity to subtle content differences and resistance to style biases. 中摘: 

奖励模型在人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）和推理缩放定律等关键技术中至关重要，它们指导语言模型对齐并选择最优响应。尽管其重要性不言而喻，但现有的奖励模型基准通常通过要求模型区分由不同能力模型生成的响应来评估模型。然而，这种方法无法评估奖励模型对微妙但关键的内容变化及风格变化的敏感性，导致与策略模型性能的相关性较低。为此，我们引入了 RM-Bench，这是一种新颖的基准，旨在根据奖励模型对微妙内容差异的敏感性以及对风格偏见的抵抗力来评估它们。大量实验表明，RM-Bench 与策略模型性能高度相关，使其成为选择奖励模型以有效对齐语言模型的可靠参考。我们在 RM-Bench 上评估了近 40 个奖励模型。我们的结果显示，即使是目前最先进的模型，在面对风格偏见干扰时，平均性能也仅为 46.6%，低于随机水平准确率（50%）。这些发现凸显了当前奖励模型仍有巨大的改进空间。相关代码和数据可在 https://github.com/THU-KEG/RM-Bench 获取。

[No.175] Weak-to-Strong Preference Optimization: Stealing Reward from Weak Aligned Model
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 弱到强的偏好优化：窃取对齐模型的奖励
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Wenhong Zhu;Zhiwei He;Xiaofeng Wang;Pengfei Liu;Rui Wang;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28894
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18640
英摘: Aligning language models (LMs) with human preferences has become a key area of research, enabling these models to meet diverse user needs better. Inspired by weak-to-strong generalization, where a strong LM fine-tuned on labels generated by a weaker model can consistently outperform its weak supervisor, we extend this idea to model alignment. In this work, we observe that the alignment behavior in weaker models can be effectively transferred to stronger models and even exhibit an amplification effect. Based on this insight, we propose a method called Weak-to-Strong Preference Optimization (WSPO), which achieves strong model alignment by learning the distribution differences before and after the alignment of the weak model. Experiments demonstrate that WSPO delivers outstanding performance, improving the win rate of Qwen2-7B-Instruct on Arena-Hard from 39.70 to 49.60, achieving a remarkable 47.04 length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2, and scoring 7.33 on MT-bench. Our results suggest that using the weak model to elicit a strong model with a high alignment ability is feasible.
中摘: 

将语言模型（LMs）与人类偏好对齐已成为关键的研究领域，使这些模型能够更好地满足多样化的用户需求。受弱到强泛化启发，即在由较弱模型生成的标签上进行微调的强语言模型能够持续优于其弱监督器，我们将这一思想扩展到了模型对齐领域。在本工作中，我们观察到较弱模型中的对齐行为可以有效地迁移到更强的模型中，甚至表现出放大效应。基于这一洞察，我们提出了一种名为“弱到强偏好优化”（Weak-to-Strong Preference Optimization, WSPO）的方法，该方法通过学习弱模型对齐前后的分布差异来实现强模型对齐。实验表明，WSPO 表现优异，将 Qwen2-7B-Instruct 在 Arena-Hard 上的胜率从 39.70 提升至 49.60，在 AlpacaEval 2 上取得了显著的 47.04 长度控制胜率，并在 MT-bench 上得分 7.33。我们的结果表明，利用弱模型来激发具备高对齐能力的强模型是可行的。

[No.177] Rethinking Reward Model Evaluation: Are We Barking up the Wrong Tree?
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 重新思考奖励模型评估：我们是否走错了方向？
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Xueru Wen;Jie Lou;Yaojie Lu;Hongyu Lin;XingYu;Xinyu Lu;Ben He;Xianpei Han;Debing Zhang;Le Sun;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Xiaohongshu;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30496
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05584
英摘: Reward Models (RMs) are crucial for aligning language models with human preferences. Currently, the evaluation of RMs depends on measuring accuracy against a validation set of manually annotated preference data. Although this method is straightforward and widely adopted, the relationship between RM accuracy and downstream policy performance remains under-explored. In this work, we conduct experiments in a synthetic setting to investigate how differences in RM measured by accuracy translate into gaps in optimized policy performance. Our findings reveal that while there is a weak positive correlation between accuracy and downstream performance, policies optimized towards RMs with similar accuracy can exhibit quite different performance. Moreover, we discover that the way of measuring accuracy significantly impacts its ability to predict the final policy performance. Through the lens of the Regressional Goodhart effect, we recognize that accuracy, when used for measuring RM quality, can fail to fully capture the potential RM overoptimization. This underscores the inadequacy of relying solely on accuracy to reflect their impact on policy optimization.
中摘: 

奖励模型（RMs）对于将语言模型与人类偏好对齐至关重要。目前，奖励模型的评估依赖于在人工标注偏好数据的验证集上测量准确率。尽管这种方法简单直接且被广泛采用，但奖励模型准确率与下游策略性能之间的关系仍未得到充分探索。在这项工作中，我们在合成设定下开展实验，以研究由准确率衡量的奖励模型差异如何转化为优化后的策略性能差距。我们的发现表明，尽管准确率与下游性能之间存在微弱的正相关，但针对具有相似准确率的奖励模型优化的策略可能表现出显著不同的性能。此外，我们发现测量准确率的方式对其预测最终策略性能的能力有显著影响。基于回归型古德哈特效应的视角，我们认识到，当准确率被用于衡量奖励模型质量时，可能无法充分捕捉奖励模型潜在的过度优化问题。这凸显了仅依靠准确率来反映奖励模型对策略优化影响的不足。

[No.178] Test-time Alignment of Diffusion Models without Reward Over-optimization

[No.179] Correlated Proxies: A New Definition and Improved Mitigation for Reward Hacking
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 相关代理：一种新的定义及改进的奖励作弊缓解措施
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Cassidy Laidlaw;Shivam Singhal;Anca Dragan;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28442
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03185
英摘: Because it is difficult to precisely specify complex objectives, reinforcement learning policies are often optimized using proxy reward functions that only approximate the true goal. However, optimizing proxy rewards frequently leads to reward hacking: the optimized reward function ceases to be a good proxy and the resulting policy performs poorly with respect to the unspecified true reward. Principled solutions to reward hacking have been impeded by the lack of a good definition for the problem. To address this gap, we introduce a definition of reward hacking based on the correlation between proxy and true rewards for states and actions seen by a "reference policy" that breaks down under optimization. We show that this definition captures reward hacking behavior across several realistic settings, including in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). Using our formulation, we show theoretically that regularization to the reference policy can effectively prevent reward hacking. While the current practice in RLHF applies a KL penalty between action distributions for this purpose, our theory suggests regularizing the $χ^2$ divergence between the policies' occupancy measures can be more effective. We intuitively show the benefits of this type of regularization and demonstrate that it better mitigates reward hacking in practice across four realistic settings, including RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/cassidylaidlaw/orpo.
中摘: 

由于难以精确指定复杂的目标，强化学习策略通常使用仅近似真实目标的代理奖励函数进行优化。然而，优化代理奖励经常会导致奖励黑客行为：优化后的奖励函数不再成为良好的代理，导致所得策略相对于未指定的真实奖励表现不佳。由于缺乏对问题的良好定义，针对奖励黑客的原则性解决方案一直受阻。为了弥补这一差距，我们引入了一种基于参考策略所访问的状态和动作上代理奖励与真实奖励之间相关性的奖励黑客定义，而这种相关性在优化过程中会失效。我们证明，这一定义在多个现实场景下均能捕捉奖励黑客行为，包括来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）。利用我们的公式，我们从理论上表明，对参考策略进行正则化可以有效防止奖励黑客。虽然 RLHF 中的现行做法为此目的在动作分布之间应用 KL 惩罚，但我们的理论表明，对策略占用测度之间的 χ² 散度进行正则化可能更为有效。我们直观地展示了此类正则化的优势，并证明在包括 RLHF 在内的四个现实场景中，它能更有效地在实践中缓解奖励黑客问题。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/cassidylaidlaw/orpo 获取。

[No.180] Rewarding Progress: Scaling Automated Process Verifiers for LLM Reasoning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 奖励进步：扩展自动化过程验证器以增强LLM推理能力
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Amrith Setlur;Chirag Nagpal;Adam Fisch;Xinyang Geng;Jacob Eisenstein;Rishabh Agarwal;Alekh Agarwal;Jonathan Berant;Aviral Kumar;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;Google;Tel Aviv University;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30649
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08146
英摘: A promising approach for improving reasoning in large language models is to use process reward models (PRMs). PRMs provide feedback at each step of a multi-step reasoning trace, potentially improving credit assignment over outcome reward models (ORMs) that only provide feedback at the final step. However, collecting dense, per-step human labels is not scalable, and training PRMs from automatically-labeled data has thus far led to limited gains. To improve a base policy by running search against a PRM or using it as dense rewards for reinforcement learning (RL), we ask: "How should we design process rewards?". Our key insight is that, to be effective, the process reward for a step should measure progress: a change in the likelihood of producing a correct response in the future, before and after taking the step, corresponding to the notion of step-level advantages in RL. Crucially, this progress should be measured under a prover policy distinct from the base policy. We theoretically characterize the set of good provers and our results show that optimizing process rewards from such provers improves exploration during test-time search and online RL. In fact, our characterization shows that weak prover policies can substantially improve a stronger base policy, which we also observe empirically. We validate our claims by training process advantage verifiers (PAVs) to predict progress under such provers, and show that compared to ORMs, test-time search against PAVs is $>8\%$ more accurate, and $1.5-5\times$ more compute-efficient. Online RL with dense rewards from PAVs enables one of the first results with $5-6\times$ gain in sample efficiency, and $>6\%$ gain in accuracy, over ORMs.
中摘: 

提高大语言模型推理能力的一种有前景的方法是采用过程奖励模型（PRMs）。PRMs 在推理轨迹的每一步提供反馈，相较于仅在最终步骤提供反馈的结果奖励模型（ORMs），这有望改进信用分配。然而，收集稠密的、逐步骤的人类标注数据并不可扩展，且迄今为止使用自动标注数据训练 PRMs 带来的收益有限。为了通过针对 PRM 运行搜索或将其用作强化学习（RL）的稠密奖励来改进基础策略，我们提出疑问：“我们应如何设计过程奖励？”我们的关键见解是，为了有效，步骤的过程奖励应衡量进展：即在采取该步骤前后，生成未来正确响应的概率的变化，这对应于 RL 中的步骤级优势概念。至关重要的是，这种进展应在一个不同于基础策略的证明策略下进行衡量。我们在理论上刻画了优质证明策略的集合，研究结果表明，优化来自此类证明策略的过程奖励，能够改善测试时搜索和在线强化学习（RL）期间的探索能力。事实上，我们的刻画表明，较弱的证明策略能够显著改进更强的基础策略，我们也通过实证观察到了这一点。我们通过训练过程优势验证器（PAVs）来预测在此类证明策略下的进展来验证我们的主张，结果表明，与 ORM 相比，针对 PAVs 的测试时搜索准确率提高超过 8%，且计算效率提升 1.5 至 5 倍。使用来自 PAVs 的稠密奖励进行的在线强化学习，实现了相对于 ORM 样本效率提升 5 至 6 倍、准确率提升超过 6% 的首批显著成果之一。

[No.181] Post-hoc Reward Calibration: A Case Study on Length Bias
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 事后奖励校准：长度偏差案例研究
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Zeyu Huang;Zihan Qiu;Zili Wang;Edoardo Ponti;Ivan Titov;
机构: University of Edinburgh;Alibaba Group;StepFun;NVIDIA;University of Amsterdam;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30144
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17407
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback aligns the outputs of Large Language Models with human values and preferences. Central to this process is the reward model (RM), which translates human feedback into training signals for optimising LLM behaviour. However, RMs can develop biases by exploiting spurious correlations in their training data, such as favouring outputs based on length or style rather than true quality. These biases can lead to incorrect output rankings, sub-optimal model evaluations, and the amplification of undesirable behaviours in LLMs alignment. This paper addresses the challenge of correcting such biases without additional data and training, introducing the concept of Post-hoc Reward Calibration. We first propose an intuitive approach to estimate the bias term and, thus, remove it to approximate the underlying true reward. We then extend the approach to a more general and robust form with the Locally Weighted Regression. Focusing on the prevalent length bias, we validate our proposed approaches across three experimental settings, demonstrating consistent improvements: (1) a 3.11 average performance gain across 33 reward models on the RewardBench dataset; (2) enhanced alignment of RM rankings with GPT-4 evaluations and human preferences based on the AlpacaEval benchmark; and (3) improved Length-Controlled win rate of the RLHF process in multiple LLM--RM combinations. Our method is computationally efficient and generalisable to other types of bias and RMs, offering a scalable and robust solution for mitigating biases in LLM alignment. Our code and results are available at https://github.com/ZeroYuHuang/Reward-Calibration.
中摘: 

基于人类反馈的强化学习将大型语言模型的输出与人类的价值观和偏好对齐。该过程的核心在于奖励模型（RM），它将人类反馈转化为用于优化大语言模型行为的训练信号。然而，奖励模型可能通过利用训练数据中的虚假相关性产生偏差，例如偏好基于长度或风格的输出，而非真实质量。这些偏差可能导致输出排名错误、模型评估次优，并在大语言模型对齐中放大不良行为。本文旨在解决无需额外数据和训练即可纠正此类偏差的挑战，提出了“事后奖励校准”的概念。我们首先提出了一种直观的方法，用于估计偏差项，进而将其移除以近似潜在的真实奖励。随后，我们利用局部加权回归将该方法扩展为一种更通用且稳健的形式。针对普遍存在的长度偏差，我们在三种实验设置下验证了所提出的方法，展示了一致的改进效果：(1) 在 RewardBench 数据集的 33 个奖励模型上实现了平均 3.11 的性能提升；(2) 基于 AlpacaEval 基准，奖励模型的排名与 GPT-4 评估及人类偏好的一致性得到增强；(3) 在多种大语言模型与奖励模型组合中，RLHF 过程的长度控制胜率得到提升。我们的方法计算高效，可泛化至其他类型的偏差和奖励模型，为解决大语言模型对齐中的偏差问题提供了一种可扩展且稳健的方案。我们的代码和结果可在 https://github.com/ZeroYuHuang/Reward-Calibration 获取。

[No.183] BAMDP Shaping: a Unified Framework for Intrinsic Motivation and Reward Shaping
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: BAMDP塑形：一个统一框架，用于内在动机和奖励塑形
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Aly Lidayan;Michael D Dennis;Stuart Russell;
机构: Google;University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28031
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.05358
英摘: Intrinsic motivation and reward shaping guide reinforcement learning (RL) agents by adding pseudo-rewards, which can lead to useful emergent behaviors. However, they can also encourage counterproductive exploits, e.g., fixation with noisy TV screens. Here we provide a theoretical model which anticipates these behaviors, and provides broad criteria under which adverse effects can be bounded. We characterize all pseudo-rewards as reward shaping in Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Processes (BAMDPs), which formulates the problem of learning in MDPs as an MDP over the agent's knowledge. Optimal exploration maximizes BAMDP state value, which we decompose into the value of the information gathered and the prior value of the physical state. Psuedo-rewards guide RL agents by rewarding behavior that increases these value components, while they hinder exploration when they align poorly with the actual value. We extend potential-based shaping theory to prove BAMDP Potential-based shaping Functions (BAMPFs) are immune to reward-hacking (convergence to behaviors maximizing composite rewards to the detriment of real rewards) in meta-RL, and show empirically how a BAMPF helps a meta-RL agent learn optimal RL algorithms for a Bernoulli Bandit domain. We finally prove that BAMPFs with bounded monotone increasing potentials also resist reward-hacking in the regular RL setting. We show that it is straightforward to retrofit or design new pseudo-reward terms in this form, and provide an empirical demonstration in the Mountain Car environment.
中摘: 

内在动机和奖励塑造通过添加伪奖励来引导强化学习（RL）智能体，这可以产生有用的涌现行为。然而，它们也可能鼓励产生适得其反的利用行为，例如对嘈杂电视屏幕的沉迷。在此，我们提供了一个理论模型，能够预测此类行为，并提供了不良影响可被限制的广泛标准。我们将所有伪奖励描述为贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）中的奖励塑造，它将 MDP 中的学习问题表述为关于智能体知识的马尔可夫决策过程。最优探索旨在最大化 BAMDP 状态价值，我们将其分解为所收集信息的价值和物理状态的先验价值。伪奖励通过奖励能增加这些价值组件的行为来引导强化学习智能体，而当它们与实际价值对齐不佳时，则会阻碍探索。我们扩展了基于势能的塑造理论，证明了 BAMDP 基于势能的塑造函数（BAMPF）在元强化学习中对奖励黑客（即收敛至最大化组合奖励而损害真实奖励的行为）具有免疫力，并通过实证展示了 BAMPF 如何帮助元强化学习智能体在伯努利多臂老虎机领域学习最优强化学习算法。最后，我们证明了具有有界单调递增势函数的 BAMPF 在常规强化学习设置中也能抵抗奖励黑客。我们表明，以这种形式改造或设计新的伪奖励项是简单直接的，并在山地车环境中提供了实证演示。

[No.184] Reward Dimension Reduction for Scalable Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning

作者: Giseung Park;Youngchul Sung;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20957
英摘: In this paper, we introduce a simple yet effective reward dimension reduction method to tackle the scalability challenges of multi-objective reinforcement learning algorithms. 在本文中，我们提出了一种简单而有效的奖励降维方法，旨在应对多目标强化学习算法的可扩展性挑战。尽管大多数现有方法专注于优化两到四个目标，但其扩展到包含更多目标的环境的能力尚不明确。我们的方法利用降维技术来增强多目标场景下的学习效率与策略性能。虽然大多数传统降维方法是为静态数据集设计的，但我们的方法专为在线学习定制，并在变换后保持帕累托最优性。我们提出了一个用于多目标强化学习中奖励降维的新训练与评估框架，并在包括拥有十六个目标的环境在内的多种环境中展示了该方法的优越性，其性能显著优于现有的在线降维方法。

[No.185] Zeroth-Order Policy Gradient for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback without Reward Inference
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于零阶策略梯度的人工反馈强化学习中的无奖励推断策略优化
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Qining Zhang;Lei Ying;
机构: University of Michigan;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29030
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17401
英摘: Reward inference (learning a reward model from human preferences) is a critical intermediate step in the Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pipeline for fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs). In practice, RLHF faces fundamental challenges such as distribution shift, reward model overfitting, and problem misspecification. An alternative approach is direct policy optimization without reward inference, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), which provides a much simpler pipeline and has shown empirical success in LLM applications. However, DPO utilizes the closed-form expression between the optimal policy and the reward function, which is only suitable under the bandit setting or deterministic MDPs. This paper develops two RLHF algorithms without reward inference for general RL problems beyond bandits and deterministic MDPs, and general preference models beyond the Bradley-Terry model. The key idea is to estimate the local value function difference from human preferences and then approximate the policy gradient with a zeroth-order gradient approximator. For both algorithms, we establish polynomial convergence rates in terms of the number of policy gradient iterations, the number of trajectory samples, and human preference queries per iteration. Numerical experiments in stochastic environments validate the performance of our proposed algorithms, outperforming popular RLHF baselines such as DPO and PPO. Our paper shows there exist provably efficient methods to solve general RLHF problems without reward inference.
中摘: 

奖励推断（从人类偏好中学习奖励模型）是微调大语言模型（LLMs）的来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）流程中的关键中间步骤。在实践中，RLHF 面临诸如分布偏移、奖励模型过拟合和问题设定不当等根本性挑战。一种替代方案是不进行奖励推断的直接策略优化，例如直接偏好优化（DPO），它提供了更为简单的流程，并在大语言模型应用中展现了实证成功。然而，DPO 利用了最优策略与奖励函数之间的闭式表达式，这仅适用于老虎机设定或确定性马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）。本文开发了两种无需奖励推断的 RLHF 算法，旨在解决超越老虎机和确定性 MDP 范畴的一般强化学习问题，以及超越 Bradley-Terry 模型范畴的一般偏好模型。核心思想是从人类偏好中估计局部价值函数的差值，随后利用零阶梯度近似器来近似策略梯度。对于这两种算法，我们就策略梯度迭代次数、轨迹样本数量以及每次迭代的人类偏好查询次数确立了多项式收敛率。随机环境中的数值实验验证了我们所提出算法的性能，其表现优于 DPO 和 PPO 等流行的 RLHF 基线。我们的论文表明，存在无需奖励推断即可解决一般 RLHF 问题的可证明高效方法。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.186] Sail into the Headwind: Alignment via Robust Rewards and Dynamic Labels against Reward Hacking
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 迎风而行：通过稳健的奖励和动态标签实现对抗奖励劫持的对齐
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Paria Rashidinejad;Yuandong Tian;
机构: Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30189
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09544
英摘: Aligning AI systems with human preferences typically suffers from the infamous reward hacking problem, where optimization of an imperfect reward model leads to undesired behaviors. In this paper, we investigate reward hacking in offline preference optimization, which aims to improve an initial model using a preference dataset. We identify two types of reward hacking stemming from statistical fluctuations in the dataset: Type I Reward Hacking due to subpar choices appearing more favorable, and Type II Reward Hacking due to decent choices appearing less favorable. We prove that many (mainstream or theoretical) preference optimization methods suffer from both types of reward hacking. To mitigate Type I Reward Hacking, we propose POWER, a new preference optimization method that combines Guiasu's weighted entropy with a robust reward maximization objective. POWER enjoys finite-sample guarantees under general function approximation, competing with the best covered policy in the data. To mitigate Type II Reward Hacking, we analyze the learning dynamics of preference optimization and develop a novel technique that dynamically updates preference labels toward certain "stationary labels", resulting in diminishing gradients for untrustworthy samples. Empirically, POWER with dynamic labels (POWER-DL) consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on alignment benchmarks, achieving improvements of up to 13.0 points on AlpacaEval 2.0 and 11.5 points on Arena-Hard over DPO, while also improving or maintaining performance on downstream tasks such as mathematical reasoning. Strong theoretical guarantees and empirical results demonstrate the promise of POWER-DL in mitigating reward hacking.
中摘: 

将人工智能系统与人类偏好对齐通常深受臭名昭著的奖励黑客问题困扰，即在不完美的奖励模型优化过程中会导致不良行为。在本文中，我们研究了离线偏好优化中的奖励黑客问题，该方法旨在利用偏好数据集改进初始模型。我们确定了两种源于数据集统计波动的奖励黑客类型：一类奖励黑客源于次优选择显得更有利，二类奖励黑客源于尚可的选择显得更不利。我们证明了众多（主流或理论上的）偏好优化方法都遭受这两种类型的奖励黑客问题。为了缓解一类奖励黑客问题，我们提出了 POWER，这是一种新的偏好优化方法，结合了 Guiasu 的加权熵与鲁棒的奖励最大化目标。在通用函数近似下，POWER 享有有限样本保证，能够与数据中表现最佳的覆盖策略相匹敌。为了缓解二类奖励黑客问题，我们分析了偏好优化的学习动态，并开发了一种新颖的技术，该技术动态地将偏好标签更新为某些“稳定标签”，从而导致不可信样本的梯度逐渐减弱。实证结果表明，带有动态标签的 POWER（POWER-DL）在对齐基准上始终优于最先进的方法。相较于 DPO，其在 AlpacaEval 2.0 上最高提升 13.0 分，在 Arena-Hard 上提升 11.5 分，同时还在数学推理等下游任务上实现性能改进或保持。强有力的理论保证和实证结果证明了 POWER-DL 在缓解奖励黑客问题方面的潜力。

[No.187] Interpreting Language Reward Models via Contrastive Explanations
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 通过对比解释解读语言奖励模型
类别: interpretability and explainable AI
作者: Junqi Jiang;Tom Bewley;Saumitra Mishra;Freddy Lecue;Manuela Veloso;
机构: Imperial College London;J.P. Morgan;JPMorgan Chase & Co.;INRIA;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28716
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16502
英摘: Reward models (RMs) are a crucial component in the alignment of large language models' (LLMs) outputs with human values. RMs approximate human preferences over possible LLM responses to the same prompt by predicting and comparing reward scores. However, as they are typically modified versions of LLMs with scalar output heads, RMs are large black boxes whose predictions are not explainable. More transparent RMs would enable improved trust in the alignment of LLMs. In this work, we propose to use contrastive explanations to explain any binary response comparison made by an RM. Specifically, we generate a diverse set of new comparisons similar to the original one to characterise the RM's local behaviour. The perturbed responses forming the new comparisons are generated to explicitly modify manually specified high-level evaluation attributes, on which analyses of RM behaviour are grounded. In quantitative experiments, we validate the effectiveness of our method for finding high-quality contrastive explanations. We then showcase the qualitative usefulness of our method for investigating global sensitivity of RMs to each evaluation attribute, and demonstrate how representative examples can be automatically extracted to explain and compare behaviours of different RMs. We see our method as a flexible framework for RM explanation, providing a basis for more interpretable and trustworthy LLM alignment.
中摘: 

奖励模型（RMs）是将大语言模型（LLMs）的输出与人类价值观对齐的关键组成部分。RMs 通过预测和比较奖励分数，来近似人类对同一提示下可能的大语言模型响应的偏好。然而，由于它们通常是带有标量输出头的 LLM 的修改版本，RMs 往往是难以解释其预测的大型黑箱。更加透明的 RMs 将有助于提高人们对大语言模型（LLMs）对齐的信任。在这项工作中，我们提出使用对比解释来阐明 RMs 所做的任何二元响应比较。具体来说，我们生成一组与原始比较相似的新比较集，以刻画 RMs 的局部行为。构成新比较的扰动响应被生成，旨在明确修改人工指定的高级评估属性，而 RMs 行为的分析正是基于这些属性。在定量实验中，我们验证了该方法在寻找高质量对比解释方面的有效性。随后，我们展示了该方法在探究 RMs 对各评估属性的全局敏感性方面的定性价值，并演示了如何自动提取代表性示例，以解释和比较不同 RMs 的行为。我们将该方法视为一个灵活的 RMs 解释框架，它为更具可解释性和可信度的大语言模型（LLMs）对齐提供了基础。

[No.188] Actions Speak Louder Than Words: Rate-Reward Trade-off in Markov Decision Processes
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 行动胜于言语：马尔可夫决策过程中的奖励-价值权衡
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Haotian Wu;Gongpu Chen;Deniz Gunduz;
机构: Imperial College London;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29211
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03335
英摘: The impact of communication on decision-making systems has been extensively studied under the assumption of dedicated communication channels. We instead consider communicating through actions, where the message is embedded into the actions of an agent which interacts with the environment in a Markov decision process (MDP) framework. We conceptualize the MDP environment as a finite-state channel (FSC), where the actions of the agent serve as the channel input, while the states of the MDP observed by another agent (i.e., receiver) serve as the channel output. Here, we treat the environment as a communication channel over which the agent communicates through its actions, while at the same time, trying to maximize its reward. We first characterize the optimal information theoretic trade-off between the average reward and the rate of reliable communication in the infinite-horizon regime. Then, we propose a novel framework to design a joint control/coding policy, termed \textit{Act2Comm}, which seamlessly embeds messages into actions. From a communication perspective, \textit{Act2Comm} functions as a learning-based channel coding scheme for non-differentiable FSCs under input-output constraints. From a control standpoint, \textit{Act2Comm} learns an MDP policy that incorporates communication capabilities, though at the cost of some control performance. Overall, \textit{Act2Comm} effectively balances the dual objectives of control and communication in this environment. Experimental results validate \textit{Act2Comm}'s capability to enable reliable communication while maintaining a certain level of control performance.
中摘: 

在专用通信信道的假设下，通信对决策系统的影响已得到广泛研究。我们转而考虑通过动作进行通信，其中消息被嵌入到智能体的动作中，该智能体在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架下与环境交互。我们将 MDP 环境概念化为有限状态信道（FSC），其中智能体的动作充当信道输入，而另一智能体（即接收者）观测到的 MDP 状态充当信道输出。在此，我们将环境视为一个通信信道，智能体通过其动作在该信道上进行通信，同时试图最大化其奖励。我们首先刻画了在无限视界情形下，平均奖励与可靠通信速率之间的最优信息论权衡。随后，我们提出了一种新颖的框架来设计一种联合控制/编码策略，称为 Act2Comm，该策略能够将消息无缝嵌入到动作中。从通信视角来看，Act2Comm 作为一种基于学习的信道编码方案，用于在输入输出约束下处理不可微的 FSC。从控制视角来看，Act2Comm 学习到一个包含通信能力的 MDP 策略，尽管这以一定的控制性能为代价。总体而言，Act2Comm 有效地平衡了该环境中控制与通信的双重目标。实验结果验证了 Act2Comm 在维持一定控制性能水平的同时实现可靠通信的能力。

[No.189] A Single Goal is All You Need: Skills and Exploration Emerge from Contrastive RL without Rewards, Demonstrations, or Subgoals
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: reward
标题: 一箭双雕：无需奖励、示范或子目标，对比强化学习即可涌现技能和探索能力
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Grace Liu;Michael Tang;Benjamin Eysenbach;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;;Princeton University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27798
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.05804
英摘: In this paper, we present empirical evidence of skills and directed exploration emerging from a simple RL algorithm long before any successful trials are observed. For example, in a manipulation task, the agent is given a single observation of the goal state and learns skills, first for moving its end-effector, then for pushing the block, and finally for picking up and placing the block. These skills emerge before the agent has ever successfully placed the block at the goal location and without the aid of any reward functions, demonstrations, or manually-specified distance metrics. Once the agent has learned to reach the goal state reliably, exploration is reduced. Implementing our method involves a simple modification of prior work and does not require density estimates, ensembles, or any additional hyperparameters. Intuitively, the proposed method seems like it should be terrible at exploration, and we lack a clear theoretical understanding of why it works so effectively, though our experiments provide some hints.
中摘: 

在本文中，我们提供了实证证据，表明技能和定向探索会从一个简单的强化学习算法中涌现，且远早于观察到任何成功尝试之时。例如，在一个操作任务中，智能体仅获得一次目标状态的观测，便学会了一系列技能：首先是移动其末端执行器，其次是推动积木，最后是拾起并放置积木。这些技能的涌现发生在智能体从未成功将积木放置在目标位置之前，且无需任何奖励函数、演示或手动设定距离度量的辅助。一旦智能体学会了可靠地到达目标状态，探索行为便会减少。实现我们的方法仅涉及对先前工作的简单修改，无需密度估计、集成或任何额外的超参数。直观来看，所提出的方法本应在探索方面表现极差，我们缺乏对其为何如此有效运作的清晰理论理解，尽管我们的实验提供了一些线索。
