[No.314] AlphaPO: Reward Shape Matters for LLM Alignment
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: AlphaPO：奖励形状对于LLM对齐至关重要
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Aman Gupta;Shao Tang;Qingquan Song;Sirou Zhu;Jiwoo Hong;Ankan Saha;Viral Gupta;Noah Lee;Eunki Kim;Siyu Zhu;Parag Agrawal;Natesh S. Pillai;Sathiya Keerthi;
机构: LinkedIn Corporation;Texas A&M University;Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;;Northeastern University;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45569
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03884
英摘: Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF) and its variants have made huge strides toward the effective alignment of large language models (LLMs) to follow instructions and reflect human values. More recently, Direct Alignment Algorithms (DAAs) have emerged in which the reward modeling stage of RLHF is skipped by characterizing the reward directly as a function of the policy being learned. Some popular examples of DAAs include Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and Simple Preference Optimization (SimPO). These methods often suffer from likelihood displacement, a phenomenon by which the probabilities of preferred responses are often reduced undesirably. In this paper, we argue that, for DAAs the reward (function) shape matters. We introduce \textbf{AlphaPO}, a new DAA method that leverages an $α$-parameter to help change the shape of the reward function beyond the standard log reward. AlphaPO helps maintain fine-grained control over likelihood displacement and over-optimization. Compared to SimPO, one of the best performing DAAs, AlphaPO leads to about 7\% to 10\% relative improvement in alignment performance for the instruct versions of Mistral-7B and Llama3-8B while achieving 15\% to 50\% relative improvement over DPO on the same models. The analysis and results presented highlight the importance of the reward shape and how one can systematically change it to affect training dynamics, as well as improve alignment performance.
中摘: 

基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）及其变体在为大型语言模型（LLMs）的有效对齐方面取得了巨大进展，使其能够遵循指令并体现人类价值观。最近，直接对齐算法（DAAs）应运而生，该方法跳过了 RLHF 的奖励建模阶段，通过将奖励直接表征为正在学习的策略的函数。DAAs 的一些流行例子包括直接偏好优化（DPO）和简单偏好优化（SimPO）。这些方法常受似然位移的困扰，这是一种偏好响应的概率被非期望地降低的现象。在本文中，我们认为，对于 DAAs，奖励（函数）的形状至关重要。我们提出了\textbf{AlphaPO}，一种新的 DAA 方法，它利用$α$参数来改变奖励函数的形状，超越标准的对数奖励。AlphaPO 有助于维持对似然位移和过度优化的细粒度控制。与 SimPO（性能最佳的 DAA 之一）相比，AlphaPO 在 Mistral-7B 和 Llama3-8B 的指令版本上带来了约 7% 到 10% 的对齐性能相对提升，同时在相同模型上相较于 DPO 实现了 15% 到 50% 的相对提升。所呈现的分析与结果强调了奖励形状的重要性，以及人们如何系统性地改变它来影响训练动态，并提高对齐性能。

[No.315] Discriminative Policy Optimization for Token-Level Reward Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于区分性策略优化的令牌级奖励模型
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Hongzhan Chen;Tao Yang;Shiping Gao;Ruijun Chen;Xiaojun Quan;Hongtao Tian;Ting Yao;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Wechat Search, Tencent Inc.;Tencent;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44776
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23363
英摘: Process reward models (PRMs) provide more nuanced supervision compared to outcome reward models (ORMs) for optimizing policy models, positioning them as a promising approach to enhancing the capabilities of LLMs in complex reasoning tasks. Recent efforts have advanced PRMs from step-level to token-level granularity by integrating reward modeling into the training of generative models, with reward scores derived from token generation probabilities. However, the conflict between generative language modeling and reward modeling may introduce instability and lead to inaccurate credit assignments. To address this challenge, we revisit token-level reward assignment by decoupling reward modeling from language generation and derive a token-level reward model through the optimization of a discriminative policy, termed the Q-function Reward Model (Q-RM). We theoretically demonstrate that Q-RM explicitly learns token-level Q-functions from preference data without relying on fine-grained annotations. In our experiments, Q-RM consistently outperforms all baseline methods across various benchmarks. For example, when integrated into PPO/REINFORCE algorithms, Q-RM enhances the average Pass@1 score by 5.85/4.70 points on mathematical reasoning tasks compared to the ORM baseline, and by 4.56/5.73 points compared to the token-level PRM counterpart. Moreover, reinforcement learning with Q-RM significantly enhances training efficiency, achieving convergence 12 times faster than ORM on GSM8K and 11 times faster than step-level PRM on MATH. Code and data are available at https://github.com/homzer/Q-RM.
中摘: 

过程奖励模型（PRMs）相较于结果奖励模型（ORMs），能够为策略模型的优化提供更细致的监督，使其成为增强大型语言模型（LLMs）在复杂推理任务能力的一种颇具前景的方法。近期的研究通过将奖励建模整合到生成模型的训练中，推动了 PRMs 从步骤级向令牌级粒度的发展，其奖励分数源自令牌生成概率。然而，生成语言建模与奖励建模之间的冲突可能会引入不稳定性，并导致信用分配不准确。为了解决这一挑战，我们重新审视令牌级奖励分配，通过将奖励建模与语言生成解耦，并优化判别式策略，推导出了一个名为 Q 函数奖励模型（Q-RM）的令牌级奖励模型。我们在理论上证明，Q-RM 能够无需依赖细粒度标注，直接从偏好数据中显式地学习令牌级 Q 函数。在我们的实验中，Q-RM 在各种基准测试中始终优于所有基线方法。例如，当集成到 PPO/REINFORCE 算法中时，与 ORM 基线相比，Q-RM 在数学推理任务上将平均 Pass@1 得分提高了 5.85/4.70 分，与令牌级 PRM 相比则提高了 4.56/5.73 分。此外，采用 Q-RM 的强化学习显著提升了训练效率，在 GSM8K 上的收敛速度比 ORM 快 12 倍，在 MATH 上比步骤级 PRM 快 11 倍。代码和数据可在 https://github.com/homzer/Q-RM 获取。

[No.316] Free Process Rewards without Process Labels
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 无过程标签的免费流程奖励
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Lifan Yuan;Wendi Li;Huayu Chen;Ganqu Cui;Ning Ding;Kaiyan Zhang;Bowen Zhou;Zhiyuan Liu;Hao Peng;
机构: ;Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Tsinghua University;Shanghai AI Laboratory;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46278
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01981
英摘: Different from its counterpart outcome reward models (ORMs), which evaluate the entire responses, a process reward model (PRM) scores a reasoning trajectory step by step, providing denser and more fine grained rewards. However, training a PRM requires labels annotated at every intermediate step, presenting significant challenges for both manual and automatic data collection. This paper aims to address this challenge. Both theoretically and empirically, we show that an \textit{implicit PRM} can be obtained at no additional cost, by simply training an ORM on the cheaper response-level labels. The only assumption is to parameterize the outcome reward as the log-likelihood ratios of the policy and reference models, which can be optimized regardless of the specific choice of loss objectives. In experiments, we instantiate our implicit PRMs with various objectives and evaluate their performance on MATH. We show that our implicit PRM outperforms a strong MCTS-based baseline \textit{á la} Math-Shepherd using less than $1/38$ of the training data. Its performance can be further improved with majority voting. We further find that scaling up instructions and responses benefits our implicit PRM, and the latter brings a larger gain. Particularly, we find that our implicit PRM, when instantiated with the cross-entropy (CE) loss, is more data-efficient and can keep improving generation models even when trained with only one response per instruction, the setup that suffers from extreme data scarcity and imbalance. Further, instructions should be relevant to downstream tasks while the diversity of responses does not bring gains. Surprisingly, training on extra Math-Shepherd step labels brings no further improvements to our implicit PRM trained on only outcome data. We hope that our work will encourage a rethinking of PRM training approaches and contribute to making training PRMs more accessible.
中摘: 

与评估整个响应的结果奖励模型（ORM）不同，过程奖励模型（PRM）逐步对推理轨迹进行评分，提供更密集且更细粒度的奖励。然而，训练 PRM 需要在每个中间步骤进行标注，这对人工和自动数据采集均带来了重大挑战。本文旨在解决这一挑战。我们在理论和实证上均表明，只需在更便宜的响应级标签上训练 ORM，即可零额外成本获得一个*隐式 PRM*。唯一的假设是将结果奖励参数化为策略模型与参考模型的对数似然比，无论具体选择何种损失目标，该设定均可优化。在实验中，我们用多种目标实例化了隐式 PRM，并在 MATH 数据集上评估了其性能。我们展示了我们的隐式 PRM 优于一个基于 MCTS 的强基线（类 Math-Shepherd），且仅使用了不到 $1/38$ 的训练数据。其性能还可通过多数投票机制进一步提升。我们进一步发现，增加指令和响应的规模有利于隐式 PRM，其中后者带来的收益更大。特别是，我们发现当使用交叉熵（CE）损失实例化时，隐式 PRM 具有更高的数据效率，即便在“每条指令仅有一个响应”的设置下（该设置面临极端的数据稀缺和不平衡问题），仍能持续改进生成模型。此外，指令应与下游任务相关，而响应的多样性并不能带来增益。令人惊讶的是，在额外的 Math-Shepherd 步骤标签上进行训练，并不能为仅基于结果数据训练的隐式 PRM 带来进一步的改进。我们希望我们的工作能促进对 PRM 训练方法的重新思考，并有助于使 PRM 的训练变得更加普及。

[No.317] GRAM: A Generative Foundation Reward Model for Reward Generalization
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 生成奖励模型：一种用于奖励泛化的生成基础模型
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Chenglong Wang;Yang Gan;Yifu Huo;Yongyu Mu;Qiaozhi He;MuRun Yang;Bei Li;Tong Xiao;Chunliang Zhang;Tongran Liu;JingBo Zhu;

英摘: In aligning large language models (LLMs), reward models have played an important role, but are standardly trained as discriminative models and rely only on labeled human preference data. In this paper, we explore methods that train reward models using both unlabeled and labeled data. Building on the generative models in LLMs, we develop a generative reward model that is first trained via large-scale unsupervised learning and then fine-tuned via supervised learning. We also show that by using label smoothing, we are in fact optimizing a regularized pairwise ranking loss. This result, in turn, provides a new view of training reward models, which links generative models and discriminative models under the same class of training objectives. The outcome of these techniques is a foundation reward model, which can be applied to a wide range of tasks with little or no further fine-tuning effort. Extensive experiments show that this model generalizes well across several tasks, including response ranking, reinforcement learning from human feedback, and task adaptation with fine-tuning, achieving significant performance improvements over several strong baseline models.
中摘: 

在对大语言模型（LLM）进行对齐时，奖励模型发挥了重要作用，但它们通常作为判别式模型进行训练，且仅依赖于标注的人类偏好数据。在本文中，我们探索了利用未标注数据和标注数据共同训练奖励模型的方法。基于大语言模型中的生成式模型，我们开发了一种生成式奖励模型，该模型首先通过大规模无监督学习进行训练，随后通过监督学习进行微调。此外，我们还表明，通过使用标签平滑，我们实际上是在优化一种正则化成对排名损失。这一结果反过来为训练奖励模型提供了新视角，它将生成式模型和判别式模型在同类训练目标下联系起来。这些技术的成果是一个基础奖励模型，它可以应用于广泛的任务，而无需或只需极少的进一步微调工作。大量实验表明，该模型在多个任务上均具有良好的泛化能力，包括回复排序、基于人类反馈的强化学习以及微调下的任务适应，与多个强基线模型相比取得了显著的性能提升。

[No.319] PARM: Multi-Objective Test-Time Alignment via Preference-Aware Autoregressive Reward Model
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 参数：基于偏好意识自回归奖励模型的多目标测试时对齐
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Baijiong Lin;Weisen Jiang;Yuancheng Xu;Hao Chen;Ying-Cong Chen;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Chinese University of Hong Kong;University of Maryland;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43455
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06274
英摘: Multi-objective test-time alignment aims to adapt large language models (LLMs) to diverse multi-dimensional user preferences during inference while keeping LLMs frozen. Recently, GenARM (Xu et al., 2025) first independently trains Autoregressive Reward Models (ARMs) for each preference dimension without awareness of each other, then combines their outputs based on user-specific preference vectors during inference to achieve multi-objective test-time alignment, leading to two key limitations: the need for \textit{multiple} ARMs increases the inference cost, and the separate training of ARMs causes the misalignment between the guided generation and the user preferences. To address these issues, we propose Preference-aware ARM (PARM), a single unified ARM trained across all preference dimensions. PARM uses our proposed Preference-Aware Bilinear Low-Rank Adaptation (PBLoRA), which employs a bilinear form to condition the ARM on preference vectors, enabling it to achieve precise control over preference trade-offs during inference. Experiments demonstrate that PARM reduces inference costs and achieves better alignment with preference vectors compared with existing methods. Additionally, PARM enables weak-to-strong guidance, allowing a smaller PARM to guide a larger frozen LLM without expensive training, making multi-objective alignment accessible with limited computing resources. The code is available at https://github.com/Baijiong-Lin/PARM.
中摘: 

多目标测试时对齐旨在使大语言模型 (LLMs) 在推理过程中适应多样化的多维用户偏好，同时保持模型参数冻结。近期，GenARM (Xu et al., 2025) 首先为每个偏好维度独立训练自回归奖励模型 (ARMs)，各模型之间互不感知，随后在推理时基于用户特定的偏好向量组合其输出，以实现多目标测试时对齐，但这导致了两个关键局限性：需要多个 ARMs 增加了推理成本，且 ARMs 的分别训练导致引导生成与用户偏好之间存在不一致。为解决这些问题，我们提出了偏好感知自回归奖励模型 (PARM)，这是一种跨所有偏好维度训练的单一统一 ARM。PARM 采用了我们提出的偏好感知双线性低秩自适应 (PBLoRA)，该方法利用双线性形式使 ARM 能够以偏好向量为条件，使其能够在推理期间实现对偏好权衡的精确控制。实验表明，与现有方法相比，PARM 不仅降低了推理成本，还实现了与偏好向量的更好对齐。此外，PARM 实现了弱指导强，允许较小的 PARM 在不进行昂贵训练的情况下指导较大的冻结 LLM，使得多目标对齐在有限计算资源下变得可行。代码可在 https://github.com/Baijiong-Lin/PARM 获取。

[No.320] PILAF: Optimal Human Preference Sampling for Reward Modeling
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 帕拉夫：最优人类偏好采样奖励建模
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Yunzhen Feng;Ariel Kwiatkowski;Kunhao Zheng;Julia Kempe;Yaqi Duan;
机构: New York University;Meta;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45334
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04270
英摘: As large language models increasingly drive real-world applications, aligning them with human values becomes paramount. Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a key technique, translating preference data into reward models when oracle human values remain inaccessible. In practice, RLHF mostly relies on approximate reward models, which may not consistently guide the policy toward maximizing the underlying human values. We propose Policy-Interpolated Learning for Aligned Feedback (PILAF), a novel response sampling strategy for preference labeling that explicitly aligns preference learning with maximizing the underlying oracle reward. PILAF is theoretically grounded, demonstrating optimality from both an optimization and a statistical perspective. The method is straightforward to implement and demonstrates strong performance in iterative and online RLHF settings where feedback curation is critical.
中摘: 

随着大型语言模型日益驱动现实世界的应用，使其与人类价值观保持一致变得至关重要。基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）已成为一项关键技术，它能在人类最优价值观难以直接获取时，将偏好数据转化为奖励模型。在实践中，RLHF 主要依赖于近似奖励模型，这可能无法始终一致地引导策略以最大化潜在的人类价值观。我们提出了“对齐反馈策略插值学习”（PILAF），这是一种用于偏好标注的新型响应采样策略，能够明确地将偏好学习与最大化潜在的最优奖励相对齐。PILAF 具有坚实的理论基础，从优化和统计两个角度均证明了其最优性。该方法实施简便，并在反馈构建至关重要的迭代和在线 RLHF 场景中表现出色。

[No.321] Reasoning Through Execution: Unifying Process and Outcome Rewards for Code Generation
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 执行推理：统一代码生成中的过程奖励和结果奖励
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Zhuohao Yu;Weizheng Gu;Yidong Wang;Xingru Jiang;Zhengran Zeng;Jindong Wang;Wei Ye;Shikun Zhang;
机构: ;South China University of Technology;Peking University;College of William & Mary;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44014
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15118
英摘: Large Language Models excel at code generation yet struggle with complex programming tasks that demand sophisticated reasoning. To bridge this gap, traditional process supervision relies on learned reward models requiring costly training data and suffering from reward misalignment, while outcome supervision fails for complex tasks needing coordinated intermediate steps. We introduce Outcome Refining Process Supervision, which unifies process and outcome supervision by leveraging executable verification: a tree-structured search framework generates strategic alternatives, profiles execution metrics, and scores candidates via self-critique mechanisms that integrate runtime feedback with reasoning. Experiments across 5 models and 3 benchmarks show consistent gains, with 26.9% higher correctness and 42.2% improved code efficiency. The results demonstrate that ORPS enables LLMs to overcome local optima in code generation, suggesting a promising direction for combining verifiable outcomes with structured reasoning to tackle complex challenges. We open-source at: https://github.com/zhuohaoyu/ORPS
中摘: 

大语言模型在代码生成方面表现出色，但在需要复杂推理的编程任务上却面临挑战。为弥补这一差距，传统过程监督依赖于需要昂贵训练数据且易受奖励不一致影响的已学习奖励模型，而结果监督在处理需要协调中间步骤的复杂任务时则失效。我们提出了结果优化过程监督（Outcome Refining Process Supervision），通过利用可执行验证统一了过程监督与结果监督：其树状搜索框架生成策略备选方案，分析执行指标，并通过整合运行时反馈与推理的自我批判机制对候选项进行评分。在 5 个模型和 3 个基准上的实验显示出一致性的提升，正确率高出 26.9%，代码效率提升 42.2%。结果表明，ORPS 使大语言模型能够克服代码生成中的局部最优问题，这表明结合可验证结果与结构化推理以应对复杂挑战是一个有前景的方向。我们已在以下地址开源：https://github.com/zhuohaoyu/ORPS

[No.322] Reward Modeling with Ordinal Feedback: Wisdom of the Crowd
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于序数反馈的奖励建模：众包的智慧
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Shang Liu;Yu Pan;Guanting Chen;Xiaocheng Li;
机构: Imperial College London;University of Sydney;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46590
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.12843
英摘: Learning a reward model (RM) from human preferences has been an important component in aligning large language models (LLMs). The canonical setup of learning RMs from pairwise preference data is rooted in the classic Bradley-Terry (BT) model that accepts binary feedback, i.e., the label being either Response 1 is better than Response 2, or the opposite. Such a setup inevitably discards potentially useful samples (such as "tied" between the two responses) and loses more fine-grained information (such as "slightly better"). In this paper, we propose a framework for learning RMs under ordinal feedback which generalizes the case of binary preference feedback to any arbitrary granularity. Specifically, we first identify a marginal unbiasedness condition, which generalizes the assumption of the BT model in the existing binary feedback setting. The condition validates itself via the sociological concept of the wisdom of the crowd. Under the condition, we develop a natural probability model for pairwise preference data under ordinal feedback and analyze its properties. We prove the statistical benefits of ordinal feedback in terms of reducing the Rademacher complexity compared to the case of binary feedback. The proposed learning objective and the theory also extend to hinge loss and direct policy optimization (DPO). In particular, the theoretical analysis may be of independent interest when applying to a seemingly unrelated problem of knowledge distillation to interpret the bias-variance trade-off therein. The framework also sheds light on writing guidance for human annotators. Our numerical experiments validate that fine-grained feedback leads to better reward learning for both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Further experiments show that incorporating a certain proportion of samples with tied preference boosts RM learning.
中摘: 

从人类偏好中学习奖励模型（RM）一直是实现大型语言模型（LLM）对齐的关键组成部分。从成对偏好数据学习奖励模型的标准范式源于经典的布拉德利 - 特里（BT）模型，该模型接受二元反馈，即标签为“回答 1 优于回答 2"，抑或相反。这种设置不可避免地舍弃了潜在有用的样本（例如两个回答之间的“平局”），并丢失了更细粒度的信息（例如“稍微更好”）。在本文中，我们提出了一个在序数反馈下学习奖励模型的框架，该框架将二元偏好反馈的情况推广到了任意粒度。具体而言，我们首先界定了一个边缘无偏条件，该条件推广了现有二元反馈设置中 BT 模型的假设。该条件的合理性通过社会学的“群体智慧”概念得到了验证。在该条件下，我们为序数反馈下的成对偏好数据构建了一个自然的概率模型，并分析了其性质。我们证明了序数反馈在降低 Rademacher 复杂度方面相对于二元反馈情况所具有的统计优势。所提出的学习目标及理论也适用于铰链损失和直接策略优化（DPO）。特别是，当应用于看似无关的知识蒸馏问题时，该理论分析可能具有独立的研究价值，可用于阐释其中存在的偏差 - 方差权衡。该框架也为人类标注者的标注指导提供了启示。我们的数值实验验证了，对于分布内和分布外设置，细粒度反馈均能促进更好的奖励学习。进一步实验表明，引入一定比例的具有平局偏好样本能够提升奖励模型的学习效果。

[No.323] Reward-Augmented Data Enhances Direct Preference Alignment of LLMs
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 奖励增强的数据提高了LLM对直接偏好的对齐程度
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Shenao Zhang;Zhihan Liu;Boyi Liu;Yufeng Zhang;Yingxiang Yang;Yongfei Liu;Liyu Chen;Tao Sun;Zhaoran Wang;
机构: Northwestern University;Georgia Institute of Technology;ByteDance;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43860
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08067
英摘: Preference alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) has significantly improved their ability to adhere to human instructions and intentions. However, existing direct alignment algorithms primarily focus on relative preferences and often overlook the qualitative aspects of responses, despite having access to preference data that includes reward scores from judge models during AI feedback. Striving to maximize the implicit reward gap between the chosen and the slightly inferior rejected responses can cause overfitting and unnecessary unlearning of the high-quality rejected responses. The unawareness of the reward scores also drives the LLM to indiscriminately favor the low-quality chosen responses and fail to generalize to optimal responses that are sparse in data. To overcome these shortcomings, our study introduces reward-conditioned LLM policies that discern and learn from the entire spectrum of response quality within the dataset, helping extrapolate to more optimal regions. We propose an effective yet simple data relabeling method that conditions the preference pairs on quality scores to construct a reward-augmented dataset. The experiments across various benchmarks and diverse models demonstrate that our approach consistently boosts DPO by a considerable margin. Through comprehensive ablation studies, we demonstrate that our method not only maximizes the utility of preference data but also mitigates the issue of unlearning, demonstrating its broad effectiveness beyond mere data expansion. Our code is available at https://github.com/shenao-zhang/reward-augmented-preference.
中摘: 

大语言模型（LLM）中的偏好对齐已显著提升了其遵循人类指令和意图的能力。然而，现有的直接对齐算法主要关注相对偏好，往往忽视了响应的质量方面，尽管它们能够利用包含 AI 反馈期间来自评估模型的奖励分数的偏好数据。试图最大化选中和略差的拒绝响应之间的隐式奖励差距，可能会导致过拟合以及对高质量拒绝响应的不必要遗忘。忽视奖励分数还会驱使 LLM 无差别地偏好低质量的选中响应，并无法泛化到数据中稀疏的最优响应。为克服这些不足，本研究引入了奖励条件化的 LLM 策略，使其能够辨别并从数据集中整个响应质量谱系中学习，从而有助于外推至更优区域。我们提出了一种有效而简单的数据重标记方法，该方法以质量分数为条件对偏好对进行处理，以构建奖励增强数据集。在各种基准和多样化模型上的实验表明，我们的方法一致地显著提升了 DPO 的性能。通过全面的消融实验，我们证明该方法不仅最大化了偏好数据的效用，还缓解了遗忘问题，证明了其超越单纯数据扩充的广泛有效性。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/shenao-zhang/reward-augmented-preference 获取。

[No.325] The Devil Is in the Details: Tackling Unimodal Spurious Correlations for Generalizable Multimodal Reward Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 细节决定成败：应对单模伪相关以构建可泛化的多模态奖励模型
类别: deep learning->robustness
作者: Zichao Li;Xueru Wen;Jie Lou;Yuqiu Ji;Yaojie Lu;Xianpei Han;Debing Zhang;Le Sun;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;;Xiaohongshu;

链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44766
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03122
英摘: Multimodal Reward Models (MM-RMs) are crucial for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences, particularly as LLMs increasingly interact with multimodal data. However, we find that MM-RMs trained on existing datasets often struggle to generalize to out-of-distribution data due to their reliance on unimodal spurious correlations, primarily text-only shortcuts within the training distribution, which prevents them from leveraging true multimodal reward functions. To address this, we introduce a Shortcut-aware MM-RM learning algorithm that mitigates this issue by dynamically reweighting training samples, shifting the distribution toward better multimodal understanding, and reducing dependence on unimodal spurious correlations. 中摘: 

多模态奖励模型（MM-RMs）对于将大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类偏好对齐至关重要，尤其是随着 LLMs 日益频繁地与多模态数据交互。然而，我们发现，基于现有数据集训练的 MM-RM 往往难以泛化到分布外数据，这是由于其依赖单模态虚假相关性（主要是训练分布内的纯文本捷径），从而阻碍了其利用真正的多模态奖励函数。为解决这一问题，我们引入了一种捷径感知的 MM-RM 学习算法，该算法通过动态重加权训练样本，引导分布向更好的多模态理解转变，并减少对单模态虚假相关性的依赖，从而缓解该问题。我们的实验结果表明，在泛化性、下游任务性能和可扩展性方面取得了显著改善，建立了一个更为稳健的多模态奖励建模框架。

[No.326] Automatic Reward Shaping from Confounded Offline Data
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 从混杂的离线数据中自动生成奖励塑造
类别: general machine learning->causality
作者: Mingxuan Li;Junzhe Zhang;Elias Bareinboim;
机构: Columbia University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45757
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11478
英摘: A key task in Artificial Intelligence is learning effective policies for controlling agents in unknown environments to optimize performance measures. Off-policy learning methods, like Q-learning, allow learners to make optimal decisions based on past experiences. This paper studies off-policy learning from biased data in complex and high-dimensional domains where \emph{unobserved confounding} cannot be ruled out a priori. Building on the well-celebrated Deep Q-Network (DQN), we propose a novel deep reinforcement learning algorithm robust to confounding biases in observed data. Specifically, our algorithm attempts to find a safe policy for the worst-case environment compatible with the observations. We apply our method to twelve confounded Atari games, and find that it consistently dominates the standard DQN in all games where the observed input to the behavioral and target policies mismatch and unobserved confounders exist.
中摘:

[No.327] Adaptive Exploration for Multi-Reward Multi-Policy Evaluation
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 多奖励多策略评估的自适应探索
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Alessio Russo;Aldo Pacchiano;
机构: ;Boston University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46555
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02516
英摘: We study the policy evaluation problem in an online multi-reward multi-policy discounted setting, where multiple reward functions must be evaluated simultaneously for different policies. We adopt an $(ε,δ)$-PAC perspective to achieve $ε$-accurate estimates with high confidence across finite or convex sets of rewards, a setting that has not been investigated in the literature. Building on prior work on Multi-Reward Best Policy Identification, we adapt the MR-NaS exploration scheme to jointly minimize sample complexity for evaluating different policies across different reward sets. Our approach leverages an instance-specific lower bound revealing how the sample complexity scales with a measure of value deviation, guiding the design of an efficient exploration policy. Although computing this bound entails a hard non-convex optimization, we propose an efficient convex approximation that holds for both finite and convex reward sets. Experiments in tabular domains demonstrate the effectiveness of this adaptive exploration scheme.
中摘: 

我们研究了在线多奖励多策略折扣设定下的策略评估问题，在此设定中需针对不同策略同时评估多个奖励函数。我们采用 $(ε,δ)$-PAC 视角，在有限或凸奖励集上以高置信度实现 $ε$-精度的估计，这一设定在现有文献中尚未得到研究。基于多奖励最佳策略识别的先前工作，我们改进了 MR-NaS 探索方案，旨在联合最小化针对不同奖励集评估不同策略的样本复杂度。我们的方法利用了一个实例特定的下界，揭示了样本复杂度如何随价值偏差的度量进行缩放，从而指导高效探索策略的设计。尽管计算该下界涉及困难的非凸优化，但我们提出了一个高效的凸近似，适用于有限及凸奖励集。表格域上的实验证明了该自适应探索方案的有效性。

[No.330] RATE: Causal Explainability of Reward Models with Imperfect Counterfactuals
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 奖励模型在不完美的反事实条件下的因果可解释性
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: David Reber;Sean M Richardson;Todd Nief;Cristina Garbacea;Victor Veitch;
机构: University of Chicago;;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43898
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11348
英摘: Reward models are widely used as proxies for human preferences when aligning or evaluating LLMs. However, reward models are black boxes, and it is often unclear what, exactly, they are actually rewarding. In this paper we develop Rewrite-based Attribute Treatment Estimator (RATE) as an effective method for measuring the sensitivity of a reward model to high-level attributes of responses, such as sentiment, helpfulness, or complexity. Importantly, RATE measures the causal effect of an attribute on the reward. RATE uses LLMs to rewrite responses to produce imperfect counterfactuals examples that can be used to measure causal effects. A key challenge is that these rewrites are imperfect in a manner that can induce substantial bias in the estimated sensitivity of the reward model to the attribute. The core idea of RATE is to adjust for this imperfect-rewrite effect by rewriting twice. We establish the validity of the RATE procedure and show empirically that it is an effective estimator.
中摘: 

在对齐或评估大语言模型（LLMs）时，奖励模型被广泛用作人类偏好的代理。然而，奖励模型是黑盒，通常不清楚它们究竟奖励的是什么。在本文中，我们提出了基于重写的属性处理估计器（RATE），作为一种有效测量奖励模型对响应高层属性（如情感、帮助性或复杂性）敏感性的方法。重要的是，RATE 衡量的是属性对奖励的因果效应。RATE 利用大语言模型重写回复，以生成不完美的反事实样本，这些样本可用于衡量因果效应。一个关键挑战在于，这些重写的不完美性会导致奖励模型对该属性敏感性的估计产生显著偏差。RATE 的核心思想是通过双重重写来校正这种因重写不完美而产生的效应。我们确立了 RATE 程序的有效性，并通过实证研究表明它是一种有效的估计器。

[No.331] TGDPO: Harnessing Token-Level Reward Guidance for Enhancing Direct Preference Optimization
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: TGDPO：利用令牌级奖励指导提升直接偏好优化
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Mingkang Zhu;Xi Chen;Zhongdao Wang;Bei Yu;Hengshuang Zhao;Jiaya Jia;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;University of Hong Kong;;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45181
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14574
英摘: Recent advancements in reinforcement learning from human feedback have shown that utilizing fine-grained token-level reward models can substantially enhance the performance of Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in aligning large language models. However, it is challenging to leverage such token-level reward as guidance for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), since DPO is formulated as a sequence-level bandit problem. To address this challenge, this work decomposes the sequence-level PPO into a sequence of token-level proximal policy optimization problems and then frames the problem of token-level PPO with token-level reward guidance, from which closed-form optimal token-level policy and the corresponding token-level reward can be derived. Using the obtained reward and Bradley-Terry model, this work establishes a framework of computable loss functions with token-level reward guidance for DPO, and proposes a practical reward guidance based on the induced DPO reward. This formulation enables different tokens to exhibit varying degrees of deviation from reference policy based on their respective rewards. Experiment results demonstrate that our method achieves substantial performance improvements over DPO, with win rate gains of up to 7.5 points on MT-Bench, 6.2 points on AlpacaEval 2, and 4.3 points on Arena-Hard. Code is available at https://github.com/dvlab-research/TGDPO.
中摘: 

近期人类反馈强化学习领域的进展表明，利用细粒度 token 级奖励模型可以显著提升近端策略优化（PPO）在大型语言模型对齐中的性能。然而，由于直接偏好优化（DPO）被公式化为序列级老虎机问题，利用此类 token 级奖励作为其指导具有挑战性。为了解决这一挑战，本工作将序列级 PPO 分解为一系列 token 级近端策略优化问题，随后构建带有 token 级奖励指导的 token 级 PPO 问题框架，从中可推导出闭式最优 token 级策略及相应的 token 级奖励。利用所得奖励和 Bradley-Terry 模型，本工作建立了带有 token 级奖励指导的 DPO 可计算损失函数框架，并提出了基于诱导 DPO 奖励的实用奖励指导。该公式化使得不同的 token 能够根据各自的奖励，表现出与参考策略不同程度的偏离。实验结果表明，我们的方法相较于 DPO 实现了显著的性能提升，在 MT-Bench 上胜率提升高达 7.5 分，在 AlpacaEval 2 上提升 6.2 分，在 Arena-Hard 上提升 4.3 分。代码可在 https://github.com/dvlab-research/TGDPO 获取。

[No.332] A Sharper Global Convergence Analysis for Average Reward Reinforcement Learning via an Actor-Critic Approach
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于演员-评论家方法的更尖锐的全局收敛性分析：平均奖励强化学习
类别: theory->reinforcement learning and planning
作者: Swetha Ganesh;Washim Uddin Mondal;Vaneet Aggarwal;
机构: Purdue University;Indian Institute of Science;Indian Institute of Technology Kanpur;

链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43810
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18878
英摘: This work examines average-reward reinforcement learning with general policy parametrization. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) guarantees for this problem are either suboptimal or hindered by several challenges, including poor scalability with respect to the size of the state-action space, high iteration complexity, and dependence on knowledge of mixing times and hitting times. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-level Monte Carlo-based Natural Actor-Critic (MLMC-NAC) algorithm. Moreover, the convergence rate does not scale with the size of the state space, therefore even being applicable to infinite state spaces.
中摘:

来源: ICML 2025

英摘: This paper investigates the potential of quantum acceleration in addressing infinite horizon Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) to enhance average reward outcomes. [No.336] AlphaDPO: Adaptive Reward Margin for Direct Preference Optimization
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: AlphaDPO：直接偏好优化的自适应奖励边际
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Junkang Wu;Xue Wang;Zhengyi Yang;Jiancan Wu;Jinyang Gao;Bolin Ding;Xiang Wang;Xiangnan He;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;;Alibaba Group;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45946
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10148
英摘: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values and intentions is crucial for their utility, honesty, and safety. Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) is a popular approach to achieve this alignment, but it faces challenges in computational efficiency and training stability. Recent methods like Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and Simple Preference Optimization (SimPO) have proposed offline alternatives to RLHF, simplifying the process by reparameterizing the reward function. However, DPO depends on a potentially suboptimal reference model, and SimPO's assumption of a fixed target reward margin may lead to suboptimal decisions in diverse data settings. In this work, we propose $α$-DPO, an adaptive preference optimization algorithm designed to address these limitations by introducing a dynamic reward margin. Specifically, $α$-DPO employs an adaptive preference distribution, balancing the policy model and the reference model to achieve personalized reward margins. We provide theoretical guarantees for $α$-DPO, demonstrating its effectiveness as a surrogate optimization objective and its ability to balance alignment and diversity through KL divergence control. Empirical evaluations on AlpacaEval 2 and Arena-Hard show that $α$-DPO consistently outperforms DPO and SimPO across various model settings, establishing it as a robust approach for fine-tuning LLMs. Our method achieves significant improvements in win rates, highlighting its potential as a powerful tool for LLM alignment. The code is available at https://github.com/junkangwu/alpha-DPO
中摘: 

将大语言模型（LLMs）与人类的价值观和意图对齐，对于其效用、诚实性和安全性至关重要。来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）是实现这种对齐的流行方法，但在计算效率和训练稳定性方面面临挑战。最近的方法，如直接偏好优化（DPO）和简单偏好优化（SimPO），提出了 RLHF 的离线替代方案，通过重新参数化奖励函数简化了过程。然而，DPO 依赖于一个可能次优的参考模型，而 SimPO 关于固定目标奖励边界的假设可能导致在多样化数据设置下做出次优决策。在本工作中，我们提出了 α-DPO，这是一种自适应偏好优化算法，旨在通过引入动态奖励边界来解决这些局限性。具体而言，α-DPO 采用自适应偏好分布，平衡策略模型与参考模型，以实现个性化的奖励边界。我们为 α-DPO 提供了理论保障，证明了其作为代理优化目标的有效性，以及通过 KL 散度控制平衡对齐与多样性的能力。在 AlpacaEval 2 和 Arena-Hard 上的实证评估表明，α-DPO 在各种模型设置下始终优于 DPO 和 SimPO，确立了它作为微调大语言模型的稳健方法。我们的方法在胜率方面取得了显著提升，突显了其作为大语言模型对齐有力工具的潜力。代码可在 https://github.com/junkangwu/alpha-DPO 获取。

[No.337] On the Robustness of Reward Models for Language Model Alignment
来源: ICML 2025
命中: reward
标题: 关于奖励模型在语言模型对齐中的稳健性
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Jiwoo Hong;Noah Lee;Eunki Kim;Guijin Son;Woojin Chung;Aman Gupta;Shao Tang;James Thorne;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Yonsei University;LinkedIn Corporation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45164
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07271
英摘: The Bradley-Terry (BT) model is widely practiced in reward modeling for reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF). Despite its effectiveness, reward models (RMs) trained with BT model loss are prone to over-optimization, losing generalizability to unseen input distributions. In this paper, we study the cause of over-optimization in RM training and its downstream effects on the RLHF procedure, accentuating the importance of distributional robustness of RMs in unseen data. First, we show that the excessive dispersion of hidden state norms is the main source of over-optimization. Then, we propose batch-wise sum-to-zero regularization (BSR) to enforce zero-centered reward sum per batch, constraining the rewards with extreme magnitudes. We assess the impact of BSR in improving robustness in RMs through four scenarios of over-optimization, where BSR consistently manifests better robustness. Subsequently, we compare the plain BT model and BSR on RLHF training and empirically show that robust RMs better align the policy to the gold preference model. Finally, we apply BSR to high-quality data and models, which surpasses state-of-the-art RMs in the 8B scale by adding more than 5% in complex preference prediction tasks. By conducting RLOO training with 8B RMs, AlpacaEval 2.0 reduces generation length by 40% while adding a 7% increase in win rate, further highlighting that robustness in RMs induces robustness in RLHF training. We release the code, data, and models: https://github.com/LinkedIn-XFACT/RM-Robustness.
中摘: 

Bradley-Terry (BT) 模型广泛应用于强化学习人类反馈（RLHF）的奖励建模中。尽管其效果显著，但使用 BT 模型损失训练的奖励模型（RMs）容易发生过优化，丧失对未见输入分布的泛化能力。在本文中，我们研究了奖励模型训练中过度优化的成因及其对 RLHF 流程的下游影响，强调了奖励模型在未见过数据上的分布鲁棒性的重要性。首先，我们表明隐藏状态范数的过度分散是过度优化的主要来源。随后，我们提出了批内求和为零正则化（BSR），以强制每批次的奖励总和为零中心化，从而约束具有极端幅值的奖励。我们通过四种过度优化场景评估了 BSR 在提升奖励模型鲁棒性方面的影响，结果显示 BSR 始终表现出更优的鲁棒性。接着，我们在 RLHF 训练中比较了原始 BT 模型和 BSR，并通过实证表明，鲁棒的奖励模型能更好地使策略与黄金偏好模型对齐。最后，我们将 BSR 应用于高质量数据和模型，其在 80 亿参数规模上超越了最先进的奖励模型，在复杂偏好预测任务中提升了 5% 以上。通过使用 80 亿参数奖励模型进行 RLOO 训练，AlpacaEval 2.0 将生成长度减少了 40%，同时将胜率提高了 7%，进一步强调了奖励模型的鲁棒性能够带来 RLHF 训练的鲁棒性。我们发布了代码、数据和模型：https://github.com/LinkedIn-XFACT/RM-Robustness。

[No.338] A Dense Reward View on Aligning Text-to-Image Diffusion with Preference

英摘: Aligning text-to-image diffusion model (T2I) with preference has been gaining increasing research attention. [No.339] A Unified Linear Programming Framework for Offline Reward Learning from Human Demonstrations and Feedback
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于人类示范和反馈的离线奖励学习统一线性规划框架
类别: 
作者: Kihyun Kim;Jiawei Zhang;Asuman E. Ozdaglar;Pablo Parrilo;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34146
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12421
英摘: Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) and Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) are pivotal methodologies in reward learning, which involve inferring and shaping the underlying reward function of sequential decision-making problems based on observed human demonstrations and feedback. Most prior work in reward learning has relied on prior knowledge or assumptions about decision or preference models, potentially leading to robustness issues. In response, this paper introduces a novel linear programming (LP) framework tailored for offline reward learning. Utilizing pre-collected trajectories without online exploration, this framework estimates a feasible reward set from the primal-dual optimality conditions of a suitably designed LP, and offers an optimality guarantee with provable sample efficiency. Our LP framework also enables aligning the reward functions with human feedback, such as pairwise trajectory comparison data, while maintaining computational tractability and sample efficiency. We demonstrate that our framework potentially achieves better performance compared to the conventional maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) approach through analytical examples and numerical experiments.
中摘: 

逆强化学习（IRL）和基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）是奖励学习中的关键方法，涉及基于观察到的人类演示和反馈，推断并塑造序贯决策问题的潜在奖励函数。以往大多数奖励学习工作依赖于关于决策或偏好模型的先验知识或假设，这可能导致鲁棒性问题。针对这一问题，本文提出了一种专为离线奖励学习设计的全新线性规划（LP）框架。该框架利用预先收集的轨迹，无需在线探索，即可从适当设计的线性规划的原始 - 对偶最优条件中估计出可行奖励集，并提供具有可证明样本效率的最优性保证。我们的 LP 框架还能实现奖励函数与人类反馈（例如成对轨迹比较数据）的对齐，同时保持计算可行性和样本效率。通过解析示例和数值实验，我们表明与传统的最大似然估计（MLE）方法相比，我们的框架有望取得更好的性能。

[No.340] AI Alignment with Changing and Influenceable Reward Functions
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: AI对变化且可影响奖励函数的对齐
类别: 
作者: Micah Carroll;Davis Foote;Anand Siththaranjan;Stuart Russell;Anca Dragan;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33333
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17713
英摘: Existing AI alignment approaches assume that preferences are static, which is unrealistic: our preferences change, and may even be influenced by our interactions with AI systems themselves. To clarify the consequences of incorrectly assuming static preferences, we introduce Dynamic Reward Markov Decision Processes (DR-MDPs), which explicitly model preference changes and the AI's influence on them. We show that despite its convenience, the static-preference assumption may undermine the soundness of existing alignment techniques, leading them to implicitly reward AI systems for influencing user preferences in ways users may not truly want. We then explore potential solutions. First, we offer a unifying perspective on how an agent's optimization horizon may partially help reduce undesirable AI influence. Then, we formalize different notions of AI alignment that account for preference change from the outset. Comparing the strengths and limitations of 8 such notions of alignment, we find that they all either err towards causing undesirable AI influence, or are overly risk-averse, suggesting that a straightforward solution to the problems of changing preferences may not exist. As there is no avoiding grappling with changing preferences in real-world settings, this makes it all the more important to handle these issues with care, balancing risks and capabilities. We hope our work can provide conceptual clarity and constitute a first step towards AI alignment practices which explicitly account for (and contend with) the changing and influenceable nature of human preferences.
中摘: 

现有的 AI 对齐方法假设偏好是静态的，这并不现实：我们的偏好会发生变化，甚至可能受到我们与人工智能系统本身交互的影响。为了阐明错误假设偏好为静态所带来的后果，我们引入了动态奖励马尔可夫决策过程（DR-MDPs），该过程明确地对偏好变化以及 AI 对偏好的影响进行了建模。我们表明，尽管静态偏好假设具有便利性，但它可能破坏现有对齐技术的可靠性，导致这些技术隐式地奖励 AI 系统，使其以用户可能并不真正希望的方式去影响用户偏好。随后，我们探讨了潜在的解决方案。首先，我们就智能体的优化视界如何能在一定程度上帮助减少不受欢迎的 AI 影响这一问题，提供了一个统一的视角。随后，我们形式化了若干不同的 AI 对齐概念，这些概念从一开始就考虑到了偏好的变化。通过比较这 8 种对齐概念的优劣与局限，我们发现它们要么倾向于导致不受欢迎的 AI 影响，要么过于规避风险，这表明针对偏好变化问题可能并不存在一个简单的解决方案。由于在现实世界环境中无法避免要应对偏好的变化，这使得更需谨慎地处理这些问题，以平衡风险与能力。我们希望我们的工作能提供概念上的清晰，并构成迈向 AI 对齐实践的第一步，这种实践将明确考虑（并应对）人类偏好具有可变性和可被影响的本性。

[No.341] BRAIn: Bayesian Reward-conditioned Amortized Inference for natural language generation from feedback
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: BRAIn：基于反馈的贝叶斯奖励调整推理自然语言生成
类别: 
作者: Gaurav Pandey;Yatin Nandwani;Tahira Naseem;Mayank Mishra;Guangxuan Xu;Dinesh Raghu;Sachindra Joshi;Asim Munawar;Ramón Fernandez Astudillo;
机构: International Business Machines Corporation;Indian Institute of Technology Delhi;International Business Machines;IBM;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33101
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02479
英摘: Distribution matching methods for language model alignment such as Generation with Distributional Control (GDC) and Distributional Policy Gradient (DPG) have not received the same level of attention in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) as contrastive methods such as Sequence Likelihood Calibration (SLiC), Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants. We identify high variance of the gradient estimate as the primary reason for the lack of success of these methods and propose a self-normalized baseline to reduce the variance. We further generalize the target distribution in DPG, GDC and DPO by using Bayes' rule to define the reward-conditioned posterior. The resulting approach, referred to as BRAIn - Bayesian Reward-conditioned Amortized Inference acts as a bridge between distribution matching methods and DPO and significantly outperforms prior art in summarization and Antropic HH tasks.
中摘: 

用于语言模型对齐的分布匹配方法，例如分布控制生成（GDC）和分布策略梯度（DPG），在人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）中所受的关注程度不及序列似然校准（SLiC）、直接偏好优化（DPO）及其变体等对比方法。我们将梯度估计的高方差视为这些方法未能成功的主要原因，并提出一种自归一化基线以降低方差。此外，我们利用贝叶斯定理定义奖励条件后验，进一步推广了 DPG、GDC 和 DPO 中的目标分布。所得方法被称为 BRAIn——贝叶斯奖励条件摊销推理（Bayesian Reward-conditioned Amortized Inference），它在分布匹配方法与 DPO 之间起到了桥梁作用，并且在摘要生成和 Anthropic HH 任务上显著优于现有技术。

[No.342] Code as Reward: Empowering Reinforcement Learning with VLMs
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 代码作为奖励：利用VLMs赋能强化学习
类别: 
作者: David Venuto;Mohammad Sami Nur Islam;Martin Klissarov;Doina Precup;Sherry Yang;Ankit Anand;

英摘: Pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are able to understand visual concepts, describe and decompose complex tasks into sub-tasks, and provide feedback on task completion. In principle, VLMs are well suited for this purpose, as they can naturally analyze image-based observations and provide feedback (reward) on learning progress. However, inference in VLMs is computationally expensive, so querying them frequently to compute rewards would significantly slowdown the training of an RL agent. VLM-CaR produces dense reward functions from VLMs through code generation, thereby significantly reducing the computational burden of querying the VLM directly. [No.343] Confronting Reward Overoptimization for Diffusion Models: A Perspective of Inductive and Primacy Biases
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 面对扩散模型的奖励过度优化：一种归纳偏置和首因偏置的观点
类别: 
作者: Ziyi Zhang;Sen Zhang;Yibing Zhan;Yong Luo;Yonggang Wen;Dacheng Tao;
机构: Wuhan University;University of Sydney;Nanyang Technological University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.08552
英摘: Bridging the gap between diffusion models and human preferences is crucial for their integration into practical generative workflows. While optimizing downstream reward models has emerged as a promising alignment strategy, concerns arise regarding the risk of excessive optimization with learned reward models, which potentially compromises ground-truth performance. In this work, we confront the reward overoptimization problem in diffusion model alignment through the lenses of both inductive and primacy biases. We first identify a mismatch between current methods and the temporal inductive bias inherent in the multi-step denoising process of diffusion models, as a potential source of reward overoptimization. Then, we surprisingly discover that dormant neurons in our critic model act as a regularization against reward overoptimization while active neurons reflect primacy bias. Motivated by these observations, we propose Temporal Diffusion Policy Optimization with critic active neuron Reset (TDPO-R), a policy gradient algorithm that exploits the temporal inductive bias of diffusion models and mitigates the primacy bias stemming from active neurons. Empirical results demonstrate the superior efficacy of our methods in mitigating reward overoptimization. Code is avaliable at https://github.com/ZiyiZhang27/tdpo.
中摘: 

弥合扩散模型与人类偏好之间的差距，对于将其整合到实际生成工作流中至关重要。虽然优化下游奖励模型已成为一种有前景的对齐策略，但关于利用学习到的奖励模型进行过度优化存在风险的担忧也随之产生，这可能会损害真实性能。在这项工作中，我们通过归纳偏置和首因偏置的视角，解决扩散模型对齐中的奖励过度优化问题。我们首先指出，当前方法与扩散模型多步去噪过程中固有的时序归纳偏置之间存在不匹配，这可能是奖励过度优化的一个潜在来源。随后，我们惊讶地发现，我们评判模型中的休眠神经元起到了防止奖励过度优化的正则化作用，而活跃神经元则反映了首因偏置。受这些观察的启发，我们提出了带有评判模型活跃神经元重置的时序扩散策略优化（TDPO-R），这是一种利用扩散模型时序归纳偏置并减轻源于活跃神经元的首因偏置的策略梯度算法。实验结果表明，我们的方法在缓解奖励过度优化方面具有卓越的效能。代码可在 https://github.com/ZiyiZhang27/tdpo 获取。

[No.344] Dense Reward for Free in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于人类反馈的强化学习中的免费密集奖励
类别: 
作者: Alex James Chan;Hao Sun;Samuel Holt;Mihaela van der Schaar;
机构: Spotify;University of Cambridge;Google;University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33476
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00782
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has been credited as the key advance that has allowed Large Language Models (LLMs) to effectively follow instructions and produce useful assistance. Classically, this involves generating completions from the LLM in response to a query before using a separate reward model to assign a score to the full completion. As an auto-regressive process, the LLM has to take many "actions" (selecting individual tokens) and only receives a single, sparse reward at the end of an episode, a setup that is known to be difficult to optimise in traditional reinforcement learning. In this work we leverage the fact that the reward model contains more information than just its scalar output, in particular, it calculates an attention map over tokens as part of the transformer architecture. We use these attention weights to redistribute the reward along the whole completion, effectively densifying the signal and highlighting the most important tokens, all without incurring extra computational cost or requiring any additional modelling. We demonstrate that, theoretically, this approach is equivalent to potential-based reward shaping, ensuring that the optimal policy remains unchanged. Empirically, we show that it stabilises training, accelerates the rate of learning, and, in practical cases, may lead to better local optima.
中摘: 

人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）被视为一项关键突破，它使得大型语言模型（LLM）能够有效遵循指令并提供有用的协助。经典做法涉及从 LLM 生成对查询的补全，随后使用独立的奖励模型为整个补全内容分配分数。作为一种自回归过程，LLM 必须执行许多“动作”（即选择单个词元），而仅在回合结束时获得单一且稀疏的奖励，这种设置在传统强化学习中已知难以优化。在本研究中，我们利用了一个事实，即奖励模型包含的信息不仅仅限于其标量输出，特别是，作为 Transformer 架构的一部分，它会计算针对词元的注意力图。我们利用这些注意力权重在整个补全过程中重新分配奖励，有效地使奖励信号稠密化并突出显示最重要的词元，且无需产生额外计算成本或进行任何额外的建模。我们证明，理论上，该方法等效于基于势能的奖励塑造，确保最优策略保持不变。在经验上，我们表明该方法能够稳定训练进程，加速学习速率，并且在实际情况下，可能引导至更好的局部最优解。

[No.345] Efficient Exploration in Average-Reward Constrained Reinforcement Learning: Achieving Near-Optimal Regret With Posterior Sampling
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 在平均奖励受限强化学习中的高效探索：通过后验采样实现接近最优的遗憾
类别: 
作者: Danil Provodin;Maurits Clemens Kaptein;Mykola Pechenizkiy;
机构: Eindhoven University of Technology;Tilburg University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33108
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19017
英摘: We present a new algorithm based on posterior sampling for learning in Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDP) in the infinite-horizon undiscounted setting. The algorithm achieves near-optimal regret bounds while being advantageous empirically compared to the existing algorithms. Our main theoretical result is a Bayesian regret bound for each cost component of $\tilde{O} (DS\sqrt{AT})$ for any communicating CMDP with $S$ states, $A$ actions, and diameter $D$. Empirical results show that our posterior sampling algorithm outperforms the existing algorithms for constrained reinforcement learning.
中摘: 

我们提出了一种基于后验采样的新算法，用于在无限视界无折扣设定下学习约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）。该算法实现了近最优的遗憾界，并且实证表现优于现有算法。我们的主要理论结果是，对于任意具有 $S$ 个状态、$A$ 个动作和直径 $D$ 的互通型 CMDP，其每个代价分量的贝叶斯遗憾界为 $\tilde{O} (DS\sqrt{AT})$。该遗憾界在时间视界 $T$ 的阶数上与下界相匹配，且是通过计算上可行的算法为互通型 CMDP 所达到的最佳已知遗憾界。实验结果表明，我们的后验采样算法优于约束强化学习的现有算法。

[No.346] Expert Proximity as Surrogate Rewards for Single Demonstration Imitation Learning
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 专家邻近性作为代理奖励的单示例模仿学习
类别: 
作者: Chia-Cheng Chiang;Li-Cheng Lan;Wei-Fang Sun;Chien Feng;Cho-Jui Hsieh;Chun-Yi Lee;
机构: University of California, Los Angeles;NVIDIA;National Tsinghua University;National Tsing Hua University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01057
英摘: In this paper, we focus on single-demonstration imitation learning (IL), a practical approach for real-world applications where acquiring multiple expert demonstrations is costly or infeasible and the ground truth reward function is not available. In contrast to typical IL settings with multiple demonstrations, single-demonstration IL involves an agent having access to only one expert trajectory. We highlight the issue of sparse reward signals in this setting and propose to mitigate this issue through our proposed Transition Discriminator-based IL (TDIL) method. TDIL is an IRL method designed to address reward sparsity by introducing a denser surrogate reward function that considers environmental dynamics. This surrogate reward function encourages the agent to navigate towards states that are proximal to expert states. In practice, TDIL trains a transition discriminator to differentiate between valid and non-valid transitions in a given environment to compute the surrogate rewards. The experiments demonstrate that TDIL outperforms existing IL approaches and achieves expert-level performance in the single-demonstration IL setting across five widely adopted MuJoCo benchmarks as well as the "Adroit Door" robotic environment.
中摘:

[No.348] Feedback Loops With Language Models Drive In-Context Reward Hacking
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 语言模型中的反馈循环推动了上下文内奖励作弊行为
类别: 
作者: Alexander Pan;Erik Jones;Meena Jagadeesan;Jacob Steinhardt;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34557
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06627
英摘: Language models influence the external world: they query APIs that read and write to web pages, generate content that shapes human behavior, and run system commands as autonomous agents. These interactions form feedback loops: LLM outputs affect the world, which in turn affect subsequent LLM outputs. In this work, we show that feedback loops can cause in-context reward hacking (ICRH), where the LLM at test-time optimizes a (potentially implicit) objective but creates negative side effects in the process. For example, consider an LLM agent deployed to increase Twitter engagement; the LLM may retrieve its previous tweets into the context window and make them more controversial, increasing engagement but also toxicity. We identify and study two processes that lead to ICRH: output-refinement and policy-refinement. For these processes, evaluations on static datasets are insufficient -- they miss the feedback effects and thus cannot capture the most harmful behavior. In response, we provide three recommendations for evaluation to capture more instances of ICRH. As AI development accelerates, the effects of feedback loops will proliferate, increasing the need to understand their role in shaping LLM behavior.
中摘: 

语言模型影响着外部世界：它们查询能够读取和写入网页的 API，生成影响人类行为的内容，并作为自主代理执行系统命令。这些交互构成了反馈循环：LLM 的输出影响世界，而世界反过来又影响后续的 LLM 输出。在本研究中，我们表明反馈循环可能导致上下文奖励黑客行为（ICRH），即 LLM 在测试时优化一个（可能是隐式的）目标，但在过程中却产生了负面副作用。例如，考虑一个被部署用于提高 Twitter 参与度的 LLM 代理；该 LLM 可能会将其之前的推文检索进上下文窗口，使其更具争议性，从而增加参与度，但也增加了内容毒性。我们识别并研究了导致 ICRH 的两个过程：输出优化和策略优化。对于这些过程而言，基于静态数据集的评估是不足的——它们遗漏了反馈效应，因此无法捕捉最具危害性的行为。为此，我们提出了三项评估建议，以捕捉更多 ICRH 实例。随着人工智能发展加速，反馈循环的影响将愈发普遍，这增加了对理解其在塑造 LLM 行为中作用的需求。

[No.350] Global Reinforcement Learning : Beyond Linear and Convex Rewards via Submodular Semi-gradient Methods
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 全球强化学习：通过子模态半梯度方法超越线性和凸奖励
类别: 
作者: Riccardo De Santi;Manish Prajapat;Andreas Krause;
机构: ETH Zurich;Swiss Federal Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/35205
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09905
英摘: In classic Reinforcement Learning (RL), the agent maximizes an additive objective of the visited states, e.g., a value function. Unfortunately, objectives of this type cannot model many real-world applications such as experiment design, exploration, imitation learning, and risk-averse RL to name a few. This is due to the fact that additive objectives disregard interactions between states that are crucial for certain tasks. To tackle this problem, we introduce Global RL (GRL), where rewards are globally defined over trajectories instead of locally over states. Global rewards can capture negative interactions among states, e.g., in exploration, via submodularity, positive interactions, e.g., synergetic effects, via supermodularity, while mixed interactions via combinations of them. By exploiting ideas from submodular optimization, we propose a novel algorithmic scheme that converts any GRL problem to a sequence of classic RL problems and solves it efficiently with curvature-dependent approximation guarantees. We also provide hardness of approximation results and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on several GRL instances.
中摘: 

在经典强化学习（RL）中，智能体旨在最大化访问状态的可加目标函数，例如价值函数。遗憾的是，此类目标函数无法对许多现实世界的应用进行建模，诸如实验设计、探索、模仿学习及风险厌恶强化学习等。其根本原因在于，可加目标函数忽略了对于某些任务至关重要的状态间交互。为解决这一问题，我们引入了全局强化学习（Global RL, GRL），其中奖励是在轨迹层面进行全局定义，而非状态层面的局部定义。全局奖励能够利用子模性捕捉状态间的负面交互（如在探索中），利用超模性捕捉正交互（如协同效应），并通过两者的组合捕捉混合交互。通过利用子模优化的思想，我们提出了一种新颖的算法方案，可将任意 GRL 问题转化为一系列经典 RL 问题，并在具备曲率相关近似保证的情况下高效求解。此外，我们还提供了关于近似难度的理论结果，并通过实验验证了该方法在多个 GRL 实例上的有效性。

[No.351] Improving Prototypical Visual Explanations with Reward Reweighing, Reselection, and Retraining
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 通过奖励重新加权、重新选择和重新训练来改进原型视觉解释
类别: 
作者: Aaron Jiaxun Li;Robin Netzorg;Zhihan Cheng;Zhuoqin Zhang;Bin Yu;
机构: Harvard University;University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34227
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.03887
英摘: In recent years, work has gone into developing deep interpretable methods for image classification that clearly attributes a model's output to specific features of the data. One such of these methods is the Prototypical Part Network (ProtoPNet), which attempts to classify images based on meaningful parts of the input. While this architecture is able to produce visually interpretable classifications, it often learns to classify based on parts of the image that are not semantically meaningful. To address this problem, we propose the Reward Reweighing, Reselecting, and Retraining (R3) post-processing framework, which performs three additional corrective updates to a pretrained ProtoPNet in an offline and efficient manner. The first two steps involve learning a reward model based on collected human feedback and then aligning the prototypes with human preferences. The final step is retraining, which realigns the base features and the classifier layer of the original model with the updated prototypes. We find that our R3 framework consistently improves both the interpretability and the predictive accuracy of ProtoPNet and its variants.
中摘: 

近年来，研究工作致力于开发用于图像分类的深度可解释方法，这些方法能够明确地将模型输出归因于数据的特定特征。此类方法中的一种是原型部件网络（ProtoPNet），它尝试基于输入中有意义的部分对图像进行分类。尽管该架构能够生成视觉上可解释的分类结果，但它往往学习基于图像中缺乏语义意义的部分进行分类。为解决这一问题，我们提出了奖励重加权、重新选择和重训练（R3）后处理框架，该框架以离线且高效的方式对预训练的 ProtoPNet 执行三次额外的校正更新。前两步涉及基于收集的人类反馈学习奖励模型，随后使原型与人类偏好对齐。最后一步为重训练，其作用是将原始模型的基础特征和分类器层与更新后的原型重新对齐。我们发现，R3 框架持续提升了 ProtoPNet 及其变体的可解释性和预测准确率。

[No.352] Iterative Data Smoothing: Mitigating Reward Overfitting and Overoptimization in RLHF
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 迭代数据平滑：减轻RLHF中的奖励过拟合和过度优化问题
类别: 
作者: Banghua Zhu;Michael Jordan;Jiantao Jiao;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33860
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.16335
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is a pivotal technique that aligns language models closely with human-centric values. The initial phase of RLHF involves learning human values using a reward model from ranking data. It is observed that the performance of the reward model degrades after one epoch of training, and optimizing too much against the learned reward model eventually hinders the true objective. This paper delves into these issues, leveraging the theoretical insights to design improved reward learning algorithm termed 'Iterative Data Smoothing' (IDS). The core idea is that during each training epoch, we not only update the model with the data, but also update the date using the model, replacing hard labels with soft labels. Our empirical findings highlight the superior performance of this approach over the traditional methods.
中摘: 

人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）是一项关键技术，能够将语言模型与以人为中心的价值观紧密对齐。RLHF 的初始阶段涉及利用奖励模型从排名数据中学习人类价值观。研究发现，奖励模型的性能在训练一轮后会下降，且对学习到的奖励模型进行过度过度优化最终会阻碍真正的目标。本文深入探讨了这些问题，利用理论见解设计了一种改进的奖励学习算法，称为“迭代数据平滑”（IDS）。其核心思想是，在每个训练轮次中，我们不仅用数据更新模型，还用模型更新数据，用软标签替换硬标签。我们的实证结果突显了该方法相较于传统方法的优越性能。

[No.353] Learning a Diffusion Model Policy from Rewards via Q-Score Matching
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 通过Q-分数匹配从奖励学习扩散模型策略
类别: 
作者: Michael Psenka;Alejandro Escontrela;Pieter Abbeel;Yi Ma;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;Covariant;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/35083
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.11752
英摘: Diffusion models have become a popular choice for representing actor policies in behavior cloning and offline reinforcement learning. This is due to their natural ability to optimize an expressive class of distributions over a continuous space. However, previous works fail to exploit the score-based structure of diffusion models, and instead utilize a simple behavior cloning term to train the actor, limiting their ability in the actor-critic setting. In this paper, we present a theoretical framework linking the structure of diffusion model policies to a learned Q-function, by linking the structure between the score of the policy to the action gradient of the Q-function. We focus on off-policy reinforcement learning and propose a new policy update method from this theory, which we denote Q-score matching. Notably, this algorithm only needs to differentiate through the denoising model rather than the entire diffusion model evaluation, and converged policies through Q-score matching are implicitly multi-modal and explorative in continuous domains. We conduct experiments in simulated environments to demonstrate the viability of our proposed method and compare to popular baselines. Source code is available from the project website: https://michaelpsenka.io/qsm.
中摘: 

扩散模型已成为行为克隆和离线强化学习中表示 Actor 策略的流行选择。这得益于它们具有在连续空间上优化一类具有高度表达能力的分布的天然能力。然而，以往工作未能利用扩散模型的基于得分的结构，而是利用简单的行为克隆项来训练 Actor，限制了它们在 Actor-Critic 设置中的能力。在本文中，我们提出了一个理论框架，通过将策略的得分结构与 Q 函数的动作梯度联系起来，将扩散模型策略的结构与学到的 Q 函数联系起来。我们专注于离策略强化学习，并基于该理论提出了一种新的策略更新方法，称之为 Q 分数匹配（Q-score matching）。值得注意的是，该算法只需对去噪模型求导，而无需对整个扩散模型评估过程求导，且通过 Q 分数匹配收敛的策略在连续域中隐式具有多模态性和探索性。我们在模拟环境中进行了实验，以证明所提出方法的可行性，并与流行基线方法进行了比较。源代码可从项目网站获取：https://michaelpsenka.io/qsm。

[No.354] Learning Reward for Robot Skills Using Large Language Models via Self-Alignment
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 利用大型语言模型通过自我对齐学习机器人技能的奖励方法

英摘: Learning reward functions remains the bottleneck to equip a robot with a broad repertoire of skills. We proposed a method to learn rewards more efficiently in the absence of humans. [No.356] Listwise Reward Estimation for Offline Preference-based Reinforcement Learning
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于离线偏好强化学习的列表级奖励估计
类别: 
作者: Heewoong Choi;Sangwon Jung;Hongjoon Ahn;Taesup Moon;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.04190
英摘: In Reinforcement Learning (RL), designing precise reward functions remains to be a challenge, particularly when aligning with human intent. Preference-based RL (PbRL) was introduced to address this problem by learning reward models from human feedback. However, existing PbRL methods have limitations as they often overlook the second-order preference that indicates the relative strength of preference. In this paper, we propose Listwise Reward Estimation (LiRE), a novel approach for offline PbRL that leverages second-order preference information by constructing a Ranked List of Trajectories (RLT), which can be efficiently built by using the same ternary feedback type as traditional methods. To validate the effectiveness of LiRE, we propose a new offline PbRL dataset that objectively reflects the effect of the estimated rewards. Our extensive experiments on the dataset demonstrate the superiority of LiRE, i.e., outperforming state-of-the-art baselines even with modest feedback budgets and enjoying robustness with respect to the number of feedbacks and feedback noise. Our code is available at https://github.com/chwoong/LiRE
中摘: 

在强化学习（RL）中，设计精确的奖励函数仍然是一项挑战，尤其是在与人类意图对齐方面。基于偏好的强化学习（PbRL）被引入用于通过从人类反馈中学习奖励模型来解决这一问题。然而，现有的 PbRL 方法存在局限性，因为它们往往忽略了指示偏好相对强度的二阶偏好。在本文中，我们提出了列表级奖励估计（LiRE），这是一种用于离线 PbRL 的新方法，它通过构建轨迹排序列表（RLT）来利用二阶偏好信息，且该列表可以利用与传统方法相同的三元反馈类型高效构建。为验证 LiRE 的有效性，我们提出了一个新的离线 PbRL 数据集，该数据集能够客观反映估计奖励的效果。我们在该数据集上的大量实验证明了 LiRE 的优越性，即即便在反馈预算有限的情况下仍能优于最先进基线，并且在反馈数量和反馈噪声方面具有鲁棒性。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/chwoong/LiRE 获取。

[No.358] ODIN: Disentangled Reward Mitigates Hacking in RLHF
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: ODIN：分离奖励减轻RLHF中的黑客攻击
类别: 
作者: Lichang Chen;Chen Zhu;Jiuhai Chen;Davit Soselia;Tianyi Zhou;Tom Goldstein;Heng Huang;Mohammad Shoeybi;Bryan Catanzaro;
机构: NVIDIA;University of Maryland;University of Maryland, College Park;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32624
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.07319
英摘: In this work, we study the issue of reward hacking on the response length, a challenge emerging in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) on LLMs. A well-formatted, verbose but less helpful response from the LLMs can often deceive LLMs or even human evaluators to achieve high scores. The same issue also holds for some reward models in RL. To address the challenges in both training and evaluation, we establish a more reliable evaluation protocol for comparing different training configurations, which inspects the trade-off between LLM evaluation score and response length obtained by varying training hyperparameters. Based on this evaluation, we conduct large-scale studies, where the results shed insights into the efficacy of hyperparameters and tricks used in RL on mitigating length bias. We further propose to improve the reward model by jointly training two linear heads on shared feature representations to predict the rewards, one trained to correlate with length, and the other trained to decorrelate with length and therefore focus more on the actual content. We then discard the length head in RL to prevent reward hacking on length. Experiments demonstrate that our approach almost eliminates the reward correlation with length, and improves the obtained policy by a significant margin.
中摘: 

在这项工作中，我们研究了大语言模型（LLM）人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）中出现的响应长度奖励操纵问题，这是一个新兴的挑战。来自大语言模型的格式良好、冗长但实用性较低的回复，往往能够欺骗大语言模型甚至人类评估者，从而获得高分。强化学习（RL）中的某些奖励模型也存在同样的问题。为了解决训练和评估方面的挑战，我们建立了一种更可靠的评估协议来比较不同的训练配置，该协议通过调整训练超参数，考察所获得的 LLM 评估分数与响应长度之间的权衡。基于此评估，我们开展了大规模研究，结果为 RL 中使用的超参数和技巧在缓解长度偏差方面的有效性提供了见解。我们进一步提出改进奖励模型的方法，即在共享特征表示上联合训练两个线性头来预测奖励，其中一个头训练为与长度相关，另一个头训练为与长度解相关，从而更侧重于实际内容。随后，我们在强化学习中丢弃长度头，以防止针对长度的奖励操纵。实验表明，我们的方法几乎消除了奖励与长度的相关性，并显著提升了所得策略的性能。

[No.360] PEARL: Zero-shot Cross-task Preference Alignment and Robust Reward Learning for Robotic Manipulation
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 珍珠：零样本跨任务偏好对齐与鲁棒奖励学习在机器人操作中的应用
类别: 
作者: Runze Liu;Yali Du;Fengshuo Bai;Jiafei Lyu;Xiu Li;
机构: Shanghai Artificial Intelligence Laboratory;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/35173
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.03615
英摘: In preference-based Reinforcement Learning (RL), obtaining a large number of preference labels are both time-consuming and costly. Furthermore, the queried human preferences cannot be utilized for the new tasks. In this paper, we propose Zero-shot Cross-task Preference Alignment and Robust Reward Learning (PEARL), which learns policies from cross-task preference transfer without any human labels of the target task. Our contributions include two novel components that facilitate the transfer and learning process. The first is Cross-task Preference Alignment (CPA), which transfers the preferences between tasks via optimal transport. The key idea of CPA is to use Gromov-Wasserstein distance to align the trajectories between tasks, and the solved optimal transport matrix serves as the correspondence between trajectories. The target task preferences are computed as the weighted sum of source task preference labels with the correspondence as weights. Moreover, to ensure robust learning from these transferred labels, we introduce Robust Reward Learning (RRL), which considers both reward mean and uncertainty by modeling rewards as Gaussian distributions. Empirical results on robotic manipulation tasks from Meta-World and Robomimic demonstrate that our method is capable of transferring preference labels across tasks accurately and then learns well-behaved policies. Notably, our approach significantly exceeds existing methods when there are few human preferences. The code and videos of our method are available at: https://sites.google.com/view/pearl-preference.
中摘: 

在基于偏好的强化学习（RL）中，获取大量偏好标签既耗时又昂贵。此外，查询到的人类偏好无法直接应用于新任务。在本文中，我们提出了零样本跨任务偏好对齐与鲁棒奖励学习（PEARL），该方法无需目标任务的任何人类标签，即可通过学习跨任务偏好转移来制定策略。我们的贡献包括两个促进转移和学习过程的新组件。第一个是跨任务偏好对齐（CPA），它通过最优传输在任务间传递偏好。CPA 的核心思想是利用 Gromov-Wasserstein 距离对齐任务间的轨迹，求解得到的最优传输矩阵则作为轨迹间的对应关系。目标任务的偏好被计算为源任务偏好标签的加权和，其中对应关系作为权重。此外，为确保能从这些转移标签中进行鲁棒学习，我们引入了鲁棒奖励学习（RRL），该方法通过将奖励建模为高斯分布，同时考虑奖励的均值和不确定性。在来自 Meta-World 和 Robomimic 的机器人操作任务上的实证结果表明，我们的方法能够准确地跨任务转移偏好标签，进而学习到表现良好的策略。值得注意的是，在人类偏好数据较少的情况下，我们的方法显著优于现有方法。我们方法的代码和视频可在以下网址获取：https://sites.google.com/view/pearl-preference.

[No.362] Policy Learning for Balancing Short-Term and Long-Term Rewards
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 短期和长期奖励平衡的学习策略
类别: 
作者: Peng Wu;Ziyu Shen;Feng Xie;Wang Zhongyao;Chunchen LIU;Yan Zeng;
机构: Beijing Technology and Business University;Alibaba Group;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34884
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03329
英摘: Empirical researchers and decision-makers spanning various domains frequently seek profound insights into the long-term impacts of interventions. While the significance of long-term outcomes is undeniable, an overemphasis on them may inadvertently overshadow short-term gains. Motivated by this, this paper formalizes a new framework for learning the optimal policy that effectively balances both long-term and short-term rewards, where some long-term outcomes are allowed to be missing. In particular, we first present the identifiability of both rewards under mild assumptions. Next, we deduce the semiparametric efficiency bounds, along with the consistency and asymptotic normality of their estimators. We also reveal that short-term outcomes, if associated, contribute to improving the estimator of the long-term reward. 中摘:

[No.363] Pragmatic Feature Preferences: Learning Reward-Relevant Preferences from Human Input
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 实用特征偏好：从人类输入中学习与奖励相关的需求
类别: 
作者: Andi Peng;Yuying Sun;Tianmin Shu;David Abel;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Johns Hopkins University;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34151
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14769
英摘: Humans use social context to specify preferences over behaviors, i.e. their reward functions. Yet, algorithms for inferring reward models from preference data do not take this social learning view into account. Inspired by pragmatic human communication, we study how to extract fine-grained data regarding why an example is preferred that is useful for learning more accurate reward models. We propose to enrich binary preference queries to ask both (1) which features of a given example are preferable in addition to (2) comparisons between examples themselves. We derive an approach for learning from these feature-level preferences, both for cases where users specify which features are reward-relevant, and when users do not. We evaluate our approach on linear bandit settings in both vision- and language-based domains. Results support the efficiency of our approach in quickly converging to accurate rewards with fewer comparisons vs. example-only labels. Finally, we validate the real-world applicability with a behavioral experiment on a mushroom foraging task. Our findings suggest that incorporating pragmatic feature preferences is a promising approach for more efficient user-aligned reward learning.
中摘: 

人类利用社会语境来指定对行为的偏好，即其奖励函数。然而，从偏好数据中推断奖励模型的算法并未考虑这种社会学习视角。受语用学人类沟通的启发，我们研究如何提取关于“为何某个示例更受偏好”的细粒度数据，这些数据有助于学习更准确的奖励模型。我们提出丰富二元偏好查询，要求同时询问（1）给定示例中哪些特征更可取，以及（2）示例本身之间的比较。我们提出了一种从这些特征级偏好中进行学习的方法，涵盖了用户指定哪些特征与奖励相关以及未指定的两种情况。我们在视觉和语言领域的线性 Bandit 设置上评估了我们的方法。结果支持了我们方法的高效性：相较于仅使用示例标签，该方法能以更少的比较次数快速收敛到准确的奖励。最后，我们通过一项关于蘑菇觅食任务的行为实验，验证了该方法的现实适用性。我们的研究结果表明，纳入语用特征偏好是一种有望实现更高效用户对齐奖励学习的方法。

[No.364] Q-Probe: A Lightweight Approach to Reward Maximization for Language Models
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: Q探针：一种轻量级的语言模型奖励最大化方法
类别: 
作者: Kenneth Li;Samy Jelassi;Hugh Zhang;Sham M. Kakade;Martin Wattenberg;David Brandfonbrener;
机构: Harvard University;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33407
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14688
英摘: We present an approach called Q-probing to adapt a pre-trained language model to maximize a task-specific reward function. At a high level, Q-probing sits between heavier approaches such as finetuning and lighter approaches such as few shot prompting, but can also be combined with either. The idea is to learn a simple linear function on a model's embedding space that can be used to reweight candidate completions. We theoretically show that this sampling procedure is equivalent to a KL-constrained maximization of the Q-probe as the number of samples increases. To train the Q-probes we consider either reward modeling or a class of novel direct policy learning objectives based on importance weighted policy gradients. With this technique, we see gains in domains with ground-truth rewards (code generation) as well as implicit rewards defined by preference data, even outperforming finetuning in data-limited regimes. Moreover, a Q-probe can be trained on top of an API since it only assumes access to sampling and embeddings. Code: https://github.com/likenneth/q_probe .
中摘: 

我们提出了一种名为 Q-probing 的方法，旨在调整预训练语言模型以最大化特定任务奖励函数。从高层面上看，Q-probing 介于微调等重型方法与少样本提示等轻量级方法之间，但也可以与其中任一种结合使用。其核心思想是在模型的嵌入空间中学习一个简单的线性函数，用于对候选补全进行重加权。我们在理论上证明，随着样本数量的增加，该采样过程等价于对 Q-probe 的 KL 约束最大化。为了训练 Q-probe，我们考虑使用奖励建模，或一类基于重要性加权策略梯度的新型直接策略学习目标。通过该技术，我们在具有真实奖励（如代码生成）的领域以及由偏好数据定义的隐式奖励领域均取得了提升，即使在数据受限的场景下，性能也能优于微调方法。此外，Q-probe 可基于 API 进行训练，因为它仅需访问采样和嵌入功能。代码：https://github.com/likenneth/q_probe

[No.365] Reward Model Learning vs. Direct Policy Optimization: A Comparative Analysis of Learning from Human Preferences
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 奖励模型学习 vs. 直接策略优化：基于人类偏好学习的比较分析
类别: 
作者: Andi Nika;Debmalya Mandal;Parameswaran Kamalaruban;Georgios Tzannetos;Goran Radanovic;Adish Singla;
机构: Max Planck Institute for Software Systems;University of Warwick;Featurespace Ltd.;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34376
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01857
英摘: In this paper, we take a step towards a deeper understanding of learning from human preferences by systematically comparing the paradigm of reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) with the recently proposed paradigm of direct preference optimization (DPO). We focus our attention on the class of loglinear policy parametrization and linear reward functions. In order to compare the two paradigms, we first derive minimax statistical bounds on the suboptimality gap induced by both RLHF and DPO, assuming access to an oracle that exactly solves the optimization problems. We provide a detailed discussion on the relative comparison between the two paradigms, simultaneously taking into account the sample size, policy and reward class dimensions, and the regularization temperature. Moreover, we extend our analysis to the approximate optimization setting and derive exponentially decaying convergence rates for both RLHF and DPO. Next, we analyze the setting where the ground-truth reward is not realizable and find that, while RLHF incurs a constant additional error, DPO retains its asymptotically decaying gap by just tuning the temperature accordingly. Finally, we extend our comparison to the Markov decision process setting, where we generalize our results with exact optimization. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to provide such a comparative analysis for RLHF and DPO.
中摘: 

在本文中，我们通过系统性地比较强化学习从人类反馈（RLHF）范式与最近提出的直接偏好优化（DPO）范式，为更深入地理解从人类偏好学习迈出了一步。我们关注对数线性策略参数化和线性奖励函数这一类别。为了比较这两种范式，我们首先假设存在能够精确求解优化问题的理想求解器（oracle），推导出了由 RLHF 和 DPO 引起的次优性差距的极小极大统计界。我们对这两种范式的相对比较进行了详细讨论，同时综合考虑了样本量、策略和奖励类的维度以及正则化温度。此外，我们将分析扩展到近似优化设定，并推导出了 RLHF 和 DPO 的指数衰减收敛率。接下来，我们分析了真实奖励不可实现的情形，发现虽然 RLHF 会产生常数级的额外误差，但 DPO 只需相应调整温度，即可保持其渐近衰减的差距。最后，我们将比较扩展到马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）设定，在此我们推广了精确优化下的结果。据我们所知，我们率先提供了 RLHF 和 DPO 的此类比较分析。

来源: ICML 2024

标题: 无奖励强化学习中的核方法
类别:

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07715
英摘: Reinforcement Learning (RL) problems are being considered under increasingly more complex structures. While tabular and linear models have been thoroughly explored, the analytical study of RL under nonlinear function approximation, especially kernel-based models, has recently gained traction for their strong representational capacity and theoretical tractability. In this context, we examine the question of statistical efficiency in kernel-based RL within the reward-free RL framework, specifically asking: how many samples are required to design a near-optimal policy? Existing work addresses this question under restrictive assumptions about the class of kernel functions. We first explore this question by assuming a generative model, then relax this assumption at the cost of increasing the sample complexity by a factor of H, the length of the episode. Our approach derives new confidence intervals for kernel ridge regression, specific to our RL setting, which may be of broader applicability. We further validate our theoretical findings through simulations.

英摘: We consider the problem of multi-objective alignment of foundation models with human preferences, which is a critical step towards helpful and harmless AI systems. 来源: ICML 2024

英摘: In this paper, we propose an off-policy deep reinforcement learning (DRL) method utilizing the average reward criterion. [No.372] Stealthy Imitation: Reward-guided Environment-free Policy Stealing
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 隐形模仿：基于奖励的无环境策略窃取
类别: 
作者: Zhixiong Zhuang;Maria-Irina Nicolae;Mario Fritz;
机构: Robert Bosch GmbH;Saarland University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34479
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.07004
英摘: Deep reinforcement learning policies, which are integral to modern control systems, represent valuable intellectual property. The development of these policies demands considerable resources, such as domain expertise, simulation fidelity, and real-world validation. These policies are potentially vulnerable to model stealing attacks, which aim to replicate their functionality using only black-box access. In this paper, we propose Stealthy Imitation, the first attack designed to steal policies without access to the environment or knowledge of the input range. This setup has not been considered by previous model stealing methods. Lacking access to the victim's input states distribution, Stealthy Imitation fits a reward model that allows to approximate it. We show that the victim policy is harder to imitate when the distribution of the attack queries matches that of the victim. We evaluate our approach across diverse, high-dimensional control tasks and consistently outperform prior data-free approaches adapted for policy stealing. Lastly, we propose a countermeasure that significantly diminishes the effectiveness of the attack.
中摘: 

深度强化学习策略是现代控制系统不可或缺的核心组成部分，代表着宝贵的知识产权。开发这些策略需要消耗大量资源，包括领域专业知识、仿真保真度以及现实世界验证。这些策略可能易受模型窃取攻击的威胁，此类攻击旨在仅通过黑盒访问复制其功能。在本文中，我们提出了“隐蔽模仿”（Stealthy Imitation），这是首个旨在无需访问环境或知晓输入范围的情况下窃取策略的攻击方法。之前的模型窃取方法尚未考虑过这一场景。由于无法获取受害者的输入状态分布，“隐蔽模仿”通过拟合一个奖励模型来近似该分布。我们发现，当攻击查询的分布与受害者的分布相匹配时，受害策略更难以被模仿。我们在多样化的、高维控制任务上评估了该方法，并始终优于先前适配于策略窃取的无数据方法。最后，我们提出了一种对策，显著削弱了攻击的有效性。

[No.373] To the Max: Reinventing Reward in Reinforcement Learning
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 最大化奖励：重定义强化学习中的奖励机制
类别:

英摘: In reinforcement learning (RL), different reward functions can define the same optimal policy but result in drastically different learning performance. For some, the agent gets stuck with a suboptimal behavior, and for others, it solves the task efficiently. Choosing a good reward function is hence an extremely important yet challenging problem. In this paper, we explore an alternative approach for using rewards for learning. We introduce \textit{max-reward RL}, where an agent optimizes the maximum rather than the cumulative reward. Unlike earlier works, our approach works for deterministic and stochastic environments and can be easily combined with state-of-the-art RL algorithms. 在强化学习（RL）中，不同的奖励函数可以定义相同的最优策略，但会导致截然不同的学习性能。在某些情况下，智能体会陷入次优行为，而在另一些情况下，它能高效地完成任务。因此，选择合适的奖励函数是一个极其重要但具有挑战性的问题。在本文中，我们探索了一种利用奖励进行学习的替代方法。我们提出了“最大奖励强化学习”（max-reward RL），其中智能体优化的是最大奖励而非累积奖励。与早期工作不同，我们的方法适用于确定性和随机环境，并且可以轻松地与最先进的 RL 算法相结合。在实验中，我们研究了该算法在 Gymnasium-Robotics 中的两个目标到达环境下的性能，并展示了其相对于标准 RL 的优势。代码可在 https://github.com/veviurko/To-the-Max 获取。

[No.374] Towards Global Optimality for Practical Average Reward Reinforcement Learning without Mixing Time Oracles
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 无需混合时间或acles下的实用平均奖励强化学习向全局最优的逼近
类别: 
作者: Bhrij Patel;Wesley A Suttle;Alec Koppel;Vaneet Aggarwal;Brian M. Sadler;Dinesh Manocha;Amrit Bedi;
机构: University of Maryland;JPMorgan Chase & Co.;Purdue University;US Army Research Laboratory;Army Research Laboratory;
