arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11925
英摘: In the context of average-reward reinforcement learning, the requirement for oracle knowledge of the mixing time, a measure of the duration a Markov chain under a fixed policy needs to achieve its stationary distribution, poses a significant challenge for the global convergence of policy gradient methods. This requirement is particularly problematic due to the difficulty and expense of estimating mixing time in environments with large state spaces, leading to the necessity of impractically long trajectories for effective gradient estimation in practical applications. To address this limitation, we consider the Multi-level Actor-Critic (MAC) framework, which incorporates a Multi-level Monte-Carlo (MLMC) gradient estimator. With our approach, we effectively alleviate the dependency on mixing time knowledge, a first for average-reward MDPs global convergence. Furthermore, our approach exhibits the tightest available dependence of $\mathcal{O}\left( \sqrt{τ_{mix}} \right)$known from prior work. With a 2D grid world goal-reaching navigation experiment, we demonstrate that MAC outperforms the existing state-of-the-art policy gradient-based method for average reward settings.
中摘: 

在平均奖励强化学习的背景下，对混合时间（衡量固定策略下马尔可夫链达到平稳分布所需持续时间的度量）的先验知识要求，给策略梯度方法的全局收敛带来了显著挑战。由于在大状态空间环境中估算混合时间既困难又昂贵，这一要求尤为成问题，导致在实际应用中，为了进行有效的梯度估计，必须使用不切实际的长轨迹。为解决这一局限性，我们采用了多级演员 - 评论家（MAC）框架，该框架集成了多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）梯度估计器。通过我们的方法，我们有效降低了对混合时间知识的依赖，这是平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）全局收敛方面的首次实现。此外，我们的方法展现了从既往工作中已知的最紧依赖关系，即 $\mathcal{O}\left( \sqrt{τ_{mix}} \right)$。通过一项二维网格世界目标达成导航实验，我们证明了 MAC 优于现有的适用于平均奖励设置的最先进基于策略梯度的方法。

[No.375] Transforming and Combining Rewards for Aligning Large Language Models
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 调整和结合奖励以使大型语言模型对齐
类别: 
作者: Zihao Wang;Chirag Nagpal;Jonathan Berant;Jacob Eisenstein;Alexander Nicholas D'Amour;Sanmi Koyejo;Victor Veitch;
机构: University of Chicago;Google;Tel Aviv University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33602
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00742
英摘: A common approach for aligning language models to human preferences is to first learn a reward model from preference data, and then use this reward model to update the language model. We study two closely related problems that arise in this approach. First, any monotone transformation of the reward model preserves preference ranking; is there a choice that is ``better'' than others? Second, we often wish to align language models to multiple properties: how should we combine multiple reward models? Using a probabilistic interpretation of the alignment procedure, we identify a natural choice for transformation for (the common case of) rewards learned from Bradley-Terry preference models. The derived transformation is straightforward: we apply a log-sigmoid function to the centered rewards, a method we term ``LSC-transformation'' (log-sigmoid-centered transformation). This transformation has two important properties. First, it emphasizes improving poorly-performing outputs, rather than outputs that already score well. This mitigates both underfitting (where some prompts are not improved) and reward hacking (where the model learns to exploit misspecification of the reward model). Second, it enables principled aggregation of rewards by linking summation to logical conjunction: the sum of transformed rewards corresponds to the probability that the output is ``good'' in all measured properties, in a sense we make precise. Experiments aligning language models to be both helpful and harmless using RLHF show substantial improvements over the baseline (non-transformed) approach.
中摘: 

将语言模型与人类偏好对齐的一种常见方法是，首先从偏好数据中学习奖励模型，随后使用该奖励模型来更新语言模型。我们研究了该方法中出现的两个密切相关的问题。首先，奖励模型的任意单调变换都会保留偏好排序；是否存在一个比其他选择“更好”的选择？其次，我们经常希望将语言模型与多个属性对齐：我们应如何组合多个奖励模型？通过采用对齐过程的一种概率解释，我们确定了从 Bradley-Terry 偏好模型学习奖励这一常见情况下的变换的自然选择。推导出的变换非常直接：我们对中心化奖励应用对数 - 西格摩德（log-sigmoid）函数，我们将这种方法称为"LSC 变换”（对数 - 西格摩德中心变换）。该变换具有两个重要特性。首先，它强调改进表现不佳的输出，而不是已经得分较高的输出。这既缓解了欠拟合（即某些提示得不到改进），也减轻了奖励滥用（即模型学会利用奖励模型的错误设定）。其次，它通过将求和与逻辑合取联系起来，实现了有原则的奖励聚合：变换后奖励的总和对应于输出在所有测量属性上均为“好”的概率，我们在文中对此给出了精确定义。使用 RLHF 将语言模型对齐为既有用又无害的实验表明，相比基线（未经变换）方法取得了显著改进。

[No.376] Uncertainty-Aware Reward-Free Exploration with General Function Approximation
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于不确定性意识的一般函数逼近无奖励探索
类别: 
作者: Junkai Zhang;Weitong Zhang;Dongruo Zhou;Quanquan Gu;
机构: Indiana University;University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34706
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16255
英摘: Mastering multiple tasks through exploration and learning in an environment poses a significant challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Unsupervised RL has been introduced to address this challenge by training policies with intrinsic rewards rather than extrinsic rewards. However, current intrinsic reward designs and unsupervised RL algorithms often overlook the heterogeneous nature of collected samples, thereby diminishing their sample efficiency. To overcome this limitation, in this paper, we propose a reward-free RL algorithm called \alg. The key idea behind our algorithm is an uncertainty-aware intrinsic reward for exploring the environment and an uncertainty-weighted learning process to handle heterogeneous uncertainty in different samples. Theoretically, we show that in order to find an $ε$-optimal policy, GFA-RFE needs to collect $\tilde{O} (H^2 \log N_{\mathcal F} (ε) \mathrm{dim} (\mathcal F) / ε^2 )$ number of episodes, where $\mathcal F$ is the value function class with covering number $N_{\mathcal F} (ε)$ and generalized eluder dimension $\mathrm{dim} (\mathcal F)$. Such a result outperforms all existing reward-free RL algorithms. We further implement and evaluate GFA-RFE across various domains and tasks in the DeepMind Control Suite. Experiment results show that GFA-RFE outperforms or is comparable to the performance of state-of-the-art unsupervised RL algorithms.
中摘: 

在强化学习（RL）中，通过在环境中进行探索和学习来掌握多个任务构成了一个重大挑战。无监督强化学习（RL）已被引入以解决这一挑战，即通过内在奖励而非外在奖励来训练策略。然而，当前的内在奖励设计和无监督强化学习算法往往忽略了所收集样本的异质性，从而降低了其样本效率。为了克服这一限制，本文提出了一种名为 GFA-RFE 的免奖励强化学习算法。我们算法的核心理念包括一种用于探索环境的不确定性感知内在奖励，以及一种用于处理不同样本中异质性不确定性的不确定性加权学习过程。理论上，我们证明为了找到一个 $ε$-最优策略，GFA-RFE 需要收集 $\tilde{O} (H^2 \log N_{\mathcal F} (ε) \mathrm{dim} (\mathcal F) / ε^2 )$ 数量的回合，其中 $\mathcal F$ 是值函数类，具有覆盖数 $N_{\mathcal F} (ε)$ 和广义 Eluder 维度 $\mathrm{dim} (\mathcal F)$。该结果优于所有现有的免奖励强化学习算法。我们进一步在 DeepMind 控制套件中的各种领域和任务上实现并评估了 GFA-RFE。实验结果表明，GFA-RFE 的性能优于或与最先进的无监督强化学习算法相当。

[No.377] Unsupervised Zero-Shot Reinforcement Learning via Functional Reward Encodings
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 无监督零样本强化学习通过功能奖励编码
类别:

链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33701

英摘: Can we pre-train a generalist agent from a large amount of unlabeled offline trajectories such that it can be immediately adapted to any new downstream tasks in a zero-shot manner? In this work, we present a functional reward encoding (FRE) as a general, scalable solution to this zero-shot RL problem. Our main idea is to learn functional representations of any arbitrary tasks by encoding their state-reward samples using a transformer-based variational auto-encoder. This functional encoding not only enables the pre-training of an agent from a wide diversity of general unsupervised reward functions, but also provides a way to solve any new downstream tasks in a zero-shot manner, given a small number of reward-annotated samples. 我们能否从大量无标签离线轨迹中预训练一个通用智能体，使其能够以零样本方式立即适应任何新的下游任务？在本工作中，我们提出了一种函数奖励编码（FRE），将其作为解决这一零样本强化学习问题的通用且可扩展的方案。我们的主要思想是利用基于 Transformer 的变分自编码器，通过编码状态 - 奖励样本来学习任意任务的功能表示。这种功能编码不仅能够实现从广泛多样的通用无监督奖励函数中对智能体进行预训练，而且在给定少量奖励标注样本的情况下，提供了一种以零样本方式解决任何新下游任务的途径。我们通过实证表明，在不同随机无监督奖励函数上训练得到的 FRE 智能体，能够泛化解决一系列模拟机器人基准测试中的新任务，通常优于之前的零样本强化学习和离线强化学习方法。本项目的代码提供如下：https://github.com/kvfrans/fre

[No.378] WARM: On the Benefits of Weight Averaged Reward Models
来源: ICML 2024
命中: reward
标题: 温控：关于加权平均奖励模型的优势
类别: 
作者: Alexandre Rame;Nino Vieillard;Leonard Hussenot;Robert Dadashi;Geoffrey Cideron;Olivier Bachem;Johan Ferret;
机构: Google;DeepMind;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32924
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12187
英摘: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences through reinforcement learning (RLHF) can lead to reward hacking, where LLMs exploit failures in the reward model (RM) to achieve seemingly high rewards without meeting the underlying objectives. We identify two primary challenges when designing RMs to mitigate reward hacking: distribution shifts during the RL process and inconsistencies in human preferences. As a solution, we propose Weight Averaged Reward Models (WARM), first fine-tuning multiple RMs, then averaging them in the weight space. This strategy follows the observation that fine-tuned weights remain linearly mode connected when sharing the same pre-training. By averaging weights, WARM improves efficiency compared to the traditional ensembling of predictions, while improving reliability under distribution shifts and robustness to preference inconsistencies. Our experiments on summarization tasks, using best-of-N and RL methods, shows that WARM improves the overall quality and alignment of LLM predictions; for example, a policy RL fine-tuned with WARM has a 79.4% win rate against a policy RL fine-tuned with a single RM.
中摘: 

通过强化学习（RLHF）将大语言模型（LLM）与人类偏好对齐可能会导致奖励黑客行为，即大语言模型利用奖励模型（RM）的缺陷来实现看似很高的奖励，却未能满足根本目标。我们在设计奖励模型（RM）以缓解奖励黑客行为时，识别出两个主要挑战：强化学习过程中的分布偏移以及人类偏好的不一致性。作为解决方案，我们提出了加权平均奖励模型（WARM），即先微调多个奖励模型，然后在权重空间中对它们进行平均。这一策略基于这样的观察：当共享相同的预训练时，微调后的权重仍然保持线性模式连通。通过平均权重，WARM 相较于传统的预测集成方法提高了效率，同时在分布偏移下提升了可靠性，并增强了对偏好不一致性的鲁棒性。我们在摘要任务上进行的实验（采用 N 选最佳和强化学习方法）表明，WARM 提高了大语言模型预测的整体质量和对齐效果；例如，使用 WARM 微调的强化学习策略对抗使用单个奖励模型微调的强化学习策略时，胜率达到 79.4%。

[No.380] Bayesian Reparameterization of Reward-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning with Energy-based Models
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 基于能量模型的奖励条件强化学习的贝叶斯重参数化
类别: 
作者: Wenhao Ding;Tong Che;Ding Zhao;Marco Pavone;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;NVIDIA;Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24590
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.11340
英摘: Recently, reward-conditioned reinforcement learning (RCRL) has gained popularity due to its simplicity, flexibility, and off-policy nature. However, we will show that current RCRL approaches are fundamentally limited and fail to address two critical challenges of RCRL -- improving generalization on high reward-to-go (RTG) inputs, and avoiding out-of-distribution (OOD) RTG queries during testing time. To address these challenges when training vanilla RCRL architectures, we propose Bayesian Reparameterized RCRL (BR-RCRL), a novel set of inductive biases for RCRL inspired by Bayes' theorem. BR-RCRL removes a core obstacle preventing vanilla RCRL from generalizing on high RTG inputs -- a tendency that the model treats different RTG inputs as independent values, which we term ``RTG Independence". BR-RCRL also allows us to design an accompanying adaptive inference method, which maximizes total returns while avoiding OOD queries that yield unpredictable behaviors in vanilla RCRL methods. We show that BR-RCRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Gym-Mujoco and Atari offline RL benchmarks, improving upon vanilla RCRL by up to 11%.
中摘: 

最近，奖励条件强化学习（RCRL）因其简单性、灵活性和离策略特性而广受欢迎。然而，我们将表明，当前的 RCRL 方法存在根本性局限，未能解决 RCRL 的两个关键挑战——提高对高剩余回报（RTG）输入的泛化能力，以及在测试期间避免分布外（OOD）的 RTG 查询。为了在训练标准 RCRL 架构时解决这些挑战，我们提出了贝叶斯重参数化 RCRL（BR-RCRL），这是一套受贝叶斯定理启发的、针对 RCRL 的新型归纳偏置。BR-RCRL 消除了一种阻碍标准 RCRL 在高剩余回报（RTG）输入上实现泛化的核心障碍——即模型倾向于将不同的 RTG 输入视为独立值，我们将这一倾向称为"RTG 独立性”。BR-RCRL 还使我们能够设计一种配套的自适应推理方法，该方法在最大化总回报的同时，避免了那些在标准 RCRL 方法中会导致不可预测行为的分布外（OOD）查询。我们表明，BR-RCRL 在 Gym-MuJoCo 和 Atari 离线强化学习基准上取得了最先进性能，相比标准 RCRL 提升了高达 11%。

[No.381] Beyond Exponentially Fast Mixing in Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Multi-Level Monte Carlo Actor-Critic
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 通过多级蒙特卡洛演员-评论家方法在平均奖励强化学习中实现超越指数级快速混合
类别: 
作者: Wesley Suttle;Amrit Bedi;Bhrij Patel;Brian M. Sadler;Alec Koppel;Dinesh Manocha;
机构: Army Research Laboratory;University of Maryland;US Army Research Laboratory;JPMorgan Chase & Co.;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.12083
英摘: Many existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods employ stochastic gradient iteration on the back end, whose stability hinges upon a hypothesis that the data-generating process mixes exponentially fast with a rate parameter that appears in the step-size selection. Unfortunately, this assumption is violated for large state spaces or settings with sparse rewards, and the mixing time is unknown, making the step size inoperable. In this work, we propose an RL methodology attuned to the mixing time by employing a multi-level Monte Carlo estimator for the critic, the actor, and the average reward embedded within an actor-critic (AC) algorithm. This method, which we call \textbf{M}ulti-level \textbf{A}ctor-\textbf{C}ritic (MAC), is developed especially for infinite-horizon average-reward settings and neither relies on oracle knowledge of the mixing time in its parameter selection nor assumes its exponential decay; it, therefore, is readily applicable to applications with slower mixing times. Nonetheless, it achieves a convergence rate comparable to the state-of-the-art AC algorithms. We experimentally show that these alleviated restrictions on the technical conditions required for stability translate to superior performance in practice for RL problems with sparse rewards.
中摘: 

许多现有的强化学习 (RL) 方法在后端采用随机梯度迭代，其稳定性依赖于一个假设：数据生成过程以指数速度快速混合，且该速率参数出现在步长选择中。不幸的是，在状态空间较大或奖励稀疏的设置中，这一假设并不成立，且混合时间未知，导致步长设置失效。在本工作中，我们提出了一种针对混合时间进行适配的 RL 方法，通过在演员 - 评论家 (AC) 算法内部，为评论家、演员及平均奖励采用多层蒙特卡洛估计器来实现。我们将该方法命名为多层演员 - 评论家 (MAC)，它专为无限时域平均奖励设置而开发，在参数选择中既不依赖混合时间的先验知识，也不假设混合时间呈指数衰减；因此，它易于应用于混合时间较长的场景。尽管如此，它实现了与最先进 AC 算法相当的收敛率。实验表明，对稳定性所需技术条件所做的这些放宽限制，在实际应用中体现为针对具有稀疏奖励的 RL 问题的优越性能。

[No.382] Beyond Reward: Offline Preference-guided Policy Optimization
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 超越奖励：离线基于偏好策略优化
类别: 
作者: Yachen Kang;Diyuan Shi;Jinxin Liu;Li He;Donglin Wang;
机构: Zhejiang University;Westlake University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24673
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.16217
英摘: This study focuses on the topic of offline preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL), a variant of conventional reinforcement learning that dispenses with the need for online interaction or specification of reward functions. Instead, the agent is provided with fixed offline trajectories and human preferences between pairs of trajectories to extract the dynamics and task information, respectively. Since the dynamics and task information are orthogonal, a naive approach would involve using preference-based reward learning followed by an off-the-shelf offline RL algorithm. However, this requires the separate learning of a scalar reward function, which is assumed to be an information bottleneck of the learning process. To address this issue, we propose the offline preference-guided policy optimization (OPPO) paradigm, which models offline trajectories and preferences in a one-step process, eliminating the need for separately learning a reward function. OPPO achieves this by introducing an offline hindsight information matching objective for optimizing a contextual policy and a preference modeling objective for finding the optimal context. OPPO further integrates a well-performing decision policy by optimizing the two objectives iteratively. Our empirical results demonstrate that OPPO effectively models offline preferences and outperforms prior competing baselines, including offline RL algorithms performed over either true or pseudo reward function specifications. Our code is available on the project website: https://sites.google.com/view/oppo-icml-2023 .
中摘: 

本研究聚焦于离线偏好强化学习（PbRL）这一课题，它是常规强化学习的一种变体，无需在线交互或奖励函数设定。相反，智能体接收固定的离线轨迹以及轨迹对之间的人类偏好，以分别提取动态信息和任务信息。由于动态信息与任务信息是正交的，一种朴素的方法会涉及先使用基于偏好的奖励学习，再辅以现成的离线强化学习算法。然而，这需要单独学习一个标量奖励函数，而这被认为是学习过程中的信息瓶颈。为解决这一问题，我们提出了离线偏好引导策略优化（OPPO）范式，该范式通过单步过程对离线轨迹和偏好进行建模，消除了单独学习奖励函数的需求。OPPO 通过引入离线后验信息匹配目标来优化上下文策略，以及引入偏好建模目标来寻找最优上下文来实现这一目标。此外，OPPO 通过迭代优化这两个目标，集成了表现良好的决策策略。我们的实证结果表明，OPPO 能够有效建模离线偏好，并优于先前的竞争基线，包括在真实或伪奖励函数设定下执行的离线强化学习算法。我们的代码可在项目网站上获取：https://sites.google.com/view/oppo-icml-2023。

[No.383] Differentially Private Episodic Reinforcement Learning with Heavy-tailed Rewards
来源: ICML 2023

类别: 
作者: Yulian Wu;Xingyu Zhou;Sayak Ray Chowdhury;Di Wang;
机构: King Abdullah University of Science and Technology;Wayne State University;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24222
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01121
英摘: In this paper, we study the problem of (finite horizon tabular) Markov decision processes (MDPs) with heavy-tailed rewards under the constraint of differential privacy (DP). Compared with the previous studies for private reinforcement learning that typically assume rewards are sampled from some bounded or sub-Gaussian distributions to ensure DP, we consider the setting where reward distributions have only finite $(1+v)$-th moments with some $v \in (0,1]$. By resorting to robust mean estimators for rewards, we first propose two frameworks for heavy-tailed MDPs, i.e., one is for value iteration and another is for policy optimization. Under each framework, we consider both joint differential privacy (JDP) and local differential privacy (LDP) models. Based on our frameworks, we provide regret upper bounds for both JDP and LDP cases and show that the moment of distribution and privacy budget both have significant impacts on regrets. Finally, we establish a lower bound of regret minimization for heavy-tailed MDPs in JDP model by reducing it to the instance-independent lower bound of heavy-tailed multi-armed bandits in DP model. We also show the lower bound for the problem in LDP by adopting some private minimax methods. Our results reveal that there are fundamental differences between the problem of private RL with sub-Gaussian and that with heavy-tailed rewards.
中摘: 

在本文中，我们研究了在差分隐私（DP）约束下具有重尾奖励的有限时域表格型马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）问题。与以往通常假设奖励采样自有界或次高斯分布以确保差分隐私（DP）的隐私强化学习研究相比，我们考虑的是奖励分布仅具有有限 $(1+v)$ 阶矩的情形，其中 $v \in (0,1]$。借助奖励的鲁棒均值估计器，我们首先针对重尾 MDP 提出了两个框架，即分别基于价值迭代和策略优化的框架。在每个框架下，我们均考虑了联合差分隐私（JDP）和局部差分隐私（LDP）模型。基于所提出的框架，我们给出了 JDP 和 LDP 情形下的遗憾上界，并表明分布的矩和隐私预算均对遗憾产生显著影响。最后，通过将问题归约为差分隐私（DP）模型中重尾多臂老虎机的实例独立下界，我们确立了 JDP 模型中重尾 MDP 遗憾最小化的下界。此外，我们还通过采用某些私有极小极大方法，展示了该问题在 LDP 下的下界。我们的结果表明，具有次高斯奖励的隐私强化学习问题与具有重尾奖励的隐私强化学习问题之间存在本质差异。

[No.384] Do the Rewards Justify the Means? Measuring Trade-Offs Between Rewards and Ethical Behavior in the Machiavelli Benchmark
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 手段是否值得所获得的奖励？在马基雅维利基准中衡量奖励与道德行为之间的权衡
类别: 
作者: Alexander Pan;Jun Shern Chan;Andy Zou;Nathaniel Li;Steven Basart;Thomas Woodside;Hanlin Zhang;Scott Emmons;Dan Hendrycks;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;OpenAI;Carnegie Mellon University;Center for AI Safety;Yale University;Harvard University;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/23817
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.03279
英摘: Artificial agents have traditionally been trained to maximize reward, which may incentivize power-seeking and deception, analogous to how next-token prediction in language models (LMs) may incentivize toxicity. So do agents naturally learn to be Machiavellian? And how do we measure these behaviors in general-purpose models such as GPT-4? Towards answering these questions, we introduce MACHIAVELLI, a benchmark of 134 Choose-Your-Own-Adventure games containing over half a million rich, diverse scenarios that center on social decision-making. Scenario labeling is automated with LMs, which are more performant than human annotators. We mathematize dozens of harmful behaviors and use our annotations to evaluate agents' tendencies to be power-seeking, cause disutility, and commit ethical violations. We observe some tension between maximizing reward and behaving ethically. To improve this trade-off, we investigate LM-based methods to steer agents' towards less harmful behaviors. Our results show that agents can both act competently and morally, so concrete progress can currently be made in machine ethics--designing agents that are Pareto improvements in both safety and capabilities.
中摘: 

智能体传统上被训练以最大化奖励，这可能会激励其寻求权力和实施欺骗，类似于语言模型（LMs）中的下一个标记预测可能助长毒性。那么，智能体会否自然地学会采取马基雅维利式的手段？我们又该如何在 GPT-4 等通用模型中衡量这些行为？为了回答这些问题，我们推出了 MACHIAVELLI，这是一个包含 134 款“选择你自己的冒险”游戏的基准测试，其中包含超过五十万个丰富且多样的场景，这些场景以社会决策为核心。场景标注通过语言模型实现自动化，其表现优于人工标注员。我们将数十种有害行为形式化，并利用标注来评估智能体在寻求权力、造成负效用及违背道德方面的倾向。我们观察到，最大化奖励与道德行为之间存在一定的张力。为了改善这一权衡，我们研究了基于语言模型的方法，以引导智能体采取危害更小的行为。我们的结果表明，智能体既可以表现胜任，又能够合乎道德，因此目前可在机器伦理领域取得切实进展——设计在安全性和能力性两方面均实现帕累托改进的智能体。

[No.385] Hierarchies of Reward Machines
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 奖励机器层次结构
类别: 
作者: Daniel Furelos-Blanco;Mark Law;Anders Jonsson;Krysia Broda;Alessandra Russo;
机构: Imperial College London;Universitat Pompeu Fabra;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24091
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2205.15752
英摘: Reward machines (RMs) are a recent formalism for representing the reward function of a reinforcement learning task through a finite-state machine whose edges encode subgoals of the task using high-level events. The structure of RMs enables the decomposition of a task into simpler and independently solvable subtasks that help tackle long-horizon and/or sparse reward tasks. We propose a formalism for further abstracting the subtask structure by endowing an RM with the ability to call other RMs, thus composing a hierarchy of RMs (HRM). We exploit HRMs by treating each call to an RM as an independently solvable subtask using the options framework, and describe a curriculum-based method to learn HRMs from traces observed by the agent. Our experiments reveal that exploiting a handcrafted HRM leads to faster convergence than with a flat HRM, and that learning an HRM is feasible in cases where its equivalent flat representation is not.
中摘: 

奖励机（RMs）是一种近期的形式化方法，用于通过有限状态机表示强化学习任务的奖励函数，其边利用高层事件编码任务的子目标。RMs 的结构使得任务能够被分解为更简单且可独立求解的子任务，从而有助于解决长视界和/或稀疏奖励任务。我们提出了一种形式化方法，通过赋予奖励机（RM）调用其他奖励机的能力，来进一步抽象子任务结构，进而构建奖励机层级（HRM）。我们利用 HRM，采用选项框架将每次对 RM 的调用视为可独立求解的子任务，并提出了一种基于课程学习的方法，用于从智能体观察到的轨迹中学习 HRM。实验结果表明，利用手工构建的 HRM 比使用扁平化 HRM 能带来更快的收敛速度，并且在等效扁平表示不可行的情况下，学习 HRM 也是可行的。

[No.387] Internally Rewarded Reinforcement Learning
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 内部奖励强化学习
类别: 
作者: Mengdi Li;Xufeng Zhao;Jae Hee Lee;Cornelius Weber;Stefan Wermter;
机构: University of Hamburg;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/23482
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.00270
英摘: We study a class of reinforcement learning problems where the reward signals for policy learning are generated by an internal reward model that is dependent on and jointly optimized with the policy. This interdependence between the policy and the reward model leads to an unstable learning process because reward signals from an immature reward model are noisy and impede policy learning, and conversely, an under-optimized policy impedes reward estimation learning. We call this learning setting $\textit{Internally Rewarded Reinforcement Learning}$ (IRRL) as the reward is not provided directly by the environment but $\textit{internally}$ by a reward model. In this paper, we formally formulate IRRL and present a class of problems that belong to IRRL. We theoretically derive and empirically analyze the effect of the reward function in IRRL and based on these analyses propose the clipped linear reward function. Experimental results show that the proposed reward function can consistently stabilize the training process by reducing the impact of reward noise, which leads to faster convergence and higher performance compared with baselines in diverse tasks.
中摘: 

我们研究一类强化学习问题，其中用于策略学习的奖励信号由一个内部奖励模型生成，该模型依赖于策略并与策略进行联合优化。策略与奖励模型之间的这种相互依赖性导致学习过程不稳定，因为来自不成熟奖励模型的奖励信号存在噪声，会阻碍策略学习；反之，优化不足的策略也会阻碍奖励估计的学习。我们将这种学习设置称为“内部奖励强化学习”（Internally Rewarded Reinforcement Learning，简称 IRRL），因为奖励并非直接由环境提供，而是由奖励模型内部生成。在本文中，我们形式化地定义了 IRRL，并提出了属于 IRRL 的一类问题。我们理论推导并实证分析了 IRRL 中奖励函数的影响，并基于这些分析提出了截断线性奖励函数。实验结果表明，所提出的奖励函数能够通过减少奖励噪声的影响来持续稳定训练过程，这使得在多样化任务中，与基线方法相比能够更快收敛并获得更高性能。

[No.388] Invariance in Policy Optimisation and Partial Identifiability in Reward Learning
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 策略优化中的不变性与奖励学习中的部分可识别性
类别: 
作者: Joar Max Viktor Skalse;Matthew Farrugia-Roberts;Stuart Russell;Alessandro Abate;Adam Gleave;
机构: University of Oxford;University of California, Berkeley;FAR.AI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24879
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.07475
英摘: It is often very challenging to manually design reward functions for complex, real-world tasks. To solve this, one can instead use reward learning to infer a reward function from data. However, there are often multiple reward functions that fit the data equally well, even in the infinite-data limit. This means that the reward function is only partially identifiable. In this work, we formally characterise the partial identifiability of the reward function given several popular reward learning data sources, including expert demonstrations and trajectory comparisons. We also analyse the impact of this partial identifiability for several downstream tasks, such as policy optimisation. We unify our results in a framework for comparing data sources and downstream tasks by their invariances, with implications for the design and selection of data sources for reward learning.
中摘: 

为复杂且现实世界中的任务手动设计奖励函数通常极具挑战性。为解决这一问题，可以转而使用奖励学习从数据中推断奖励函数。然而，即使在无限数据的极限下，也常有多个奖励函数能以同等程度拟合数据。这意味着奖励函数仅是部分可识别的。在本工作中，我们形式化地刻画了给定几种流行的奖励学习数据源后奖励函数的部分可识别性，包括专家演示和轨迹比较。我们还分析了这种部分可识别性对若干下游任务（如策略优化）的影响。我们将结果统一于一个框架中，该框架依据不变性对数据源和下游任务进行比较，这对于奖励学习中数据源的设计与选择具有启示意义。

[No.390] LIV: Language-Image Representations and Rewards for Robotic Control
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: LIV：语言-图像表示及其在机器人控制中的奖励机制
类别: 
作者: Yecheng Jason Ma;Vikash Kumar;Amy Zhang;Osbert Bastani;Dinesh Jayaraman;
机构: Meta;University of Pennsylvania;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/23808
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.00958
英摘: We present Language-Image Value learning (LIV), a unified objective for vision-language representation and reward learning from action-free videos with text annotations. Exploiting a novel connection between dual reinforcement learning and mutual information contrastive learning, the LIV objective trains a multi-modal representation that implicitly encodes a universal value function for tasks specified as language or image goals. We use LIV to pre-train the first control-centric vision-language representation from large human video datasets such as EpicKitchen. Given only a language or image goal, the pre-trained LIV model can assign dense rewards to each frame in videos of unseen robots or humans attempting that task in unseen environments. Further, when some target domain-specific data is available, the same objective can be used to fine-tune and improve LIV and even other pre-trained representations for robotic control and reward specification in that domain. In our experiments on several simulated and real-world robot environments, LIV models consistently outperform the best prior input state representations for imitation learning, as well as reward specification methods for policy synthesis. Our results validate the advantages of joint vision-language representation and reward learning within the unified, compact LIV framework.

[No.391] Model-Free Robust Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 无模型稳健平均回报强化学习
类别: 
作者: Yue Wang;Alvaro Velasquez;George K. Atia;Ashley Prater-Bennette;Shaofeng Zou;

英摘: Robust Markov decision processes (MDPs) address the challenge of model uncertainty by optimizing the worst-case performance over an uncertainty set of MDPs. In this paper, we focus on the robust average-reward MDPs under the model-free setting. We first theoretically characterize the structure of solutions to the robust average-reward Bellman equation, which is essential for our later convergence analysis. We then design two model-free algorithms, robust relative value iteration (RVI) TD and robust RVI Q-learning, and theoretically prove their convergence to the optimal solution. [No.392] Multi-User Reinforcement Learning with Low Rank Rewards
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 低秩奖励下的多用户强化学习
类别: 
作者: Dheeraj Mysore Nagaraj;Suhas S Kowshik;Naman Agarwal;Praneeth Netrapalli;Prateek Jain;

类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24880
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.05355
英摘: In this work, we consider the problem of collaborative multi-user reinforcement learning. In this setting there are multiple users with the same state-action space and transition probabilities but with different rewards. Under the assumption that the reward matrix of the $N$ users has a low-rank structure -- a standard and practically successful assumption in the offline collaborative filtering setting -- the question is can we design algorithms with significantly lower sample complexity compared to the ones that learn the MDP individually for each user. Our main contribution is an algorithm which explores rewards collaboratively with $N$ user-specific MDPs and can learn rewards efficiently in two key settings: tabular MDPs and linear MDPs. When $N$ is large and the rank is constant, the sample complexity per MDP depends logarithmically over the size of the state-space, which represents an exponential reduction (in the state-space size) when compared to the standard ``non-collaborative'' algorithms.
中摘: 

在这项工作中，我们研究了协作多用户强化学习问题。在此设定下，存在多个用户，他们拥有相同的状态 - 动作空间和转移概率，但具有不同的奖励。假设 $N$ 个用户的奖励矩阵具有低秩结构——这是离线协同过滤场景中一种标准且在实践中行之有效的假设——核心问题在于，我们能否设计出样本复杂度显著低于分别为每个用户单独学习 MDP 的算法。我们的主要贡献是提出了一种算法，该算法与 $N$ 个用户特定的 MDP 协作探索奖励，并能在两种关键设置中高效地学习奖励：表格型 MDP 和线性 MDP。当 $N$ 很大且秩为常数时，每个 MDP 的样本复杂度与状态空间大小呈对数依赖关系，与标准的“非协作”算法相比，这代表了状态空间大小上的指数级降低。

[No.393] Off-Policy Average Reward Actor-Critic with Deterministic Policy Search
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 基于平均奖励的离策actor-critic与确定性策略搜索
类别: 
作者: Naman Saxena;Subhojyoti Khastagir;Shishir N Y;Shalabh Bhatnagar;
机构: Indian Institute of Science;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/23730
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.12239
英摘: The average reward criterion is relatively less studied as most existing works in the Reinforcement Learning literature consider the discounted reward criterion. There are few recent works that present on-policy average reward actor-critic algorithms, but average reward off-policy actor-critic is relatively less explored. In this work, we present both on-policy and off-policy deterministic policy gradient theorems for the average reward performance criterion. Using these theorems, we also present an Average Reward Off-Policy Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (ARO-DDPG) Algorithm. We first show asymptotic convergence analysis using the ODE-based method. Subsequently, we provide a finite time analysis of the resulting stochastic approximation scheme with linear function approximator and obtain an $ε$-optimal stationary policy with a sample complexity of $Ω(ε^{-2.5})$. We compare the average reward performance of our proposed ARO-DDPG algorithm and observe better empirical performance compared to state-of-the-art on-policy average reward actor-critic algorithms over MuJoCo-based environments.
中摘:

链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24547
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.10165
英摘: We study reward-free reinforcement learning (RL) with linear function approximation, where the agent works in two phases: (1) in the exploration phase, the agent interacts with the environment but cannot access the reward; and (2) in the planning phase, the agent is given a reward function and is expected to find a near-optimal policy based on samples collected in the exploration phase. The sample complexities of existing reward-free algorithms have a polynomial dependence on the planning horizon, which makes them intractable for long planning horizon RL problems. In this paper, we propose a new reward-free algorithm for learning linear mixture Markov decision processes (MDPs), where the transition probability can be parameterized as a linear combination of known feature mappings. At the core of our algorithm is uncertainty-weighted value-targeted regression with exploration-driven pseudo-reward and a high-order moment estimator for the aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. When the total reward is bounded by $1$, we show that our algorithm only needs to explore $\tilde O( d^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ episodes to find an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy, where $d$ is the dimension of the feature mapping. The sample complexity of our algorithm only has a polylogarithmic dependence on the planning horizon and therefore is "horizon-free". In addition, we provide an $Ω(d^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ sample complexity lower bound, which matches the sample complexity of our algorithm up to logarithmic factors, suggesting that our algorithm is optimal.
中摘: 

我们研究具有线性函数近似的无奖励强化学习（RL），其中智能体分两个阶段工作：(1) 在探索阶段，智能体与环境交互但无法访问奖励；(2) 在规划阶段，智能体被赋予一个奖励函数，并需基于探索阶段收集的样本来找到一个近似最优策略。现有无奖励算法的样本复杂度与规划周期呈多项式依赖关系，这使得它们难以处理具有长规划周期的强化学习问题。在本文中，我们提出了一种用于学习线性混合马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）的新无奖励算法，其中转移概率可以被参数化为已知特征映射的线性组合。我们算法的核心是不确定性加权值目标回归，其包含探索驱动的伪奖励，以及用于估计偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的高阶矩估计器。当总奖励不超过 $1$ 时，我们表明我们的算法仅需探索 $\tilde O( d^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ 个回合即可找到一个 $\varepsilon$-最优策略，其中 $d$ 是特征映射的维度。我们算法的样本复杂度仅与规划周期呈多对数依赖关系，因此是“无规划周期依赖”的。此外，我们给出了一个 $Ω(d^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ 的样本复杂度下界，该下界在对数因子范围内与我们算法的样本复杂度相匹配，表明我们的算法是最优的。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

类别:

[No.397] Scaling Laws for Reward Model Overoptimization
来源: ICML 2023
命中: reward
标题: 奖励模型过度优化的缩放法则
类别: 
作者: Leo Gao;John Schulman;Jacob Hilton;
机构: OpenAI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24924
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.10760
英摘: In reinforcement learning from human feedback, it is common to optimize against a reward model trained to predict human preferences. Because the reward model is an imperfect proxy, optimizing its value too much can hinder ground truth performance, in accordance with Goodhart's law. This effect has been frequently observed, but not carefully measured due to the expense of collecting human preference data. In this work, we use a synthetic setup in which a fixed "gold-standard" reward model plays the role of humans, providing labels used to train a proxy reward model. We study how the gold reward model score changes as we optimize against the proxy reward model using either reinforcement learning or best-of-$n$ sampling. We find that this relationship follows a different functional form depending on the method of optimization, and that in both cases its coefficients scale smoothly with the number of reward model parameters. We also study the effect on this relationship of the size of the reward model dataset, the number of reward model and policy parameters, and the coefficient of the KL penalty added to the reward in the reinforcement learning setup. We explore the implications of these empirical results for theoretical considerations in AI alignment.
中摘: 

在人类反馈强化学习中，通常的做法是针对一个经过训练以预测人类偏好的奖励模型进行优化。由于奖励模型只是一个不完美的代理，根据古德哈特定律，过度优化其数值可能会损害真实性能。这种效应虽经常被观察到，但由于收集人类偏好数据的成本高昂，尚未得到仔细测量。在本文中，我们采用一种合成设置，其中固定的“黄金标准”奖励模型扮演人类的角色，提供用于训练代理奖励模型的标签。我们研究了当分别使用强化学习或最佳-$n$ 采样优化代理奖励模型时，“黄金”奖励模型评分的变化情况。我们发现，这种关系遵循的函数形式取决于优化方法，并且在两种情况下，其系数均随奖励模型参数数量的增加而平滑缩放。我们还研究了奖励模型数据集的大小、奖励模型和策略参数的数量，以及在强化学习设置中添加到奖励函数中的 KL 惩罚系数对该关系的影响。最后，我们探讨了这些实证结果对 AI 对齐理论研究的启示。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

来源: ICML 2023

来源: ACL 2024

英摘: We introduce ALaRM, the first framework modeling hierarchical rewards in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), which is designed to enhance the alignment of large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. [No.401] Arithmetic Control of LLMs for Diverse User Preferences: Directional Preference Alignment with Multi-Objective Rewards
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: 针对多样用户偏好的算术控制大语言模型：多目标奖励的方向偏好对齐
类别: 
作者: Haoxiang Wang;Yong Lin;Wei Xiong;Rui Yang;Shizhe Diao;Shuang Qiu;Han Zhao;Tong Zhang;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.468/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.18571
英摘: Fine-grained control over large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge, hindering their adaptability to diverse user needs. While Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) shows promise in aligning LLMs, its reliance on scalar rewards often limits its ability to capture diverse user preferences in real-world applications. To address this limitation, we introduce the Directional Preference Alignment (DPA) framework. Unlike the scalar-reward RLHF, DPA incorporates multi-objective reward modeling to represent diverse preference profiles. Additionally, DPA models user preferences as directions (i.e., unit vectors) in the reward space to achieve user-dependent preference control. Our method involves training a multi-objective reward model and then fine-tuning the LLM with a preference-conditioned variant of Rejection Sampling Finetuning (RSF), an RLHF method adopted by Llama 2. This method enjoys a better performance trade-off across various reward objectives. In comparison with the scalar-reward RLHF, DPA offers users intuitive control over LLM generation: they can arithmetically specify their desired trade-offs (e.g., more helpfulness with less verbosity). We also validate the effectiveness of DPA with real-world alignment experiments on Mistral-7B. Our method provides straightforward arithmetic control over the trade-off between helpfulness and verbosity while maintaining competitive performance with strong baselines such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO).
中摘: 

对大语言模型（LLMs）的细粒度控制仍然是一个重大挑战，阻碍了其适应多样化用户需求的能力。尽管人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）在使大语言模型对齐方面展现出潜力，但其对标量奖励的依赖往往限制了其在实际应用中捕捉多样化用户偏好的能力。为解决这一局限，我们引入了定向偏好对齐（DPA）框架。与基于标量奖励的 RLHF 不同，DPA 采用了多目标奖励建模来表征多样的偏好配置。此外，DPA 将用户偏好建模为奖励空间中的方向（即单位向量），以实现用户依赖的偏好控制。我们的方法包括训练一个多目标奖励模型，然后使用偏好条件变体的拒绝采样微调（RSF）对大语言模型进行微调，这是一种由 Llama 2 采用的 RLHF 方法。该方法在各种奖励目标上享有更好的性能权衡。与基于标量奖励的 RLHF 相比，DPA 为用户提供了对大语言模型生成的直观控制：他们可以按算术方式指定所需的权衡（例如，更具帮助性且更简洁）。我们还通过在 Mistral-7B 上进行真实对齐实验验证了 DPA 的有效性。我们的方法在保持与直接偏好优化（DPO）等强大基线具有竞争力的性能的同时，提供了对帮助性与冗长性之间权衡的直接算术控制。

[No.402] Countering Reward Over-Optimization in LLM with Demonstration-Guided Reinforcement Learning
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: 通过示范引导强化学习对抗奖励过优化在大语言模型中的表现
类别: 
作者: Mathieu Rita;Florian Strub;Rahma Chaabouni;Paul Michel;Emmanuel Dupoux;Olivier Pietquin;
机构: INRIA;Cohere;DeepMind;EHESS;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.740/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19409
英摘: While Reinforcement Learning (RL) has been proven essential for tuning large language models (LLMs), it can lead to reward over-optimization (ROO). Existing approaches address ROO by adding KL regularization, requiring computationally expensive hyperparameter tuning. Additionally, KL regularization focuses solely on regularizing the language policy, neglecting a potential source of regularization: the reward function itself. Inspired by demonstration-guided RL, we here introduce the Reward Calibration from Demonstration (RCfD), which leverages human demonstrations and a reward model to recalibrate the reward objective. Formally, given a prompt, the RCfD objective minimizes the distance between the demonstrations' and LLM's rewards rather than directly maximizing the reward function. This objective shift avoids incentivizing the LLM to exploit the reward model and promotes more natural and diverse language generation. We show the effectiveness of RCfD on three language tasks, which achieves comparable performance to carefully tuned baselines while mitigating ROO.
中摘: 

虽然强化学习（RL）已被证明对于调优大型语言模型（LLMs）至关重要，但它可能导致奖励过度优化（ROO）。现有方法通过添加 KL 正则化来解决 ROO 问题，但这需要计算成本高昂的超参数调优。此外，KL 正则化仅专注于对语言策略进行正则化，忽视了一个潜在的正则化来源：奖励函数本身。受演示引导强化学习的启发，我们在此引入基于演示的奖励校准（RCfD），该方法利用人类演示和奖励模型对奖励目标进行重新校准。形式上，给定一个提示词（prompt），RCfD 的目标是最小化人类演示奖励与大型语言模型奖励之间的距离，而不是直接最大化奖励函数。这一目标的转变避免了激励大型语言模型利用奖励模型，并促进了更自然和多样的语言生成。我们在三种语言任务上展示了 RCfD 的有效性，其性能与精心调优的基线相当，同时缓解了奖励过度优化问题。

[No.403] Direct Large Language Model Alignment Through Self-Rewarding Contrastive Prompt Distillation
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: 直接通过自我奖励对比提示精炼实现大型语言模型对齐
类别: 
作者: Aiwei Liu;Haoping Bai;Zhiyun Lu;Xiang Kong;Xiaoming Wang;Jiulong Shan;Meng Cao;Lijie Wen;
机构: Tsinghua University;Apple;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.523/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11907
英摘: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human expectations without human-annotated preference data is an important problem. In this paper, we propose a method to evaluate the response preference by using the output probabilities of response pairs under contrastive prompt pairs, which could achieve better performance on LLaMA2-7B and LLaMA2-13B compared to RLAIF. Based on this, we propose an automatic alignment method, Direct Large Model Alignment (DLMA). First, we use contrastive prompt pairs to automatically generate preference data. Then, we continue to evaluate the generated preference data using contrastive prompt pairs and calculate a self-rewarding score. Finally, we use the DPO algorithm to effectively align LLMs by combining this self-rewarding score. In the experimental stage, our DLMA method could surpass the \texttt{RLHF} method without relying on human-annotated preference data.

在无人类标注偏好数据的情况下，将大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类期望对齐是一个重要问题。在本文中，我们提出了一种利用对比提示对下响应对的输出概率来评估响应偏好的方法。与 RLAIF 相比，该方法在 LLaMA2-7B 和 LLaMA2-13B 上取得了更好的性能。基于此，我们提出了一种自动对齐方法，称为直接大模型对齐（DLMA）。首先，我们利用对比提示对自动生成偏好数据。接着，我们继续使用对比提示对评估生成的偏好数据，并计算自奖励分数。最后，结合该自奖励分数，我们利用 DPO 算法有效地对齐了大型语言模型。在实验阶段，我们的 DLMA 方法在不依赖人工标注偏好数据的情况下，性能超越了 RLHF 方法。

[No.405] DMoERM: Recipes of Mixture-of-Experts for Effective Reward Modeling
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: DMoERM：专家混合的有效奖励建模方法
类别: 
作者: Shanghaoran Quan;
机构: Beihang University;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.418/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01197
英摘: The performance of the reward model (RM) is a critical factor in improving the effectiveness of the large language model (LLM) during alignment fine-tuning. There remain two challenges in RM training: 1) training the same RM using various categories of data may cause its generalization performance to suffer from multi-task disturbance, and 2) the human annotation consistency rate is generally only $60\%$ to $75\%$, causing training data to contain a lot of noise. To tackle these two challenges, we introduced the idea of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) into the field of RM for the first time. We propose the Double-Layer MoE RM (DMoERM). The outer layer MoE is a sparse model. After classifying an input into task categories, we route it to the corresponding inner layer task-specific model. The inner layer MoE is a dense model. We decompose the specific task into multiple capability dimensions and individually fine-tune a LoRA expert on each one. Their outputs are then synthesized by an MLP to compute the final rewards. To minimize costs, we call a public LLM API to obtain the capability preference labels. The validation on manually labeled datasets confirms that our model attains superior consistency with human preference and outstrips advanced generative approaches. Meanwhile, through BoN sampling and RL experiments, we demonstrate that our model outperforms state-of-the-art ensemble methods of RM and mitigates the overoptimization problem. Our code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/quanshr/DMoERM-v1.
中摘: 

奖励模型（RM）的性能是在对齐微调过程中提升大语言模型（LLM）效果的关键因素。RM 的训练仍面临两个挑战：1) 使用各类别数据训练同一 RM 可能导致其泛化性能遭受多任务干扰；2) 人类标注的一致性率通常仅为 60% 至 75%，导致训练数据包含大量噪声。为了解决这两个挑战，我们首次将混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）的思想引入 RM 领域。我们提出了双层混合专家奖励模型（DMoERM）。外层 MoE 是一个稀疏模型。在将输入分类到任务类别后，我们将其路由至相应的内层特定任务模型。内层 MoE 是一个稠密模型。我们将特定任务分解为多个能力维度，并在每个维度上分别微调一个 LoRA 专家。它们的输出随后由多层感知机（MLP）进行合成，以计算最终奖励。为了最小化成本，我们调用公开的 LLM API 来获取能力偏好标签。在手标注数据集上的验证表明，我们的模型实现了与人类偏好更高的一致性，并超越了先进的生成方法。此外，通过最佳-of-N（BoN）采样和强化学习（RL）实验，我们证明我们的模型优于当前最先进的 RM 集成方法，并缓解了过度优化问题。我们的代码和数据集可在以下地址获取：https://github.com/quanshr/DMoERM-v1。

[No.406] Enhancing Reinforcement Learning with Label-Sensitive Reward for Natural Language Understanding
来源: ACL 2024

标题: 利用标签敏感奖励增强自然语言理解的强化学习
类别: 
作者: Kuo Liao;Shuang Li;Meng Zhao;Liqun Liu;Mengge Xue;Zhenyu Hu;Honglin Han;Chengguo Yin;

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.231/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19763
英摘: Recent strides in large language models (LLMs) have yielded remarkable performance, leveraging reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) to significantly enhance generation and alignment capabilities. However, RLHF encounters numerous challenges, including the objective mismatch issue, leading to suboptimal performance in Natural Language Understanding (NLU) tasks. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Reinforcement Learning framework enhanced with Label-sensitive Reward (RLLR) to amplify the performance of LLMs in NLU tasks. By incorporating label-sensitive pairs into reinforcement learning, our method aims to adeptly capture nuanced label-sensitive semantic features during RL, thereby enhancing natural language understanding. Experiments conducted on five diverse foundation models across eight tasks showcase promising results. In comparison to Supervised Fine-tuning models (SFT), RLLR demonstrates an average performance improvement of 1.54%. Compared with RLHF models, the improvement averages at 0.69%. These results reveal the effectiveness of our method for LLMs in NLU tasks. Code and data available at: https://github.com/MagiaSN/ACL2024_RLLR.
中摘: 

大型语言模型（LLM）近期取得的显著进展带来了卓越的性能表现，并借助人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）显著增强了生成与对齐能力。然而，RLHF 面临诸多挑战，包括目标不匹配问题，导致其在自然语言理解（NLU）任务上的性能表现未达最优。为克服这一局限，我们提出了一种基于标签敏感奖励增强的新型强化学习框架（RLLR），旨在提升大型语言模型（LLM）在自然语言理解（NLU）任务中的性能。通过将标签敏感对融入强化学习，我们的方法旨在精准捕捉强化学习过程中细微的标签敏感语义特征，进而增强自然语言理解能力。我们在五种不同的基础模型上针对八个任务进行的实验展现了令人信服的结果。与监督微调（SFT）模型相比，RLLR 展现了平均 1.54% 的性能提升；相较于 RLHF 模型，改进幅度平均达到 0.69%。这些结果证实了我们的方法在自然语言理解任务中对于大型语言模型的有效性。代码和数据可在以下地址获取：https://github.com/MagiaSN/ACL2024_RLLR.

[No.407] Exploring Domain Robust Lightweight Reward Models based on Router Mechanism
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于路由器机制探索领域鲁棒轻量级奖励模型
类别: 
作者: Hyuk Namgoong;Jeesu Jung;Sangkeun Jung;YoonHyung Roh;
机构: Chungnam National University;Electronics and Telecommunications Research Institute;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.511/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17546
英摘: Recent advancements in large language models have heavily relied on the large reward model from reinforcement learning from human feedback for fine-tuning. However, the use of a single reward model across various domains may not always be optimal, often requiring retraining from scratch when new domain data is introduced. To address these challenges, we explore the utilization of small language models operating in a domain-specific manner based on router mechanisms. Our three approaches are: 1) utilize mixture of experts to form a single reward model by modularizing an internal router and experts, 2) employing external router to select the appropriate reward model from multiple domain-specific models, and 3) the framework reduces parameter size by loading reward models and router adapters onto a single small language model using adapters. Experimental validation underscores the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating performance comparable to baseline methods while also reducing the total parameter size.
中摘: 

大语言模型的最新进展在很大程度上依赖于基于人类反馈强化学习的大型奖励模型来进行微调。然而，跨多个领域使用单个奖励模型并不总是最优的，一旦引入新领域数据，往往需要从头开始重新训练。为了应对这些挑战，我们探索了基于路由机制、以特定领域方式运行的小语言模型。我们的三种方法如下：1) 利用专家混合机制，通过对内部路由器和专家进行模块化来构建单一奖励模型；2) 采用外部路由器，从多个特定领域模型中选择合适的奖励模型；3) 利用适配器将奖励模型和路由器适配器加载到单个小语言模型上，从而减小参数量。实验验证凸显了我们方法的有效性，表明其性能与基线方法相当，同时也减少了总参数量。

[No.408] LIRE: listwise reward enhancement for preference alignment
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: 列表级奖励增强以实现偏好对齐
类别: 
作者: Mingye Zhu;Yi Liu;Lei Zhang;Junbo Guo;Zhendong Mao;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;State Key Laboratory of Communication Content Cognition;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.201/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13516
英摘: Recently, tremendous strides have been made to align the generation of Large Language Models (LLMs) with human values to mitigate toxic or unhelpful content. Leveraging Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) proves effective and is widely adopted by researchers. However, implementing RLHF is complex, and its sensitivity to hyperparameters renders achieving stable performance and scalability challenging. Furthermore, prevailing approaches to preference alignment primarily concentrate on pairwise comparisons, with limited exploration into multi-response scenarios, thereby overlooking the potential richness within the candidate pool. For the above reasons, we propose a new approach: Listwise Reward Enhancement for Preference Alignment (LIRE), a gradient-based reward optimization approach that incorporates the offline rewards of multiple responses into a streamlined listwise framework, thus eliminating the need for online sampling during training. LIRE is straightforward to implement, requiring minimal parameter tuning, and seamlessly aligns with the pairwise paradigm while naturally extending to multi-response scenarios. Moreover, we introduce a self-enhancement algorithm aimed at iteratively refining the reward during training. Our experiments demonstrate that LIRE consistently outperforms existing methods across several benchmarks on dialogue and summarization tasks, with good transferability to out-of-distribution data, assessed using proxy reward models and human annotators.
中摘: 

近期，为了使大型语言模型（LLMs）的生成与人类价值观保持一致，从而减轻有害或无用内容的产生，人们已取得巨大进展。利用来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）被证明行之有效，并已被研究人员广泛采用。然而，实施 RLHF 过程复杂，且其对超参数的高度敏感性使得实现稳定性能和可扩展性面临挑战。此外，主流的偏好对齐方法主要集中于成对比较，对多响应场景的探索有限，从而忽视了候选池中潜在的丰富性。鉴于上述原因，我们提出了一种新方法：用于偏好对齐的列表级奖励增强（LIRE）。这是一种基于梯度的奖励优化方法，它将多个响应的离线奖励整合进一个精简的列表级框架中，从而消除了训练期间对在线采样的需求。LIRE 实现简便，所需参数微调极少，既能与成对范式无缝衔接，又能自然地扩展到多响应场景。此外，我们引入了一种自我增强算法，旨在在训练过程中迭代优化奖励。我们的实验表明，LIRE 在对话和摘要任务的多个基准测试中持续优于现有方法，并且在分布外数据上展现出良好的迁移能力，相关评估由代理奖励模型和人类标注者完成。

[No.410] Reward-based Input Construction for Cross-document Relation Extraction
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于奖励的输入构造在跨文档关系提取中的应用

英摘: Relation extraction (RE) is a fundamental task in natural language processing, aiming to identify relations between target entities in text. Given the nature of long documents in cross-document RE, extracting document embeddings is challenging due to the length constraints of pre-trained language models. Therefore, we propose REward-based Input Construction (REIC), the first learning-based sentence selector for cross-document RE. REIC extracts sentences based on relational evidence, enabling the RE module to effectively infer relations. [No.411] RLHFPoison: Reward Poisoning Attack for Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback in Large Language Models
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: RLHFPoison：针对大型语言模型的强化学习带有人类反馈的奖励中毒攻击
类别: 
作者: Jiongxiao Wang;Junlin Wu;Muhao Chen;Yevgeniy Vorobeychik;Chaowei Xiao;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;Washington University in St. Louis;University of California, Davis;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.140/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.09641
英摘: Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF) is a methodology designed to align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences, playing an important role in LLMs alignment. Despite its advantages, RLHF relies on human annotators to rank the text, which can introduce potential security vulnerabilities if any adversarial annotator (i.e., attackers) manipulates the ranking score by up-ranking any malicious text to steer the LLM adversarially. To assess the red-teaming of RLHF against human preference data poisoning, we propose RankPoison, a poisoning attack method on candidates' selection of preference rank flipping to reach certain malicious behaviors (e.g., generating longer sequences, which can increase the computational cost). With poisoned dataset generated by RankPoison, we can perform poisoning attacks on LLMs to generate longer tokens without hurting the original safety alignment performance. Moreover, applying RankPoison, we also successfully implement a backdoor attack where LLMs can generate longer answers under questions with the trigger word. Our findings highlight critical security challenges in RLHF, underscoring the necessity for more robust alignment methods for LLMs.
中摘: 

基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）是一种旨在使大型语言模型（LLM）与人类偏好对齐的方法，在大型语言模型的对齐中发挥着重要作用。尽管 RLHF 具有诸多优势，但它依赖人类标注员对文本进行排序。如果任何对抗性标注员（即攻击者）通过提升恶意文本的排序来操纵排名分数，从而对抗性地引导 LLM，则可能引入潜在的安全漏洞。为了评估 RLHF 在人类偏好数据投毒方面的红队测试情况，我们提出了 RankPoison。这是一种通过翻转偏好排序来干预候选项选择的投毒攻击方法，旨在实现某些恶意行为（例如，生成长度更长的序列，从而增加计算成本）。利用 RankPoison 生成的中毒数据集，我们可以对大型语言模型实施投毒攻击，使其生成长度更长的词元，同时不损害原有的安全对齐性能。此外，应用 RankPoison，我们还成功实现了一种后门攻击，使得大型语言模型在接收到包含触发词的提问时，能够生成更长的回答。我们的研究结果突显了 RLHF 中关键的安全挑战，强调了为大型语言模型开发更鲁棒对齐方法的必要性。

[No.412] TLCR: Token-Level Continuous Reward for Fine-grained Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于令牌级连续奖励的细粒度强化学习（从人类反馈）
类别: 
作者: Eunseop Yoon;Hee Suk Yoon;SooHwan Eom;Gunsoo Han;Daniel Nam;Daejin Jo;Kyoung-Woon On;Mark Hasegawa-Johnson;Sungwoong Kim;Chang Yoo;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Kakao Brain;Korea University;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.889/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16574
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) leverages human preference data to train language models to align more closely with human essence. These human preference data, however, are labeled at the sequence level, creating a mismatch between sequence-level preference labels and tokens, which are autoregressively generated from the language model. Although several recent approaches have tried to provide token-level (i.e., dense) rewards for each individual token, these typically rely on predefined discrete reward values (e.g., positive: +1, negative: -1, neutral: 0), failing to account for varying degrees of preference inherent to each token. To address this limitation, we introduce TLCR (Token-Level Continuous Reward) for RLHF, which incorporates a discriminator trained to distinguish positive and negative tokens, and the confidence of the discriminator is used to assign continuous rewards to each token considering the context. Extensive experiments show that our proposed TLCR leads to consistent performance improvements over previous sequence-level or token-level discrete rewards on open-ended generation benchmarks.
中摘: 

来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）利用人类偏好数据训练语言模型，使其与人类偏好本质更紧密地对齐。然而，这些人类偏好数据是在序列级别进行标注的，导致序列级别的偏好标签与由语言模型自回归生成的词元之间存在不匹配。尽管近期一些方法尝试为每个单独的词元提供词元级别（即稠密）奖励，但这些方法通常依赖于预定义的离散奖励值（例如：正面：+1，负面：-1，中性：0），未能考虑到每个词元固有的不同程度的偏好。为解决这一局限性，我们提出了面向 RLHF 的 TLCR（词元级连续奖励），该方法集成了用于区分正负词元的判别器，并利用判别器的置信度，在考虑上下文的情况下为每个词元分配连续奖励。大量实验表明，在开放生成基准上，我们提出的 TLCR 相比之前的序列级别或词元级别离散奖励方法，带来了持续一致的性能提升。

[No.413] Training Language Models to Generate Text with Citations via Fine-grained Rewards
来源: ACL 2024
命中: reward
标题: 通过细粒度奖励训练语言模型生成带有引用的文字

英摘: While recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have proven useful in answering user queries, they are prone to hallucination, and their responses often lack credibility due to missing references to reliable sources. [No.414] ClarifyDelphi: Reinforced Clarification Questions with Defeasibility Rewards for Social and Moral Situations
来源: ACL 2023
命中: reward
标题: 强化反驳性澄清问题以应对社会和道德情境
类别: 
作者: Valentina Pyatkin;Jena D. Hwang;Vivek Srikumar;Ximing Lu;Liwei Jiang;Yejin Choi;Chandra Bhagavatula;

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.acl-long.630/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.10409
英摘: Context is everything, even in commonsense moral reasoning. Changing contexts can flip the moral judgment of an action; "Lying to a friend" is wrong in general, but may be morally acceptable if it is intended to protect their life.
  We present ClarifyDelphi, an interactive system that learns to ask clarification questions (e.g., why did you lie to your friend?) in order to elicit additional salient contexts of a social or moral situation. We posit that questions whose potential answers lead to diverging moral judgments are the most informative. Thus, we propose a reinforcement learning framework with a defeasibility reward that aims to maximize the divergence between moral judgments of hypothetical answers to a question. Human evaluation demonstrates that our system generates more relevant, informative and defeasible questions compared to competitive baselines. Our work is ultimately inspired by studies in cognitive science that have investigated the flexibility in moral cognition (i.e., the diverse contexts in which moral rules can be bent), and we hope that research in this direction can assist both cognitive and computational investigations of moral judgments.
中摘:

我们提出了 ClarifyDelphi，这是一种交互系统，它通过学习提出澄清性问题（例如，“你为什么要对朋友撒谎？”），以获取社会或道德情境中的其他显著情境。我们认为，那些潜在答案会导致道德判断出现分歧的问题最具信息量。因此，我们提出了一种带有可废止性奖励的强化学习框架，旨在最大化针对某一问题的假设性回答所产生的道德判断之间的分歧程度。人类评估表明，与竞争性基线相比，我们的系统能生成更相关、信息量更丰富且更具可废止性的问题。我们的工作最终受认知科学研究的启发，这些研究调查了道德认知的灵活性（即道德规则可被变通的多样化情境），我们希望这一方向的研究能够辅助道德判断的认知与计算研究。

[No.415] Nano: Nested Human-in-the-Loop Reward Learning for Few-shot Language Model Control
来源: ACL 2023
命中: reward
标题: 纳米：嵌套的人工循环奖励学习在少量示例语言模型控制中的应用
类别:

类型: Findings

英摘: Pretrained language models have demonstrated extraordinary capabilities in language generation. However, real-world tasks often require controlling the distribution of generated text in order to mitigate bias, promote fairness, and achieve personalization. Existing techniques for controlling the distribution of generated text only work with quantified distributions, which require pre-defined categories, proportions of the distribution, or an existing corpus following the desired distributions. However, many important distributions, such as personal preferences, are unquantified. In this work, we tackle the problem of generating text following arbitrary distributions (quantified and unquantified) by proposing Nano, a few-shot human-in-the-loop training algorithm that continuously learns from human feedback. Nano achieves state-of-the-art results on single topic/attribute as well as quantified distribution control compared to previous works. We also show that Nano is able to learn unquantified distributions, achieves personalization, and captures differences between different individuals' personal preferences with high sample efficiency.
中摘: 

预训练语言模型在语言生成方面已展现出卓越的能力。然而，现实世界中的任务通常需要对生成文本的分布进行控制，以减轻偏见、促进公平并实现个性化。现有的控制生成文本分布的技术仅适用于量化分布，这要求有预定义的类别、分布比例，或存在遵循期望分布的现有语料库。然而，许多重要的分布（如个人偏好）却是未量化的。在这项工作中，我们通过提出 Nano 来解决生成遵循任意分布（包括量化和未量化）文本的问题。Nano 是一种少样本人机回环训练算法，能够从人类反馈中持续学习。与以往工作相比，Nano 在单一主题/属性以及量化分布控制方面均达到了最先进水平。我们还表明，Nano 能够学习未量化分布，实现个性化，并以高样本效率捕捉不同个体间个人偏好的差异。

[No.416] Reward Gaming in Conditional Text Generation
来源: ACL 2023
命中: reward
标题: 基于条件文本生成的奖励游戏
类别: 
作者: Richard Yuanzhe Pang;Vishakh Padmakumar;Thibault Sellam;Ankur Parikh;He He;
机构: New York University;Google;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.acl-long.262/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.08714
英摘: To align conditional text generation model outputs with desired behaviors, there has been an increasing focus on training the model using reinforcement learning (RL) with reward functions learned from human annotations. Under this framework, we identify three common cases where high rewards are incorrectly assigned to undesirable patterns: noise-induced spurious correlation, naturally occurring spurious correlation, and covariate shift. We show that even though learned metrics achieve high performance on the distribution of the data used to train the reward function, the undesirable patterns may be amplified during RL training of the text generation model. While there has been discussion about reward gaming in the RL or safety community, in this discussion piece, we would like to highlight reward gaming in the natural language generation (NLG) community using concrete conditional text generation examples and discuss potential fixes and areas for future work.
中摘: 

为使条件文本生成模型的输出与期望行为对齐，人们日益关注利用强化学习（RL）结合从人类标注中学习到的奖励函数来训练模型。在该框架下，我们确定了三种常见情况，即高奖励被错误地分配给了不良模式：噪声诱发的虚假相关性、自然发生的虚假相关性以及协变量偏移。我们表明，尽管学习到的指标在用于训练奖励函数的数据分布上表现优异，但在文本生成模型的强化学习训练过程中，这些不良模式可能会被放大。尽管强化学习或安全社区已有关于奖励博弈的讨论，但在这篇讨论文章中，我们希望通过具体的条件文本生成示例，重点探讨自然语言生成（NLG）社区中的奖励博弈问题，并讨论潜在的解决方案及未来工作方向。

[No.417] Autoregressive Multi-trait Essay Scoring via Reinforcement Learning with Scoring-aware Multiple Rewards
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于强化学习的自回归多特质文章评分方法，带有评分意识的多重奖励

英摘: Recent advances in automated essay scoring (AES) have shifted towards evaluating multiple traits to provide enriched feedback. Like typical AES systems, multi-trait AES employs the quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) to measure agreement with human raters, aligning closely with the rating schema; however, its non-differentiable nature prevents its direct use in neural network training. In this paper, we propose Scoring-aware Multi-reward Reinforcement Learning (SaMRL), which integrates actual evaluation schemes into the training process by designing QWK-based rewards with a mean-squared error penalty for multi-trait AES. [No.420] Dynamic Multi-Reward Weighting for Multi-Style Controllable Generation

英摘: Textual style expresses a diverse set of information, including interpersonal dynamics (e.g., formality) and the author's emotions or attitudes (e.g., disgust). [No.421] Dynamic Rewarding with Prompt Optimization Enables Tuning-free Self-Alignment of Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 动态优化提示以实现无需调优的语言模型自我对齐奖励机制
类别: 
作者: Somanshu Singla;Zhen Wang;Tianyang Liu;Abdullah Ashfaq;Zhiting Hu;Eric P. Xing;
机构: 
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.1220/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.08733
英摘: Aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) traditionally relies on costly training and human preference annotations. Self-alignment seeks to reduce these expenses by enabling models to align themselves. To further lower costs and achieve alignment without any expensive tuning or annotations, we introduce a new tuning-free approach for self-alignment, Dynamic Rewarding with Prompt Optimization (DRPO). Our approach leverages a search-based optimization framework that allows LLMs to iteratively self-improve and craft the optimal alignment instructions, all without additional training or human intervention. The core of DRPO is a dynamic rewarding mechanism, which identifies and rectifies model-specific alignment weaknesses, allowing LLMs to adapt efficiently to diverse alignment challenges. Empirical evaluations on eight recent LLMs, both open- and closed-sourced, demonstrate that DRPO significantly enhances alignment performance, with base models outperforming their SFT/RLHF-tuned counterparts. Moreover, the prompts automatically optimized by DRPO surpass those curated by human experts, further validating the effectiveness of our approach. Our findings highlight the great potential of current LLMs to achieve adaptive self-alignment through inference-time optimization, complementing tuning-based alignment methods.
中摘: 

对齐大型语言模型（LLMs）传统上依赖于成本高昂的训练和人类偏好标注。自对齐旨在通过使模型能够自我对齐来降低这些成本。为了进一步降低成本，并在无需任何昂贵微调或标注的情况下实现对齐，我们提出了一种新的无微调自对齐方法——动态奖励与提示优化（DRPO）。该方法利用了一个基于搜索的优化框架，允许 LLM 迭代地自我改进并生成最优对齐指令，全程无需额外的训练或人工干预。DRPO 的核心是一种动态奖励机制，能够识别并纠正模型特定的对齐弱点，使 LLM 能够高效适应多样化的对齐挑战。在八个近期 LLM（包括开源和闭源模型）上的实证评估表明，DRPO 显著增强了对齐性能，且基础模型的表现优于其经过 SFT/RLHF 微调的对应模型。此外，由 DRPO 自动优化的提示词优于人类专家精心设计的提示词，进一步验证了我们方法的有效性。我们的研究强调了当前 LLM 通过推理时优化实现自适应自对齐的巨大潜力，这为基于微调的对齐方法提供了有益补充。

[No.425] Interpretable Preferences via Multi-Objective Reward Modeling and Mixture-of-Experts
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 可解释的偏好通过多目标奖励建模和专家混合实现
类别: 
作者: Haoxiang Wang;Wei Xiong;Tengyang Xie;Han Zhao;Tong Zhang;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;University of Wisconsin–Madison;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.620/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12845
英摘: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as the primary method for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. The RLHF process typically starts by training a reward model (RM) using human preference data. Conventional RMs are trained on pairwise responses to the same user request, with relative ratings indicating which response humans prefer. The trained RM serves as a proxy for human preferences. However, due to the black-box nature of RMs, their outputs lack interpretability, as humans cannot intuitively understand why an RM thinks a response is good or not. As RMs act as human preference proxies, we believe they should be human-interpretable to ensure that their internal decision processes are consistent with human preferences and to prevent reward hacking in LLM alignment. To build RMs with interpretable preferences, we propose a two-stage approach: i) train an Absolute-Rating Multi-Objective Reward Model (ArmoRM) with multi-dimensional absolute-rating data, each dimension corresponding to a human-interpretable objective (e.g., honesty, verbosity, safety); ii) employ a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) strategy with a gating network that automatically selects the most suitable reward objectives based on the context. We efficiently trained an ArmoRM with Llama-3 8B and a gating network consisting of a shallow MLP on top of the ArmoRM. Our trained model, ArmoRM-Llama3-8B, obtains state-of-the-art performance on RewardBench, a benchmark evaluating RMs for language modeling. Notably, the performance of our model surpasses the LLM-as-a-judge method with GPT-4 judges by a margin, and approaches the performance of the much larger Nemotron-4 340B reward model.
中摘: 

基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）已成为使大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类偏好对齐的主要方法。RLHF 过程通常始于利用人类偏好数据训练一个奖励模型（RM）。传统的奖励模型（RM）是基于同一用户请求的成对响应进行训练的，相对评级表明了人类更偏好哪个响应。训练好的奖励模型充当人类偏好的代理。然而，由于奖励模型具有黑盒性质，其输出缺乏可解释性，因为人类无法直观地理解奖励模型为何认为某个响应是好的或不好的。鉴于奖励模型充当人类偏好的代理，我们认为它们应具备人类可解释性，以确保其内部决策过程与人类偏好保持一致，并防止大型语言模型对齐中的奖励黑客行为。为了构建具有可解释性偏好的奖励模型，我们提出了一种两阶段方法：i) 使用多维绝对评级数据训练一个绝对评级多目标奖励模型（ArmoRM），每个维度对应一个人类可解释的目标（例如，诚实度、冗长性、安全性）；ii) 采用一种带有门控网络的专家混合（MoE）策略，该网络能够根据上下文自动选择最合适的奖励目标。我们高效地训练了一个基于 Llama-3 8B 的 ArmoRM 以及一个叠加在该 ArmoRM 之上、由浅层多层感知机（MLP）构成的门控网络。我们训练出的模型 ArmoRM-Llama3-8B 在 RewardBench 上取得了最先进的性能，RewardBench 是一个评估语言模型奖励模型的基准。值得注意的是，我们的模型性能以显著优势超越了使用 GPT-4 作为评判者的“大模型即评判者”（LLM-as-a-judge）方法，并且接近了规模大得多的 Nemotron-4 340B 奖励模型的性能。

[No.426] Learning Planning-based Reasoning by Trajectories Collection and Process Reward Synthesizing
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于轨迹的学习计划推理和过程奖励合成
类别: 
作者: Fangkai Jiao;Chengwei Qin;Zhengyuan Liu;Nancy F. Chen;Shafiq Joty;
机构: Nanyang Technological University;Institute for Infocomm Research;Salesforce Research;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.20/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00658
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in handling complex reasoning tasks through step-by-step rationale generation. However, recent studies have raised concerns regarding the hallucination and flaws in their reasoning process. Substantial efforts are being made to improve the reliability and faithfulness of the generated rationales. Some approaches model reasoning as planning, while others focus on annotating for process supervision. Nevertheless, the planning-based search process often results in high latency due to the frequent assessment of intermediate reasoning states and the extensive exploration space. Additionally, supervising the reasoning process with human annotation is costly and challenging to scale for LLM training. To address these issues, in this paper, we propose a framework to learn planning-based reasoning through Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) on collected trajectories, which are ranked according to synthesized process rewards. Our results on challenging logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our learning framework, showing that our 7B model can surpass the strong counterparts like GPT-3.5-Turbo.
中摘: 

大型语言模型（LLMs）通过逐步生成推理依据，在处理复杂推理任务方面已展现出显著潜力。然而，近期研究引发了对其推理过程中存在的幻觉和缺陷的担忧。人们正投入大量努力以提高生成推理依据的可靠性和忠实性。一些方法将推理建模为规划，而另一些则专注于利用标注进行过程监督。尽管如此，基于规划的搜索过程通常会导致较高的延迟，这是由于对中间推理状态的频繁评估以及广阔的探索空间所致。此外，利用人工标注对推理过程进行监督不仅成本高昂，而且在 LLM 训练中难以扩展。为解决上述问题，本文提出了一种框架，通过直接偏好优化（DPO）在收集的轨迹上学习基于规划的推理，这些轨迹根据合成的过程奖励进行排序。我们在具有挑战性的逻辑推理基准上的实验结果证明了我们的学习框架的有效性，表明我们的 7B 模型能够超越如 GPT-3.5-Turbo 这样的强劲模型。

[No.427] LLM-Based Offline Learning for Embodied Agents via Consistency-Guided Reward Ensemble
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于LLM的一体化体态智能体离线学习通过一致性引导的奖励集成
类别: 
作者: Yujeong Lee;Sangwoo Shin;Wei-Jin Park;Honguk Woo;
机构: Sungkyunkwan University;Acryl Inc.;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.170/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17135
英摘: Employing large language models (LLMs) to enable embodied agents has become popular, yet it presents several limitations in practice. In this work, rather than using LLMs directly as agents, we explore their use as tools for embodied agent learning. Specifically, to train separate agents via offline reinforcement learning (RL), an LLM is used to provide dense reward feedback on individual actions in training datasets. In doing so, we present a consistency-guided reward ensemble framework (CoREN), designed for tackling difficulties in grounding LLM-generated estimates to the target environment domain. The framework employs an adaptive ensemble of spatio-temporally consistent rewards to derive domain-grounded rewards in the training datasets, thus enabling effective offline learning of embodied agents in different environment domains. Experiments with the VirtualHome benchmark demonstrate that CoREN significantly outperforms other offline RL agents, and it also achieves comparable performance to state-of-the-art LLM-based agents with 8B parameters, despite CoREN having only 117M parameters for the agent policy network and using LLMs only for training.
中摘: 

利用大型语言模型（LLM）为具身智能体赋能已成为一种流行趋势，但在实践中仍面临若干局限性。在本工作中，我们并非直接将 LLM 作为智能体，而是探索将其作为具身智能体学习的工具。具体而言，为了通过离线强化学习（RL）训练独立的智能体，我们利用 LLM 为训练数据集中的单个动作提供密集的奖励反馈。在此过程中，我们提出了一种一致性引导的奖励集成框架（CoREN），旨在解决将 LLM 生成的评估值与目标环境领域对齐的难题。该框架采用时空一致性奖励的自适应集成，从训练数据集中生成领域对齐奖励，从而实现具身智能体在不同环境领域中的有效离线学习。VirtualHome 基准测试实验表明，CoREN 显著优于其他离线强化学习智能体；此外，尽管 CoREN 的智能体策略网络参数规模仅为 1.17 亿，且 LLM 仅用于训练过程，其性能仍能达到与拥有 80 亿参数的最先进基于 LLM 的智能体相当的水平。

[No.428] On the Limited Generalization Capability of the Implicit Reward Model Induced by Direct Preference Optimization
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 直接偏好优化诱导的隐含奖励模型的有限泛化能力
类别: 
作者: Yong Lin;Skyler Seto;Maartje Ter Hoeve;Katherine Metcalf;Barry-John Theobald;Xuan Wang;Yizhe Zhang;Chen Huang;Tong Zhang;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Apple;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.940/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03650
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is an effective approach for aligning language models to human preferences. Central to RLHF is learning a reward function for scoring human preferences. Two main approaches for learning a reward model are 1) training an EXplicit Reward Model (EXRM) as in RLHF, and 2) using an implicit reward learned from preference data through methods such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Prior work has shown that the implicit reward model of DPO (denoted as DPORM) can approximate an EXRM in the limit. DPORM's effectiveness directly implies the optimality of the learned policy, and also has practical implication for LLM alignment methods including iterative DPO. However, it is unclear how well DPORM empirically matches the performance of EXRM. This work studies the accuracy at distinguishing preferred and rejected answers for both DPORM and EXRM. Our findings indicate that even though DPORM fits the training dataset comparably, it generalizes less effectively than EXRM, especially when the validation datasets contain distribution shifts. Across five out-of-distribution settings, DPORM has a mean drop in accuracy of 3% and a maximum drop of 7%. These findings highlight that DPORM has limited generalization ability and substantiates the integration of an explicit reward model in iterative DPO approaches.
中摘: 

来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）是一种将语言模型与人类偏好对齐的有效方法。RLHF 的核心在于学习一个用于衡量人类偏好的奖励函数。学习奖励模型的两种主要方法是：1）训练一个显式奖励模型（EXRM），如 RLHF 中所用；2）利用诸如直接偏好优化（DPO）等方法从偏好数据中学习得到的隐式奖励。先前的研究表明，DPO 的隐式奖励模型（记为 DPORM）在极限情况下可以近似于 EXRM。DPORM 的有效性直接意味着所学策略的最优性，同时也对包括迭代 DPO 在内的 LLM 对齐方法具有实际意义。然而，DPORM 在经验上如何与 EXRM 的性能相匹配尚不清楚。本研究探讨了 DPORM 和 EXRM 在区分偏好答案和拒绝答案方面的准确率。我们的研究结果表明，尽管 DPORM 对训练数据集的拟合效果相当，但其泛化能力不如 EXRM，尤其是在验证数据集存在分布偏移时。在五种分布外设置中，DPORM 的平均准确率下降了 3%，最大下降幅度为 7%。这些发现突显了 DPORM 泛化能力的局限性，并证实了在迭代 DPO 方法中整合显式奖励模型的必要性。

[No.430] Predicting Rewards Alongside Tokens: Non-disruptive Parameter Insertion for Efficient Inference Intervention in Large Language Model
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 预测奖励与标记并行：高效的大语言模型推理干预中的非侵扰性参数插入
类别: 
作者: Chenhan Yuan;Fei Huang;Ru Peng;Keming Lu;Bowen Yu;Chang Zhou;Jingren Zhou;
机构: University of Manchester;Alibaba Group;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.316/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10764
英摘: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) exhibit limitations such as generating unsafe responses, unreliable reasoning, etc. Existing inference intervention approaches attempt to mitigate these issues by finetuning additional models to produce calibration signals (such as rewards) that guide the LLM's decoding process. However, this solution introduces substantial time and space overhead due to the separate models required. This work proposes Non-disruptive parameters insertion (Otter), inserting extra parameters into the transformer architecture to predict calibration signals along with the original LLM output. Otter offers state-of-the-art performance on multiple demanding tasks while saving up to 86.5\% extra space and 98.5\% extra time. Furthermore, Otter seamlessly integrates with existing inference engines, requiring only a one-line code change, and the original model response remains accessible after the parameter insertion. Our code is publicly available at \url{https://github.com/chenhan97/Otter}
中摘: 

基于 Transformer 的大语言模型（LLMs）存在生成不安全响应、推理不可靠等局限性。现有的推理干预方法试图通过微调额外模型来生成校准信号（如奖励），以引导 LLM 的解码过程从而缓解这些问题。然而，由于需要独立的模型，该方案引入了显著的时间和空间开销。本研究提出了非破坏性参数插入（Otter），通过在 Transformer 架构中插入额外参数，与原始 LLM 输出一同预测校准信号。Otter 在多个具有挑战性的任务上展现了最先进的性能，同时节省了高达 86.5% 的额外空间和 98.5% 的额外时间。此外，Otter 能够与现有推理引擎无缝集成，仅需修改一行代码，且在参数插入后原始模型响应仍然可访问。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/chenhan97/Otter 公开获取。

[No.431] Rethinking the Role of Proxy Rewards in Language Model Alignment
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 重新思考代理奖励在语言模型对齐中的作用
类别: 
作者: Sungdong Kim;Minjoon Seo;
机构: NAVER Cloud;NAVER Corporation;Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.1150/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03469
英摘: Learning from human feedback via proxy reward modeling has been studied to align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human values. However, achieving reliable training through that proxy reward model (RM) is not a trivial problem, and its behavior remained as a black-box. In this paper, we study the role of proxy rewards in the LLM alignment via `reverse reward engineering' by composing interpretable features as a white-box reward function. We aim to replicate the ground truth (gold) reward signal by achieving a monotonic relationship between the proxy and gold reward signals after training the model using the proxy reward in reinforcement learning (RL). Our findings indicate that successfully emulating the gold reward requires generating responses that are relevant with enough length to open-ended questions, while also ensuring response consistency in closed-ended questions. Furthermore, resulting models optimizing our devised white-box reward show competitive performances with strong open-source RMs in alignment benchmarks. We highlight its potential usage as a simple but strong reward baseline for the LLM alignment, not requiring explicit human feedback dataset and RM training. Our code is available at https://github.com/naver-ai/rethinking-proxy-reward.
中摘: 

通过代理奖励建模从人类反馈中学习已被用于使大语言模型（LLMs）与人类价值观对齐。然而，通过该代理奖励模型（RM）实现可靠训练并非易事，且其行为如同黑盒。在本文中，我们通过将可解释特征构建成白盒奖励函数，以“反向奖励工程”的方式探讨代理奖励在 LLM 对齐中的作用。我们的目标是，通过在强化学习（RL）中使用代理奖励训练模型，建立代理奖励信号与真实（gold）奖励信号之间的单调关系，从而复现真实奖励信号。研究结果表明，成功模拟真实奖励信号需要针对开放式问题生成长度足够且内容相关的回复，同时也要确保针对封闭式问题的回复一致性。此外，优化我们设计的白盒奖励的模型在对齐基准测试中表现出与强劲开源奖励模型（RMs）相匹敌的性能。我们强调其作为大语言模型对齐的简单而强大的奖励基线的潜在用途，无需依赖显式的人类反馈数据集和奖励模型训练。我们的代码托管于 https://github.com/naver-ai/rethinking-proxy-reward。

[No.432] Reuse Your Rewards: Reward Model Transfer for Zero-Shot Cross-Lingual Alignment
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 重用您的奖励：奖励模型转移在零样本跨语言对齐中的应用
类别:

英摘: Aligning language models (LMs) based on human-annotated preference data is a crucial step in obtaining practical and performant LM-based systems. However, multilingual human preference data are difficult to obtain at scale, making it challenging to extend this framework to diverse languages. In this work, we evaluate a simple approach for zero-shot cross-lingual alignment, where a reward model is trained on preference data in one source language and directly applied to other target languages. [No.433] Reward Difference Optimization For Sample Reweighting In Offline RLHF
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 离线RLHF中基于样本重加权的奖励差异优化
类别: 
作者: Shiqi Wang;Zhengze Zhang;Rui Zhao;Fei Tan;Nguyen Cam-Tu;
机构: Nanjing University;Chinese University of Hong Kong;New Jersey Institute of Technology;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.115/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09385
英摘: With the rapid advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), aligning LLMs with human preferences become increasingly important. Although Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF) proves effective, it is complicated and highly resource-intensive. As such, offline RLHF has been introduced as an alternative solution, which directly optimizes LLMs with ranking losses on a fixed preference dataset. Current offline RLHF only captures the "ordinal relationship" between responses, overlooking the crucial aspect of how much one is preferred over the others. To address this issue, we propose a simple yet effective solution called Reward Difference Optimization, shorted as RDO. Specifically, we introduce reward difference coefficients to reweigh sample pairs in offline RLHF. We then develop a difference model which captures rich interactions between a pair of responses for predicting these difference coefficients. Experiments with 7B LLMs on the HH and TL;DR datasets substantiate the effectiveness of our method in both automatic metrics and human evaluation, thereby highlighting its potential for aligning LLMs with human intent and values
中摘: 

随着大型语言模型（LLM）的快速发展，将 LLM 与人类偏好对齐变得越来越重要。尽管具有人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）被证明是有效的，但它既复杂又高度依赖资源。因此，离线 RLHF 作为一种替代方案被提出，它利用固定偏好数据集上的排名损失直接优化 LLM。当前的离线 RLHF 仅能捕捉响应之间的“序数关系”，却忽略了响应之间偏好程度差异这一关键方面。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种简单而有效的解决方案，称为奖励差异优化（Reward Difference Optimization），简称 RDO。具体而言，我们引入了奖励差异系数，以重新加权离线 RLHF 中的样本对。随后，我们开发了一个差异模型，该模型捕捉一对响应之间丰富的交互信息，用于预测这些差异系数。在 HH 和 TL;DR 数据集上使用 7B 规模 LLM 进行的实验证实了该方法在自动指标和人类评估方面的有效性，从而突显了其在使 LLM 与人类意图和价值观对齐方面的潜力。
