[No.435] Rewarding What Matters: Step-by-Step Reinforcement Learning for Task-Oriented Dialogue
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 以结果为导向：面向任务的对话逐步强化学习
类别: 
作者: Huifang Du;Shuqin Li;Minghao Wu;Xuejing Feng;Yuan-Fang Li;Haofen Wang;
机构: Tongji University;Hangzhou Dianzi University;Monash University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.472/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14457
英摘: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful approach to enhance task-oriented dialogue (TOD) systems. However, existing RL methods tend to mainly focus on generation tasks, such as dialogue policy learning (DPL) or response generation (RG), while neglecting dialogue state tracking (DST) for understanding. This narrow focus limits the systems to achieve globally optimal performance by overlooking the interdependence between understanding and generation. Additionally, RL methods face challenges with sparse and delayed rewards, which complicates training and optimization. To address these issues, we extend RL into both understanding and generation tasks by introducing step-by-step rewards throughout the token generation. The understanding reward increases as more slots are correctly filled in DST, while the generation reward grows with the accurate inclusion of user requests. Our approach provides a balanced optimization aligned with task completion. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach effectively enhances the performance of TOD systems and achieves new state-of-the-art results on three widely used datasets, including MultiWOZ2.0, MultiWOZ2.1, and In-Car. Our approach also shows superior few-shot ability in low-resource settings compared to current models.
中摘: 

强化学习（RL）是增强任务导向对话（TOD）系统的有力方法。然而，现有的 RL 方法往往主要关注生成任务，如对话策略学习（DPL）或响应生成（RG），而忽视用于理解的对话状态追踪（DST）。这种狭隘的关注点限制了系统实现全局最优性能，因为它忽视了理解与生成之间的相互依赖性。此外，RL 方法面临奖励稀疏和延迟的挑战，这使得训练和优化变得复杂。为了解决这些问题，我们将 RL 扩展到理解和生成任务中，通过在 token 生成过程中引入逐步奖励来实现。随着 DST 中正确填充的槽位增多，理解奖励随之增加，而生成奖励则随着用户请求被准确包含而增长。我们的方法提供了一种与任务完成目标相一致的平衡优化。实验结果表明，我们的方法有效提升了 TOD 系统的性能，并在三个广泛使用的数据集上取得了新的最先进结果，包括 MultiWOZ2.0、MultiWOZ2.1 和 In-Car。与现有模型相比，我们的方法在低资源设置下也显示出优越的少样本能力。

[No.436] Self-Explore: Enhancing Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Fine-grained Rewards
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 自我探索：通过精细粒度的奖励提升语言模型的数学推理能力
类别: 
作者: Hyeonbin Hwang;Doyoung Kim;Seungone Kim;Seonghyeon Ye;Minjoon Seo;

英摘: Training on large amounts of rationales (i.e., CoT Fine-tuning) is effective at improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, acquiring human-authored rationales or augmenting rationales from proprietary models is costly and not scalable. In this paper, we study the problem of whether LLMs could self-improve their reasoning capabilities. 在大量推理依据上进行训练（即思维链微调）对于提升大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力是有效的。然而，获取人工撰写的推理依据或利用专有模型增强推理依据成本高昂且不可扩展。在本文中，我们研究了大语言模型是否能够实现推理能力的自我提升。为此，我们提出了 Self-Explore，该方法要求 LLM 探索推理依据中的第一个错误步骤（即第一个“坑”），并将此类信号作为细粒度奖励以进行进一步改进。在 GSM8K 和 MATH 测试集上，与监督微调（SFT）相比，Self-Explore 在三种 LLM 上的平均提升幅度分别为 11.57% 和 2.89%。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/hbin0701/Self-Explore 获取。

[No.437] Semi-Supervised Reward Modeling via Iterative Self-Training
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward
标题: 半监督奖励建模通过迭代自我训练
类别: 
作者: Yifei He;Haoxiang Wang;Ziyan Jiang;Alexandros Papangelis;Han Zhao;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Amazon;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.434/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06903
英摘: Reward models (RM) capture the values and preferences of humans and play a central role in Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF) to align pretrained large language models (LLMs). Traditionally, training these models relies on extensive human-annotated preference data, which poses significant challenges in terms of scalability and cost. To overcome these limitations, we propose Semi-Supervised Reward Modeling (SSRM), an approach that enhances RM training using unlabeled data. Given an unlabeled dataset, SSRM involves three key iterative steps: pseudo-labeling unlabeled examples, selecting high-confidence examples through a confidence threshold, and supervised finetuning on the refined dataset. Across extensive experiments on various model configurations, we demonstrate that SSRM significantly improves reward models without incurring additional labeling costs. Notably, SSRM can achieve performance comparable to models trained entirely on labeled data of equivalent volumes. Overall, SSRM substantially reduces the dependency on large volumes of human-annotated data, thereby decreasing the overall cost and time involved in training effective reward models.
中摘: 

奖励模型（RM）捕捉人类的价值观和偏好，并在基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）中发挥核心作用，旨在对齐预训练大型语言模型（LLMs）。传统上，训练这些模型依赖于大量人工标注的偏好数据，这在可扩展性和成本方面带来了显著挑战。为克服这些限制，我们提出了半监督奖励建模（SSRM），这是一种利用无标签数据改进奖励模型训练的方法。给定一个无标签数据集，SSRM 包含三个关键迭代步骤：对无标签样本进行伪标注，通过置信度阈值筛选高置信度样本，并在筛选后的数据集上进行监督微调。在各种模型配置的广泛实验中，我们证明 SSRM 能显著提升奖励模型性能，且无需产生额外的标注成本。值得注意的是，SSRM 能够实现与使用同等规模全标签数据训练的模型相当的性能。总体而言，SSRM 大幅降低了对大量人工标注数据的依赖，从而减少了训练有效奖励模型所需的总体成本和时间。

[No.438] SeRTS: Self-Rewarding Tree Search for Biomedical Retrieval-Augmented Generation
来源: EMNLP 2024

标题: SeRTS：自我奖励树搜索在生物医学检索增强生成中的应用

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.71/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11258
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great potential in the biomedical domain with the advancement of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). However, existing retrieval-augmented approaches face challenges in addressing diverse queries and documents, particularly for medical knowledge queries, resulting in sub-optimal performance. To address these limitations, we propose a novel plug-and-play LLM-based retrieval method called Self-Rewarding Tree Search (SeRTS) based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and a self-rewarding paradigm. By combining the reasoning capabilities of LLMs with the effectiveness of tree search, SeRTS boosts the zero-shot performance of retrieving high-quality and informative results for RAG. We further enhance retrieval performance by fine-tuning LLMs with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) objectives using the trajectories collected by SeRTS as feedback. Controlled experiments using the BioASQ-QA dataset with GPT-3.5-Turbo and LLama2-7b demonstrate that our method significantly improves the performance of the BM25 retriever and surpasses the strong baseline of self-reflection in both efficiency and scalability. Moreover, SeRTS generates higher-quality feedback for PPO training than self-reflection. Our proposed method effectively adapts LLMs to document retrieval tasks, enhancing their ability to retrieve highly relevant documents for RAG in the context of medical knowledge queries. This work presents a significant step forward in leveraging LLMs for accurate and comprehensive biomedical question answering.
中摘: 

随着检索增强生成（RAG）技术的进步，大型语言模型（LLMs）在生物医学领域已展现出巨大的潜力。然而，现有的检索增强方法在应对多样化的查询和文档方面面临挑战，尤其是针对医学知识查询时，导致性能未达最优。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种新颖的即插即用型大型语言模型检索方法，名为自我奖励树搜索（Self-Rewarding Tree Search, SeRTS），该方法基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）和自我奖励范式。SeRTS 通过结合大型语言模型的推理能力与树搜索的有效性，显著提升了检索增强生成（RAG）中检索高质量、高信息量结果的零样本性能。我们进一步利用 SeRTS 收集的轨迹作为反馈，通过近端策略优化（PPO）目标对大型语言模型进行微调，从而进一步提升检索性能。在 BioASQ-QA 数据集上，利用 GPT-3.5-Turbo 和 Llama2-7b 进行的受控实验表明，我们的方法显著提升了 BM25 检索器的性能，并在效率和可扩展性方面超越了强大的自我反思基线。此外，相比于自我反思，SeRTS 能生成质量更高的 PPO 训练反馈。我们提出的方法有效地将大型语言模型适应于文档检索任务，增强了其在医学知识查询背景下为检索增强生成（RAG）检索高度相关文档的能力。本工作是在利用大型语言模型实现准确且全面的生物医学问答方面迈出的重要一步。

[No.440] Weak Reward Model Transforms Generative Models into Robust Causal Event Extraction Systems
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: reward

英摘: The inherent ambiguity of cause and effect boundaries poses a challenge in evaluating causal event extraction tasks. Traditional metrics like Exact Match and BertScore poorly reflect model performance, so we trained evaluation models to approximate human evaluation, achieving high agreement. We used them to perform Reinforcement Learning with extraction models to align them with human preference, prioritising semantic understanding. We successfully explored our approach through multiple datasets, including transferring an evaluator trained on one dataset to another as a way to decrease the reliance on human-annotated data. In that vein, we also propose a weak-to-strong supervision method that uses a fraction of the annotated data to train an evaluation model while still achieving high performance in training an RL model. Our code is available at https://github.com/oyarsa/event_extraction/tree/causal-event-extraction.
中摘: 

因果边界固有的模糊性给评估因果事件抽取任务带来了挑战。诸如精确匹配和 BertScore 之类的传统指标无法准确反映模型性能，因此我们训练了评估模型以近似人类评估，实现了高一致性。我们利用这些模型与抽取模型进行强化学习，使其与人类偏好保持一致，优先考虑语义理解。我们已通过多个数据集成功探索了该方法，包括将在一个数据集上训练的评估器迁移至另一个数据集，以此降低对人工标注数据的依赖。顺着这一思路，我们还提出了一种由弱到强的监督方法，该方法仅使用一部分标注数据来训练评估模型，同时仍能在训练强化学习模型时取得高性能。我们的代码托管在 https://github.com/oyarsa/event_extraction/tree/causal-event-extraction。

[No.442] PRCA: Fitting Black-Box Large Language Models for Retrieval Question Answering via Pluggable Reward-Driven Contextual Adapter
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: reward
标题: 普适计算协会：通过可插拔奖励驱动上下文适配器为黑盒大型语言模型进行检索问答调整
类别: 
作者: Haoyan Yang;Zhitao Li;Yong Zhang;Jianzong Wang;Ning Cheng;Ming Li;Jing Xiao;
机构: United International College;PingAn Technology;Texas A&M University;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gI11vXg1W4
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.18347
英摘: The Retrieval Question Answering (ReQA) task employs the retrieval-augmented framework, composed of a retriever and generator. The generator formulates the answer based on the documents retrieved by the retriever. Incorporating Large Language Models (LLMs) as generators is beneficial due to their advanced QA capabilities, but they are typically too large to be fine-tuned with budget constraints while some of them are only accessible via APIs. To tackle this issue and further improve ReQA performance, we propose a trainable Pluggable Reward-Driven Contextual Adapter (PRCA), keeping the generator as a black box. Positioned between the retriever and generator in a Pluggable manner, PRCA refines the retrieved information by operating in a token-autoregressive strategy via maximizing rewards of the reinforcement learning phase. Our experiments validate PRCA's effectiveness in enhancing ReQA performance on three datasets by up to 20% improvement to fit black-box LLMs into existing frameworks, demonstrating its considerable potential in the LLMs era.
中摘:

[No.446] Sequence to Sequence Reward Modeling: Improving RLHF by Language Feedback
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: reward
标题: 序列到序列奖励建模：通过语言反馈改进RLHF
类别: ai alignment
作者: Jiayi Zhou;Jiaming Ji;Josef Dai;Yaodong Yang;
机构: Peking University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34992
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00162
英摘: Aligning the behavior of Large language models (LLMs) with human intentions and values remains a critical challenge. Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) aligns LLMs by training a reward model (RM) on human preferences and fine-tuning the LLMs to maximize RM feedback. Despite its effectiveness and popularity, RLHF is prone to biased local optimization. It means RM fails to provide feedback that accurately aligns with human preference, causing LLMs to explore unexpected generalizations, and failing to achieve alignment objectives. To mitigate this issue, we propose a novel \textit{sequence-to-sequence (seq2seq) reward modeling} method. Its key insight is that learning from language feedback rather than scalar feedback improves RLHF without additional annotations. We replaced the reward modeling target from binary maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) with sequence MLE. This method enables richer and fine-grained language feedback without additional annotations, models, or training stages. Our experiments demonstrated its effectiveness, specifically, reducing the refusal-to-response paradigm in single-turn safety dialogues and the long-response bias in text summarization tasks. We provide further analysis that seq2seq RM improves RLHF performance across 2B and 7B LLMs on 3 NLP tasks, achieving an average win rate of 76.9\%. We further show that seq2seq RM can still improve the performance of RLHF under out-of-distribution prompts.
中摘: 

使大型语言模型（LLM）的行为与人类意图和价值观保持一致仍是一个关键挑战。来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）通过在人类偏好上训练奖励模型（RM），并微调大型语言模型以最大化 RM 反馈来实现对齐。尽管其有效且受欢迎，RLHF 易出现偏差的局部优化。这意味着奖励模型未能提供准确对齐人类偏好的反馈，导致大型语言模型探索出意外的泛化结果，从而无法实现对齐目标。为缓解这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的“序列到序列（seq2seq）奖励建模”方法。其关键见解在于，从语言反馈中学习而非标量反馈学习，可以在无需额外标注的情况下提升 RLHF 效果。我们将奖励建模目标从二元最大似然估计（MLE）替换为序列最大似然估计。该方法能够在无需额外标注、模型或训练阶段的情况下，提供更为丰富和细粒度的语言反馈。实验证明了其有效性，具体表现为减少了单轮安全对话中的拒答模式以及文本摘要任务中的长响应偏差。我们提供了进一步分析，表明 seq2seq RM 在三个 NLP 任务上提升了 2B 和 7B 参数规模大型语言模型的 RLHF 性能，实现了 76.9% 的平均胜率。我们还进一步表明，seq2seq RM 在分布外提示下仍能提升 RLHF 的性能。

[No.448] MVReward: Better Aligning and Evaluating Multi-View Diffusion Models with Human Preferences
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: reward
标题: MVReward：更好地与人类偏好对齐和评估多视图扩散模型
类别: computer vision vii
作者: Weitao Wang;Haoran Xu;Yuxiao Yang;Zhifang Liu;Jun Meng;Haoqian Wang;
机构: Tsinghua University;Zhejiang University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32851
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06614
英摘: Recent years have witnessed remarkable progress in 3D content generation. However, corresponding evaluation methods struggle to keep pace. Automatic approaches have proven challenging to align with human preferences, and the mixed comparison of text- and image-driven methods often leads to unfair evaluations. In this paper, we present a comprehensive framework to better align and evaluate multi-view diffusion models with human preferences. To begin with, we first collect and filter a standardized image prompt set from DALL$\cdot$E and Objaverse, which we then use to generate multi-view assets with several multi-view diffusion models. Through a systematic ranking pipeline on these assets, we obtain a human annotation dataset with 16k expert pairwise comparisons and train a reward model, coined MVReward, to effectively encode human preferences. With MVReward, image-driven 3D methods can be evaluated against each other in a more fair and transparent manner. Building on this, we further propose Multi-View Preference Learning (MVP), a plug-and-play multi-view diffusion tuning strategy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MVReward can serve as a reliable metric and MVP consistently enhances the alignment of multi-view diffusion models with human preferences.
中摘: 

近年来，3D 内容生成领域取得了显著进展。然而，相应的评估方法却难以跟上步伐。自动方法被证明难以与人类偏好对齐，而文本驱动与图像驱动方法的混合比较往往导致评估不公。在本文中，我们提出了一种综合框架，旨在更好地使多视图扩散模型与人类偏好对齐并进行评估。首先，我们从 DALL·E 和 Objaverse 收集并筛选出一个标准化的图像提示词集，随后利用该提示词集结合多个多视图扩散模型生成多视图资源。通过对这些资源进行系统的排序流程，我们获得了包含 1.6 万项专家成对比较的人类标注数据集，并训练了一个名为 MVReward 的奖励模型，以有效编码人类偏好。借助 MVReward，图像驱动的 3D 方法得以在更加公平和透明的方式下相互评估。在此基础上，我们进一步提出了多视图偏好学习（MVP），这是一种即插即用的多视图扩散微调策略。大量实验表明，MVReward 可作为一种可靠的评估指标，而 MVP 则持续增强了多视图扩散模型与人类偏好的对齐程度。

[No.450] Efficient Language-instructed Skill Acquisition via Reward-Policy Co-Evolution
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: reward
标题: 通过奖励-策略共进化实现高效的语言指导技能获取
类别: intelligent robots
作者: Changxin Huang;Yanbin Chang;Junfan Lin;Junyang Liang;Runhao Zeng;Jianqiang Li;
机构: Shenzhen University;Pengcheng Laboratory;Shenzhen MSU-BIT University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33597
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13492
英摘: The ability to autonomously explore and resolve tasks with minimal human guidance is crucial for the self-development of embodied intelligence. Although reinforcement learning methods can largely ease human effort, it's challenging to design reward functions for real-world tasks, especially for high-dimensional robotic control, due to complex relationships among joints and tasks. Recent advancements large language models (LLMs) enable automatic reward function design. However, approaches evaluate reward functions by re-training policies from scratch placing an undue burden on the reward function, expecting it to be effective throughout the whole policy improvement process. We argue for a more practical strategy in robotic autonomy, focusing on refining existing policies with policy-dependent reward functions rather than a universal one. To this end, we propose a novel reward-policy co-evolution framework where the reward function and the learned policy benefit from each other's progressive on-the-fly improvements, resulting in more efficient and higher-performing skill acquisition. Specifically, the reward evolution process translates the robot's previous best reward function, descriptions of tasks and environment into text inputs. These inputs are used to query LLMs to generate a dynamic amount of reward function candidates, ensuring continuous improvement at each round of evolution. For policy evolution, our method generates new policy populations by hybridizing historically optimal and random policies. Through an improved Bayesian optimization, our approach efficiently and robustly identifies the most capable and plastic reward-policy combination, which then proceeds to the next round of co-evolution. Despite using less data, our approach demonstrates an average normalized improvement of 95.3% across various high-dimensional robotic skill learning tasks.
中摘: 

以最少的人类指导自主探索并解决任务的能力，对于具身智能的自主发展至关重要。尽管强化学习方法在很大程度上减轻了人力投入，但由于关节与任务之间的复杂关系，为真实世界任务（尤其是高维机器人控制）设计奖励函数仍然充满挑战。近年来大型语言模型（LLMs）的进展使得自动设计奖励函数成为可能。然而，现有方法通过从头开始重新训练策略来评估奖励函数，这给奖励函数施加了不当的负担，期望它在整个策略改进过程中都保持有效。我们主张在机器人自主领域采取更实际的策略，即利用与策略相关的奖励函数来改进现有策略，而非使用通用奖励函数。为此，我们提出了一种新颖的奖励 - 策略协同进化框架，在该框架中，奖励函数与已学习到的策略能够受益于彼此的渐进式即时改进，从而实现更高效、性能更高的技能获取。具体而言，奖励进化过程将机器人之前的最佳奖励函数以及任务和环境的描述转化为文本输入。这些输入用于查询大型语言模型，生成动态数量的奖励函数候选，以确保每一轮进化中的持续改进。对于策略进化，我们的方法通过混合历史最优策略与随机策略来生成新的策略种群。通过改进的贝叶斯优化，我们的方法能够高效且鲁棒地识别出最具能力且可塑性强的奖励 - 策略组合，随后该组合将进入下一轮协同进化。尽管使用的数据更少，但在各种高维机器人技能学习任务中，我们的方法展现了平均 95.3% 的归一化提升。

[No.451] Bootstrapped Reward Shaping
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: reward
标题: 自助式奖励塑形
类别: machine learning i
作者: Jacob Adamczyk;Volodymyr Makarenko;Stas Tiomkin;Rahul V. Kulkarni;
机构: University of Massachusetts Boston;NSF Institute for Artificial Intelligence and Fundamental Interactions;San José State University;Texas Tech University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33679
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00989
英摘: In reinforcement learning, especially in sparse-reward domains, many environment steps are required to observe reward information. In order to increase the frequency of such observations, "potential-based reward shaping" (PBRS) has been proposed as a method of providing a more dense reward signal while leaving the optimal policy invariant. However, the required "potential function" must be carefully designed with task-dependent knowledge to not deter training performance. In this work, we propose a "bootstrapped" method of reward shaping, termed BSRS, in which the agent's current estimate of the state-value function acts as the potential function for PBRS. We provide convergence proofs for the tabular setting, give insights into training dynamics for deep RL, and show that the proposed method improves training speed in the Atari suite.
中摘: 

在强化学习中，尤其是在稀疏奖励领域，需要大量的环境步数才能观察到奖励信号。为了增加此类观察的频率，“基于势函数的奖励塑形”（PBRS）被提出作为一种在保持最优策略不变的同时提供更稠密奖励信号的方法。然而，所需的“势函数”必须结合任务特定知识进行精心设计，以免阻碍训练性能。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种名为 BSRS 的“自举”奖励塑形方法，其中智能体对状态价值函数的当前估计充当 PBRS 的势函数。我们提供了表格设置下的收敛性证明，深入分析了深度强化学习的训练动态，并表明所提出的方法提高了 Atari 游戏套件中的训练速度。

[No.452] DiffExp: Efficient Exploration in Reward Fine-tuning for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: reward
标题: DiffExp：高效探索奖励微调中的文本到图像扩散模型的探索方法
类别: machine learning i
作者: Daewon Chae;June Suk Choi;Jinkyu Kim;Kimin Lee;
机构: Korea University;Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33723
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14070
英摘: Fine-tuning text-to-image diffusion models to maximize rewards has proven effective for enhancing model performance. However, reward fine-tuning methods often suffer from slow convergence due to online sample generation. Therefore, obtaining diverse samples with strong reward signals is crucial for improving sample efficiency and overall performance. In this work, we introduce DiffExp, a simple yet effective exploration strategy for reward fine-tuning of text-to-image models. Our approach employs two key strategies: (a) dynamically adjusting the scale of classifier-free guidance to enhance sample diversity, and (b) randomly weighting phrases of the text prompt to exploit high-quality reward signals. We demonstrate that these strategies significantly enhance exploration during online sample generation, improving the sample efficiency of recent reward fine-tuning methods, such as DDPO and AlignProp.
中摘: 

微调文生图扩散模型以最大化奖励已被证明能有效提升模型性能。然而，奖励微调方法常因在线样本生成而面临收敛缓慢的问题。因此，获取具有强奖励信号的多样化样本对于提高样本效率和整体性能至关重要。在本工作中，我们提出了 DiffExp，一种用于文生图模型奖励微调的简单而有效的探索策略。该方法采用两个关键策略：(a) 动态调整无分类器引导的尺度以增强样本多样性，(b) 随机加权文本提示中的短语以利用高质量奖励信号。我们证明，这些策略显著增强了在线样本生成过程中的探索能力，提高了 DDPO 和 AlignProp 等近期奖励微调方法的样本效率。

[No.453] The Distributional Reward Critic Framework for Reinforcement Learning Under Perturbed Rewards
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: reward
标题: 基于扰动奖励的强化学习分布奖励评论框架
类别: machine learning i
作者: Xi Chen;Zhihui Zhu;Andrew Perrault;
机构: Ohio State University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33745
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05710
英摘: The reward signal plays a central role in defining the desired behaviors of agents in reinforcement learning (RL). Rewards collected from realistic environments could be perturbed, corrupted, or noisy due to an adversary, sensor error, or because they come from subjective human feedback. Thus, it is important to construct agents that can learn under such rewards. Existing methodologies for this problem make strong assumptions, including that the perturbation is known in advance, clean rewards are accessible, or that the perturbation preserves the optimal policy. We study a new, more general, class of unknown perturbations, and introduce a distributional reward critic framework for estimating reward distributions and perturbations during training. Our proposed methods are compatible with any RL algorithm. Despite their increased generality, we show that they achieve comparable or better rewards than existing methods in a variety of environments, including those with clean rewards. Under the challenging and generalized perturbations we study, we win/tie the highest return in 44/48 tested settings (compared to 11/48 for the best baseline). Our results broaden and deepen our ability to perform RL in reward-perturbed environments.
中摘: 

奖励信号在定义强化学习（RL）中智能体的期望行为方面起着核心作用。从真实环境中收集到的奖励可能会因对抗者、传感器误差或源于主观人类反馈而受到扰动、污染或带有噪声。因此，构建能够在这些奖励条件下学习的智能体至关重要。针对此问题的现有方法通常依赖于较强的假设，包括假设扰动预先已知、纯净奖励可得，或扰动保留了最优策略。我们研究了一类新的、更通用的未知扰动，并提出了一种分布奖励评估框架，用于在训练期间估计奖励分布和扰动。我们提出的方法与任何 RL 算法兼容。尽管通用性有所增强，但我们表明，在各种环境中（包括纯净奖励的环境），它们能取得与现有方法相当甚至更好的奖励。在我们研究的具有挑战性的广义扰动条件下，我们在 48 个测试场景中有 44 个获得了最高回报或与最高回报并列（相比之下，最佳基线仅为 11/48）。我们的结果拓宽并加深了我们在奖励受扰动环境中进行强化学习的能力。

来源: AAAI 2024

英摘: Toddlers evolve from free exploration with sparse feedback to exploiting prior experiences for goal-directed learning with denser rewards. Central to our inquiry is the transition from sparse to potential-based dense rewards, which share optimal strategies regardless of reward changes. [No.465] UNEX-RL: Reinforcing Long-Term Rewards in Multi-Stage Recommender Systems with UNidirectional EXecution
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: reward
标题: UNEX-RL：在多阶段推荐系统中通过单向执行强化长期奖励

作者: Gengrui Zhang;Yao Wang;Xiaoshuang Chen;Hongyi Qian;Kaiqiao Zhan;Ben Wang;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06470
英摘: In recent years, there has been a growing interest in utilizing reinforcement learning (RL) to optimize long-term rewards in recommender systems. Since industrial recommender systems are typically designed as multi-stage systems, RL methods with a single agent face challenges when optimizing multiple stages simultaneously. The reason is that different stages have different observation spaces, and thus cannot be modeled by a single agent. To address this issue, we propose a novel UNidirectional-EXecution-based multi-agent Reinforcement Learning (UNEX-RL) framework to reinforce the long-term rewards in multi-stage recommender systems. We show that the unidirectional execution is a key feature of multi-stage recommender systems, bringing new challenges to the applications of multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), namely the observation dependency and the cascading effect. To tackle these challenges, we provide a cascading information chain (CIC) method to separate the independent observations from action-dependent observations and use CIC to train UNEX-RL effectively. We also discuss practical variance reduction techniques for UNEX-RL. Finally, we show the effectiveness of UNEX-RL on both public datasets and an online recommender system with over 100 million users. Specifically, UNEX-RL reveals a 0.558% increase in users' usage time compared with single-agent RL algorithms in online A/B experiments, highlighting the effectiveness of UNEX-RL in industrial recommender systems.
中摘: 

近年来，利用强化学习（RL）优化推荐系统中的长期奖励受到了越来越多的关注。由于工业推荐系统通常被设计为多阶段系统，基于单智能体的强化学习方法在同时优化多个阶段时面临挑战。这是因为不同阶段拥有不同的观测空间，因此无法由单个智能体进行建模。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的基于单向执行的多元智能体强化学习（UNEX-RL）框架，旨在优化多阶段推荐系统中的长期奖励。我们指出，单向执行是多阶段推荐系统的一个关键特征，它为多智能体强化学习（MARL）的应用带来了新的挑战，即观测依赖性和级联效应。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种级联信息链（CIC）方法，用于分离独立观测与动作依赖观测，并利用 CIC 有效地训练 UNEX-RL。我们还探讨了适用于 UNEX-RL 的实际方差缩减技术。最后，我们在公开数据集和一个拥有超过 1 亿用户的在线推荐系统上展示了 UNEX-RL 的有效性。具体而言，与单智能体强化学习算法相比，UNEX-RL 在在线 A/B 实验中显示出用户使用时长的 0.558% 的提升，突显了 UNEX-RL 在工业推荐系统中的有效性。

[No.466] Principal-Agent Reward Shaping in MDPs
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: reward
标题: MDP中的委托-代理奖励塑造
类别: game theory and economic paradigms
作者: Omer Ben-Porat;Yishay Mansour;Michal Moshkovitz;Boaz Taitler;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.00298
英摘: Principal-agent problems arise when one party acts on behalf of another, leading to conflicts of interest. The economic literature has extensively studied principal-agent problems, and recent work has extended this to more complex scenarios such as Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). In this paper, we further explore this line of research by investigating how reward shaping under budget constraints can improve the principal's utility. We study a two-player Stackelberg game where the principal and the agent have different reward functions, and the agent chooses an MDP policy for both players. The principal offers an additional reward to the agent, and the agent picks their policy selfishly to maximize their reward, which is the sum of the original and the offered reward. Our results establish the NP-hardness of the problem and offer polynomial approximation algorithms for two classes of instances: Stochastic trees and deterministic decision processes with a finite horizon.

委托 - 代理问题产生于一方代表另一方行动时，从而导致利益冲突。经济文献已对委托 - 代理问题进行了广泛研究，近期工作已将其扩展到更复杂的场景，如马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）。在本文中，我们进一步探索这一研究方向，通过研究预算约束下的奖励塑造如何改善委托人的效用。我们研究了一个二人斯坦格尔博弈，其中委托人和代理人拥有不同的奖励函数，且代理人选择针对双方的 MDP 策略。委托人向代理人提供额外奖励，代理人则出于自利选择其策略以最大化其总奖励，该总奖励为原始奖励与所提供奖励之和。我们的研究结果确立了该问题的 NP 难性，并为两类实例提供了多项式近似算法：随机树和具有有限时域的确定性决策过程。

[No.467] Learning Optimal Advantage from Preferences and Mistaking It for Reward
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: reward
标题: 从偏好中学习最优优势并将之误认为奖励
类别: humans and ai
作者: W. Bradley Knox;Stephane Hatgis-Kessell;Sigurdur Orn Adalgeirsson;Serena Booth;Anca Dragan;Peter Stone;Scott Niekum;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;Google;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;University of California, Berkeley;Sony;University of Massachusetts Amherst;

链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/28870
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.02456
英摘: We consider algorithms for learning reward functions from human preferences over pairs of trajectory segments, as used in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). Most recent work assumes that human preferences are generated based only upon the reward accrued within those segments, or their partial return. Recent work casts doubt on the validity of this assumption, proposing an alternative preference model based upon regret. We investigate the consequences of assuming preferences are based upon partial return when they actually arise from regret. We argue that the learned function is an approximation of the optimal advantage function, $\hat{A^*_r}$, not a reward function. We find that if a specific pitfall is addressed, this incorrect assumption is not particularly harmful, resulting in a highly shaped reward function. Nonetheless, this incorrect usage of $\hat{A^*_r}$ is less desirable than the appropriate and simpler approach of greedy maximization of $\hat{A^*_r}$. From the perspective of the regret preference model, we also provide a clearer interpretation of fine tuning contemporary large language models with RLHF. This paper overall provides insight regarding why learning under the partial return preference model tends to work so well in practice, despite it conforming poorly to how humans give preferences.
中摘: 

我们研究了基于人类对成对轨迹片段偏好来学习奖励函数的算法，这正是人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）中所采用的方法。大多数近期工作假设人类偏好仅根据这些片段内累积获得的奖励或其部分回报生成。近期工作对这一假设的有效性提出了质疑，并提出了一种基于后悔的替代偏好模型。我们探究了在偏好实际上源于后悔却假设其基于部分回报的情况下产生的后果。我们认为，学习到的函数是对最优优势函数 $\hat{A^*_r}$ 的近似，而不是奖励函数。我们发现，如果解决了特定陷阱，这种错误假设并不会特别有害，结果会导致一个高度塑造的奖励函数。尽管如此，这种对 $\hat{A^*_r}$ 的不当使用不如直接贪婪最大化 $\hat{A^*_r}$ 的恰当且更简单的方法理想。从后悔偏好模型的角度，我们也提供了关于利用 RLHF 微调当代大型语言模型的更清晰解释。总体而言，本文提供了关于为何尽管部分回报偏好模型与人类给出偏好的方式契合度较差，但在实践中学习往往效果显著的见解。

英摘: In this paper, we consider an infinite horizon average reward Markov Decision Process (MDP). [No.471] Episodic Return Decomposition by Difference of Implicitly Assigned Sub-trajectory Reward
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: reward
标题: 基于隐式分配子轨迹奖励差别的 episodic 回报分解
类别: machine learning iii
作者: Haoxin Lin;Hongqiu Wu;Jiaji Zhang;Yihao Sun;Junyin Ye;Yang Yu;
机构: Nanjing University;Polixir Technologies;Pengcheng Laboratory;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/29287
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.10642
英摘: Real-world decision-making problems are usually accompanied by delayed rewards, which affects the sample efficiency of Reinforcement Learning, especially in the extremely delayed case where the only feedback is the episodic reward obtained at the end of an episode. Episodic return decomposition is a promising way to deal with the episodic-reward setting. Several corresponding algorithms have shown remarkable effectiveness of the learned step-wise proxy rewards from return decomposition. However, these existing methods lack either attribution or representation capacity, leading to inefficient decomposition in the case of long-term episodes. In this paper, we propose a novel episodic return decomposition method called Diaster (Difference of implicitly assigned sub-trajectory reward). Diaster decomposes any episodic reward into credits of two divided sub-trajectories at any cut point, and the step-wise proxy rewards come from differences in expectation. We theoretically and empirically verify that the decomposed proxy reward function can guide the policy to be nearly optimal. Experimental results show that our method outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods in terms of both sample efficiency and performance.
中摘: 

现实世界的决策问题通常伴随着延迟奖励，这会影响强化学习的样本效率，尤其是在极度延迟的情况下，唯一的反馈是在一个回合结束时获得的回合奖励。回合回报分解是处理回合奖励设置的一种有前景的方法。几种相应的算法已经证明了从回报分解中学习到的逐步代理奖励的显著有效性。然而，这些现有方法缺乏归因或表征能力，导致在长回合情况下分解效率低下。本文提出了一种新颖的回合回报分解方法，称为 Diaster（隐式分配子轨迹奖励之差）。Diaster 将任何回合奖励在任意切割点分解为两个分割子轨迹的信用，且逐步代理奖励源于期望值的差异。我们通过理论和实证验证了分解后的代理奖励函数能够引导策略达到接近最优。实验结果表明，我们的方法在样本效率和性能方面均优于先前的最先进方法。

In this work, we present the first approach for robustness verification of DRL-based control systems by introducing reward martingales, which offer a rigorous mathematical foundation to characterize the impact of state perturbations on system performance in terms of cumulative rewards. [No.477] Reward Certification for Policy Smoothed Reinforcement Learning
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: reward
标题: 平稳策略强化学习的奖励认证
类别: safe robust and responsible ai track
作者: Ronghui Mu;Leandro Soriano Marcolino;Yanghao Zhang;Tianle Zhang;Xiaowei Huang;Wenjie Ruan;

链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/30139
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.06436
英摘: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has achieved remarkable success in safety-critical areas, but it can be weakened by adversarial attacks. Recent studies have introduced "smoothed policies" in order to enhance its robustness. Yet, it is still challenging to establish a provable guarantee to certify the bound of its total reward. Prior methods relied primarily on computing bounds using Lipschitz continuity or calculating the probability of cumulative reward above specific thresholds. However, these techniques are only suited for continuous perturbations on the RL agent's observations and are restricted to perturbations bounded by the $l_2$-norm. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a general black-box certification method capable of directly certifying the cumulative reward of the smoothed policy under various $l_p$-norm bounded perturbations. Furthermore, we extend our methodology to certify perturbations on action spaces. Our approach leverages f-divergence to measure the distinction between the original distribution and the perturbed distribution, subsequently determining the certification bound by solving a convex optimisation problem. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis and run sufficient experiments in multiple environments. Our results show that our method not only improves the certified lower bound of mean cumulative reward but also demonstrates better efficiency than state-of-the-art techniques.
中摘:

[No.478] Solving Non-rectangular Reward-Robust MDPs via Frequency Regularization

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.01107
英摘: In robust Markov decision processes (RMDPs), it is assumed that the reward and the transition dynamics lie in a given uncertainty set. By targeting maximal return under the most adversarial model from that set, RMDPs address performance sensitivity to misspecified environments. Yet, to preserve computational tractability, the uncertainty set is traditionally independently structured for each state. This so-called rectangularity condition is solely motivated by computational concerns. As a result, it lacks a practical incentive and may lead to overly conservative behavior. In this work, we study coupled reward RMDPs where the transition kernel is fixed, but the reward function lies within an $α$-radius from a nominal one. We draw a direct connection between this type of non-rectangular reward-RMDPs and applying policy visitation frequency regularization. We introduce a policy-gradient method and prove its convergence. Numerical experiments illustrate the learned policy's robustness and its less conservative behavior when compared to rectangular uncertainty.

[No.479] Integrating Reward Maximization and Population Estimation: Sequential Decision-Making for Internal Revenue Service Audit Selection
来源: AAAI 2023

英摘: We introduce a new setting, optimize-and-estimate structured bandits. Here, a policy must select a batch of arms, each characterized by its own context, that would allow it to both maximize reward and maintain an accurate (ideally unbiased) population estimate of the reward. This setting is inherent to many public and private sector applications and often requires handling delayed feedback, small data, and distribution shifts. We demonstrate its importance on real data from the United States Internal Revenue Service (IRS). The IRS performs yearly audits of the tax base. Two of its most important objectives are to identify suspected misreporting and to estimate the "tax gap" -- the global difference between the amount paid and true amount owed. Based on a unique collaboration with the IRS, we cast these two processes as a unified optimize-and-estimate structured bandit. We analyze optimize-and-estimate approaches to the IRS problem and propose a novel mechanism for unbiased population estimation that achieves rewards comparable to baseline approaches. This approach has the potential to improve audit efficacy, while maintaining policy-relevant estimates of the tax gap. This has important social consequences given that the current tax gap is estimated at nearly half a trillion dollars. We suggest that this problem setting is fertile ground for further research and we highlight its interesting challenges. The results of this and related research are currently being incorporated into the continual improvement of the IRS audit selection methods.

[No.480] Collusion-Proof and Sybil-Proof Reward Mechanisms for Query Incentive Networks
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 抗合谋和抗Sybil攻击的查询激励机制
类别: game theory and economic paradigms
作者: Youjia Zhang;Pingzhong Tang;

链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25730
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.06061
英摘: This paper explores reward mechanisms for a query incentive network in which agents seek information from social networks. In a query tree issued by the task owner, each agent is rewarded by the owner for contributing to the solution, for instance, solving the task or inviting others to solve it. The reward mechanism determines the reward for each agent and motivates all agents to propagate and report their information truthfully. In particular, the reward cannot exceed the budget set by the task owner. However, our impossibility results demonstrate that a reward mechanism cannot simultaneously achieve Sybil-proof (agents benefit from manipulating multiple fake identities), collusion-proof (multiple agents pretend as a single agent to improve the reward), and other essential properties. In order to address these issues, we propose two novel reward mechanisms. The first mechanism achieves Sybil-proof and collusion-proof, respectively; the second mechanism sacrifices Sybil-proof to achieve the approximate versions of Sybil-proof and collusion-proof. Additionally, we show experimentally that our second reward mechanism outperforms the existing ones.

[No.481] The Effect of Modeling Human Rationality Level on Learning Rewards from Multiple Feedback Types
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 基于建模人类理性水平对多类型反馈学习奖励的影响
类别: humans and ai
作者: Gaurav R. Ghosal;Matthew Zurek;Daniel S. Brown;Anca D. Dragan;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;University of Wisconsin;University of Utah;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25740
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2208.10687
英摘: When inferring reward functions from human behavior (be it demonstrations, comparisons, physical corrections, or e-stops), it has proven useful to model the human as making noisy-rational choices, with a "rationality coefficient" capturing how much noise or entropy we expect to see in the human behavior. Prior work typically sets the rationality level to a constant value, regardless of the type, or quality, of human feedback. However, in many settings, giving one type of feedback (e.g. a demonstration) may be much more difficult than a different type of feedback (e.g. answering a comparison query). Thus, we expect to see more or less noise depending on the type of human feedback. In this work, we advocate that grounding the rationality coefficient in real data for each feedback type, rather than assuming a default value, has a significant positive effect on reward learning. We test this in both simulated experiments and in a user study with real human feedback. We find that overestimating human rationality can have dire effects on reward learning accuracy and regret. We also find that fitting the rationality coefficient to human data enables better reward learning, even when the human deviates significantly from the noisy-rational choice model due to systematic biases. Further, we find that the rationality level affects the informativeness of each feedback type: surprisingly, demonstrations are not always the most informative -- when the human acts very suboptimally, comparisons actually become more informative, even when the rationality level is the same for both. Ultimately, our results emphasize the importance and advantage of paying attention to the assumed human-rationality level, especially when agents actively learn from multiple types of human feedback.
中摘: 

在从人类行为（无论是演示、对比、物理修正还是急停）中推断奖励函数时，将人类建模为做出噪声理性选择已被证明是有益的，其中“理性系数”用于刻画我们预期在人类行为中观察到的噪声或熵的程度。以往的研究通常将理性水平设定为一个常数值，而不管人类反馈的类型或质量如何。然而，在许多情境下，提供某种类型的反馈（例如演示）可能比提供另一种类型的反馈（例如回答比较查询）困难得多。因此，我们期望看到的噪声多少取决于人类反馈的类型。在这项工作中，我们主张将理性系数建立在每种反馈类型的真实数据基础之上，而非假设一个默认值，这对奖励学习具有显著的积极影响。我们通过在模拟实验以及使用真实人类反馈的用户研究中测试了这一点。我们发现，高估人类理性会对奖励学习的准确性和遗憾造成严重影响。我们还发现，将理性系数拟合到人类数据能够实现更好的奖励学习，即使由于系统偏差，人类行为显著偏离了噪声理性选择模型。此外，我们发现理性水平会影响每种反馈类型的信息量：令人惊讶的是，演示并不总是最具信息量的——当人类行为非常次优时，比较实际上变得更具信息量，即使两者的理性水平相同。最终，我们的结果强调了关注所假设的人类理性水平的重要性和优势，尤其是在智能体从多种类型的人类反馈中主动学习时。

[No.483] Exploiting Multiple Abstractions in Episodic RL via Reward Shaping
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 通过奖励塑形利用 episodic RL 中的多种抽象层次
类别: machine learning i
作者: Roberto Cipollone;Giuseppe De Giacomo;Marco Favorito;Luca Iocchi;Fabio Patrizi;
机构: Università degli Studi di Roma “La Sapienza”;University of Oxford;Banca d'Italia;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25881
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.00516
英摘: One major limitation to the applicability of Reinforcement Learning (RL) to many practical domains is the large number of samples required to learn an optimal policy. To address this problem and improve learning efficiency, we consider a linear hierarchy of abstraction layers of the Markov Decision Process (MDP) underlying the target domain. Each layer is an MDP representing a coarser model of the one immediately below in the hierarchy. In this work, we propose a novel form of Reward Shaping where the solution obtained at the abstract level is used to offer rewards to the more concrete MDP, in such a way that the abstract solution guides the learning in the more complex domain. In contrast with other works in Hierarchical RL, our technique has few requirements in the design of the abstract models and it is also tolerant to modeling errors, thus making the proposed approach practical. We formally analyze the relationship between the abstract models and the exploration heuristic induced in the lower-level domain. Moreover, we prove that the method guarantees optimal convergence and we demonstrate its effectiveness experimentally.
中摘: 

强化学习 (RL) 应用于许多实际领域时存在一个主要局限，即学习最优策略需要大量样本。为解决这一问题并提高学习效率，我们考虑了目标域底层马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的抽象层线性层级。每一层都是一个 MDP，代表了层级中紧邻其下的模型更为粗略的抽象。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新颖的奖励塑造形式，利用在抽象层级获得的解为更具体的 MDP 提供奖励，从而使得抽象解能够引导更复杂领域的学习。与分层强化学习中的其他工作相比，我们的技术对抽象模型的设计要求较少，且对建模误差具有容错性，从而使所提出的方法具有实用性。此外，我们形式化分析了抽象模型与在低层领域诱导出的探索启发式之间的关系。最后，我们证明了该方法能够保证最优收敛，并通过实验验证了其有效性。

[No.484] Utilizing Prior Solutions for Reward Shaping and Composition in Entropy-Regularized Reinforcement Learning
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 利用先前解决方案进行熵正则化强化学习中的奖励塑造与组合
类别: machine learning i
作者: Jacob Adamczyk;Argenis Arriojas;Stas Tiomkin;Rahul V. Kulkarni;
机构: University of Massachusetts Boston;San José State University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25817
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.01174
英摘: In reinforcement learning (RL), the ability to utilize prior knowledge from previously solved tasks can allow agents to quickly solve new problems. In some cases, these new problems may be approximately solved by composing the solutions of previously solved primitive tasks (task composition). Otherwise, prior knowledge can be used to adjust the reward function for a new problem, in a way that leaves the optimal policy unchanged but enables quicker learning (reward shaping). In this work, we develop a general framework for reward shaping and task composition in entropy-regularized RL. To do so, we derive an exact relation connecting the optimal soft value functions for two entropy-regularized RL problems with different reward functions and dynamics. We show how the derived relation leads to a general result for reward shaping in entropy-regularized RL. We then generalize this approach to derive an exact relation connecting optimal value functions for the composition of multiple tasks in entropy-regularized RL. We validate these theoretical contributions with experiments showing that reward shaping and task composition lead to faster learning in various settings.
中摘: 

在强化学习（RL）中，能够利用先前已解决任务的先验知识，可以使智能体快速解决新问题。在某些情况下，这些新问题可以通过组合先前解决的基础任务的解决方案来近似解决（任务组合）。否则，可以利用先验知识调整新问题的奖励函数，即在保持最优策略不变的情况下实现更快的学习（奖励塑形）。在本工作中，我们开发了一个适用于熵正则化强化学习（RL）中奖励塑形和任务组合的通用框架。为此，我们推导出了一个精确关系式，将具有不同奖励函数和动力学特性的两个熵正则化强化学习问题的最优软值函数联系起来。我们展示了该推导出的关系式如何导出熵正则化强化学习中奖励塑形的通用结果。随后，我们将该方法推广，推导出一个精确关系式，用于连接熵正则化强化学习中多任务组合的最优值函数。我们通过实验验证了这些理论贡献，结果表明奖励塑形和任务组合在各种设置下均能带来更快的学习。

[No.487] Stochastic Contextual Bandits with Long Horizon Rewards
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 长期奖励下的随机上下文多臂bandit问题
类别: machine learning iii
作者: Yuzhen Qin;Yingcong Li;Fabio Pasqualetti;Maryam Fazel;Samet Oymak;
机构: University of California, Riverside;University of Washington;University of Michigan;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/26140
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.00814
英摘: The growing interest in complex decision-making and language modeling problems highlights the importance of sample-efficient learning over very long horizons. This work takes a step in this direction by investigating contextual linear bandits where the current reward depends on at most $s$ prior actions and contexts (not necessarily consecutive), up to a time horizon of $h$. In order to avoid polynomial dependence on $h$, we propose new algorithms that leverage sparsity to discover the dependence pattern and arm parameters jointly. We consider both the data-poor ($T<h$) and data-rich ($T\ge h$) regimes, and derive respective regret upper bounds $\tilde O(d\sqrt{sT} +\min\{ q, T\})$ and $\tilde O(\sqrt{sdT})$, with sparsity $s$, feature dimension $d$, total time horizon $T$, and $q$ that is adaptive to the reward dependence pattern. Complementing upper bounds, we also show that learning over a single trajectory brings inherent challenges: While the dependence pattern and arm parameters form a rank-1 matrix, circulant matrices are not isometric over rank-1 manifolds and sample complexity indeed benefits from the sparse reward dependence structure. Our results necessitate a new analysis to address long-range temporal dependencies across data and avoid polynomial dependence on the reward horizon $h$. Specifically, we utilize connections to the restricted isometry property of circulant matrices formed by dependent sub-Gaussian vectors and establish new guarantees that are also of independent interest.
中摘: 

随着对复杂决策和语言建模问题日益增长的关注，在极长视界下进行样本高效学习的重要性愈发凸显。本工作朝这一方向迈出了探索性一步，研究了上下文线性赌博机问题，其中当前奖励取决于最多 $s$ 个先前动作和上下文（不必连续），时间视界可达 $h$。为了避免对 $h$ 的多项式依赖，我们提出了利用稀疏性来联合发现依赖模式和臂参数的新算法。我们同时考虑了数据匮乏（$T<h$）和数据丰富（$T\ge h$）两种情形，并推导出了相应的后悔上界 $\tilde O(d\sqrt{sT} +\min\{ q, T\})$ 和 $\tilde O(\sqrt{sdT})$，其中 $s$ 为稀疏度，$d$ 为特征维度，$T$ 为总时间视界，$q$ 为自适应于奖励依赖模式的参数。作为上界分析的补充，我们还表明在单轨迹上进行学习会带来固有挑战：尽管依赖模式和臂参数构成一个秩 -1 矩阵，但循环矩阵在秩 -1 流形上并非等距，且样本复杂度确实得益于稀疏的奖励依赖结构。我们的结果需要一种新的分析方法，以解决跨数据的长程时间依赖问题，并避免对奖励视界 $h$ 的多项式依赖。具体而言，我们利用了由依赖亚高斯向量构成的循环矩阵的受限等距性质之间的联系，并建立了新的保证，这些结果本身也具有独立的学术价值。

[No.488] Reward Poisoning Attacks on Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 离线多智能体强化学习中的奖励中毒攻击
类别: machine learning iv
作者: Young Wu;Jeremy McMahan;Xiaojin Zhu;Qiaomin Xie;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/26240
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.01888
英摘: In offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), agents estimate policies from a given dataset. We study reward-poisoning attacks in this setting where an exogenous attacker modifies the rewards in the dataset before the agents see the dataset. The attacker wants to guide each agent into a nefarious target policy while minimizing the $L^p$ norm of the reward modification. Unlike attacks on single-agent RL, we show that the attacker can install the target policy as a Markov Perfect Dominant Strategy Equilibrium (MPDSE), which rational agents are guaranteed to follow. This attack can be significantly cheaper than separate single-agent attacks. We show that the attack works on various MARL agents including uncertainty-aware learners, and we exhibit linear programs to efficiently solve the attack problem. We also study the relationship between the structure of the datasets and the minimal attack cost. Our work paves the way for studying defense in offline MARL.
中摘: 

在离线多智能体强化学习（MARL）中，智能体从给定数据集中推断策略。我们在该设定下研究奖励投毒攻击，其中外部攻击者在智能体接触数据集之前修改数据集中的奖励。攻击者旨在将每个智能体引导至一个恶意的目标策略，同时最小化奖励修改的 $L^p$ 范数。与针对单智能体强化学习的攻击不同，我们证明攻击者可以将目标策略实现为马尔可夫完美主导策略均衡（MPDSE），理性智能体保证会遵循该均衡。这种攻击的成本可以显著低于单独的单智能体攻击。我们展示了该攻击适用于各种 MARL 智能体，包括不确定性感知学习者，并提出了线性规划以高效解决攻击问题。我们还研究了数据集结构与最小攻击成本之间的关系。我们的工作为离线 MARL 中的防御研究奠定了基础。

[No.489] Learning to Shape Rewards Using a Game of Two Partners
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 学习使用两合伙人游戏塑造奖励
类别: multiagent systems
作者: David Mguni;Taher Jafferjee;Jianhong Wang;Nicolas Perez-Nieves;Wenbin Song;Feifei Tong;Matthew Taylor;Tianpei Yang;Zipeng Dai;Hui Chen;Jiangcheng Zhu;Kun Shao;Jun Wang;Yaodong Yang;
机构: Huawei;University of Manchester;Imperial College London;Shanghai Tech University;University of Alberta;Alberta Machine Intelligence Institute;University College London;Peking University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/26371
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2103.09159
英摘: Reward shaping (RS) is a powerful method in reinforcement learning (RL) for overcoming the problem of sparse or uninformative rewards. However, RS typically relies on manually engineered shaping-reward functions whose construction is time-consuming and error-prone. It also requires domain knowledge which runs contrary to the goal of autonomous learning. We introduce Reinforcement Learning Optimising Shaping Algorithm (ROSA), an automated reward shaping framework in which the shaping-reward function is constructed in a Markov game between two agents. A reward-shaping agent (Shaper) uses switching controls to determine which states to add shaping rewards for more efficient learning while the other agent (Controller) learns the optimal policy for the task using these shaped rewards. We prove that ROSA, which adopts existing RL algorithms, learns to construct a shaping-reward function that is beneficial to the task thus ensuring efficient convergence to high performance policies. We demonstrate ROSA's properties in three didactic experiments and show its superior performance against state-of-the-art RS algorithms in challenging sparse reward environments.
中摘: 

奖励塑造（RS）是强化学习（RL）中一种强有力的方法，旨在解决奖励稀疏或不具信息性的问题。然而，奖励塑造通常依赖于手动设计的奖励塑造函数，其构建过程既耗时又容易出错。此外，它还需要领域知识，这与自主学习的目标背道而驰。我们提出了强化学习优化塑造算法（ROSA），这是一种自动化奖励塑造框架，在该框架中，奖励塑造函数是在两个智能体之间的马尔可夫博弈中构建的。奖励塑造智能体（Shaper）利用切换控制来决定为哪些状态添加塑造奖励以实现更高效的学习，而另一个智能体（Controller）则利用这些塑造奖励来学习任务的最优策略。我们证明，采用现有 RL 算法的 ROSA 能够学习构建对任务有益的奖励塑造函数，从而确保高效收敛到高性能策略。我们通过三个示例实验展示了 ROSA 的特性，并表明其在具有挑战性的稀疏奖励环境中，相较于最先进的奖励塑造算法具有优越的性能。

[No.497] Robust Reward Placement under Uncertainty
来源: IJCAI 2024
命中: reward
标题: 稳健的奖励放置策略在不确定性条件下的应用
类别: Planning and Scheduling
作者: Petros Petsinis;Kaichen Zhang;Andreas Pavlogiannis;Jingbo Zhou;Panagiotis Karras;
机构: Aarhus University;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Baidu;University of Copenhagen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/748
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05433
英摘: We consider a problem of placing generators of rewards to be collected by randomly moving agents in a network. In many settings, the precise mobility pattern may be one of several possible, based on parameters outside our control, such as weather conditions. The placement should be robust to this uncertainty, to gain a competent total reward across possible networks. To study such scenarios, we introduce the Robust Reward Placement problem (RRP). Agents move randomly by a Markovian Mobility Model with a predetermined set of locations whose connectivity is chosen adversarially from a known set $Π$ of candidates. We aim to select a set of reward states within a budget that maximizes the minimum ratio, among all candidates in $Π$, of the collected total reward over the optimal collectable reward under the same candidate. We prove that RRP is NP-hard and inapproximable, and develop $Ψ$-Saturate, a pseudo-polynomial time algorithm that achieves an $ε$-additive approximation by exceeding the budget constraint by a factor that scales as $O(\ln |Π|/ε)$. In addition, we present several heuristics, most prominently one inspired by a dynamic programming algorithm for the max-min 0-1 KNAPSACK problem. We corroborate our theoretical analysis with an experimental evaluation on synthetic and real data.
中摘: 

我们研究了一个在网络中放置奖励生成器的问题，以便由随机移动的智能体收集奖励。在许多场景下，确切的移动模式可能是几种可能模式之一，这取决于不受我们控制的外部参数，例如天气状况。该放置方案应针对这种不确定性保持鲁棒性，从而在各种可能的网络中获取有效的总奖励。为了研究此类场景，我们引入了鲁棒奖励放置问题（RRP）。智能体依据马尔可夫移动模型随机移动，该模型包含一组预定的位置，其连通性是从一组已知的候选集 $\Pi$ 中以对抗方式选择的。我们的目标是在预算范围内选择一组奖励状态，以最大化在所有 $\Pi$ 候选者中的最小比率，即收集到的总奖励与该候选者下最优可收集奖励之比。我们证明了 RRP 是 NP 难且不可近似的，并开发了 $\Psi$-Saturate 算法，这是一种伪多项式时间算法，它通过以 $O(\ln |\Pi|/\varepsilon)$ 的因子超出预算约束，实现了 $\varepsilon$-加性近似。此外，我们提出了几种启发式算法，其中最突出的是受 max-min 0-1 背包问题动态规划算法启发的一种。我们通过合成数据和真实数据的实验评估来验证我们的理论分析。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.498] Solving Long-run Average Reward Robust MDPs via Stochastic Games
来源: IJCAI 2024
命中: reward
标题: 通过随机博弈求解长期平均奖励鲁棒MDP
类别: Planning and Scheduling
作者: Krishnendu Chatterjee;Ehsan Kafshdar Goharshady;Mehrdad Karrabi;Petr Novotný;Đorđe Žikelić;
机构: Institute of Science and Technology Austria;Masaryk University;Singapore Management University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/741
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.13912
英摘: Markov decision processes (MDPs) provide a standard framework for sequential decision making under uncertainty. However, MDPs do not take uncertainty in transition probabilities into account. Robust Markov decision processes (RMDPs) address this shortcoming of MDPs by assigning to each transition an uncertainty set rather than a single probability value. In this work, we consider polytopic RMDPs in which all uncertainty sets are polytopes and study the problem of solving long-run average reward polytopic RMDPs. We present a novel perspective on this problem and show that it can be reduced to solving long-run average reward turn-based stochastic games with finite state and action spaces. This reduction allows us to derive several important consequences that were hitherto not known to hold for polytopic RMDPs. First, we derive new computational complexity bounds for solving long-run average reward polytopic RMDPs, showing for the first time that the threshold decision problem for them is in $NP \cap coNP$ and that they admit a randomized algorithm with sub-exponential expected runtime. Second, we present Robust Polytopic Policy Iteration (RPPI), a novel policy iteration algorithm for solving long-run average reward polytopic RMDPs. Our experimental evaluation shows that RPPI is much more efficient in solving long-run average reward polytopic RMDPs compared to state-of-the-art methods based on value iteration.
中摘: 

马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）为不确定环境下的序列决策提供了标准框架。然而，MDPs 并未考虑转移概率中的不确定性。鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDPs）通过为每个转移分配一个不确定集而非单个概率值，解决了 MDPs 的这一不足。在这项工作中，我们考虑所有不确定集均为多面体的多面体 RMDPs，并研究求解长期平均奖励多面体 RMDPs 的问题。我们提出了该问题的一种新颖视角，并证明它可以归约为求解具有有限状态和动作空间的长期平均奖励轮流随机博弈的问题。这一归约使我们能够推导出一些此前尚未知对多面体 RMDPs 成立的重要结论。首先，我们推导了解决长期平均奖励多面体 RMDPs 的新计算复杂度界限，首次表明其阈值决策问题属于 $NP \cap coNP$，且它们存在一种具有亚指数期望运行时间的随机算法。其次，我们提出了鲁棒多面体策略迭代（RPPI），这是一种用于求解长期平均奖励多面体 RMDPs 的新型策略迭代算法。我们的实验评估表明，与基于值迭代的最先进方法相比，RPPI 在求解长期平均奖励多面体 RMDPs 方面要高效得多。

[No.502] BARA: Efficient Incentive Mechanism with Online Reward Budget Allocation in Cross-Silo Federated Learning
来源: IJCAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: BARA：高效的跨孤岛联邦学习在线奖励预算分配激励机制
类别: Machine Learning
作者: Yunchao Yang;Yipeng Zhou;Miao Hu;Di Wu;Quan Z. Sheng;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Guangdong Key Laboratory of Big Data Analysis and Processing;Macquarie University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2023/498
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.05221
英摘: Federated learning (FL) is a prospective distributed machine learning framework that can preserve data privacy. In particular, cross-silo FL can complete model training by making isolated data islands of different organizations collaborate with a parameter server (PS) via exchanging model parameters for multiple communication rounds. In cross-silo FL, an incentive mechanism is indispensable for motivating data owners to contribute their models to FL training. However, how to allocate the reward budget among different rounds is an essential but complicated problem largely overlooked by existing works. The challenge of this problem lies in the opaque feedback between reward budget allocation and model utility improvement of FL, making the optimal reward budget allocation complicated. To address this problem, we design an online reward budget allocation algorithm using Bayesian optimization named BARA (\underline{B}udget \underline{A}llocation for \underline{R}everse \underline{A}uction). Specifically, BARA can model the complicated relationship between reward budget allocation and final model accuracy in FL based on historical training records so that the reward budget allocated to each communication round is dynamically optimized so as to maximize the final model utility. We further incorporate the BARA algorithm into reverse auction-based incentive mechanisms to illustrate its effectiveness. Extensive experiments are conducted on real datasets to demonstrate that BARA significantly outperforms competitive baselines by improving model utility with the same amount of reward budget.
中摘: 

联邦学习（FL）是一种具有广阔前景的分布式机器学习框架，能够保护数据隐私。具体而言，跨机构联邦学习能够促使不同组织的孤立数据孤岛通过交换模型参数，与参数服务器（PS）在多个通信轮次中协作，从而完成模型训练。在跨机构联邦学习中，激励机制对于激励数据所有者参与联邦学习训练是不可或缺的。然而，如何在不同通信轮次之间分配奖励预算是一个至关重要但复杂的问题，且很大程度上被现有研究所忽视。该问题的挑战在于奖励预算分配与联邦学习模型效用提升之间存在不透明的反馈关系，这使得确定最优奖励预算分配变得复杂。为解决这一问题，我们设计了一种基于贝叶斯优化的在线奖励预算分配算法，命名为 BARA（反向拍卖预算分配）。具体而言，BARA 能够基于历史训练记录对联邦学习中奖励预算分配与最终模型精度之间的复杂关系进行建模，从而动态优化分配给每个通信轮次的奖励预算，以最大化最终模型效用。我们进一步将 BARA 算法集成到基于反向拍卖的激励机制中，以验证其有效性。我们在真实数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明，在相同奖励预算下，BARA 通过提升模型效用，显著优于竞争性基线方法。

[No.504] DEIR: Efficient and Robust Exploration through Discriminative-Model-Based Episodic Intrinsic Rewards
来源: IJCAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: DEIR：通过判别模型基于经验内在奖励的有效和稳健探索
类别: Machine Learning
作者: Shanchuan Wan;Yujin Tang;Yingtao Tian;Tomoyuki Kaneko;
机构: University of Tokyo;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2023/477
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.10770
英摘: Exploration is a fundamental aspect of reinforcement learning (RL), and its effectiveness is a deciding factor in the performance of RL algorithms, especially when facing sparse extrinsic rewards. Recent studies have shown the effectiveness of encouraging exploration with intrinsic rewards estimated from novelties in observations. However, there is a gap between the novelty of an observation and an exploration, as both the stochasticity in the environment and the agent's behavior may affect the observation. To evaluate exploratory behaviors accurately, we propose DEIR, a novel method in which we theoretically derive an intrinsic reward with a conditional mutual information term that principally scales with the novelty contributed by agent explorations, and then implement the reward with a discriminative forward model. Extensive experiments on both standard and advanced exploration tasks in MiniGrid show that DEIR quickly learns a better policy than the baselines. Our evaluations on ProcGen demonstrate both the generalization capability and the general applicability of our intrinsic reward. Our source code is available at https://github.com/swan-utokyo/deir.
中摘: 

探索是强化学习（RL）的一个基本方面，其有效性是决定 RL 算法性能的关键因素，尤其是在面对稀疏的外部奖励时。近期研究表明，通过基于观察中新颖性估计的内在奖励来鼓励探索是有效的。然而，观察的新颖性与探索之间存在差异，因为环境中的随机性和智能体的行为都可能影响观察结果。为了准确评估探索行为，我们提出了 DEIR，这是一种新颖方法，该方法理论推导了一个包含条件互信息项的内在奖励，该奖励主要与智能体探索所贡献的新颖性成正比，随后利用判别式前向模型实现了该奖励。在 MiniGrid 的标准和高级探索任务上进行的广泛实验表明，DEIR 比基线方法更快地学习到更优策略。我们在 ProcGen 上的评估展示了我们内在奖励的泛化能力与通用性。我们的源代码可在 https://github.com/swan-utokyo/deir 获取。

[No.505] Generative Flow Networks for Precise Reward-Oriented Active Learning on Graphs
来源: IJCAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 生成流网络：面向精确奖励导向的图上主动学习
类别: Machine Learning
作者: Yinchuan Li;Zhigang Li;Wenqian Li;Yunfeng Shao;Yan Zheng;Jianye Hao;
机构: Huawei;Tianjin University;National University of Singapore;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2023/438
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.11989
英摘: Many score-based active learning methods have been successfully applied to graph-structured data, aiming to reduce the number of labels and achieve better performance of graph neural networks based on predefined score functions. However, these algorithms struggle to learn policy distributions that are proportional to rewards and have limited exploration capabilities. In this paper, we innovatively formulate the graph active learning problem as a generative process, named GFlowGNN, which generates various samples through sequential actions with probabilities precisely proportional to a predefined reward function. Furthermore, we propose the concept of flow nodes and flow features to efficiently model graphs as flows based on generative flow networks, where the policy network is trained with specially designed rewards. Extensive experiments on real datasets show that the proposed approach has good exploration capability and transferability, outperforming various state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 

许多基于评分的主动学习方法已成功应用于图结构数据，旨在基于预定义评分函数减少标签数量并提升图神经网络的性能。然而，这些算法难以学习与奖励成正比的策略分布，且探索能力有限。在本文中，我们创新性地将图主动学习问题形式化为一种生成过程，命名为 GFlowGNN，该方法通过顺序动作生成多种样本，其概率与预定义奖励函数精确成正比。此外，我们提出了流节点和流特征的概念，基于生成流网络有效地将图建模为流，其中策略网络采用专门设计的奖励进行训练。在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法具有良好的探索能力和迁移能力，优于各种最先进的方法。

[No.507] Mitigating Disparity while Maximizing Reward: Tight Anytime Guarantee for Improving Bandits
来源: IJCAI 2023
命中: reward
标题: 减小差异同时最大化奖励：改进多臂老虎机算法的紧密任意时保证
类别: Machine Learning
作者: Vishakha Patil;Vineet Nair;Ganesh Ghalme;Arindam Khan;
机构: Indian Institute of Science;Arithmic Labs;Indian Institute of Technology Hyderabad;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2023/456
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2208.09254
英摘: We study the Improving Multi-Armed Bandit (IMAB) problem, where the reward obtained from an arm increases with the number of pulls it receives. This model provides an elegant abstraction for many real-world problems in domains such as education and employment, where decisions about the distribution of opportunities can affect the future capabilities of communities and the disparity between them. A decision-maker in such settings must consider the impact of her decisions on future rewards in addition to the standard objective of maximizing her cumulative reward at any time. In many of these applications, the time horizon is unknown to the decision-maker beforehand, which motivates the study of the IMAB problem in the technically more challenging horizon-unaware setting. We study the tension that arises between two seemingly conflicting objectives in the horizon-unaware setting: a) maximizing the cumulative reward at any time based on current rewards of the arms, and b) ensuring that arms with better long-term rewards get sufficient opportunities even if they initially have low rewards. We show that, surprisingly, the two objectives are aligned with each other in this setting. Our main contribution is an anytime algorithm for the IMAB problem that achieves the best possible cumulative reward while ensuring that the arms reach their true potential given sufficient time. Our algorithm mitigates the initial disparity due to lack of opportunity and continues pulling an arm till it stops improving. We prove the optimality of our algorithm by showing that a) any algorithm for the IMAB problem, no matter how utilitarian, must suffer $Ω(T)$ policy regret and $Ω(k)$ competitive ratio with respect to the optimal offline policy, and b) the competitive ratio of our algorithm is $O(k)$.
中摘: 

我们研究了改善型多臂老虎机（IMAB）问题，其中从某一臂获得的奖励随着该臂被抽取次数的增加而增加。该模型为教育和就业等领域中的许多现实问题提供了优雅的抽象，在这些领域中，关于机会分配的决定会影响社区的未来能力及其之间的差距。在此类环境中，决策者除了最大化截至任意时刻累积奖励的标准目标外，还必须考虑其决策对未来奖励的影响。在许多此类应用中，决策者事先并不知道时间范围，这促使我们在技术上更具挑战性的时间范围未知设定下研究 IMAB 问题。我们研究了在时间范围未知设定下出现的、看似冲突的两个目标之间的张力：a) 基于臂的当前奖励最大化截至任意时刻的累积奖励；以及 b) 确保长期奖励更好的臂获得充足的机会，即使它们初始奖励较低。我们要表明，令人惊讶的是，这两个目标在此设定下是相互一致的。我们的主要贡献是提出了一种用于 IMAB 问题的任时算法，该算法在确保臂在给定足够时间的情况下达到其真正潜力的同时，实现了可能的最佳累积奖励。我们的算法缓解了因缺乏机会而导致的初始差距，并持续抽取某臂直至其停止改进。我们通过证明 a) 任何针对 IMAB 问题的算法，无论多么功利，相对于最优离线策略都必须承受 $Ω(T)$ 的策略遗憾和 $Ω(k)$ 的竞争比，以及 b) 我们算法的竞争比为 $O(k)$，从而证明了该算法的最优性。
